══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 19 Holy Land? They showed no respect whatsoever!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

His aura was completely gone, like an ordinary person.

Impossible!

How could an ordinary person stand still under the pressure of a peak Half-Saint?

Absolutely impossible.

The old man felt a chill run down his spine.

But behind him were three warships, the very face of the Jade Balance Holy Land.

He was naturally unafraid; attacking him was futile without considering whether the Holy Land behind him would allow it!

"Did you kill my Holy Land's disciple?" the old man questioned in a deep voice.

No matter what, he was a peak Half-Saint!

There was no need for excessive politeness when questioning someone!

Gu Xuan remained silent.

The surrounding people whispered amongst themselves, saying that the Gu family was finished, what to do, how to face the Holy Land.

These words were overheard by the Half-Saint.

So the person before him was from the Gu family.

However, he had also heard that the Gu family had destroyed a Holy Land, indicating their extraordinary strength.

But attacking a disciple of the Jade Balance Holy Land was a slap in the face to the Jade Balance Holy Land, and it wouldn't end so easily!

"So you're from the Gu family. Do you know that the Jade Balance Holy Land is an ancient holy land, its foundation far surpassing that of the Purple Cloud Holy Land?"

"Destroying the Purple Cloud Holy Land certainly has the ability, but not much. Before absolute power, you'll still have to kneel!"

He paused, his voice rising!

Just then, a young voice came from the warship.

"Hand over the murderer! Otherwise, not a single member of your Gu family will live!"

A young man in a black Daoist robe stepped out of the warship, his face pale, his eyes icy.

This man was the Jade Balance Holy Son, Yin Wuji.

He looked down at Gu Xuan as if he were a dead man.

"Who in Qingyun Prefecture doesn't know the ways of the Jade Balance Holy Land?"

"Anyone who offends us will only face death!"

He was right.

The Jade Balance Holy Land had an extremely bad reputation; bullying men and women, and extortion were commonplace.

But because of their overwhelming power, ordinary people dared not speak out.

Many could only swallow their anger, daring not to voice any objections to the Jade Balance Holy Land.

At this moment, Yin Wuji stood on the warship, overlooking all living beings, as if everything was under his control.

The onlookers sighed.

"It's over, the Gu family has offended the Jade Balance Holy Land!"

"Back when there were five great holy lands, the Purple Cloud Holy Land was at the bottom. Jade Balance has a foundation spanning ten thousand years!"

"I heard that Jade Balance still has several Saint-level ancestors alive! Much stronger than Purple Cloud!"

"Even if the Gu family's ancestor is strong, can he be stronger than a ten-thousand-year-old holy land? The Gu family's ancestor is at most only a Saint!"

"What a pity!"

Just then, another voice rang out.

"Oh, I didn't expect it to be someone from the Gu family."

A woman dressed in white, with an exquisitely beautiful face, slowly stepped out.

She was the Heavenly Pivot Saintess, Su Xinghe!

A half-smile played on her lips as her gaze fell on Gu Xuan, carrying a hint of mockery.

"You think you're invincible just because you destroyed the Purple Cloud Holy Land? You even dared to kill people from the Jade Balance Holy Land! What a show of arrogance!"

"Don't you even respect our Heavenly Pivot Holy Land?"

Her words caused the onlookers to gasp.

The Heavenly Pivot Holy Land ranks second among the five great holy lands.

The Jade Balance Holy Land ranks third.

The two families have a close relationship, with generations of intermarriage.

Both have extremely bad reputations!

Su Xinghe's words clearly showed her support for the Jade Balance Holy Land.

The two great holy lands are joining forces to exert pressure.

Gu Chen's palms were sweating profusely.

This situation was completely unexpected; he had suddenly stirred up such a huge hole.

"This has truly caused the Ancestor great suffering!"

"I wonder if the Ancestor can handle it, after all, these are two great holy lands!"

"Ancestor!" Gu Chen called out softly.

Gu Xuan didn't turn around, only saying calmly.

"What are you afraid of?"

"Remember, from now on, the Gu family is the law when it comes to the outside world. If anyone is blind, tell the Ancestor, and the Ancestor will teach them a lesson!"

Gu Chen took a deep breath and straightened his back.

Yes, what's there to be afraid of!

The Ancestor is here!

Seeing Gu Xuan remain silent, Su Xinghe assumed he was afraid and smiled even more brightly.

"What, have you become mute? Weren't you quite imposing when you were killing people?"

"Now you know fear? Too late."

She turned to Yin Wuji and smiled, "Brother Yin, this kind of arrogant little family should be crushed. Let them know that in Qingyun Prefecture, the holy lands are the law."

Yin Wuji nodded, a cold glint flashing in his eyes.

"Since the Holy Maiden has spoken, then I will!"

Before he could finish speaking,

Gu Xuan moved.

Not by attacking.

He simply looked up and glanced at him!

Just one glance!

Boom!!!

Yin Wuji's mind went completely blank.

An indescribable fear surged from the depths of his soul!

It was as if an invisible giant hand had gripped his heart!

His face instantly turned deathly pale, his legs went weak, and he knelt directly on the warship!

"Holy Son!"

The elder beside him exclaimed, rushing to help him up.

But Yin Wuji trembled all over, unable to stand up at all!

He opened his mouth, wanting to speak, but no sound came out!

The entire arena fell silent.

Everyone stared blankly at Yin Wuji kneeling on the warship, their minds reeling!

What happened?

That person only glanced at him?!

Su Xinghe's smile froze on his face.

She instinctively took a step back, alarm bells ringing in her mind.

This person is strange!

The half-saint elder from the Jade Balance Holy Land's expression changed drastically; he was closest to Gu Xuan.

Just a moment ago, he had sensed an aura that sent shivers down his spine!

That wasn't a half-saint, not a saint, not even a Saint King!

That was!

He dared not think further.

"You…who exactly are you?" The elder's voice trembled.

Gu Xuan finally spoke.

His voice was soft, but everyone heard him clearly.

"Ancestor of the Gu family!"

"I'll give you three breaths to get out!"

"One."

The half-saint elder's face turned ashen.

He was an elder of the Jade Balance Holy Land, a peak half-saint; to be told to get out in front of so many people—where would he put his face?

But that aura just now was too terrifying!

"Two."

Su Xinghe gritted his teeth, wanting to say something, but seeing Yin Wuji still kneeling and trembling on the warship, he swallowed his words.

"Three."

The instant Gu Xuan's voice fell.

BOOM!!!

Three warships of the Jade Balance Holy Land exploded simultaneously!

Not attacked, but exploded out of thin air.

Runes shattered, spirit wood scattered everywhere.

Three priceless warships turned into countless fragments.

The elders and disciples on board were thrown about, landing awkwardly on the ground.

Yin Wuji remained kneeling, falling from the sky, his knees slamming into the ground, creating a deep crater!

The entire arena fell silent!

This was no joke!

Everyone was dumbfounded.

Three warships exploded at the same time?!

"What did he do? He didn't do anything!"

"He just said three numbers and the warships exploded?!"

"What the hell is this strength?!"

"A Saint? No! A Saint couldn't do that!"

"Could it be a Saint King?!"

Some people's legs went weak, some collapsed, and some even cried out in fear!

They never expected that the Gu family patriarch would actually attack the Jade Balance Holy Land!

This shattered their understanding!

The people from the Azure Cloud Holy Land and the Yao Guang Holy Land in the distance stood frozen in place, thankful they hadn't offended the Gu family!

Having one less competitor before even entering the secret realm was a huge advantage for them!

The elders of the Jade Balance Holy Land crawled out of the ruins, covered in wounds and in a sorry state.

They looked at Gu Xuan, their eyes filled with terror.

The elder at the peak of the Half-Saint realm was trembling all over, unable to even speak coherently.


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 20: The Secret Realm Opens, and We're Going to Make You Breathe!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Xuan didn't even glance at him.

He turned to Su Xinghe.

"You just said the Holy Land is heaven?"

Su Xinghe's face turned deathly pale, her lips trembled, and she couldn't utter a single word.

This scene was a complete shock to her!

Gu Xuan smiled.

His smile was very calm.

"In my eyes, what is the Holy Land?"

Su Xinghe trembled, staggering back several steps, almost falling off the imperial carriage.

The elders of the Tianxuan Holy Land quickly stepped forward to protect her, but fear was written on every face.

The entire arena was deathly silent!

The people from the Qingyun Holy Land and the Yaoguang Holy Land hadn't uttered a word from beginning to end.

Now, they silently retreated a distance.

They didn't care; it wasn't their problem.

Just then, a beam of light suddenly flashed in the distance, instantly shooting into the sky!

As the light appeared, someone immediately shouted excitedly.

"The secret realm has opened!"

"It's open! It's open! It's finally open! Charge!"

Instantly, countless cultivators soared into the air, rushing towards the secret realm!

Completely forgetting what had just happened.

For these rogue cultivators, obtaining an opportunity was paramount!

They hadn't offended the Holy Lands or the Gu family. If the two fought, they could be the first to rush in and perhaps even the first to obtain an opportunity!

However, the true powers and Holy Lands wouldn't be the first to enter; someone had to scout ahead!

After all, it was a Saint King's secret realm; they might have set up a magic array at the entrance!

The people from the Qingyun Holy Land and Yaoguang Holy Land didn't rush in but stood still to observe.

It was the people from the Yuheng Holy Land and Tianxuan Holy Land who reacted.

Yin Wuji scrambled to his feet, his face ashen, his eyes filled with resentment.

He glanced at the half-saint elder kneeling on the ground, still not recovered, then at Gu Xuan, his teeth itching with rage!

"And there's nothing we can do!"

"Let's go! First, into the secret realm!"

He lowered his voice and ordered those around him.

"Once we get the goods, we'll come back to settle accounts with the Gu family."

The Heavenly Pivot Saintess, Su Xinghe, also calmed down, a cold glint flashing in her eyes.

"Brother Yin is right, business is important. With our two holy lands joining forces, even the Azure Cloud Holy Land will have to think twice."

"The Gu family alone can't turn the world upside down!"

The two exchanged a glance and led their respective groups towards the entrance of the secret realm.

Just then!

Bang!

The few disciples of the Jade Balance Holy Land at the forefront seemed to have crashed into an invisible wall,

and were directly bounced back.

Several of them were bruised and battered!

"What happened?!"

Yin Wuji's expression changed, and he raised his hand forward.

His palm had only extended a short distance when it touched a transparent barrier.

It was incredibly hard, completely unmoved.

“There’s a barrier!”

Su Xinghe tried as well, but was also blocked.

She frowned and looked at Gu Xuan.

Gu Xuan stood with his hands behind his back, a faint smile playing on his lips.

“You!”

Yin Wuji instantly understood.

“It was you who did this!” Yin Wuji glared at Gu Xuan, his anger barely contained!

“Did I say you could go in? Everyone else can go in, but not a single person from either of your two holy lands can go in!”

Gu Xuan’s voice was calm, but every word exuded the oppressive power of a superior!

Yin Wuji trembled with rage.

When had he, the dignified Jade Balance Holy Son, ever suffered such humiliation?

"Don't think you're so great just because you destroyed the Purple Cloud Holy Land! The Jade Balance Holy Land has more than one Saint-level expert with ten thousand years of history! Can you afford to offend them?!"

"Even if you are a Saint, you'll face the consequences if you don't let us in today!"

Su Xinghe chimed in, "The Heavenly Pivot Holy Land and the Jade Balance Holy Land are interdependent. Can you, alone, withstand two great Holy Lands?"

The onlookers gasped.

With two great Holy Lands joining forces to exert pressure, could this Gu family patriarch withstand it?

Gu Xuan smiled.

His smile was faint.

Then, he raised his hand.

*Slap!*

A slap.

Slapped out from a distance.

The elder, at the peak of the Half-Saint realm, exploded!

Blood and flesh flew everywhere; he didn't even have time to scream.

Peak Half-Saint.

One slap!

Gone!

This was Gu Xuan's attitude!

"Get out of here within three breaths! I don't mind wiping out two more holy lands!"

We won't let them in; let them be driven mad!

Strength is the only true measure!

Yin Wuji's lips trembled. He wanted to say something, but seeing the pool of blood on the ground, he swallowed his words.

Su Xinghe tugged at his sleeve and whispered, "Let's go."

"But!"

"Do you want to die?"

Yin Wuji fell silent.

He glared fiercely at Gu Xuan, then turned and left.

"You wait! Our Yuheng Holy Land won't let you get away with this!"

He left with a harsh threat and slunk away from the entrance to the secret realm.

Su Xinghe followed with the people from Tianxuan Holy Land. Before leaving, he glanced back at Gu Xuan, his eyes filled with complex emotions.

The two great holy lands left just like that.

Still disheveled and utterly defeated.

The rogue cultivators who hadn't yet entered were dumbfounded.

"My god! Two great holy lands! They were just driven away like that?"

"Killed a peak half-saint with a single slap! Just what kind of monster is this Gu family ancestor?"

"Even a saint would probably need some time to kill a peak half-saint! He just slapped him to death like that?"

"Who would dare to provoke the Gu family now?"

Gu Chen stood aside, completely dumbfounded!

He knew his ancestor was strong!

But killing a peak half-saint with a single slap!

That was a half-saint!

A being above the Emperor realm, second only to a saint!

Just one slap?

He thought it would take some time!

Looks like destroying the Purple Cloud Holy Land was effortless!

Gu Qingshan's legs went weak, and he had to support Gu Chen to keep from collapsing.

"What realm is Gu Chen's ancestor at?!"

Gu Chen took a deep breath and uttered three words.

"I don't know."

He truly didn't know.

Great Emperor?

He wasn't sure.

Even a Great Emperor wouldn't be so nonchalant, would he?

Gu Xuan turned to look at them, his expression returning to calm.

"Alright, stop daydreaming. The secret realm is open. You should all go in quickly."

He looked at Gu Qingshan, then at the injured Gu family disciples.

"Your injuries have just healed. Once inside, follow Xiao Chenchen. Don't overexert yourselves."

Gu Qingshan and the others nodded emphatically: "Yes, Ancestor!"

Gu Xuan then looked at Gu Chen.

"Take good care of them. If you encounter danger, crush the jade talisman."

Gu Chen gripped his sword hilt, straightening his back.

"Don't worry, Ancestor, I will definitely bring them back safely!"

"Go."

Gu Chen, leading Gu Qingshan, Gu Qingqing, Gu Huahua, and several other Gu family disciples, headed towards the entrance of the secret realm.

This time, no one stopped them.

Even the people from the Qingyun Holy Land and Yaoguang Holy Land silently made way for them.

They watched the Gu family enter the secret realm.

They let the Gu family go first, none daring to walk ahead.

A flash of light, and the group disappeared at the entrance.

The leading elder of the Qingyun Holy Land withdrew his gaze and whispered to those around him:

"Report back to the Holy Lord, the Gu family must not be provoked!"

"This Gu family patriarch's strength is unfathomable, probably already at the Saint King level!"

They had only entered a Saint King-level secret realm, and now a living Saint King stood before them.

How could they not shudder!

…


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 21 Obtaining the Ancient Year Fruit, Heavenly Sword Mansion!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Time ticked by!

Within the secret realm, battles erupted constantly in every corner.

“Kid, I saw that first! Hand over that Ancient Fruit, and I might spare your life!”

“Heh, if you want it, come and get it yourself. I'm afraid you don't have the strength!”

“Arrogant!”

…

“Someone discovered the Ancient Fruit, and there are several! Let's go join the fun!”

“The one that ripens once every thousand years, each one guaranteeing a hundred years of lifespan increase, priceless and unavailable?!”

“The Ancient Fruit? No need to go, it's long gone. What are you going to do there?”

“So fast? Who got it? I'm willing to offer something of high value in exchange!”

“It was the Gu family. Those who saw the Gu family members ran away long ago. What's the point of fighting? Life is more important!”

“Damn it!”

“We definitely have to run! Otherwise, we don't know if we'll even get out!”

After all, Gu Xuan's pressure was too strong. If they attacked the Gu family in the secret realm…

He'd probably be greeted with a slap as soon as he stepped outside!

...

"Be careful, everyone! I heard the people from the Youluo Palace in Ziyun Prefecture are here too. They stole a lot of our things!"

"I heard the Qingyun Holy Son even fought them, and it was a close call!"

"Really? We'd better be careful!"

Deep within the secret realm.

Gu Chen and the others had no idea what was happening in the secret realm.

Gu Chen looked at the fruit in his hand, a pure gold color radiating a rich life force.

He couldn't be happier.

"One fruit adds a hundred years to my life! I'll present it to the Ancestor when I get back; he'll definitely be happy!"

In Gu Chen's eyes, adding a hundred years to the Ancestor's life was more important than any cultivation technique or weapon!

Little did he know, Gu Xuan had plenty of these, and each one added more than ten thousand years to his lifespan!

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua, however, were whispering amongst themselves, discussing something.

"Alright, put the fruit away and let's continue." Gu Chen put the Ancient Year Fruit into his storage bag.

"Brother Gu Chen!"

Gu Qingqing suddenly leaned over and blinked.

"What's wrong?"

"Which way do we go next? I feel like there's something on the left."

Gu Huahua nodded as well: "I feel like there's something on the right too."

Gu Chen frowned: "Don't do anything reckless! The Ancestor said to listen to me!"

"But the Ancestor also said to protect us!" Gu Qingqing said matter-of-factly.

"Yes, yes, if we get hurt, the Ancestor will definitely blame you!" Gu Huahua said with a half-smile.

They acted obediently in front of Gu Xuan, but things were different when Gu Xuan wasn't around!

The Demon Orb descended!

Gu Chen: "..."

He suddenly felt like he'd been tricked.

These two little girls were as obedient as cats in front of the Ancestor, how come they changed as soon as they entered the secret realm?

If he failed to protect those two little girls, his ancestor would definitely blame him!

He was truly caught between two equally unlucky men!

“You two!”

“Let’s go, let’s go, to the left!”

Gu Qingqing pulled Gu Huahua and ran to the left.

“Come back!”

Gu Chen quickly chased after them.

Gu Qingshan watched this scene and couldn’t help but laugh.

But his laughter quickly faded.

Because he realized that he couldn’t catch those two little girls at all.

The speed of a peak Spirit Sea Realm cultivator was several times faster than his Profound Realm cultivation!

If it weren't for Gu Chen leading the remaining people with his Imperial Sword, they wouldn't have been able to catch up with those two little girls at all.

"When did they become so strong?" Gu Qingshan asked in astonishment!

Everything that had happened was beyond his comprehension!

Gu Chen smiled wryly: "The Ancestor helped them activate their physiques and gave them rare and precious herbs. Now, the two of them together can even fight a King-level expert."

Gu Qingshan gasped.

"Then their strange illness?"

"It's not an illness, it's a top-tier physique. That's what the Ancestor said."

Gu Chen then went on to recount the events that had occurred in the Gu family over the past few days.

Every single event left Gu Qingshan speechless.

What had he missed during his months of training away from home?

The Ancestor emerged from seclusion, destroyed the Heavenly Nether Sect, helped Gu Chen awaken his physique, and then destroyed the Purple Cloud Holy Land.

He seemingly recruited a Saint as an underling, and then helped two little girls activate their physiques.

Every single event was beyond his comprehension!

"Gu Chen, what exactly is the Ancestor's realm?"

Gu Chen shook his head. "I don't know, but I think he's stronger than a Saint!"

Gu Qingshan's legs went weak.

Stronger than a Saint?

What does that even mean?

He didn't know, and dared not even think about it.

Inside the secret realm, Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua were like two wild horses.

They traversed mountains and waded through water, rushing towards any fluctuation of spiritual energy.

They weren't worried at all.

But this caused Gu Chen a lot of trouble!

"Left! Left! That flower is glowing!"

"Right! Right! There's fruit on that tree!"

The two little girls, one on each side, dragged Gu Chen in two different directions, almost tearing him in two!

"You two, calm down!"

But it was no use!

They encountered many cultivators along the way.

But those cultivators turned tail and ran as soon as they saw them, faster than if they'd seen a ghost.

Everyone knew they were from the Gu family.

They were terrified that if they ran too slow, they wouldn't be able to escape!

"Run! Run! They're from the Gu family!"

"Don't block the way! Let them pass first!"

"Those two little girls must be from the Gu family patriarch's side, right? We can't afford to offend them!"

A rogue cultivator was a little slower and was stopped by Gu Qingqing for directions. He turned pale with fright, stammering as he pointed, almost kneeling down and calling her "Grandma!"

Gu Qingqing blinked and turned to Gu Huahua, saying, "They seem really afraid of us."

"Of course! The patriarch is so powerful, who would dare mess with us?"

Gu Chen, listening from behind, couldn't help but twitch his lips.

These two little girls had really mastered the patriarch's reputation.

"Alright, alright, stop fooling around, let's go deeper in."

Gu Chen urged.

They came here for opportunities, not to scare people.

The deeper they ventured into the secret realm, the denser the spiritual energy became, and the ancient trees around them grew ever taller.

An ancient aura permeated the air.

Ahead lay a massive mansion, paved with bluestone, its steps made of white jade, its gates tightly shut.

Inscribed on the gate were two ancient characters—Heavenly Sword!

Gu Chen stopped, gazing at the scene before him.

“Heavenly Sword Mansion?” Gu Qingshan murmured.

“Could this be the former residence of the Heavenly Sword Saint King?”

Gu Chen’s eyes lit up.

He had heard from cultivators outside that the Heavenly Sword Saint King’s cultivation techniques and sword were within the secret realm.

If this truly was his mansion, those things were very likely inside!

“Let’s go in and take a look.”

Gu Chen pushed open the gate.

*Creak!*

The heavy stone door slowly opened, revealing a passage.

As soon as Gu Chen stepped across the threshold, he felt something watching them.

And not just one pair of eyes.

It was as if countless eyes were watching them.

Countless cold gazes from the darkness were scrutinizing their every move.

"Be careful." Gu Chen drew his Emperor Sword.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua also sensed it, and unusually quieted down, standing on either side of Gu Chen.

But the silence only lasted three seconds.

"What are you afraid of! Let's go in and see!"

Gu Qingqing pulled Gu Huahua and rushed inside.

"You two."

Gu Chen tried to stop them.

But before he could finish speaking, the two girls had already disappeared into the passage.

"Don't run so fast!"

Gu Chen sighed helplessly, and led Gu Qingshan and several disciples after them.

At the end of the passage was a spacious, empty hall.

The hall was empty, except for the floor covered with intricate runes.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua stood in the center of the hall, looking around.


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 22 Insulting my ancestor, I will make sure you die a gruesome death!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"There's nothing here!" Gu Qingqing felt that her brother Gu Chen was being overly cautious.

There seemed to be no danger here.

Boom!!!

Suddenly!

The hall shook violently!

The ground lit up, a blood-red light filling the entire space.

Several cracks appeared in the walls, and then figures emerged from the cracks.

Not living people, but puppets!

The puppets were entirely black, their eyes filled with eerie green flames.

Clearly, this was set up by the Saint King to prevent others from obtaining the items.

Each puppet exuded the aura of the peak of the Spirit Sea Realm, densely packed, numbering over a hundred.

Behind them were three even larger puppets, entirely silver-white, their auras incredibly powerful!

Fifth level of the King Realm!

"Brother Gu Chen, so many ghosts!" Gu Qingqing shrank back.

"What are you afraid of? Fight!" Gu Huahua, on the other hand, perked up.

Gu Chen drew his sword.

The Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword was unsheathed, its roar echoing like a dragon's roar!

"Brother Qingshan, retreat with the men!"

Before the words were even finished, hundreds of puppets moved simultaneously.

A dark, surging mass, like a tidal wave.

Gu Chen took a step forward, his sword light sweeping across!

Where the sword light passed, puppets shattered.

But there were too many of them; one strike felled one group, and more surged forward.

"Burn you all!"

Gu Qingqing struck out with her palm, crimson-gold flames transforming into a raging sea of ​​fire!

"Freeze you all!"

Gu Huahua unleashed her chilling energy, condensing it into countless ice spikes, pinning the puppets to the ground!

Flame and ice intertwined!

Gu Chen's sword moved like a dragon, each strike precisely striking the core of the puppets.

The three of them charged through the horde of puppets as if they were nothing.

In less than half an incense stick's time, all of the hundreds of Spirit Sea Realm puppets were reduced to fragments.

Only three silver-white King Realm puppets remained.

The three puppets activated simultaneously, their speed astonishing, rushing towards Gu Chen from three directions!

Gu Chen closed his eyes.

He drew his sword and slashed!

*Whoosh!*

A sword light, faster than lightning!

The three puppets froze simultaneously!

The next second, they exploded!

They shattered into countless fragments, scattering across the ground.

Gu Chen sheathed his sword: "That's it?"

This stunned Gu Qingshan and some of the Gu family disciples.

"Young Master has become so powerful?!"

"He can easily defeat King Realm puppets, is this still the Young Master I know?"

Gu Qingshan swallowed hard, looking at the puppets on the ground!

Suddenly, he felt that going out for training might have been a mistake!

Passing through the main hall, the group arrived at a secret chamber.

The secret chamber was devoid of any superfluous decorations, containing only a stone platform.

On the platform lay two objects:

A sword.

A book.

The sword was entirely black, its blade gleaming faintly.

On the yellowed book were three large characters: Heavenly Sword Manual!

Gu Chen was stunned.

The cultivators outside were right; the secret realm truly possessed the Heavenly Sword Saint King's sword and cultivation technique!

"Found it!" Gu Chen's hands trembled with excitement.

This was an item of Saint King level.

Returning it to his clan would surely earn him the ancestor's favor!

He stepped forward, preparing to reach for it.

Just then!

"Stop!"

A chilling voice came from behind.

Gu Chen turned around.

A group of people entered from outside the secret chamber.

Leading them was a young man in a long black robe, exuding a cold aura.

Behind him followed seven or eight men in black, each exuding a profound aura, the lowest being a King Realm expert.

“Our Youluo Palace wants this!”

The leader was Jiuyou of the Youluo Palace, who had come specifically to obtain the Saint King's weapon and cultivation technique!

Gu Chen frowned: “Youluo Palace?”

“The Youluo Palace of Ziyun Prefecture?” Gu Qingshan's expression changed, as if he had overthought something!

He leaned close to Gu Chen's ear and whispered, “It’s the Youluo Palace of Ziyun Prefecture. They specialize in murder and robbery.”

Gu Chen had heard of this force.

Ziyun Prefecture was a larger prefecture than Qingyun Prefecture.

The Youluo Palace was one of the most feared forces in Ziyun Prefecture, ruthless and never leaving survivors.

Most importantly, the Youluo Palace had three Saint Kings residing there, their strength far surpassing that of the holy lands of Qingyun Prefecture.

Seeing Gu Chen remain silent, Jiuyou assumed he was afraid and a cold smile crept across his face.

"If you know what's good for you, put your things down, and I might spare your lives. You people of Qingyun Prefecture may fear the Gu family, but I don't!"

"Your ancestor is nothing but a fart in front of my Youluo Palace! Even if I killed you all in the secret realm, he wouldn't dare do anything to me."

Gu Chen's eyes turned cold.

Daring to speak ill of his ancestor, this child is courting death!

"Brother Gu Chen, leave this guy to us!"

"Yes! Let him taste our power!"

Jiuyou sneered at the two girls' actions: "Two little girls at the Spirit Sea Realm dare to be arrogant in front of me? I, a fifth-level King Realm expert, can crush you with a single finger!"

"Since you're courting death, then I'll grant your wish!"

He raised his hand, and a cloud of black mist condensed in his palm.

Gu Chen stepped in front of the two girls.

"I'll handle it!"

He drew the Qianyuan Emperor Dragon Sword, its blade emitting a series of dragon roars.

The dragon's roar made Jiu You feel a little uneasy, but looking at the broken, scrap metal of his sword, it didn't look like a good sword at all.

"Using any kind of junk now, it's truly a case of the person being ruined and the sword being ruined too!"

With that, he flashed, black mist transforming into a giant claw that grabbed at Gu Chen!

A full-force attack from the fifth level of the King Realm, incredibly fast!

Gu Chen didn't retreat but advanced, drawing his sword and slashing!

Swish!

A flash of sword light, and the black giant claw was split in two!

Jiu You's expression changed drastically: "Not good!"

He immediately retreated, simultaneously using defensive techniques!

He didn't know that Gu Chen's swordsmanship was taught by Jian Wuji.

With the Emperor personally instructing him, even a single move was not something a King Realm expert could easily block!

Seeing that he couldn't block it, he immediately unleashed a powerful force, not only burning his life force but also using a forbidden secret technique!

"Enough!"

Jiuyou was enraged, unleashing his full power as a fifth-level King Realm cultivator!

The black mist surged, transforming into a black dragon that opened its maw to devour Gu Chen!

Gu Chen's eyes turned cold!

"Sword Draw Strike, Second Form!"

This strike was faster than before!

And more ruthless!

A flash of sword light, and the black dragon was cleaved in two from head to tail!

Jiuyou's pupils constricted, and he instinctively retreated.

But it was too late!

The sword light grazed his chest, leaving a deep wound!

Blood gushed out!

"You!"

Jiuyou clutched his chest, his face filled with disbelief!

Gu Chen didn't give him a chance to speak!

Another sword strike!

A single strike to the throat!

Jiuyou collapsed to the ground, his eyes wide open, dying with unfinished business!

The remaining men in black stood frozen in place, stunned. They had only exchanged two blows before being killed instantly by a single sword strike to the throat!

"How can he be so strong! Even the Holy Son of Qingyun couldn't defeat Jiuyou, how could this boy possess such power!"

"None of you will escape!"

Insulting the Ancestor, a crime deserving death!

The men turned and fled, but they dared not escape the Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword in Gu Chen's hand!

In swift strokes, not a single one remained alive!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 23 Accidentally Stumbled into the World, Turns Out It's My Home!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Chen and his companions were still exploring the secret realm.

Gu Xuan wasn't worried.

With Jian Wuji's guidance, the Q Emperor Sword in hand, and the two little girls helping out,

it would be too embarrassing for Gu Chen to have any problems.

[Ding: Please help your clan members quickly improve their cultivation. Within three days, please ensure the average cultivation level of your clan members reaches the King Realm!]

[Reward for completing the task: Emperor Summoning Card x3]

These past few days, Gu Xuan had been preoccupied with his own enjoyment and hadn't managed any clan affairs.

Now, the system had given him this task, clearly wanting the Gu clan to grow even faster!

Reaching the King Realm on average wasn't difficult for Gu Xuan.

Most of the Gu clan members had mediocre talent; reaching the King Realm through normal cultivation would be impossible.

That's because their talent was too poor, but he had previously obtained a Divine-level Marrow Cleansing Pill from a system check-in.

He could eat it like candy. As long as he cleansed the talent of his clan members, reaching the King Realm would be a piece of cake.

Thinking this, he turned and took a step forward.

The scenery before him shifted, and the back mountain of the Gu family was now at his feet.

Jian Wuji sat cross-legged atop the mountain, eyes closed in meditation.

Sensing Gu Xuan's return, Jian Wuji opened his eyes and immediately stood up.

Gu Xuan didn't speak, but walked straight to the main hall.

"Summon all the elders."

His voice wasn't loud, but it was clearly heard throughout the entire Gu family mountain range.

A moment later.

Inside the main hall.

Clan Chief Gu Tianhua.

First Elder Gu Yushan.

Second Elder Gu Kong.

Third Elder Gu Yun.

Fourth Elder Gu Shan.

Fifth Elder Gu Enming.

The six of them knelt on the ground.

"Ancestor, you've returned," Gu Tianhua said respectfully.

Gu Xuan sat in the main seat, glancing down at those below.

"Your cultivation levels are too low. You need to cultivate more diligently."

These words were blunt!

But no one dared to object!

The lowest level was the Profound Realm, and the highest was only the Spirit Sea Realm.

In Qingyun Prefecture, they wouldn't even qualify as a second-rate power.

If it weren't for Gu Xuan, they might only be a second-rate power at best.

Gu Tianhua lowered his head: "The Ancestor is right, but cultivation isn't something that can be achieved overnight."

"Who said it isn't something that can be achieved overnight?"

Gu Xuan interrupted him, raising his hand and waving it.

Six beams of light landed in the hands of the six people.

Each person held a golden pill, emitting a rich medicinal fragrance.

"This is a Marrow Cleansing Pill. Eat it."

The six people didn't hesitate and swallowed it directly.

Boom!!!

The instant the pill entered their stomachs, all six people's bodies trembled simultaneously!

A terrifying power exploded within their bodies!

The power of Marrow Cleansing rapidly spread throughout their bodies!

Gu Yushan was the first to sense the change.

All the impurities, blockages, and hidden injuries within his body were crushed and expelled by that power.

His blood boiled, and his skin oozed black filth, emitting a foul odor!

"This…this is!"

Gu Kong exclaimed in surprise, but before he could finish, he was interrupted by the earth-shattering changes within his body.

Spiritual energy circulated wildly in his meridians, each cycle several times faster than before.

The spiritual energy in the air seemed to have found its direction, surging frantically into their bodies.

The Gu Family Mountain Range already possessed a spiritual energy concentration 6666 times higher than normal, and now it was being frantically absorbed by the six of them, forming a swirling vortex of spiritual energy!

This Marrow Cleansing Pill not only cleansed their bodies, but also allowed them to comprehend a deeper level of meaning on a spiritual level!

Adding to their previous insights from the Enlightenment Tea!

Their cultivation began to skyrocket!

Gu Yushan's cultivation progressed from the third level of the Spirit Sea Realm…the fifth level of the Spirit Sea Realm…the eighth level of the Spirit Sea Realm…the peak of the Spirit Sea Realm…

The Emperor Realm!

Breakthrough!

The first level of the Emperor Realm…the third level of the Emperor Realm…the fifth level of the Emperor Realm…

Half-Saint!

The first level of Half-Saint…the fifth level of Half-Saint…the peak of Half-Saint!

It stopped.

But the power was still there, only his physical body couldn't withstand any more, suppressed by Gu Xuan!

Gu Kong experienced the same thing, soaring from the Profound Realm all the way, directly skipping the Spirit Sea Realm, the King Realm, and the Emperor Realm, forcefully reaching the peak of Half-Saint.

Third Elder Gu Yun, Fourth Elder Gu Shan, Fifth Elder Gu Enming.

Six people, five at the peak of Half-Saint!

Only one person was different!

That was Gu Tianhua!

As the clan leader, Gu Xuan gave him not only the Marrow Cleansing Pill, but also an extra source of power.

He felt the clan leader couldn't be weak!

Plus, Gu Tianhua's talent was considered the best among those present, so Gu Xuan added a little extra.

At the same time, Gu Tianhua's cultivation level soared from the Spirit Sea Realm, breaking through the Emperor Realm, the Half-Saint Realm, and directly approaching the Saint Realm!

Saint Realm Level Three…Saint Realm Level Six…Saint Realm Peak!

Stop.

Just one step away from Saint King!

Gu Tianhua opened his eyes, his entire body radiating the aura of a peak Saint, as if he had been reborn!

He looked younger.

His appearance had returned to that of someone in their twenties or thirties.

He looked at his hands, his face filled with disbelief!

"My God! I've become a Saint?"

He never dared to dream of it, never imagined he would actually become a Saint!

The hall was deathly silent!

The five elders stared at Gu Tianhua, then at themselves, speechless, their mouths agape.

Not only had they all become much younger, but their strength had also increased dramatically!

It was utterly unbelievable!

After a long pause.

Gu Yushan's voice trembled: "This! How is this possible?"

They had cultivated for decades, even centuries, trapped in the Spirit Sea Realm, unable to move beyond the Profound Realm.

Now, a single pill had propelled them directly to the peak of the Half-Saint Realm?

What was the point of all their previous hard work?

Wasn't fortune-telling better?

They wouldn't even dare to dream of doing this!

"What's impossible about it?" Gu Xuan leaned back in his chair, his tone calm.

"Now that you have cleansed your ordinary spiritual roots and reshaped them, reaching the Quasi-Emperor level shouldn't be a problem."

Quasi-Emperor?

Everyone's minds went blank.

They thought they were hallucinating from lack of sleep!

Quasi-Emperor? That was a legendary existence second only to the Great Emperor!

The Ancient Desolate Continent hasn't produced a Great Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years; Quasi-Emperors are the ceiling!

"Of course, that's assuming you don't slack off," Gu Xuan added.

Gu Tianhua took a deep breath, suppressing the turmoil in his heart.

"Ancestor's great kindness, Tianhua will never forget it!"

He knelt and kowtowed.

The five elders also knelt down.

"Ancestor's great kindness!"

Gu Xuan waved his hand: "Get up, it's not over yet."

Not over yet?

The six of them stared at each other, all seeing shock in each other's eyes.

Gu Xuan raised his hand and pulled things out of the system's inventory.

One, two, three…

Cultivation techniques.

Dozens of cultivation techniques were piled on the ground, stacked together like trash.

These were all cultivation techniques Gu Xuan didn't want.

The elders stared intently, almost fainting.

* **Saint-Level Cultivation Technique: Profound Heaven Overlord Body Technique**

* **Saint-King Level Cultivation Technique: Nine Revolutions Sacred Heart Technique**

* **Eternal Realm Cultivation Technique: Azure Sky Myriad Phenomena Scripture**

* **Quasi-Emperor Level Cultivation Technique: Great Void Divine Scripture**

...

Each one is more outrageous than the last!

Each one is more terrifying than the last!

“These cultivation techniques, pick the suitable ones to cultivate. They’re all junk, just make do with them,” Gu Xuan said casually.

What?!

This junk?

Gu Tianhua: “I’ve accidentally trespassed into the Heavenly Family…”

Wait!

This is my home!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 24 The Strike Hall is established, and the Ancestor asked me to pick up my son!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"Ancestor, are these... all for us?" Gu Yun's voice changed.

"Yes, the cultivation techniques in the clan's treasury are too old; it's time to replace them."

Gu Xuan spoke casually; these techniques were utterly useless to him.

Gu Tianhua picked up a quasi-Emperor-level cultivation technique, his hands trembling!

Quasi-Emperor-level cultivation techniques—not even the Gu family, let alone the Qingyun Holy Land, possessed them!

They couldn't possibly have them!

Otherwise, they'd be called an imperial clan!

Only quasi-Emperor-level forces could be called that!

The Ancestor just casually tossed them out?

And called them junk?

They were increasingly unable to understand their Ancestor's actions.

But for Gu Xuan, it was all basic stuff!

Gu Xuan continued, "Furthermore, I've designated an area on the front mountain and set up a Marrow Cleansing Array. From now on, every member of the Gu family can go in for a marrow cleansing once."

"After the marrow cleansing, talent will increase, and cultivation speed will be at least ten times faster."

What?!

Not only has their talent improved, but their cultivation speed has also increased accordingly. The Ancestor is taking care of them in every way!

Wait a minute!

Could the Ancestor be making arrangements for his own funeral?

The arrangements are so thorough; could it be that the Ancestor's time is near?

Thinking this, their eyes welled up with tears. The Ancestor has considered so much for them!

"Ancestor, we will take good care of the Gu family!"

"Yes! Ancestor, please take care!"

They were both pleading and lamenting.

Gu Xuan looked at them; it seemed like they were cursing him?

He saw through their thoughts.

"What are you thinking? I am still young. I don't want the Gu family to decline. I will make my Gu family the strongest force on this continent!"

Upon hearing this,

everyone present was shocked!

The strongest force!

That requires a force with a Great Emperor realm expert!

Great Emperor.

The highest realm on the Ancient Desolate Continent!

A realm that no one has reached for hundreds of thousands of years.

They were speechless for a moment; what had happened today had shattered their understanding of the world.

However, such things would happen frequently in the future!

Seeing their silence, Gu Xuan stood up: "You should first stabilize your cultivation levels, choose your cultivation techniques, and begin practicing tomorrow."

"Three months from now, I want to see a different Gu family."

He glanced at the six people still kneeling on the ground.

"Get up, all of you. Stop kneeling. From now on, the Gu family will live standing, not begging on their knees."

"Furthermore, I have established a 'Helper Hall.' If the Gu family encounters any trouble, they can seek help from the 'Helper Hall.'"

A 'Helper Hall'?

That sounds incredibly powerful.

And a place specifically for handling troublesome matters—the Ancestor's thoughtfulness is truly remarkable!

The group murmured amongst themselves.

Then the six stood up, but their legs were still weak.

Not out of fear, but because they were too shocked!

Gu Xuan walked out of the hall, leaving the six people standing there in a daze.

Gu Yushan looked down at his hands, then at the pile of "junk" on the ground, and suddenly laughed.

Gu Tianhua smacked him on the head from behind, "Look at you, so pathetic! How are you going to follow the Ancestor in the future?"

"Don't stand there like an idiot. The Ancestor gave us so much, not so we could cry. Now choose your cultivation method, and then cultivate!"

The five nodded emphatically.

That night, a forbidden area appeared on the back mountain of the Gu family.

The Marrow Cleansing Array.

The family members lined up to enter, and when they came out, they were all refreshed and invigorated.

Never before had they felt so refreshed!

"Awesome! I've never felt like this before! No wonder it's the Ancestor; even my mind has become sharper!"

"It's even better than being in a pipe! I want to stay here forever!"

"I feel like I'm about to break through!"

Those Gu family members with mediocre talent, after the marrow cleansing, saw their talent rival that of geniuses.

Those clansmen who had been stuck at their bottlenecks for years broke through on the spot after the marrow cleansing.

Overnight, the Gu family's average cultivation level jumped from the Profound Realm to the King Realm.

This left the elders in disbelief!

But it was the truth!

Back Mountain.

The system's voice rang out.

[Congratulations on completing the mission; the clan's average cultivation level has reached the King Realm. Reward: Great Emperor Realm Summoning Card x3]

Gu Xuan looked at the reward summoning cards, not in a hurry to summon them. Including the Great Emperor Summoning Cards he already had,

he already had four.

Ten cards were enough to upgrade; maybe one upgrade would even summon an immortal!

Jian Wuji walked over and stood beside him.

"Master."

Gu Xuan opened his eyes and glanced at the starry sky.

"By the way, tomorrow you'll give those elders a lesson, teach them how to use the power of a half-saint."

"A bunch of newly-broken-saints who can't even control their power, what a disgrace!"

Jian Wuji nodded: "Yes."

Just as Gu Xuan finished instructing Jian Wuji, Gu Tianhua emerged from the back mountain.

His steps were swift as the wind, radiating the aura of a peak saint, making him appear twenty years younger.

"Ancestor." Gu Tianhua bowed respectfully.

Gu Xuan glanced at him: "Something wrong?"

"Ancestor, about the secret realm… I want to go pick up Chen'er and the others." Gu Tianhua rubbed his hands together and chuckled.

"You see, I just broke through to the peak of sainthood, I need to find an opportunity to practice, don't I?"

"Besides, as a father, I need to let my son know that his father is now a saint too!"

Gu Xuan glanced at him.

"Just say you want to show off."

"And you've made it so complicated!"

"Go," Gu Xuan waved his hand, not caring at all.

Gu Tianhua was overjoyed: "Thank you, Ancestor!"

He turned to leave, but Gu Xuan called him back.

"Wait."

Gu Tianhua turned back.

Gu Xuan casually tossed him a jade talisman: "If you encounter something you can't handle, crush it."

Gu Tianhua caught the talisman, nodded emphatically, and disappeared into the night.

That very night, Gu Tianhua set off.

At the peak of the Saint realm, the journey from the Gu family to the secret realm was merely the time it takes for an incense stick to burn.

But he didn't leave the secret realm directly. Instead, he found a mountain outside, sat down cross-legged, and familiarized himself with his power.

He was waiting!

Waiting for his son to emerge.

And also waiting to see if anyone dared to cause trouble.

He wanted them to know his strength as a peak Saint!

If not now, when?

...

Inside the secret realm.

The group was completely unaware of the earth-shattering changes that had occurred within the Gu family!

They continued deeper into the realm.

Over the next few days, they reaped a rich harvest.

Ancient Year Fruit, the Heavenly Sword Saint's sword and cultivation techniques, and various rare and precious materials—their storage bags were almost overflowing.

More importantly, Gu Chen had broken through.

The bottleneck at the peak of the Spirit Sea Realm loosened during a series of battles, and after killing Jiu You, he broke through in one fell swoop.

Reaching the first level of the King Realm!

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua were not lagging behind either; the two little girls were getting better and better at coordinating their attacks in battle.

The combination of ice and fire was seamless, and both of them broke through to the first level of the King Realm.

Three King Realm experts, two of whom possessed special physiques, plus the Emperor Sword in Gu Chen's hand, their combat power far surpassed that of others of the same level.

“Brother Gu Chen, aren’t we amazing now?” Gu Qingqing asked, raising her little face.

“Not bad.” Gu Chen sheathed his sword.

“Tch.” Gu Qingqing pouted.

“You’re just jealous that we broke through faster than you.”

Gu Chen ignored her.

That’s right, she was jealous!

They broke through too quickly!

Gu Qingshan followed behind, watching the three figures, his feelings complicated.

A few days ago, he thought his first level of the Spirit Sea Realm was pretty good.

Now?

With three King Realm experts clearing the way ahead, he didn’t even have the right to interfere!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 25: I thought it was going to be a fight, but it turned out to be about making friends.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"Brother Qingshan, don't just stand there, let's go." Gu Chen called back.

Gu Qingshan smiled wryly and quickly followed.

The group continued forward, encountering more and more cultivators along the way.

But everyone who saw the Gu family's attire silently made way; their names had already resounded throughout the entire secret realm.

No one dared to provoke them!

Not because they feared Gu Chen, but because they feared Gu Xuan!

Until they reached an open area, they encountered two other great holy lands.

The Yao Guang Holy Land and the Qing Yun Holy Land.

The people from the two holy lands were gathered together, resting.

"The Holy Son has received news." A disciple from the Qing Yun Holy Land whispered a few words in Li Shuangxu's ear.

Li Shuangxu's pupils constricted upon hearing this.

"What did you say? Jiu You is dead?"

The disciple nodded: "Jiu You of the You Luo Palace was killed. Right here in the secret realm."

"Who did it?"

The disciple glanced at Gu Chen, not speaking, but the meaning was clear.

Li Shuangxu fell silent.

Jiuyou, a fifth-level King Realm cultivator, a genius disciple of the Youluo Palace.

He knew that facing Jiuyou was unpredictable.

Gu Chen actually killed Jiuyou?

How could this be!

Outside, he was only at the peak of the Spirit Sea Realm, and now he's only at the King Realm!

He actually managed to kill Jiuyou, a fifth-level King Realm cultivator, despite being a much higher level!

"Is the information reliable?" Li Shuangxu's voice was hoarse.

"Reliable. A rogue cultivator witnessed it firsthand. Gu Chen slit Jiuyou's throat with a single sword strike; Jiuyou couldn't even escape."

He never expected that his evenly matched opponent would be killed by Gu Chen with a single sword strike!

It seems the Gu family is even more terrifying than he imagined!

In this situation, the only option is to be on good terms, not to antagonize them!

The other rogue cultivators in the secret realm avoided the Gu family altogether.

"Have you heard? Gu Chen killed Jiu You of the Youluo Palace!"

"Really? Jiu You was a fifth-level King Realm expert!"

"Absolutely! Someone saw it with their own eyes, a single sword strike to the throat!"

"My god... Gu family patriarch was terrifying enough, but this younger generation is this powerful too?"

"So never mess with the Gu family. You can't beat the younger ones, and you definitely can't beat the older ones."

Amidst the discussions, Gu Chen led his men past the two holy lands.

Gu Chen led his men past the two holy lands.

His steps didn't stop, his gaze didn't waver, as if he hadn't seen them at all.

Li Shuangxu watched Gu Chen's retreating figure, his brows slightly furrowed.

Jiu You was dead.

Killed by Gu Chen.

He considered himself talented and powerful; the title of the number one young talent in Qingyun Prefecture wasn't for nothing.

But against Jiu You, he wasn't confident of victory.

At best, a fifty-fifty chance.

But Gu Chen, with a single, swift strike to the throat, delivered a clean and decisive blow.

What did this mean?

It meant that Gu Chen's strength was at least on par with his own.

A mere youth who had just broken through to the King Realm had managed to kill a fifth-level King Realm expert with a single, decisive strike!

Li Shuangxu took a deep breath.

This wasn't a matter of talent.

The Gu family's ancestor was definitely a powerful figure!

"Follow them."

Li Shuangxu suddenly spoke.

The person beside him was taken aback: "Holy Son?"

Gu Chen walked on, sensing someone following him, and frowned.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua also became alert, turning their heads to the left and right respectively.

"Brother Gu Chen, someone is following us."

Li Shuangxu approached with two men, stopping about ten paces away.

"Brother Gu, please wait," Li Shuangxu said, cupping his hands in a smile.

Gu Chen glanced at him. "What is it?"

His attitude wasn't exactly friendly.

Li Shuangxu didn't seem to mind, smiling. "I am Li Shuangxu, the Holy Son of the Qingyun Holy Land. I mean no harm, I simply wish to make friends with Brother Gu."

Make friends?

Gu Chen was slightly taken aback.

He had thought they were coming to fight.

After all, these past few days in the secret realm had been filled with people stealing, causing trouble, and threatening others.
