He continued, "I know the Gu family is currently very powerful, and many people are watching you. But having one more friend is always better than having one more enemy."

"Although the Qingyun Holy Land isn't a particularly formidable force, it still carries some weight in Qingyun Prefecture."

Gu Chen was silent for a moment.

He remembered his ancestor's words: the Gu family is like heaven when they're out in the world.

Heaven doesn't need to be subservient to others, but it also doesn't need to reject goodwill.

"Since Brother Li thinks so highly of me, I'll accept this friendship."

Gu Chen didn't refuse; he also wanted to see just how strong this so-called number one genius youth in Qingyun Prefecture really was.

If there's an opportunity, we can spar!

Li Shuangxu smiled, a genuine smile.

He took out a token from his storage bag and handed it to Gu Chen.

"This is my guest elder token from the Qingyun Holy Land. Brother Gu, if you have time in the future, you are welcome to visit the Qingyun Holy Land. I can't guarantee anything else, but good tea and wine will be plentiful."

Gu Chen took the token, glanced at it, and put it into his storage bag.

"I will definitely come when I have the chance."

After a pause, he also took something out of his storage bag.

It was a wooden plaque engraved with the character "Gu," made by the Gu family themselves, originally used to identify individuals.

Now it could serve as a token.

"This is a token of the Gu family. Brother Li, if you have time, you are also welcome to visit the Gu family. The place isn't large, but our ancestor is hospitable."

Li Shuangxu took the wooden plaque and solemnly put it into his robes.

The disciple beside him twitched his lips.

Is a mere wooden plaque really worth this much trouble?

But Li Shuangxu didn't think so.

In his view, the value of this wooden plaque far exceeded any guest elder token.

It represented the Gu family's attitude.

"Certainly." Li Shuangxu cupped his hands in greeting.

"The secret realm is about to close, Brother Gu, be careful on your journey. Things may not be peaceful outside."

Gu Chen nodded, turned, and led his men away.

Li Shuangxu stood there, watching them depart.

A disciple beside him couldn't help but ask, "Holy Son, was it worth it? Although the Gu family has a powerful ancestor, they've offended quite a few people—Yuheng, Tianxuan, and now even the Youluo Palace of Ziyun Prefecture."

Li Shuangxu's smile faded, and he glanced at him indifferently.

"What do you know? I couldn't even subdue Jiuyou, but Gu Chen did. This shows the Gu family's foundation far exceeds our imagination. With people like that, not befriending them is tantamount to making enemies."

"As for Yuheng and Tianxuan?"

He scoffed.

"When the Gu family's ancestor truly makes his move, you'll know what those two holy lands are worth."

"As for the Youluo Palace, it wasn't originally part of our Qingyun Prefecture. This secret realm is only so big; if others want a piece, no one will agree."

The disciple opened his mouth, but then remained silent.

...


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 26 A Saint Blocks the Way, Father! What brings you here?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The journey to the secret realm was nearing its end.

At the entrance, light flashed, and groups of cultivators were teleported out.

Gu Tianhua sat cross-legged atop the mountain. Seeing people emerge, his eyes lit up.

But after scanning the area, he didn't see his son.

He continued to wait.

Another group emerged.

Still no one.

Gu Tianhua wasn't in a hurry and closed his eyes to rest.

Suddenly, he sensed several hostile gazes.

He opened his eyes and saw several groups of people staring at him in the distance.

Judging by their attire, they were from the Jade Balance Holy Land and the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land.

They had been driven out of the secret realm by Gu Xuan and had returned to their holy lands to fetch reinforcements. They were waiting outside the secret realm with a large force.

They planned to wipe them out as soon as they emerged!

They wanted to show them that the holy lands could not be insulted!

The two holy lands joining forces to deal with them was a great show of respect for the Gu family!

Seeing the Gu family's attire, their eyes turned icy, as if they were looking at an enemy!

An elder from the Jade Balance Holy Land sneered, "Gu family members? Are you here to wait for death?"

Gu Tianhua glanced at him but remained silent.

He didn't know what had happened and didn't want to have any further dealings with them.

What kind of strength dared to speak to him like that?

But with his current strength, none of those present could defeat him!

Seeing his silence, the elder assumed he was afraid and continued, "Your Gu family's ancestor won't be arrogant for long. When our Holy Land's Saint Ancestor emerges from seclusion, it will be the time of your Gu family's annihilation!"

Gu Tianhua still didn't speak.

He silently memorized the elder's face.

How dare he speak ill of his own ancestor! This person must not be allowed to live!

He'd deal with it after his son came out.

No rush.

He was already thinking about whether to handcuff or hang that man!

Either way, it was a certain death!

Seeing Gu Tianhua remain silent, the elder of the Jade Balance Holy Land assumed he had backed down, sneered, and ignored him.

"Just wait, when that brat from the Gu family comes out, we'll take care of him too!"

Let him enjoy his last moments here!

Behind him stood an old man in gray robes, his aura profound, who remained silent throughout.

That was the Grand Elder of the Jade Balance Holy Land, a Saint-level expert.

Beside him stood another old man in gray robes from the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land, also a Saint-level expert.

Each of the two holy lands had dispatched one Saint, one peak Half-Saint, and dozens of outer sect elders of the Emperor realm.

This display, ostensibly to annihilate a family, was more of a show of force.

They wanted to see just how powerful the Gu family was, daring to disregard them so blatantly!

"The two holy lands have joined forces, sending out Saints; the Gu family is probably doomed," a rogue cultivator muttered softly.

"It's hard to say. The Gu family's ancestor killed Yuheng's half-saint with a single slap!"

"Can a half-saint be compared to a saint? A saint is on a completely different level!"

"That's true. One saint might be able to fight, but two saints... it's doubtful!"

"Exactly! One person against two saints, how could they possibly win?"

Amidst the discussions, the teleportation lights of the secret realm flashed one after another.

For these rogue cultivators, the two great holy lands joining forces against a single family was unprecedented.

They wondered if the Gu family truly possessed genuine strength.

After all, both of these holy lands had been established for tens of thousands of years!

Batch after batch of cultivators were teleported out, and upon seeing the scene outside, their expressions changed, and they quickly hid to the side.

Gu Tianhua sat atop the mountain, remaining motionless from beginning to end.

He glanced at the gray-robed elder from the Jade Balance Holy Land, then at the gray-robed elder from the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land.

Two Saints.

One at the Second Heaven, the other at the Fourth Heaven.

He weighed the options in his mind.

He could defeat them.

But it would take some effort.

He then looked at his own hands; the power of a peak Saint flowed within him.

It didn't seem to require too much effort!

Just then, the entrance to the secret realm shone brightly.

Gu Chen, Gu Qingqing, Gu Huahua, Gu Qingshan, and several other Gu family disciples emerged from the light.

"They're out!"

Someone shouted!

Gu Chen had barely steadied himself when he sensed something was wrong.

Two terrifying pressures descended from the sky, locking onto them.

One was a Saint at the Second Heaven, the other a Saint at the Fourth Heaven!

Immediately afterward, two more pressures from peak Half-Saints joined in.

The four auras intertwined, pressing down relentlessly.

Gu Chen's expression changed slightly.

The gap between a first-level King Realm cultivator and a Saint was too vast!

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua turned pale, but gritted their teeth and didn't retreat.

Gu Qingshan fared even worse, kneeling directly, his knees slamming into the ground, drawing blood.

"Brother Qingshan!" Gu Chen tried to help him up, but the oppressive pressure rendered him immobile.

An elder from the Jade Balance Holy Land stood not far away, looking down at the scene with a mocking smile.

"The Gu family? Is that all? I thought they were so powerful."

"Weren't you very arrogant before? Killing my Holy Land disciples, preventing my Holy Land from entering, stealing my Holy Land's opportunities!"

"Now, show me how arrogant you can be!"

He pointed at Gu Chen.

"Kneel down!"

Those two words, carrying the pressure of a peak Half-Saint, pressed down like a mountain.

Gu Chen's knees bent, but he stubbornly held on.

"I told you to kneel!" the elder's voice rose, the pressure intensifying.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua couldn't hold on any longer; the two little girls were almost collapsing to the ground, but they were still desperately trying to lift their heads.

"Brother Gu Chen won't kneel, and neither will we!"

"That's right! We won't kneel!"

The elder of the Jade Balance Holy Land had a grim face.

"You're courting death."

He raised his hand and struck down with a palm.

This palm strike, a full-force attack from a peak Half-Saint, was enough to turn Gu Chen and the others into mincemeat.

Just then!

A hand reached out from the side!

It effortlessly caught the palm strike.

The elder's pupils shrank.

Gu Tianhua had appeared in front of Gu Chen at some point, holding the elder's wrist firmly with one hand.

"Father?!" Gu Chen was stunned.

Gu Tianhua didn't turn around, but simply looked at the elder of the Jade Balance Holy Land and smiled.

"Who did you just say you wanted to kneel?"

The elder's face changed drastically. He tried to pull his hand back, but Gu Tianhua's hand was like an iron clamp, unmoving.

"You!"

Before he could finish speaking,

Gu Tianhua casually flicked his wrist.

The elder was sent flying thousands of meters, crashing into a mountain with a deafening roar, causing half the mountain to collapse.

He was left half-dead!

Hiss!

The entire arena fell silent.

The people of the Jade Balance Holy Land were dumbfounded.

The people of the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land were also dumbfounded.

The scattered cultivators watching were even more dumbfounded.

"This...who is this person?!"

"A member of the Gu family?! Could it be that a Saint from the Gu family has made a move?!"

"That flick, and a peak Half-Saint was powerless to resist. This strength must be that of a Saint!"

Gu Tianhua clapped his hands and turned to look at Gu Chen.

"Are you alright?"

Gu Chen opened his mouth, then finally managed to stammer out, "Father, when did you become a Saint?"

Although he didn't know why his father had come all this way, what the hell was this Saint Realm?

Gu Tianhua grinned, "Just these past two days, the Ancestor gave me a piece of candy."

Gu Chen fell silent.

Given by the Ancestor.

Given a piece of candy?

What kind of justice is this?

His son toiled his way to the King Realm, while his father became a Saint overnight with just a piece of candy?

What about all his hard work then?

Those few words sounded light, but carried a terrifying weight.

Giving someone a peak Saint Realm, as easily as giving them a piece of candy.

The gray-robed elder opened his eyes, looking at Gu Tianhua, his expression shifting from composure to shock!

"Peak Saint Realm!"

His voice wasn't loud, but everyone present heard it!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 27: They're all saints! I didn't expect it to turn into a blow!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The moment those four words were uttered, the gray-robed elder's face turned ashen.

The second-level Saint of the Tianxuan Holy Land instinctively took a step back!

Peak Saint!

What did that mean?!

The ninth level of Sainthood was a chasm, each level a formidable barrier. Even if they, a second-level and a fourth-level Saint, joined forces, they wouldn't be enough to defeat a peak Saint.

“Retreat!”

The gray-robed elder made a decisive decision.

But as soon as he turned around, he crashed into an invisible wall.

Boom!

He was bounced back, staggering several steps before regaining his footing.

“Trying to run?”

Gu Tianhua stood still, not even raising a hand, merely glancing at them.

The surrounding air suddenly solidified, an invisible barrier completely enveloping the area within a thousand miles.

“The Four-Sided Prison!”

The name Gu Tianhua casually coined was actually the domain power of a peak Saint.

Although he didn't possess many supernatural abilities, the suppression of his cultivation level was more than enough!

"You!" The gray-robed elder's expression changed drastically!

"Oh! Weren't you quite the braggart just now? You even made my son kneel? You wanted my Gu family destroyed? Why are you running now?"

Gu Tianhua laughed.

The gray-robed elder gritted his teeth, looking at the saint from the Tianxuan Holy Land. The two exchanged a glance and attacked simultaneously!

Two saint-level attacks, one from the left and one from the right, aimed directly at Gu Tianhua!

Gu Tianhua didn't even move.

He raised his hand and pressed down with a palm.

This palm strike, without any fancy moves, contained an incomparably powerful strike from a peak saint!

The power of a peak saint was simply beyond the capabilities of a second or fourth level cultivator!

Boom!!!

The two attacks were crushed in mid-air.

The residual force of the palm strike didn't diminish, directly striking the two men.

The gray-robed elder's chest caved in, blood gushing out as he crashed to the ground, twitching twice before going still!

The saint from Tianxuan Holy Land fared even worse; he was slammed into the ground, only his head visible, eyes wide open, lifeless.

Two saints, annihilated with a single palm strike.

Another elder at the peak of the half-saint realm, witnessing this scene, collapsed to his knees!

"Spare me, spare my life!"

But it was too late!

Gu Tianhua didn't even glance at him, casually waving his hand.

The half-saint's body exploded, turning into a cloud of blood mist.

The disciples of Yuheng Holy Land and Tianxuan Holy Land collapsed to the ground, too exhausted even to run.

The onlookers, the rogue cultivators, gaped, their minds blank.

"Two...two Saints killed with a single palm strike?!"

"Peak Saint! This person from the Gu family must be a peak Saint!"

"My god! Just what kind of monsters are the people in the Gu family?!"

"The ancestor is unfathomable, there's a peak Saint expert, and another King-level expert who can kill enemies beyond their level!"

"It's over, it's over, the world of Qingyun Prefecture is about to change."

Gu Chen stood behind Gu Tianhua, looking at his father's back.

His mouth opened and closed, closed and opened again.

Not only was he incredibly strong, but he also looked much younger; he could practically be his brother!

He had so much to say, but he couldn't utter a single word.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua, the two little girls, were the first to react.

"The Clan Leader is amazing!" Gu Qingqing jumped up.

"He killed two bad guys with a single slap!" Gu Huahua's eyes sparkled.

Gu Tianhua chuckled and turned to look at Gu Chen.

"How was it? Wasn't your dad awesome? Handsome?"

Gu Chen took a deep breath, holding it in for a long time before finally managing to stammer, "Father, how did you become a Saint?"

"The Ancestor gave me a piece of candy, and I broke through after eating it."

Gu Chen: "..."

He suddenly felt like a fool for risking his life in the secret realm!

Gu Qingshan was even more exhausted. What about all his previous arduous cultivation outside?

"Clan Leader, are you really at the peak of Sainthood?"

"Of course!" Gu Tianhua puffed out his chest.

Gu Qingshan's legs went weak.

What was all this about?! He clearly remembered that the Clan Leader was only at the peak of the Profound Realm before!

Now he's at the peak of Sainthood?

He went out to train for a few months, and when he returned, he found that the Gu family had destroyed the Heavenly Nether Sect, destroyed the Purple Cloud Holy Land, killed the Saint of Jade Balance Heavenly Pivot, and the Clan Leader had become a peak Saint?

How much had he missed?

He hadn't believed before that the Ancestor was so powerful,

but now the facts proved it was all true!

"Alright, alright, I've come to take you home. Let's talk about everything once we're home!"

He glanced at the entrance to the secret realm, where cultivators were continuously being teleported out.

The disciples of Yuheng Tianxuan were still sprawled on the ground, none daring to move.

Gu Tianhua pointed at them. "You, go back and tell your Holy Lord that if you want revenge, the Gu family will take care of it!"

"But next time, it won't be as simple as just losing two Saints!"

This was the confidence that came with strength; if you're not satisfied, then keep quiet!

With that, he waved his hand, taking Gu Chen and the others into the air, flying towards the Gu family.

Behind them, the cultivators outside the entrance to the secret realm watched them leave, speechless for a long time.

...

After another half-incense stick's time, the entrance to the secret realm flashed, and Li Shuangxu emerged with the people of Qingyun Holy Land.

He had barely stood still when he sensed something was wrong.

The air smelled of blood, and there were large pools of blood on the ground.

Half of the distant mountainside had collapsed, and signs of battle were everywhere.

A vague unease crept in.

“What happened?” Li Shuangxu frowned.

An elder from the Qingyun Holy Land, who had witnessed the entire event from outside the secret realm, quickly stepped forward and whispered the story.

Li Shuangxu's pupils contracted slightly upon hearing it.

“The Gu family patriarch, a peak Saint?”

“Yes. He killed two Saints, Yuheng and Tianxuan, with a single palm strike, and easily annihilated a peak Half-Saint.”

Li Shuangxu was silent for a moment, then took a deep breath.

He subconsciously touched the wooden plaque engraved with the character “Gu” in his pocket.

Thank goodness!

Thank goodness he had made friends in the secret realm.

This Gu family was becoming increasingly incomprehensible to him.

Li Shuangxu turned around: "Let's go, we must report this to the Holy Lord!"

He paused, then added, "Tell the Holy Lord that the Gu family is best treated as an ally, not an enemy!"

Then, the people from the Qingyun Holy Land quickly departed.

On the other side, Huo Lie from the Yaoguang Holy Land also emerged.

After hearing his subordinate's report, his expression changed several times.

"Holy Son Huo Lie, what do we do?" his subordinate asked.

Huo Lie glanced in the direction of the Gu family and said in a deep voice, "Go back and report to the Holy Lord that the Gu family's strength far exceeds our expectations. In the future, if you encounter anyone from the Gu family, avoid them!"

He recalled how he hadn't clashed with Gu Chen in the secret realm and felt a surge of relief.

"Let's go."

The people from the Yaoguang Holy Land also hurriedly departed.

The news spread like wildfire throughout Qingyun Prefecture.

The two holy lands joined forces, sending out their saints, only to be wiped out by the Gu family patriarch with a single palm strike.

The Gu family has produced a peak Saint!

This news is even more shocking than the destruction of the Purple Cloud Holy Land.

After all, the Purple Cloud Holy Land was at the bottom of the hierarchy, while Jade Balance and Heavenly Pivot were true ten-thousand-year-old holy lands.

Even their Saint was killed with a single palm strike, who would dare to provoke the Gu family?

A family suddenly appeared, possessing a peak Saint expert, and even an unfathomable ancestor.

…


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 28: The Two Holy Lands Unite, A Great War Is Imminent
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The Gu Family Residence.

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, sensing the group flying back from afar.

They're back.

Gu Tianhua landed first, striding to Gu Xuan and kneeling on one knee.

"Ancestor, I have fulfilled my mission! I have brought them back."

Gu Xuan glanced at him.

"Finished showing off?"

Gu Tianhua chuckled: "Finished."

"Get up." Gu Xuan waved his hand, looking at Gu Chen and the others behind him.

Gu Chen stepped forward, taking out the Ancient Year Fruit, the Heavenly Sword Saint's sword, and cultivation manual from his storage bag, and respectfully placed them before Gu Xuan.

"Ancestor, these were obtained from the secret realm."

Gu Xuan glanced at the pile of things.

What kind of junk is this?

An Ancient Year Fruit? Adds a hundred years to lifespan?

He has plenty in his warehouse, some adding ten thousand years.

The Heavenly Sword Saint King's sword? Cultivation manual?

"It's all junk!"

But he didn't say it aloud, only nodded.

"Not bad, that's thoughtful."

Gu Chen felt a warmth in his heart, and remembering the candy his father had given him, he couldn't help but ask, "Ancestor, my father, he..."

"The Marrow Cleansing Pill changed his aptitude, and combined with his century of accumulation, that allowed him to break through to the peak of the Saint realm," Gu Xuan explained.

Otherwise, changing his aptitude wouldn't have allowed him to break through to the peak of the Saint realm in such a short time; it was his century of cultivation combined with Gu Xuan's guidance that enabled him to break through to the peak of the Saint realm.

Gu Tianhua thought his son wanted to compare himself to him, and immediately said, "What? Not comparing yourself to your old man who's the same age? Your father's success is called accumulation, don't you understand?"

Gu Xuan found the father and son amusing.

Then he had the few Gu family members who had returned also undergo marrow cleansing.

The group left impatiently, and Gu Chen also went into seclusion to comprehend the insights gained over the past few days.

The back mountain quickly returned to calm.

...

Tianxuan Holy Land.

The Holy Lord sat enthroned, his face ashen.

Below, the elders knelt, none daring to raise their heads.

“A Saint, a peak Half-Saint, and dozens of Emperor-level disciples went to the secret realm, and not a single one returned!”

The Holy Lord’s voice was terrifyingly calm, but each word seemed to be squeezed out from between clenched teeth.

“Who can explain this to me?”

No one answered.

After a long while, an elder forced a smile and said, “Holy Lord, the Gu family patriarch… is a peak Saint. The Saint we sent, at the fourth level of the Saint realm, was no match for him.”

“Peak Saint?” The Holy Lord sneered, “The Gu family is an unheard-of, insignificant clan, how could they possibly have a peak Saint?”

“It’s said to be the Gu family patriarch’s doing…”

“Enough!”

The Holy Lord slammed his hand, shattering the armrest. “I don’t care what methods the Gu family has. They killed a Saint of my Holy Land; if this grudge isn’t avenged, what face will the Tianxuan Holy Land have?”

Just as he finished speaking, a deep voice came from outside the hall.

"Well said."

Everyone turned to look.

The Holy Master of the Jade Balance Holy Land strode in, his face equally grim.

"Brother Tianyu, our two families have been on good terms for generations. Now, we've been humiliated by the same family. This score must be settled together."

Upon seeing the newcomer, the Holy Master of the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land immediately stood up.

The Jade Balance Holy Land had also sent people, and they too suffered heavy losses.

They had come now to jointly attack the Gu family, hoping to stir up trouble in Qingyun Continent. They would have to ask the two Holy Lands for their permission!

“Brother Yushan is right, but the Gu family has a peak Saint; it won't be so easy to fight them.”

“What are you afraid of?” Saint Lord Yuheng interrupted him.

“You and I are both eighth-level Saints; even if we joined forces, we couldn't take down a peak Saint?”

Saint Lord Tianxuan pondered, “And what about that Gu family patriarch?”

Just as Saint Lord Tianxuan was still struggling with this,

Saint Lord Yuheng walked up to him and lowered his voice, “I heard that the Heavenly Sword Saint King's inheritance in the secret realm has been obtained by the Gu family.”

Saint Lord Tianxuan's eyes lit up.

The Heavenly Sword Saint King's inheritance—the cultivation technique and the sword—was a Saint King-level treasure.

“You mean…?”

“Destroy the Gu family, and the inheritance will be divided equally between our two families.”

Saint Lord Tianxuan was tempted.

But reason prevailed: "We must wait for the Ancestor to emerge from seclusion. Without a Saint King to oversee things, if the Gu family's ancestor truly is a Saint King, we'll be sending ourselves to our deaths."

The Jade Balance Saint Lord nodded: "My ancestor has been in seclusion for a thousand years; he should be breaking through soon."

Just then!

Two terrifying auras descended from the sky, enveloping the entire Tianxuan Holy Land.

Everyone in the hall paled.

"That's the aura of a Saint King?!"

"No, there are two!"

The Tianxuan Saint Lord abruptly stood up and rushed out of the hall.

In the sky, two figures stood suspended in mid-air.

One in white robes, the other in black robes, both elderly men with white hair and beards, radiating an aura that made the heavens and earth tremble.

"Ancestor!" the Tianxuan Saint Lord exclaimed in shock.

The white-robed elder nodded slightly: "A thousand years of seclusion, a fortunate breakthrough."

The black-robed elder, the patriarch of the Jade Balance Holy Land, also had piercing eyes: "Me too! A Saint King, achieved."

Inside the hall, all the elders knelt on the ground.

"Congratulations on your breakthrough, Ancestor!"

"Heaven blesses the Holy Land!"

The Heavenly Pivot Holy Lord and the Jade Balance Holy Lord exchanged a glance, both seeing ecstatic joy in each other's eyes.

A Saint King!

Each of the two Holy Lands now has a Saint King presiding over them!

What does this mean?

It means that from this day forward, the heavens of Qingyun Prefecture are in their hands.

What Qingyun Holy Land, what Gu family, all step aside!

The Heavenly Pivot Holy Lord stepped forward respectfully, "Ancestor! The Gu family has gone too far, killing a Saint of my Holy Land and stealing my opportunities. I beg you, Ancestor, to intervene."

The white-robed elder frowned: "The Gu family?"

The Heavenly Pivot Holy Lord quickly recounted the recent events.

The white-robed elder sneered after hearing this: "A nobody family dares to mess with people from a sacred land?"

The black-robed elder was even more direct: "Killing my Jade Balance Saint is an irreconcilable enmity. Issue the order: three days later, we will attack the Gu family."

But what they cared about more was the Saint King's cultivation technique and weapon!

Reaching the Saint King level meant it was time to get a better weapon!

The news spread like wildfire throughout Qingyun Prefecture.

…

In Qingyun Prefecture, in the largest market town.

The storyteller slammed his fist on the table and shouted, "Ladies and gentlemen, earth-shattering news!"

"The Tianxuan and Jade Balance Saint Lands have joined forces to attack the Gu family!"

"Three days later, the Saint King will personally lead the expedition!"

The crowd erupted in uproar.

"The two great sacred lands have joined forces? They're going to destroy the Gu family!"

"I heard that the patriarchs of both sacred lands have broken through to the Saint King level! This is the first time in thousands of years that this has happened in Qingyun Prefecture!"

"The Gu family is finished. No matter how strong a family is, it can't withstand two Holy Lands."

"Not necessarily? The Gu family's ancestor is unfathomable. What if he's a Saint King?"

"Even if he is a Saint King, how can one fight two?"

"Besides, does the Gu family have any other strong individuals? The patriarch is at the peak of the Saint realm, what about the others?"

"What a pity. I heard that the Gu family's young master is quite powerful. He can kill enemies above his level even at the King realm. If he had time to grow, he might become another monster!"

"No time. The war starts in three days. I'll go watch the show!"

Discussions rose and fell. Some lamented, some gloated, and some simply enjoyed the spectacle.

But no one was optimistic about the Gu family.

Two Saint Kings joining forces—this had never happened before in the history of Qingyun Prefecture.

What could the Gu family use to stop them?

...


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 29 Isolated and Helpless? The Gu Family Doesn't Care!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The Gu Family.

News from the outside world swept in like a whirlwind.

Yet, the Gu family remained remarkably calm.

There was no panic, no chaos, not even a whisper of gossip.

Everyone was cultivating.

Before the Marrow Cleansing Array, clansmen lined up to enter, and upon exiting, plunged headlong into the spiritually rich mountain range.

The pure spiritual energy of the immortal veins allowed them to absorb it without hindrance; they absorbed it frantically, breaking through to new heights.

As long as their ancestor was still lying in the back mountain, they feared nothing, not even the collapse of the sky.

The ancestor was their anchor!

The Back Mountain.

Gu Yushan sat cross-legged on a giant rock, his aura surging.

The bottleneck of his peak Half-Saint level was loosening violently, as if something was about to burst forth.

“Give me! Break!”

Boom!!!

The aura of a Saint soared into the sky!

Gu Yushan broke through, reaching the first level of Sainthood.

Almost simultaneously, Gu Kong, Gu Yun, Gu Shan, and Gu Enming also erupted with the aura of breakthrough!

Five elders, all breaking through to the Saint Realm!

Although not at the peak of the Saint Realm, five Saints were still top-tier combat power in any Saint Land of Qingyun Prefecture.

Gu Kong opened his eyes, feeling the surging power within him, and stared blankly for a long time.

"I...became a Saint?"

A few months ago he was still in the Profound Realm, and now he was a Saint.

This speed was unimaginable, even in his wildest dreams.

"Don't just stand there, stabilize your realm." Gu Yushan stood up, dusting himself off. "There's a tough battle to fight in three days."

At the mention of three days later, the group exchanged glances and laughed.

A tough battle?

With the Ancestor here, is that even a battle?

The Ancestor's strength is unfathomable; even a Saint King wouldn't gain an advantage.

Moreover, Gu Chen brought back weapons and cultivation techniques at the Saint King level. If the ancestor used them, his strength would undoubtedly rise to a higher level!

But these weapons and techniques were utterly useless to Gu Xuan!

Difficult to use versus useless—he chose garbage!

…

Qingyun Holy Land.

The atmosphere inside the main hall was heavy.

Li Shuangxu stood in the hall, facing the Qingyun Holy Lord Huo Dongyuan on the main seat, his voice earnest.

“Holy Lord, the ancestor of the Tianxuan and Yuheng families has broken through to the Saint King level. This is no small matter. They have joined forces to attack the Gu family. If the Gu family is truly destroyed, our next target will likely be us.”

Huo Dongyuan frowned: “What do you mean?”

“Help the Gu family.” Li Shuangxu enunciated each word clearly, “At least, we can’t stand idly by!”

“Absurd!”

An elder beside him shouted sharply.

"Why should the Gu family, a newly emerging small clan, risk everything for our Qingyun Holy Land?"

"Exactly! Two Saint Kings, what can we offer them? Are we going to throw our lives away? Those old Saints of our generation are still alive. The Gu family only knows about the peak Saints; what other information do they have?"

"Holy Son, you went to the secret realm. Were you bewitched by the Gu family?"

Li Shuangxu gritted his teeth: "No need to send troops. Even sending some resources as a gesture of support would be enough. Let the Gu family know that not everyone in Qingyun Prefecture is on Tianxuan Yuheng's side."

"Whether the Gu family can survive this calamity is still uncertain. If we choose sides now and the Gu family loses, Tianxuan Yuheng will be next."

Li Shuangxu recognized the Gu family's extraordinary nature, but this decision wasn't his to make.

Clearly, the elders of the Qingyun Holy Land didn't think the Gu family was worth it!

They felt the Holy Son was gambling with the fate of the Azure Cloud Holy Land, and with an unknown family no less.

The Azure Cloud Holy Land couldn't afford to lose, nor could it afford to gamble!

Huo Dongyuan remained silent for a moment, then slowly said, "Shuangxu, you're still too young."

Huo Dongyuan naturally considered things more carefully; his every move concerned the Azure Cloud Holy Land.

One wrong step could mean total defeat.

He had also heard about what happened in the secret realm, but offending two major holy lands at once was too unwise!

He didn't know if that Gu family patriarch had real talent or was just all show and no substance!

Regardless, not getting involved was the best choice.

"But!"

"Enough!" The Azure Cloud Holy Lord slammed his hand on the armrest.

"Guards, escort the Holy Son back to his room to rest. He is not to leave without my order."

Li Shuangxu's expression changed: "Holy Lord!"

Two elders stepped forward, one on each side, to restrain him.

The Holy Lord of Qingyun turned his back, refusing to look at him again.

Li Shuangxu was dragged out of the main hall, his eyes filled with resentment.

He pulled out the wooden plaque engraved with the character "Gu" from his robes.

Indeed, there's only self-interest among the Holy Lands!

But the Gu family is different.

He had met Gu Chen, Gu Tianhua, and that Gu family patriarch.

That confidence that emanated from their very bones wasn't feigned!

…

Yaoguang Holy Land.

Huo Lie stood before the Holy Lord, recounting everything that had happened inside and outside the secret realm.

The Holy Lord of Yaoguang listened in silence for a long time.

"Holy Lord, what do we do?" Huo Lie asked.

"No help," the Holy Lord of Yaoguang shook his head. "With two Holy Kings, the Gu family is doomed to defeat. We don't need to get involved in this mess."

"But!"

"No buts," the Holy Lord of Yaoguang interrupted him. "Send out the order: the entire Yaoguang Holy Land is under seclusion. No one is allowed to leave."

Huo Lie opened his mouth, but ultimately said nothing.

He recalled Gu Chen's heroic act of slaying a man with a single sword strike in the secret realm, and then he remembered the indifferent gaze of the Gu family patriarch.

For some reason, he had a vague feeling that this battle was not so simple.

In the market town.

The news was still spreading.

"I heard that neither the Qingyun Holy Land nor the Yaoguang Holy Land is getting involved in this. The Gu family is completely isolated this time."

"Nonsense, who dares to interfere? Two Saint Kings? Are they out of their minds?"

"No matter how powerful the Gu family's ancestor is, he's only one person. How can he fight two?"

"I think the Gu family is doomed."

"Not doomed, but finished!"

The storyteller slammed his gavel: "Three days from now, we'll see!"

The outside world was in an uproar.

On the Gu family's side, not a single person was discussing it.

It wasn't that they didn't take the two holy lands seriously; they simply didn't have the time.

The Marrow Cleansing Array ran day and night, the immortal vein's spiritual energy so dense it seemed to condense into water, and the clansmen cultivated like madmen.

Today you break through to King, tomorrow I break through to Emperor.

The day after tomorrow?

The day after tomorrow, even the elders will become Saints.

Who has time to care about holy lands anymore?

The back mountain.

Gu Xuan lay on the recliner, watching the bustling scene below, and suddenly felt a little bored.

Cultivation is cultivation, but without practical experience, it's just empty show.

He needed to practice.

He sat up and beckoned.

Gu Tianhua came running over excitedly: "Ancestor, what's up?"

"Don't just focus on breaking through these next few days. Make some arrangements for a sparring competition."

Gu Tianhua was taken aback. "Sparring? Ancestor, the battle starts in three days. What if you get injured sparring now?"

Before he could finish speaking,

Gu Xuan waved his hand.

A massive pagoda descended from the sky, landing between the peaks of the Gu family's mountain.

It was entirely black, radiating an ancient and powerful aura.

The pagoda had nine stories, each with flowing colors, and faint sounds of dragon roars and phoenix cries could be heard.

Gu Xuan said, "This is the God-Locking Pagoda. Sparring inside won't cause real injury. Even if you lose a hand or foot, it will heal completely when you come out."

"Even if you're killed, you can be resurrected outside the pagoda."


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 30: Thrilling Battle! Warships Set Sail
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Tianhua's eyes widened: "There's such a good thing?"

"Enough nonsense, go arrange it." Gu Xuan lay back down.

Gu Tianhua was overjoyed and turned to run.

"Stop! Stop!"

He stood in the square, shouting at the top of his lungs.

"The Ancestor said there will be a sparring competition these next few days! Everyone enter the God-Locking Tower for a life-or-death battle!"

"You can be resurrected if you die inside! You'll be fine as soon as you come out injured!"

"Show me your true skills! Anyone who holds back, I'll personally deal with them!"

The square fell silent for a moment, then erupted.

What?

You can be resurrected after dying, and you can even have a sparring competition?

Doesn't that mean you can go all out?

"Can he be resurrected? What's there to be afraid of?"

"Attack! Attack him to death!"

"I've wanted to spar with the Grand Elder for a long time. Before, I was afraid of being beaten to death because I couldn't win, but now I'm not afraid anymore!"

Gu Yushan stroked his beard and laughed, "You bunch of brats, you want to fight this old man? Come on, try entering the tower!"

"I'll see if I, a saint, don't slap you all to death!"

"I'll show you what a Grand Elder is!"

In an instant, the entire Gu family was in an uproar.

Forget about the two great holy lands, forget about the Saint King.

Upon hearing the news, Gu Chen immediately rushed over with his sword.

He also wanted to test his strength!

The first level of the God-Locking Tower.

As soon as Gu Chen stepped inside, the scene before him changed.

It wasn't inside the tower, but an endless wasteland.

Standing opposite him was Gu Qingshan.

"Brother Qingshan?" Gu Chen was taken aback.

Gu Qingshan chuckled, "Young Master, I'm sorry, the clan leader said anyone can challenge me. I've always wanted to test your sword!"

After returning, Gu Qingshan quickly reached the King Realm through marrow cleansing and cultivation.

However, thinking of Gu Chen in the secret realm, he sensed Gu Chen's extraordinary nature, coupled with the remarkable sword in his hand.

He wanted to see what it would be like to face him in the secret realm.

His realm seemed similar to Gu Chen's.

But Gu Chen wasn't just about realm; he also possessed a sword body and the enhancement of the Emperor Sword!

Gu Chen smiled, "Then you'd better be careful!"

As soon as he finished speaking, the two attacked simultaneously.

Gu Chen unleashed a sword strike!

Gu Qingshan tried his best to block, but couldn't withstand Gu Chen's casual swing!

"Again!"

Gu Qingshan revived, got up, and charged forward again.

Once, twice, three times…

Each time, Gu Chen knocked him down with a single strike, but each time he got back up.

On the fifth time, Gu Chen noticed that Gu Qingshan's reactions had become faster.

On the seventh time, Gu Qingshan actually managed to catch one of his swords before falling.

On the tenth time, Gu Qingshan withstood another sword strike, ultimately losing because his spiritual energy was exhausted.

Gu Chen sheathed his sword: "Not bad, you've improved quickly."

Gu Qingshan lay on the ground, muttering to himself.

This Gu Chen is terrifyingly strong, practically inhuman!

Thinking back to how those black-clad men in the secret realm were defeated with a single sword strike, it's understandable!

They're not even on the same level!

He's even two years older than him, yet he's been completely crushed!

"I'm not fighting anymore, I'm not fighting anymore! What kind of milk powder did you drink to grow up? You're terrifyingly strong!"

Gu Chen chuckled: "It's mainly because the things the Ancestor gave me were too good!"

Gu Qingshan ignored all that, lying on the ground, utterly dejected.

Outside the Tower of Gods, Gu Tianhua stared at the battle data transmitted from within, his mouth agape.

"This tower can actually record battles? It can even analyze weaknesses?"

He swallowed hard.

Just how many more treasures does the Ancestor possess?

Inside the tower, the battle intensified.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua, the two little girls, one wielding fire and the other ice, were beating their challengers to a pulp.

"Come on! One more!" Gu Qingqing shouted, hands on her hips, standing amidst the flames.

"Next!" A row of ice sculptures stood before Gu Huahua.

All of them were challengers!

They wanted to speak, but their mouths were frozen; they wanted to open their mouths, but they were too hot!

It was indescribable!

Gu Yushan and Gu Kong, the two old men, were also fighting with their full fury. A battle at the Saint level raged within the tower, causing the heavens and earth to tremble.

"You big cockroach, I haven't even settled the score with you for stealing my spirit fruit last time! This time, I'll teach you a lesson!"

"Oh, oh, oh! Little Kongkong, anyone can talk big, and it's just a spirit fruit, is it really necessary to be like this?"

"You have the nerve to say that? I spent a fortune to get that! Enough nonsense, take this!"

"Good lad! I'll have to use love to reform you!"

Gu Yushan wouldn't waste words; today he'd show him why flowers are so red!

Now that the dead can be resurrected, who's afraid of who?

The entire Gu family was fighting like madmen inside the Locking God Tower.

No one mentioned what would happen three days later.

Because it didn't need to be mentioned.

Three days later, what's meant to happen will happen.

What's meant to be fought will be fought.

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan listened to the shouts of battle below, a slight smile playing on his lips.

Jian Wuji stood to the side, looking at the Locking God Tower, a hint of emotion flashing in his eyes.

"Master, this tower is a Great Emperor-level spiritual treasure, isn't it?"

Gu Xuan, with his eyes closed, replied, "Yes, I obtained it before, but I've never used it."

Jian Wuji remained silent.

A Great Emperor-level spiritual treasure, used as a training ground for a group of King-level Saints.

Extravagant.

But he didn't say anything.

Because he was already used to it.

Meanwhile!

Yuheng Holy Land!

Holy Land Plaza!

Twelve warships hovered in the air!

They were filled with core disciples, top elders, and the sect's resources!

A full twelve ships of powerful individuals, plus the sect's resources, were all ready to set sail!

As for why so many people were brought!

The reason was simple: a family at the peak of the Saint level must possess countless treasures, and transporting them would require manpower!

Plus, there was the need to hunt down those who had fled, and deal with those insignificant henchmen!

They deserved to be thoroughly ravaged and slaughtered!

Let them know the true majesty of a Holy Land!

Killing two of their Holy Elders won't be a simple matter!

Let the world know the Holy Land's power, and we'll gain access to vast resources!

They're already imagining their lavish return before even setting out!

Below the warship are four Half-Saint level experts—the last remaining Half-Saints of the Jade Balance Holy Land.

Including their Holy Lord, one Saint, and two ancestors who recently broke through to the first level of Saint King.

The Jade Balance Holy Land has deployed one Saint King, two Saints, and four Half-Saints!

The number of experts is truly impressive!

This is the strength of the two Holy Lands!

"Hmph! The Gu family is nothing to worry about!"

"Once we slaughter them all, the treasures and riches will be countless, including cultivation techniques at the Saint King level!" The Jade Balance Saint Lord roused the disciples with exhilarating enthusiasm!

The disciples were as if they'd been injected with adrenaline!

Fight!

Fight!

Fight!

Kill!

Kill!

"Kill!"

"Move out!"

With a command from the Jade Balance Saint Lord!

Twelve warships slowly set sail.

At the same time, the Heavenly Pivot Holy Land also dispatched its respective experts.

The two holy lands were located in different places, but they had agreed to sail together to the Gu family at the same time!

The news spread! The entire Qingyun Prefecture was in an uproar!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 31 Dad! Your punches weren't fast enough! And they weren't ruthless enough!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"They've moved! The warships of the two holy lands have moved!"

"Twelve ships! Twelve ships from Yuheng, twelve ships from Tianxuan, a total of twenty-four warships!"

"Two Saint Kings, four Saints, eight Half-Saints, and over a hundred Emperors—they're going to wipe the Gu family out!"

In the market, the storyteller's voice was hoarse from shouting!

The audience was packed—cultivators, mortals, and even people who had come from neighboring states!

"This is a once-in-a-millennium spectacle! A Saint King in action—we haven't seen anything like it in thousands of years!"

"Indeed! I heard people from several surrounding states have come to watch!"

"How can we not watch? Two holy lands against one family—this is a spectacle to keep us entertained for a lifetime."

"How long do you think the Gu family can hold out?"

"Hold out? What makes them think they can? One peak Saint, and an ancestor whose cultivation level is unknown, facing two Saint Kings! They definitely can't withstand it!"

"I bet they won't last even a stick of incense!"

"I'll bet half a stick of incense!"

Discussions rose and fell; no one had any faith in the Gu family.

In the distance, a long, dark line of warships stretched across the sky, exuding a menacing aura.

Cultivators along the way quickly made way, afraid of being caught in the crossfire.

Some bolder rogue cultivators followed from a distance, wanting to witness the battle firsthand.

"Follow them, follow them! Maybe we can snag a bargain."

"Snag a bargain? Are you out of your mind? Even the aftershocks of a battle between saints could kill you!"

"Then let's watch from afar, from afar."

The news spread far and wide, even reaching the neighboring Purple Cloud Prefecture.

Purple Cloud Prefecture, Netherworld Palace.

Inside the main hall, an old man in black robes listened to his subordinate's report.

"The Gu family? The Gu family that killed Jiu You of my Netherworld Palace?"

"Indeed."

"Good! Good indeed!" The old man in black robes stood up.

A surge of anger welled up within him.

They were originally going to attack the Gu family, but now it seemed they could let these two holy lands attack them first.

To weaken the Gu family's power!

It's just a pity for Jiu You!

He was originally his most prized disciple in the Netherworld Palace, the most likely successor.

Now he was killed by an unknown family!

And they even heard he was stabbed to death with a single sword!

This is blatant slander against the Youluo Palace's strength!

The Youluo Palace is known for its murder and robbery; now that one of their own has been killed, they won't let this go so easily!

"Yes."

Other forces also sprang into action.

This battle is no longer just a matter for Qingyun Prefecture.

...

The Gu Family.

Inside the Locking God Tower.

Gu Chen stood on a desolate plain, facing his own father, Gu Tianhua!

Peak Saint vs. Second Level King.

It seemed like a crushing defeat.

But Gu Tianhua's expression was grim!

"Father! Your punches weren't fast enough! And not ruthless enough!"

Gu Tianhua gritted his teeth in anger: "You little brat, when your father became a Saint, you were still eating dirt in the secret realm!"

"Then why don't you hit me!" Gu Chen said with a slight smile.

But that smile was nothing short of mockery to Gu Tianhua!

"You little brat! Take this!"

Gu Tianhua unleashed a punch!

A punch from a peak Saint, powerful enough to shatter mountains and split the earth.

However, within the Locking God Tower, it was suppressed to the King Realm.

This was a sparring match between Gu Chen and his father, where neither was allowed to bully the other based on their cultivation level!

This meant Gu Tianhua couldn't gain any advantage against someone of the same realm.

The only advantage he could have was that he was Gu Chen's father!

Although he couldn't win inside, things were different outside!

He was in deep trouble!

Gu Tianhua's fist whistled past, as fast as lightning.

Gu Chen sidestepped and immediately unleashed a powerful punch!

Boom!

The two fists collided, each taking three steps back.

Gu Tianhua's fist went numb, his eyes wide: "You brat!"

"I told you, not fast enough!" Gu Chen had already charged forward.

One punch, two punches, three punches!

Each punch was so fast its trajectory was invisible!

Gu Tianhua blocked left and right, a dignified peak Saint, being overwhelmed by his own son.

"You motherfucker..."

No, no, hold back! Hold back!

Don't curse! Don't curse!

That's his wife!

Gu Chen didn't care about any of that and unleashed a punch!

Gu Tianhua blocked the attack but was instantly sent flying, rolling several times on the ground.

Gu Chen withdrew his fist, looked at his father on the ground, and shook his head.

“Dad, have you been too busy being the clan leader all these years?”

Gu Tianhua got up, his face flushed with anger: “Again!”

He charged forward, this time serious!

His fist technique was no longer brute force, but imbued with a trace of Daoist wisdom!

After all, he was a peak Saint; even suppressed to the King realm, he was no ordinary King!

The two fought fiercely on the wasteland!

On the tenth move, Gu Tianhua's fist grazed Gu Chen's shoulder.

On the twentieth move, Gu Chen's fist struck Gu Tianhua's chest!

On the thirtieth move…

“I'm not fighting anymore! I'm not fighting anymore!”

Gu Tianhua waved his hand, panting heavily!

"You little monster!"

Gu Chen chuckled. "It's all thanks to the Ancestor's excellent teaching!"

Gu Tianhua rolled his eyes.

A hundred years of my cultivation is less valuable than my son spending a few days with the Ancestor.

Where can you find the justice in this?

I'll make sure he knows why flowers are so red when we get out of here!

A good punching bag is indestructible!

Outside the tower, the Gu family members stared in disbelief at the battle emanating from within.

"The young master is overwhelming the patriarch? Is this for real?"

"At the same cultivation level, the patriarch is no match for the young master."

"Of course! The young master possesses the Emperor Sword and the Hidden Dragon Sword Body. What can the patriarch possibly use? Besides, the young master hasn't even used his sword yet!"

Gu Yushan stroked his beard and said with a smile, "It would be strange if the patriarch could defeat the young master at the same cultivation level."

Gu Kong nodded: "The young master was personally trained by the patriarch!"

The other three elders unanimously agreed.

Even the clan disciples at the same cultivation level couldn't stop Gu Chen.

"Perhaps only the Ancestor can defeat him!"

Tsk tsk tsk!

Gu Siming clicked his tongue a few times.

"What's wrong, Siming?" Gu Yun asked疑惑地.

"He beat the Clan Chief so badly, do you think that old guy will beat the Young Master just as badly when he gets out?"

After all, it's embarrassing to watch with so many people watching!

"That makes sense!" Gu Shan agreed.

After all, Gu Tianhua is quite concerned about his reputation!

Several elders chuckled.

Indeed!

At the top of the God-Locking Tower, Gu Xuan watched the battle below.

"Little Chenchen has made good progress!"

Jian Wuji stood to the side: "The Young Master has excellent talent; given time, he will surely achieve great things!"

On the other side!

The Holy Land fleet flew majestically for half a day, then suddenly stopped!

On the flagship, the Jade Balance Holy Lord's face was ashen: "Where is the Gu family?"

The elders around him exchanged bewildered glances, none of them answering.

"I'm asking you, where is the Gu family!"

One elder, forcing a smile, spoke up: "Holy Lord, the Gu family used to be a small clan, not marked on the map..."

At this moment, everyone realized that none of them knew where this so-called Gu family was!

After all this time, they didn't even know where the Gu family was!

If this got out, the entire Holy Land would lose face!

The two Holy Lands were fully prepared, yet they didn't even know where the enemy was!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 32 Can't even find the door! Impotent rage!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"No marker? What are you all good for?!"

The Jade Balance Saint Lord slammed his fist through the railing.

"A family that doesn't even know its location?"

The Jade Balance Saint Lord was furious. What were these people good for?!

The Heavenly Pivot Saint Lord's face was equally grim: "We can't find it here either. The men we sent out captured a few rogue cultivators, and they all said the Gu family was over there!"

He pointed to a distant, continuous mountain range.

But no one knew exactly where!

"Su Xinghe, Yin Wuji, each of you take a group of men and capture a few more people. Dig three feet into the ground, find the Gu family!"

"Yes!"

A ruthless glint flashed in Su Xinghe and Yin Wuji's eyes.

Last time, the Gu family had utterly humiliated them. This time, their Saint Lord and Ancestor were here; they were determined to make the Gu family pay a heavy price!

Especially that Gu family patriarch, preventing them from entering the secret realm from the outside—a huge disgrace!

If it weren't for his superior cultivation level preventing them from entering back then, but with two Saint Kings present this time, they refused to believe they could be stopped!

"This time, I'll make sure your Gu family is wiped out!"

Yin Wuji and his group quickly departed.

Su Xinghe did the same.

Su Xinghe and Yin Wuji moved swiftly.

In less than half an hour, the two returned with their men, followed by several rogue cultivators.

"Holy Lord, the people have been brought," Su Xinghe said, bowing.

The rogue cultivators were pushed forward, their legs trembling.

"Speak, where is the Gu family?" the Jade Balance Saint Lord said coldly.

A rogue cultivator pointed tremblingly to the distant, rolling mountain range: "It's...it's over there...in those mountains!"

"Are you sure?"

"Absolutely! I remember passing by the Gu family's gates before, I remember it clearly!"

The Jade Balance Saint Lord glanced at the mountain range, then at the rogue cultivator, and coldly snorted: "Let's go!"

Twenty-four warships changed direction and flew majestically towards the mountain range.

Upon arriving, everyone was stunned.

A desolate mountain; not a single family was to be seen, not even a soul.

"Are you sure this is the place?" The Jade Balance Saint Lord looked at the scene before him, somewhat angry!

The rogue cultivator's face turned pale with fright: "Yes, I'm sure! The Gu family used to be here, they were here just a few months ago! Then suddenly...they disappeared!"

"Disappeared? A family can vanish into thin air?"

A small family would have at least a hundred people; a hundred people disappearing together—who would believe it!

Are they playing a joke on him?

"I...I don't know either!"

The Jade Balance Saint Lord tossed the object aside, his face grim. "Search!"

Two great holy lands, two great saints, led by Saint Wang Si, twenty-four warships, tens of thousands of cultivators.

They couldn't even find the entrance?

If word got out, where would they put their faces?

Dozens of warships quickly dispersed, and disciples flew down to search everywhere.

Nothing!

Nothing at all!

Not even a brick!

"Saint Lord, it's not here!"

"It's not here either!"

"Nothing at all!"

The Jade Balance Saint Lord was furious: "How could it be nothing!"

He slammed his palm into the ground!

A large crater exploded in the ground, sending debris flying.

But that was all!

There was no sound of an array breaking, no hidden buildings revealed.

Just ordinary mountains and ordinary, utterly ordinary rocks!

"Bombard it!" The Jade Balance Saint Lord pointed at the mountain range.

"Bomb this entire mountain flat! I refuse to believe it won't emerge!"

A dozen warships opened fire simultaneously.

Various techniques, spiritual treasures, and array formations rained down on the barren mountain.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Dust filled the sky, and debris flew everywhere.

In the distance, the scattered cultivators who had followed were completely bewildered.

"What are they fighting? There's nothing there!"

"I don't know! Are they driven mad by the Gu family?"

"Don't talk nonsense, how could they be mad? That's a Saint King leading the charge!"

A rogue cultivator who had passed by the Gu family's area a few times suddenly spoke up: "I remember! The Gu family used to be there! Look at that mountain, that open space, I remember it clearly!"

"Then why is it gone now?"

"Maybe it's hidden by a great formation! I heard that a truly powerful mountain-protecting formation can conceal the entire mountain gate!"

"Is that possible?"

"You're just a rogue cultivator from the mountains, what do you know about the grand schemes of the immortal realms? You're just inexperienced!"

Discussions rose and fell.

From the distant horizon, more and more cultivators arrived upon hearing the news.

Some were from Qingyun Prefecture, some came from neighboring prefectures, and even some from far away.

Everyone wanted to see how this once-in-a-millennium battle would end.

The mountain collapsed, the earth cracked!

But when the smoke and dust cleared, everyone fell silent!

The mountain range had been blasted through!

Yet, there was still nothing!

The Heavenly Pivot Saint Master frowned, “How is this possible! How can a family's stronghold be completely empty?”

Just then, the black-robed patriarch of the Jade Balance Holy Land opened his eyes.

“No need to fight anymore.”

Everyone looked at him.

The black-robed elder's gaze swept across the mountain range, and he slowly spoke, “It shouldn't be empty; it's probably hidden!”

“Hidden?” The Jade Balance Saint Master was stunned.

“It should be a hidden mountain-protecting formation, and a very high-grade one at that, capable of concealing the entire mountain gate. Let me see if there's a formation core!”

The black-robed elder raised his hand, and a Saint King-level perception swept across the entire mountain range.

He frowned. How could this be!

He couldn't sense any fluctuations of spiritual energy!

There was nothing!

What he didn't know was that the Gu family was an existence even more terrifying than an Emperor's Array; even immortals couldn't break it!

With their cultivation levels, they couldn't even see it, let alone break it!

The people from the two holy lands froze in place.

Twenty-four warships, tens of thousands of cultivators, two Saint Kings, four Saints, and eight Half-Saints.

They hadn't even touched the Gu family's gate!

They couldn't break through it by fighting, they couldn't see it by looking, and they were unwilling to retreat!

The two Saint Kings remained silent, their Saint Kings' senses scanning again and again, finding nothing!

"Ancestor, what should we do?" the Jade Balance Saint Lord asked in a low voice.

The black-robed elder didn't speak.

He didn't know what to do either.

Just then!

Splash.

A sound of water.

The Jade Balance Saint Lord felt a coolness on his face.

What was that?

He reached out and touched it; it was wet.

But there was actually a pure spiritual energy within!

This was top-grade tonic!

But why was there also a… foul smell?

The Heavenly Pivot Saint Master was also stunned, his face similarly wet.

The two looked up.

There was nothing there.

There was no rain in the sky, and no puddles around.

Then where did this water come from?

The Jade Balance Saint Lord subconsciously licked his lips.

Salty.

A little astringent.

“This…”

Before he could react, an elder beside him suddenly widened his eyes and pointed to the void ahead.

“Saint Lord! There! There are words!”

Everyone looked in the direction he was pointing.

In the void, several large characters faintly appeared, crooked and illegible, as if written by something.

“Gu family members are not allowed to urinate or defecate indiscriminately?!”

The Jade Balance Saint Lord read aloud, his face instantly turning ashen.

He understood.

That water was urine!

It was as if someone had urinated on his face through an invisible barrier!

“The Gu family!!!”


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 33 Like a Fairyland! Fearless is fearless!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The Jade Balance Saint Lord roared, his voice so powerful it made the ears of the surrounding disciples ring!

At the same time, the transparent barrier trembled violently.

Not because of an attack, but because someone was behind the barrier.

On the other side, a disciple of the Gu family, his pants unfastened, stared blankly outside.

His name was Gu Xiaoliu, seventeen years old, a first-level King Realm cultivator.

He was tired from fighting in the tower and came out to relieve himself!

He found the edge of the great formation, unzipped his pants, and started to urinate!

While urinating, he realized something was wrong!

Why did his urine seem to have touched something?

He looked up.

Then he was dumbfounded!

Outside was a dense mass of people!

Dozens of warships, tens of thousands of cultivators, all staring at him!

Gu Xiaoliu's legs went weak, and he almost couldn't hold back his urine!

"What...what the hell?!"

He stared wide-eyed at the people outside.

Those people were also looking at him.

They stared at each other for three seconds!

Gu Xiaoliu glanced down at his pants, then looked up at the scene outside.

"I'm going!"

"Run!"

He turned and ran, his pants still unfastened, shouting as he ran.

"Enemy attack!!! Enemy attack!!! They've broken in!!!"

His voice grew louder and louder, reaching the entire Gu family.

As he turned and ran, the transparent barrier suddenly became clear.

The scene behind the barrier was revealed to everyone for the first time.

Everyone was stunned.

It wasn't a desolate mountain, nor a wild wilderness!

It was a true paradise!

Nine mountain peaks pierced the clouds, each radiating a faint spiritual light!

Waterfalls cascaded between the peaks, the spray not of water, but of spiritual mist!

The spiritual energy of the immortal veins was so dense it was visible to the naked eye, condensing into wisps of spiritual mist along the mountainside!

The spiritual energy emanating from it was even denser than that of the two great holy lands!

Large clusters of buildings stood on the mountain, constructed not of ordinary stone and wood, but of spiritual jade, spiritual gold, and celestial crystals!

Every brick and every tile gleamed brilliantly!

It was dazzling!

It looked even more luxurious and extravagant than anything in the holy lands!

It was simply a place of intoxication!

And then, upon closer inspection!

What flowed beneath the waterfall wasn't water, but spiritual liquid!

This was something extremely rare even in the holy lands; the Gu family had truly opened their eyes today!

The cultivators from the two holy lands stood in the void, staring wide-eyed at the scene behind the barrier!

This was no small family; it was clearly a celestial realm!

"This…this is more than ten times stronger than our holy lands…" an elder named Yuheng murmured to himself.

No one refuted him!

Because what he said was true!

The black-robed elder's eyes narrowed.

He wasn't afraid; he was calculating.

Such a good place, why should it belong to the Gu family? The Gu family patriarch was only at the peak of the Saint realm, and there might be a few other Saint-level experts within the clan.

Even if the Gu family patriarch was strong, how strong could he be? At most, a Saint King!

He and the white-robed old ghost, joining forces, might not be unable to fight!

Moreover, the spiritual energy here was so dense; there might be rare and precious treasures that could prolong life.

The black-robed elder had lived for nearly five thousand years, and his lifespan was nearing its end, which was why he was in a hurry to break through to the Saint King realm.

A Saint King could live for ten thousand years, but he had broken through too late; his physical body had already weakened, and his actual lifespan was only one or two thousand years left.

But if one could possess a treasure that prolongs life, living another three to five thousand years wouldn't be a problem!

This Gu family must be taken down!

The white-robed elder was clearly thinking the same thing.

The two exchanged a glance, both seeing greed in each other's eyes!

"This great formation, though strong, is bound to have weaknesses."

The black-robed elder said in a deep voice, "We can wear it down. Pass down the order: surround the Gu family and attack day and night. I don't believe it can hold out forever."

"Yes!"

Twenty-four warships dispersed, completely surrounding the Gu family!

Gu Family Square!

Completely opposite to the tense atmosphere outside, the Gu family was having a bonfire party.

After fighting all day in the God-Locking Tower, they came out to relax in the evening.

Several large pots were set up in the square, simmering spirit beast meat, filling the air with a fragrant aroma.

Several elders sat in a circle, enjoying enlightenment tea and chatting with Gu Xuan!

"Ancestor, let me tell you, back when that big cockroach was chasing his wife, he actually fought a black bear at the Transcendent Realm for a pretty flower! It was a brutal fight! That girl took care of him for a year before she even became his wife..." Gu Kong whispered to Gu Xuan, gossiping.

But Gu Yushan still discovered him.

"Little Kong, you can't just say things like that. You seem to have gotten yourself into your own little troubles back then!"

"Ancestor, let me tell you, he got drunk and faked alcohol, took off his pants, and started fighting in the square..." Gu Kong immediately covered his mouth!

"Alright, alright, Elder, I was wrong!" Gu Kong immediately shut Gu Yushan up!

This matter had to remain a secret!

Gu Xuan smiled knowingly.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua, the two little girls, weaved through the crowd, laughing like two flowers.

The atmosphere was wonderful.

The only discordant element was a chase unfolding in a corner of the square!

"You little brat! Stop right there!" Gu Tianhua chased Gu Chen all over the square, his face flushed with anger!

Gu Chen ran incredibly fast, glancing back as he ran: "Dad, you were the one who couldn't beat me in the tower, why are you still holding a grudge?"

"In the tower is the tower! Now is now! If you've got the guts, don't run, fight me head-on!"

"Suppress your cultivation to the King Realm first!"

"Suppress your cultivation my foot! I'll fight you at the Saint Realm!"

Gu Tianhua grabbed a shoe and threw it.

Gu Chen ducked, the shoe grazing his forehead, hitting Gu Yushan right in the face!

Gu Yushan was sipping his Enlightenment Tea, about to take a slow sip!

*Smack!*

The shoe slapped his face, spilling tea all over him.

He slowly removed the shoe, looking at Gu Tianhua.

"Clan leader, you did this on purpose, didn't you?"

"My mistake, my mistake!" Gu Tianhua quickly apologized with a smile.

Gu Yushan snorted and threw the shoe back.

Gu Tianhua caught the shoe and looked for Gu Chen, but he had already disappeared.

"This little brat, don't let me catch you! Otherwise, you'll know why flowers are so red!"

Several elders laughed at the scene.

They had expected this!

Before they finished speaking, Gu Xiaoliu rushed in from outside, panting.

"Clan...Clan leader! Something terrible has happened!"

Gu Tianhua frowned: "What's the panic? What's all the fuss about!"

"No! I just peed, and I think I peed on someone's face! Then I realized there are people everywhere outside! People from the two holy lands have surrounded us! Dozens of warships, and tens of thousands of cultivators!"

The plaza fell silent instantly!

All eyes turned to Gu Xiaoliu!

Gu Tianhua paused for a moment, then asked, "Was that pee you did?"

Good heavens!

He peed on someone's face!

Gu Xiaoliu froze, his face flushing red: "I, I didn't know there were people outside..."

Gu Tianhua nodded, turning to look at his clansmen in the plaza.

No one spoke.

"Are you scared?"

"No!"

The voices were in unison.


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 34: Two Great Emperors for Free, Are Three Enough?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The crowd wasn't faking it; they were genuinely fearless!

They'd died and come back to life inside the God-Locking Tower countless times!

Easier said than done!

Although Gu Tianhua was at the peak of the Saint realm, facing a Saint King, even 100 peak Saints wouldn't be enough!

This was a complete dimensional reduction attack!

But Gu Tianhua wasn't afraid at all!

Wasn't there still the Ancestor?!

Gu Xuan held a teacup in the square, taking a sip of Enlightenment Tea.

It wasn't that the tea was bad, it was just too noisy outside!

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The attacks came wave after wave, like hundreds of mosquitoes buzzing in his ear, impossible to shoo away.

[Ding! Detected continuous invasion of external enemies, severely disrupting the host family's quality of life!]

[Mission Issued: Suppress the external enemies with swift and decisive action, awe the world, and enhance the Gu family's prestige!]

[Mission Requirements: After suppressing the two holy lands, subdue eighteen vassal forces, demonstrating the Gu family's unshakeable position!]

[Directly obtained 2 Emperor Summoning Cards (cannot be upgraded or synthesized) to add helpers to the host!]

Gu Xuan's eyes lit up.

He could directly obtain two Emperor Summoning Cards?!

Although they couldn't be upgraded or synthesized, something was better than nothing, especially as a direct reward!

With Jian Wuji added, the Henchman Hall could gain two more Emperors!

Ninety-nine Saints would practically turn the Gu family into a place where Saints were everywhere!

As for the three random summoning cards, it was a bit like opening a blind box!

At least three Great Emperors would be worthy of him!

But right now, the most important thing was to swat those mosquitoes outside!

[Ding! Great Emperor Summoning Card in use...]

[Congratulations, Host! You have obtained a Great Emperor Realm expert—Yue Qingshuang!]

[Congratulations, Host! You have obtained a Great Emperor Realm expert—Yue Qingxue!]

Two beams of light descended from the void, transforming into two women.

Both were dressed in white, with flowing long hair, and their appearances were identical!

Absolutely stunning!

One was as aloof as frost, the other as gentle as snow.

The two women appeared in the night sky like celestial beings, leaving the Gu family members in the square completely stunned!

The meat in his hand suddenly lost its flavor; saliva dripped from his mouth, and his eyes widened!

"This is too beautiful! Who are those two girls?!"

"Wow! Wow! Fairies! Fairies! So beautiful!"

"I've never seen these two girls before!"

But when they saw the two girls heading towards Gu Xuan, they all shut their mouths!

"The Ancestor played it well!"

"Yue Qingshuang."

"Yue Qingxue."

Gu Xuan said to the Gu family elders and Gu Tianhua and the others.

Where did the Ancestor find these two fairies? They're too beautiful!

And they even willingly came to the Gu family!

The Ancestor is too capable, we must learn from him!

But was sending only three people enough?

And the Ancestor actually called the Holy Land mosquitoes!

"That's incredibly arrogant!"

Gu Tianhua immediately sent a message, telling his son to come quickly. This was a perfect opportunity to observe powerful figures!

"I have a feeling that the three people in front of me are incredibly strong, even though I haven't seen them fight."

"After all, there are two Saint King level beings outside, and tens of thousands of cultivators!"

Several figures soared into the air, flying towards the outside of the great formation."

On the plaza.

Gu Chen was eating meat when he suddenly sensed three terrifying auras rising from the back mountain.

One was Jian Wuji's, and the other two… he hadn't seen them before!

But those auras were comparable to Jian Wuji's."

Suddenly, he received a telepathic message from his father.

“Hurry up!”

Could it be the people from the Holy Land?!

Gu Chen almost choked on the meat in his mouth.

Seeing three figures fly out of the great formation, Gu Chen put down his bowl and immediately followed!

Outside the great formation.

The black-robed elder and the white-robed elder were still directing the attack.

“Keep bombarding! Don’t stop!”

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Suddenly, the black-robed elder’s heart skipped a beat.

He looked up at the transparent barrier.

Three cracks appeared on the barrier!

They weren’t cracked by force, they opened from the inside!

Three figures emerged from the cracks!

Jian Wuji walked in front, with Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue following on either side.

The three didn’t deliberately release their auras, yet the spiritual power and bloodlines of all the cultivators present simultaneously froze.

The black-robed elder's pupils constricted sharply.

He couldn't sense the cultivation levels of these three individuals.

It wasn't that he couldn't see through them, it was that he simply couldn't sense them!

It was as if the people standing before him weren't human!

At this point, the two holy lands were completely unprepared to retreat!

The thought of slaughtering the entire Gu family meant this blessed land would be theirs!

Danger had long been forgotten!

Outside the great formation, tens of thousands of cultivators stood ready.

Inside the formation, members of the Gu family sat in rows on small stools, munching on melon seeds and watching the show.

"Do you think those three can defeat them?" a clansman asked in a low voice.

"They were sent out by the Ancestor, what do you think?"

"That's right! The Ancestor is powerful and forceful!"

In the distance, the scattered cultivators who had come to watch the spectacle hid on the hilltop, observing from afar.

Seeing that the Gu family had only sent three people, a flurry of discussion immediately erupted.

"What the hell? Only three people? The Gu family only sent three people?"

"Isn't this suicide? The other side has two Saint Kings and tens of thousands of cultivators!"

"It's over, it's over, the Gu family has given up the fight."

"I knew it, how strong can a family be? They're finished now."

"What a pity for those two fairies, they looked like celestial beings, and they're already... their fate!"

"Shh! Keep your voice down! Don't they value their lives?"

Chapter 35 Three World-Destroying Palms! Two Swords Unleashed, Tearing Through the Void!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The black-robed elder and the white-robed elder were taken aback when they saw only three people emerge from the opposite side, then they laughed.

Is the Gu family out of options?!

The black-robed elder sneered, "These three are just here to die, aren't they?"

The white-robed elder stroked his beard: "Three? Enough to fill our teeth!"

Their gazes fell on Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue, and suddenly they couldn't look away!

Clad in white as snow, their long hair fluttered gently in the night breeze.

One was a stunning beauty, cold as ice.

The other a gentle and graceful beauty.

The black-robed elder narrowed his eyes and licked his lips!

Having secluded himself for a thousand years, he had seen very few women.

These two before him were the most beautiful he had ever seen in a thousand years, and undoubtedly the most radiant!

These two girls are quite something!

The black-robed elder spoke in a low, slightly hoarse voice, "Don't injure them later, keep them alive!"

The white-robed elder nodded, his eyes also gleaming, "That's exactly what I intended! Take them back and take good care of them!"

Su Xinghe and Yin Wuji, behind them, also saw Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue, their eyes flashing with greed.

Then the entire Qingyun Prefecture would belong to their two holy lands; the Qingyun Holy Land would be nothing but a roadside nuisance!

The black-robed elder nodded and raised his hand to give the order!
