Behind him, the energy-gathering light of twenty-four warships soared into the sky.

Tens of thousands of cultivators surged with spiritual power, waiting only for his command to tear the three before them to shreds!

Suddenly, an extremely terrifying fluctuation of spiritual energy swept in!

Instantly, the tens of thousands of cultivators below trembled, immediately silencing themselves and looking up!

Above the sky, three enormous hands appeared out of thin air.

Each hand was larger than the twenty-four warships combined, obscuring the entire night sky.

The lines on the hands were clear, radiating an aura that made heaven and earth tremble.

The air was torn apart, and spiritual energy was stirred up!

The space within a radius of ten thousand miles was distorted and deformed under the influence of these three hands!

The black-robed elder's hand froze in mid-air, his pupils shrinking to pinpoints!

He tried to shout "Defend!", but his throat felt like it was being choked, and he couldn't utter a sound!

The white-robed elder's face turned deathly pale, and he instinctively retreated!

But his feet seemed nailed to the void, unable to move!

That was absolute suppression from a superior!

A Saint King? Before these three palm strikes, a Saint King was no different from an ant!

"This...this is impossible!" the black-robed elder finally managed to utter.

His voice was hoarse, almost inhuman!

"This is at least...the peak of a Saint King or the Eternal Realm!"

But he was wrong!

All three were Great Emperors!

But he no longer had the chance to know!

In an instant, heaven and earth fell silent, all things were still!

Only three world-destroying hands pressed down, covering the heavens and earth!

All the Holy Land disciples on the twenty-four warships were in complete panic!

They felt death approaching!

“Full defense!!!”

“Defend now!!!”

The Jade Balance Saint Lord finally shouted, his voice hoarse!

Boom!

The defensive shields of the twenty-four warships simultaneously reached their maximum, and tens of thousands of cultivators frantically poured their spiritual energy into them.

The spiritual light surged, and layers upon layers of defense intertwined in the air, as thick as a city wall.

But before the spiritual energy defense could fully deploy, the three palms crashed down!

No explosion, no fire!

Tens of thousands of cultivators, two Saint Kings, four Saints, eight Half-Saints, twenty-four warships, tens of thousands of cultivators.

In this instant, they were all reduced to dust!

Dissipated into the air as if it had never happened!

Not even ashes remained!

Only the lingering spiritual energy fluctuations in the night sky proved that a battle had taken place here!

The black-robed elder's last thought before death was: Just what kind of monster was the Gu family?

Why did such a powerful force exist?

They didn't even have a chance to resist; they were instantly reduced to nothingness!

Three palm strikes landed, and the world returned to silence!

It was as if those tens of thousands of cultivators, twenty-four warships, and two Saint Kings had never existed!

On a distant mountaintop, the rogue cultivators were still reeling from the shock!

Some collapsed to the ground, some clutched their heads and muttered to themselves, some trembled uncontrollably!

Just then, a detached voice echoed from the depths of the Gu family compound. It wasn't loud, but it resonated clearly in everyone's ears.

"The two holy lands have attacked my Gu family. This cannot be tolerated. You three, go and wipe out their lair. Leave no one alive!"

Gu Xuan's words were like the heavens splitting open, resounding throughout the mountains!

Wipe out the holy lands? Leave no one alive?

This meant wiping the two holy lands from the world completely!

In the world of cultivation, the strong prey on the weak, and eliminating all traces is the norm.

But the two holy lands have been passed down for ten thousand years, deeply rooted, with disciples scattered throughout the land.

To completely annihilate them is no easy task.

"The Gu family is determined to wipe them out..."

"Nonsense! They've already come knocking on our door, why would we let them live?"

"But the two holy lands are so scattered, how long will it take to finish?"

The rogue cultivators discussed amongst themselves, no one believing this could be easily accomplished.

Outside the great formation, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, and Jian Wuji stopped simultaneously upon hearing Gu Xuan's voice.

"Master has ordered that neither of the two holy lands be spared." Yue Qingshuang looked at Jian Wuji and Yue Qingxue.

"How will they divide it?"

This was a question worth pondering!

After all, there were three of them, yet they could only share two pieces of the pie!

Yue Qingxue thought for a moment: "Yuheng is mine, Tianxuan is yours?"

"Okay!"

Yue Qingshuang nodded, looking at Jian Wuji: "Senior Jian, would you like to rest?"

Jian Wuji shook his head, his tone calm: "No need for that. If you want to fight, you can have it, but this time, let me have it!"

He glanced at Gu Chen, who was peeking out from behind the barrier inside the formation, and a slight smile appeared on his lips.

"Perfect timing, let the young master see what a sword truly is!"

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue exchanged a glance and took a step back.

"Then let's see what Senior has to show us!"

Jian Wuji nodded and took a step forward.

He raised his hand, and two swords appeared out of thin air!

One was completely transparent, appearing and disappearing...

The sword bore no light, no patterns, only endless nothingness!

“This sword is called Illusion!” Jian Wuji’s voice was soft, yet everyone heard it clearly.

Another sword followed closely behind, entirely black.

It had no decorations, yet it exuded a chilling aura!

“This sword is called Innocent!”

The two swords hovered in the void, their tips pointing towards the distant Jade Balance Holy Land and Heavenly Pivot Holy Land!

Jian Wuji didn’t swing his sword, didn’t gather his strength, he merely glanced at the two swords.

“Go.”

A single word, spoken with utter nonchalance.

The two swords moved simultaneously.

Two swords were enough!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 36: Two Great Holy Lands Destroyed! Limited Recruitment of Subordinate Forces
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

No sound of air being torn apart, no sword light, not even a trace could be seen!

It simply tore through the void and vanished from its spot!

Jade Balance Holy Land!

The mountain-protecting array is always active, its spiritual light flowing, enveloping the entire mountain range.

Inside the mountain gate, the disciples left behind awaited news of the victory at the front.

The warehouses were open, awaiting the Holy Lord and his entourage's triumphant return!

Each of them was already imagining the scene of having so many spoils that their hands would be sore, and the life of chatting and laughing with their junior sister that night.

Suddenly, a crack appeared in the sky!

A completely transparent sword fell from the crack, silently, ignoring the mountain-protecting array, landing straight in the center of the Holy Land!

Boom!!!

From the point of impact!

An invisible ripple spread outwards!

Wherever the ripples passed, palaces collapsed, mountains shattered, spiritual veins broke, and cultivators vanished into nothingness!

Targeting individuals connected to the Holy Land was like using a cannon to kill a mosquito for Jian Wuji, a Great Emperor!

The entire Yuheng Holy Land, spanning a thousand miles, was wiped off the map in an instant!

Mountain gates, formations, spiritual veins, disciples.

Everything was reduced to a desolate wasteland!

Tianxuan Holy Land.

A long, jet-black sword fell.

Boom!!!

The same ripples, the same destruction.

Tianxuan Holy Land, too, vanished completely under this sword strike!

Two swords!

Two Holy Lands!

Ten thousand years of heritage, destroyed in a single day!

From Jian Wuji uttering the word "go" to the two Holy Lands turning to nothingness, only a breath had passed.

Outside the grand formation, Jian Wuji withdrew his hand. The illusory and the wicked flew back from the void, hovering beside him, their swords spotless.

Yue Qingshuang glanced into the distance and nodded. “Clean.”

Yue Qingxue smiled slightly. “Decisive.”

Jian Wuji didn't speak, turning and walking back into the grand formation.

On the distant mountaintop, the rogue cultivators were speechless.

Destroying two great holy lands with just two sword strikes???

Tens of thousands of miles apart, one sword strike each???

What level of cultivation is this? What kind of swordsmanship is this?

No one knew, no one dared to ask!

"This...this...this...tearing through space! Destroying a sacred land with two sword strikes! So such powerful beings really exist in this world!"

The Gu family members sat in a row, still holding sunflower seeds, but no one was eating them!

Gu Yushan's teacup fell to the ground, shattering without him noticing!

He knew the people his ancestor had brought were strong!

But this level of strength was completely beyond his comprehension!

Sacred King, one slap.

Two great sacred lands, one person, one sword!

He didn't even struggle!

Escape felt like a pipe dream!

He looked at Gu Chen beside him, seeking some validation.

Then he found Gu Chen's expression was even more exaggerated than his!

His mouth gaped open, his eyes wide open!

He was completely frozen in place!

“Son?”

Gu Chen didn't react.

“Son!”

Gu Chen snapped back to reality, swallowing hard.

“Father…”

“Senior Jian said I have good talent, and that I can spar with him sometime!”

“And then? I think I want to live a few more years!”

A sword can tear through the void, just how strong is that?!

He couldn't even imagine it!

This was far beyond his comprehension!

Gu Tianhua was silent for a long time, then patted his son's shoulder.

“Mingzhi! As expected of my son!”

Outside the great formation, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, and Jian Wuji had returned.

Gu Xuan picked up his teacup and took a sip.

The bombardment outside stopped.

The world was quiet.

The system panel appeared before his eyes.

【Suppressing Foreign Enemies - Completed!】

【Please continue to subdue vassal forces to complete all tasks: 0/18】

Gu Xuan put down his teacup and looked into the distance.

Eighteen vassal forces?

No rush!

He believed the news would soon spread throughout Qingyun Prefecture, to other major prefectures, and even further afield.

He needed to wait for things to develop!

A gentle night breeze blew, carrying the aroma of tea.

Tonight was destined to be a peaceful night!

The news spread faster than the wind.

Overnight, Qingyun Prefecture was in an uproar!

No! Not just Qingyun Prefecture.

Cultivators in Ziyun Prefecture, Cangwu Prefecture, Tiannan Prefecture, and several surrounding prefectures were all talking about the same thing!

A small family had destroyed two ten-thousand-year-old holy lands!

"Have you heard? The Gu family of Qingyun Prefecture wiped out Yuheng and Tianxuan with just two slaps!"

"Two slaps? I heard it was two swords?"

Cangwu Prefecture, Heavenly Sword Sect.

"The Gu family...when did such a family emerge in Qingyun Prefecture?"

"Yes!"

The Gu family, a name previously unknown, became the focus of the entire Eastern Region overnight!

Qingyun Prefecture, the Gu family!

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, not even opening his eyes: "The news has spread?"

"It has spread, Ancestor."

Gu Tianhua respectfully said, "Now the entire Qingyun Prefecture is talking about our Gu family."

The more vassal forces the better, but the patriarch even set a limit!

"Patriarch, there will definitely be many who come voluntarily, why only eighteen?"

"Too many and we can't manage them all, too few and we won't have enough to see."

Gu Xuan opened his eyes and glanced at him.

Although Gu Tianhua didn't quite understand, he still nodded: "Yes, I'll take care of it right away."

The message was sent out again, this time initiated by the Gu family.

Everyone was in an uproar again!

Chapter 37 Famous Throughout the Eastern Region! Cultivators Everywhere

In a short while, those who had never heard of the Gu family showed expressions of shock!!

They never expected such power from a small family!

Most of the people in the Eastern Region were talking about the Gu family!

They were also discussing the Gu family's announcement of recruiting vassal forces, hoping to be fortunate enough to become one of them.

That was the family that had destroyed three holy lands!

Although they were only accepting eighteen vassal forces!

Other forces would accept as many vassal forces as possible, but the Gu family was limiting the number!

But no one dared to question it!

Because the Gu family had the power!

Destroying holy lands and limiting the number of vassal forces—that was their confidence!

In an instant, forces large and small sprang into action.

There were top-tier sects, struggling small families, and even several alliances of rogue cultivators.

With a bit of luck, they might rise to the top, and most importantly, the Gu family said that if they became one of them,

the Gu family would become their protector!

Everyone was preparing, waiting for the day the mountain gates would open in a week.

Meanwhile.

Behind the Gu family mountain.

"You little tiger, if you can talk, why aren't you saying anything? I thought you couldn't!" Gu Qingqing rode the white tiger, carrying Gu Huahua as they ran.

The white tiger never imagined it would one day become someone else's mount!

It, a mighty emperor…

The white tiger turned its head and licked Gu Qingqing's little hand.

"Miss, I'm rather aloof."

"Oh! Aloof tiger indeed!"

Gu Huahua slapped the white tiger's rump.

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua immediately made the tiger their personal mount, feeling incredibly cool.

While the phoenix was nice, the tiger was even cooler!

Gu Xuan didn't say anything, which conveniently allowed the white tiger to watch over their safety.

"Ancestor, can we ride it outside for a bit?"

"Sure, but don't go too far."

Gu Xuan chuckled, then mentally instructed the white tiger to protect the two women.

The white tiger hadn't expected to have this kind of service.

Taking beasts was fine, but taking people was something he'd never done before!

Plus, he had a bad feeling about the two women, sensing they were demonic beings.

However, since Gu Xuan had spoken, he naturally had no objections.

With a shout from Gu Qingqing, the white tiger sprang into action, disappearing in a flash.

The two sisters sat on the tiger's back, the white tiger running fast and steadily, careful not to jostle the two little darlings!

"Run! Run! Let's go outside and see!" Gu Qingqing patted the tiger's head.

"I heard a lot of people have come, let's go see what's going on!" Gu Huahua chimed in.

The white tiger was miserable!

It, a mighty Emperor-level divine beast, had been reduced to a mount, and not only that, a mount for two little girls!

...

Outside the mountain gate.

Gu Tianhua stood at the edge of the great formation, looking at the dense crowd outside, his lips twitching!

Only a few days?

The message had just been sent out, and people were already here!

And so many of them!

The open space within a radius of dozens of miles outside the mountain gate was densely packed with cultivators.

There were disciples from grand sects dressed in magnificent robes, members of small families in tattered clothes, and groups of independent cultivators.

Everyone was staring at the scene inside the great formation!

Spiritual liquid cascaded down, the splashes of water radiating spiritual energy.

Nine mountain peaks, shrouded in spiritual energy, were breathtakingly beautiful, like a fairyland!

"My god! This...this is a hundred times better than our sect! The Gu family is practically a fairyland!"

"They use spiritual liquid like water? That's too extravagant!"

"No wonder they could destroy a holy land. With this foundation, the Gu family's ancestor must be at the Eternal Realm!"

"Eternal Realm?! I'm going! With that kind of strength, we'd die without regrets as vassals! And they even said they'd protect us!"

Some people's eyes were red with envy, but no one dared to take a step forward.

The aftershocks of the great battle outside the great formation hadn't yet dissipated; the ashes of the two holy lands were still drifting in the wind!

They dared not offend the Gu family; the rules couldn't be broken!

Suddenly, Gu Tianhua stepped forward, cleared his throat, and his voice echoed throughout the entire area.

"Everyone, you've probably all heard about the Gu family's recruitment of vassals. The rule is simple: they don't consider background or cultivation level, only sincerity!

'Those who are loyal to the Gu family, even if you're just a mortal, the Gu family will accept! Those who are disloyal, even if you're a saint, the Gu family won't want you!'"

This statement caused an uproar!

"No cultivation level? Then we rogue cultivators have a chance too!"

"Sincerity? How do we prove sincerity?"

"The Gu family said they'll open the mountain gates in a week. There will be a test then, and those who pass will be free to enter!"

"I don't care! I'm not leaving! Being able to become a vassal of the Gu family would be an incredible stroke of luck!"

Some people immediately started setting up camp.

Some pitched tents, some started fires, and some even sat down on the ground to cultivate.

And then, trouble struck again!

"Holy crap! The spiritual energy here is so dense! Is there something different about this place?!"

"Can I even feel it from outside?! Imagine how invigorated I've been inside! I've never felt so refreshed!"

"I feel like I'm about to break through!"

"Me too! The bottleneck I've been stuck on for ten years has finally loosened!"

"Don't push! Don't push! Let me take a few more breaths!"

In an instant, spiritual energy surged outside the mountain gate, and one after another, people broke through!

Several rogue cultivators who had been stuck at their bottlenecks for years managed to take a few breaths of spiritual energy outside the Gu family's mountain gate and advanced on the spot!

Now, no one dared to leave!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 38 What?! The Holy Land is actually trying to steal our spots too!

Everyone stood like nails outside the Gu family's mountain gate, impossible to shoo away!

They didn't want to leave either. They'd all broken through so easily; what would happen inside?

He then turned back to report to the patriarch.

Back Mountain!

Gu Tianhua explained the situation, and Gu Xuan nodded.

"Let them wait. The mountain gate will open in a week, and there will be a test then."

Outside the mountain gate.

They had to get in a week later!

Qingyun Holy Land.

Inside the main hall, the atmosphere was so oppressive it was hard to breathe!

Huo Dongyuan sat in the main seat, his face extremely grim.

Li Shuangxu stood in the hall.

When the news arrived, Huo Dongyuan was drinking tea.

Those were two Saint Kings, and ten-thousand-year-old holy lands at that!

Just so easily destroyed by the Gu family?

And the Gu family only sent three people, yet in the end, the territories of two major holy lands were all wiped out!

No, it was three holy lands!

Moreover, hearing that cultivators outside the Gu family could easily break through to higher realms, and that the scene inside was even more inhumane than in the Holy Land!

The Gu family was far beyond what the Holy Land could compare to; they were at least a family at the Eternal Realm!

Eternal Realm!

Those were among the most powerful figures in the entire Eastern Region!

He suddenly remembered what he had said to Li Shuangxu before:

"You're too young."

"Whether the Gu family can survive this calamity is still uncertain."

Now it seemed that he himself was the one who was too young!

Indeed, old age prevents one from seeing the whole picture!

"Shuangxu."

Huo Dongyuan spoke, his voice somewhat dry, "About what happened before..."

Li Shuangxu interrupted him, "Holy Lord, there's no need to explain. Everything you did was for the Holy Land; I understand."

Understanding was one thing, but the thorn in his heart remained.

"Holy Master! Holy Master! Something terrible has happened!"

"The Yao Guang Holy Land! The people from the Yao Guang Holy Land have already set off! They're carrying lavish gifts and heading to the Gu family!"

The hall fell silent instantly.

"The Yao Guang Holy Land?" an elder exclaimed, "They moved so quickly?"

"Not just Yao Guang! As far as we know, at least fifteen of the top twenty forces in Qingyun Prefecture have already begun preparations! Some smaller families even set off overnight, afraid of missing out!"

Huo Dongyuan's expression darkened further.

He hadn't even decided whether to go or not, and they were already on their way!

That's the difference!

Not a difference in strength, but a difference in vision!

If they were truly at the Eternal Realm, being a vassal would be a very prestigious thing!

The Eternal Realm is among the top forces in the Eastern Region!

The Holy Lord himself is going? This is unprecedented for the Qingyun Holy Land!

But no one objected!

Because everyone knew that from this day forward, the world of Qingyun Prefecture had changed!

The Gu family was now the true power!

The next morning, as soon as the sun rose, chaos erupted outside the mountain gate!

"The people from the Qingyun Holy Land have arrived!"

"What?! The Qingyun Holy Land? They're coming to become vassals too? How is that possible!"

"Impossible! They were the number one holy land in Qingyun Prefecture before!"

In the distance, a blue spirit boat slowly approached, and standing at the bow was Huo Dongyuan himself.

Behind him were seven elders, some half-saints and some saints!

The ship was piled high with gifts—spirit stones, pills, spiritual materials, and magical treasures—like a small mountain!

The spirit boat docked outside the Gu family's mountain gate, and Huo Dongyuan stepped down.

"No way? It really is the Qingyun Holy Lord himself! How are we supposed to compete?"

"Isn't this bullying? Even a saint is trying to steal from us!"

Complaints rose and fell!

Some sighed, some shook their heads, and some immediately packed their things to leave.

"That's right! The Gu family said they wouldn't consider cultivation level, but with the Qingyun Holy Land here, how could they not give them face?"

"Let's go, let's go, don't embarrass ourselves here!"

An atmosphere of despair permeated the crowd.

Huo Dongyuan looked around, his expression complex.

He had lived for over a thousand years and never imagined he would one day personally beg to become someone else's vassal.

But the Gu family was worth it!

A family that could kill a Saint King with two slaps—let alone become a vassal, even as a lackey, there were plenty of powerful forces lining up to serve.

He took a deep breath, cupped his hands towards the direction of the grand formation, and spoke respectfully.

"Huo Dongyuan of the Qingyun Holy Land requests an audience with the Gu family's patriarch."

His voice wasn't loud, but everyone heard it.

Everyone stared at the grand formation, waiting for a response.

A moment later, a crack appeared in the grand formation, and Gu Tianhua emerged.

He glanced at Huo Dongyuan, then at the mountain of gifts behind him, and smiled.

“You’re too kind, Holy Lord Qingyun. Our ancestor said that when accepting vassals, we don’t consider lineage or cultivation level, only sincerity. You don’t need to go to such lengths.”

Huo Dongyuan shook his head: “The Gu family is worthy. What happened before was my blunder. I come today not seeking to become a vassal, but only to admire the Gu family’s splendor!”

Gu Tianhua nodded: “Holy Lord Huo is kind, but the rules cannot be broken. The mountain gates will open in a week, and everyone will be treated equally. Please wait outside.”

The entire hall erupted again.

“Even the Holy Lord Qingyun has to wait?!”

“My god… the Gu family completely disregards the Holy Land!”

“It’s not that they disregard it, it’s that they have the strength!”

That's so lame!

"Okay."

A dragon flying in the sky is much cooler than a white tiger running on the ground!

That feeling of looking down from the sky is awesome!

"Let's go, let's go out and broaden our horizons."

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua rode the white tiger, sitting on either side of him behind him.

The Azure Dragon roared and soared into the air, flying towards the outside of the great formation.

A colossal white tiger and dragon emerged from the grand formation, appearing before the crowd!

Even their breath sent chills down the spines of the Saint King.

The dragon and tiger's eyes swept across the crowd, their powerful pressure causing everyone to instinctively lower their heads!

Gu Chen sat atop the dragon's back, looking down at the densely packed crowd below, his palms sweating.

He had never seen anything like this before—a mountainside teeming with people! This was the first time he had witnessed such a sight!

Yet, he stood ramrod straight, his voice steady.

"You have all come from afar, the Gu family welcomes you. The mountain gates will open in a few days, and there will be a test then. Until then, please wait patiently!"

Not intimidated by Gu Chen's cultivation level, but by the white tiger and the azure dragon.

They were actually serving as mounts for the young master of the Gu family?

And it seemed to be entirely willing!

This completely shattered their understanding. Just how much more did the Gu family know that they didn't?!

They were utterly shocked!

Those with weak hearts were practically suffocating!

Powerful demonic beasts as mounts, spiritual liquid as water!

This Gu family was far beyond what the Qingyun Holy Land could compare to!

Calling the young master of the Gu family by his name directly? This was incredibly disrespectful!

The Gu family is currently at its zenith, even he has to be careful. How could his own holy son be so reckless?

That's the young master of the Gu family, his status is no ordinary person!

He couldn't bear the consequences of angering the Gu family!

Gu Chen, however, smiled without hesitation.

He patted the Azure Dragon's head, and the dragon roared, swooping down from the sky.

The massive dragon body stirred up a gust of wind, obscuring the path for everyone, but no one dared to retreat.

The Azure Dragon stopped in front of Li Shuangxu, its eyes fixed on him, its breath carrying a faint spiritual mist.

But Li Shuangxu remained unfazed, even reaching out to touch the Azure Dragon's scales, feeling their incredible hardness!

The aura emanating from it chilled him to the bone!

"What a fine dragon!"

Li Shuangxu didn't forget to praise it!

Gu Chen jumped off the dragon's back.

"Why didn't you tell me you were coming? I could have come out to meet you."

"That's true. I didn't expect you to come too. I was thinking of visiting you after our last trip to the secret realm."

Li Shuangxu hadn't expected this day to come so soon, but it wasn't Gu Chen coming to see him, but him coming to see Gu Chen!

"I wonder if you'll welcome me this time?" Li Shuangxu asked with a wry smile.

"Of course, of course. You're my friend, Gu Chen's friend, how could I not welcome you?"

Huo Dongyuan stood there, stunned, his mind reeling.

His own Holy Son and the young master of the Gu family were so close?

They were even laughing and talking?

This...this...

How could he not know?

He recalled Li Shuangxu being locked in his room, muttering to himself while touching a wooden plaque.

He thought his Holy Son had gone mad!

“Come on, let’s go in and take a look.” Gu Chen pulled Li Shuangxu towards the great formation.

After taking a few steps, he turned back. “Qingyun Holy Lord, you all come along too.”

Gu Chen invited everyone from the Qingyun Holy Land.

Huo Dongyuan was stunned.

Go in together? So many people?

“Young Master Gu, is this… convenient?”

Gu Chen waved his hand: “The Ancestor said that guests are always welcome, and besides, Shuangxu is my friend. I have to give him face.”

Inside the grand formation.

Soon,

they traversed layers of stairs and arrived near the Gu family square.

Huo Dongyuan's steps slowed, his eyes growing increasingly fixed.

Not because of rudeness, but because he simply couldn't move.

Along both sides of the road, spiritual liquid gathered into streams, their surfaces shimmering with a faint spiritual light.

Rows of spiritual plants grew on both sides, the most common being over ten thousand years old!

Older than him!

The spiritual plants were even larger than him!

This…

This is the foundation of the Gu family!

Not far ahead, dozens of large trees attracted everyone's attention!

That tree was entirely emerald green, with over a dozen fruits hanging from it.

Shaped like infants, pure white, emitting a faint, sweet fragrance.

Huo Dongyuan felt every pore on his body open, and the bottleneck within him, dormant for many years, began to loosen!

"This… what kind of fruit is this?" Huo Dongyuan's voice trembled.

Could it be the one he was thinking of?!

The Ginseng Fruit Tree?!

Gu Chen glanced back and casually said, "Oh, those are ginseng fruits. The ancestor planted a few dozen casually, saying eating them would increase lifespan."

Casually planted? Ginseng fruits? Increase lifespan?!

These words, put together, left Huo Dongyuan's mind reeling.

He'd only ever seen ginseng fruits in ancient texts. Legend says they bloom every three thousand years, bear fruit every three thousand, and mature every three thousand, with one fruit adding forty-seven thousand years to one's lifespan!

He'd never heard of anyone in the entire Eastern Region having them. The Gu family just casually planted a few dozen?

He could have planted one or two!

What did "a few dozen" mean?

Were they eaten as food?

That's utterly inhumane!

"Young Master Gu, this…is really a ginseng fruit?" an elder asked in a trembling voice.

Gu Chen nodded: "Yes, the taste is not bad, but it ripens too slowly. It takes thousands of years to eat it. The Ancestor sometimes speeds up the ripening process because he finds it troublesome."

???

Waiting thousands of years to eat it, then speeding up the ripening because he finds it troublesome?

Every word was like a hammer blow to the hearts of everyone in the Qingyun Holy Land!

Is this something a human would say?!

Gu Chen walked to the tree, casually picked a few fruits, and handed them to Huo Dongyuan and Li Shuangxu, then distributed some to a few elders: "Try some, they're fresh."

These couldn't be fresher!

They were picked right in front of him!

Huo Dongyuan took the ginseng fruit, his hands trembling.

He had lived for over a thousand years and never imagined he would one day be able to eat something like this.

He held it to his nose and took a sniff, and he clearly felt his lifespan increase by more than ten years!

Just one sniff!

And it had such an effect!

What would happen if he ate it!

Holy crap!!!

The elders behind him were even more distraught; some held the ginseng fruit, tears streaming down their faces; some trembled all over; and one even knelt on the ground, muttering...!

"This life has been worth it!"

Huo Dongyuan took a bite!

He broke through!

Right by the roadside near the Gu family's residence, he ate a piece of fruit and broke through.

Stuck at the peak of the Saint realm for thousands of years, he actually broke through today!

He broke through to the first level of the Saint King realm!

“Gu Chen, this!” Li Shuangxu’s eyes widened.

He was incredulous at his Saint Lord’s breakthrough!

He knew how many years Huo Dongyuan had been stuck at this bottleneck!

And today he had actually broken through!

Gu Chen smiled: “It’s normal. This ginseng fruit is a good thing. I broke through the first time I ate it.”

"This...this isn't a dream, is it?" an elder muttered to himself, then slapped himself hard.

*Smack!*

A crisp, loud sound!

His face immediately swelled up!

It hurt! It wasn't a dream!

It was all real!!

"It's not a dream! It's not a dream! It's real!" The elder was so excited that tears streamed down his face, and he slid to his knees before Gu Chen with a thud.

"Thank you, Young Master Gu! Thank you, Gu family!"

Gu Chen quickly helped him up: "No, no, no, old man, don't be like that. It's just a few fruits. The Ancestor said, guests are always welcome."

Just a few fruits?!

Is he even speaking human language?

Huo Dongyuan took a deep breath, calming his emotions after his breakthrough, feeling smug.

He continued walking forward!

After a few steps, he stopped again.

Several tea trees stood by the roadside, not tall, but with lush foliage, each leaf exuding a faint Daoist aura.

Enlightenment Tea Trees?!

Huo Dongyuan felt his heart pounding.

Ginseng fruit was one thing, but Enlightenment Tea too?

And planted just like that by the roadside?

Without even a fence?

Aren't they afraid of being stolen?

Oh no, this is the Gu family's territory, who would dare steal it?

"These Enlightenment Tea Trees…were they also planted by your ancestor?" Huo Dongyuan's voice was hoarse.

Gu Chen nodded: "Yes, our ancestor said that drinking Enlightenment Tea brewed in water can help one attain enlightenment, so he planted a few. We usually drink it as water."

Planted a few! Drink it as water!

This string of words had completely disoriented them!

Huo Dongyuan turned to look at the elders behind him. All seven elders had the same expression!

Eyes wide open!

Their mouths were agape!

Some were drooling and didn't seem to be wiping it away, while others clutched their chests as if their hearts couldn't take it!

"Holy Lord, I want to stay with the Gu family!" an elder suddenly spoke up!

"Me too!"

"Me too!"

Huo Dongyuan's lips twitched, and he glared at them: "Mind your manners!"

But in his heart, he was also thinking that if he could, he would want to stay with the Gu family too!

This was simply too good; their Holy Land couldn't compare!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 41 was terrifying; the Holy Land bowed down in submission.

The group continued forward.

Passing through a bamboo forest, they arrived at a massive pavilion with the words "Cultivation Technique Hall" inscribed on the door.

"This is the Gu family's Cultivation Technique Hall."

Gu Chen pushed open the door. "There aren't many cultivation techniques inside, but the quality is decent."

Huo Dongyuan stepped inside, his feet rooted to the ground once more!

Rows of jade slips were neatly stacked on shelves, each slip labeled with the name and rank of the cultivation technique.

*Mysterious Heaven Overlord Body Technique* (Saint-level)

*Nine Revolutions Sacred Heart Technique* (Saint-King level)

*Azure Sky Myriad Phenomena Scripture* (Eternal Realm)

*Great Void Divine Scripture* (Quasi-Emperor level)

Quasi-Emperor level?!

Huo Dongyuan couldn't believe his eyes!

He had lived for thousands of years, and the best cultivation technique he had ever seen was only Saint-King level, and even that was incomplete!

Quasi-Emperor level cultivation techniques—he'd only heard of them in legends. Only Quasi-Emperor level experts could create such techniques; there were only a handful in the entire Eastern Region!

Wasn't the Gu family supposed to be a small family?

This was clearly an imperial family!

"These…these are all from the Gu family?" an elder's voice trembled.

Gu Chen nodded: "The Ancestor said these cultivation techniques are too low-grade, and told us to make do with them until we find better ones."

Too low-grade? Make do?!

Is he even speaking human language?

Huo Dongyuan took a deep breath, trying to appear less shocked.

But his hands were trembling, and his legs were shaking!

After forcing himself to calm down for a moment, he gave up on the act; anyone would be trembling!

Leaving the cultivation technique hall, the pill hall was right next door.

Pushing open the door, a rich medicinal fragrance wafted out. Shelves were filled with jade bottles, each labeled.

Emperor-grade pills, Saint-grade pills, ten-thousand-year-old spiritual herbs…

Huo Dongyuan was numb!

His brain selectively filtered out this information, because he feared his heart wouldn't be able to take it if he looked any longer!

“These…were all refined by the Ancestor?” Li Shuangxu asked.

Gu Chen smiled: “The Ancestor wouldn't know how to refine pills. These were all obtained from a Ruyi Treasure Bag.”

“I don’t know what this Ruyi Treasure Bag is?”

Li Shuangxu didn’t delve into it.

Where they came from wasn’t important; what mattered was that the Gu family had them!

After touring the Cultivation Techniques Hall and the Pill Hall, Huo Dongyuan’s mindset had completely changed.

Before coming, he was the Holy Lord of the First Holy Land of Qingyun Prefecture, and he still had some reserve.

Now? He just wanted to kneel down and call him “father”!

And then beg the Gu family to take him in!

“Shuangxu.” Huo Dongyuan grabbed Li Shuangxu.

His voice was lowered to a whisper, "You have a good relationship with Young Master Gu, so put in a good word for our Holy Land. Our Qingyun Holy Land must become a vassal!"

Li Shuangxu looked at his Holy Master's old face, his lips twitching!

This shrewd man, with thousands of years of experience, could change his expression faster than flipping through a book!

Just a moment ago he was putting on airs as the Holy Master, and now he wants him to say a few good words!

"Holy Master, didn't you just say to be mindful of your status?"

"Mindful of what status? What status do I have in front of the Gu family?"

Huo Dongyuan glared.

"Hurry up!"

Li Shuangxu was somewhat speechless, but still nodded.

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan was lying on a recliner, with Gu Tianhua sitting beside him, the two leisurely drinking tea.

Gu Qingshan stood to the side, attentively pouring tea for Gu Xuan.

He had spent a few days by the Ancestor's side and had figured something out.

He didn't possess Gu Chen's Hidden Dragon Sword Body, nor the special physiques of the two little girls. His talent was mediocre; he couldn't compete in a contest of innate ability.

But by following the patriarch, serving tea and water, and becoming familiar with him, opportunities were bound to come!

As it turned out, he was right. Just these past few days, the patriarch casually gave him a few pills, saying they were delicious candies.

And then!

His cultivation level skyrocketed from the King Realm to the Emperor Realm!

Delicious and not cloying, his cultivation doubled!

"Qingshan, the tea's gone weak," Gu Xuan said.

"Coming, coming," Gu Qingshan quickly changed the tea leaves, his movements swift and efficient.

Footsteps approached.

Gu Chen led the people of the Azure Cloud Holy Land up the back mountain.

Huo Dongyuan saw the white-clad figure on the reclining chair in the distance and paused.

An invisible pressure washed over him, not a forceful aura, but an instinctive reaction from the depths of his soul!

Like a rabbit seeing a tiger!

An ant gazing at the sky!

A thought flashed through his mind: the slightest misbehavior would reduce him to ashes in the next second!

No verification was needed; he was 100% certain!

The Gu family was indeed terrifying!

“Huo Dongyuan of the Azure Cloud Holy Land pays respects to the Gu family patriarch.” Huo Dongyuan bowed deeply, his voice utterly respectful!

The elders behind him bowed in unison, some even kneeling due to weakness in their legs!

It wasn't intentional; they genuinely couldn't stand!

Li Shuangxu followed suit, but wasn't as nervous as the others.

He had met Gu Chen and Gu Tianhua, and knew the Gu family wasn't as terrifying as some thought.

Gu Xuan picked up his teacup, took a sip, and glanced at Huo Dongyuan.

“Just broke through to Saint King; you should contemplate this carefully.”

Huo Dongyuan's heart skipped a beat. He had indeed just broken through under the Ginseng Fruit Tree; how could they tell?

"Ancestor, you have keen insight. Thanks to the Gu family, I ate a ginseng fruit and was fortunate enough to break through."

Gu Xuan nodded, saying nothing more, and looked at Li Shuangxu, a slight smile playing on his lips.

"You're the friend Little Chenchen mentioned?"

Li Shuangxu respectfully replied, "Junior Li Shuangxu greets Ancestor."

Gu Xuan smiled: "Not bad, more composed than Little Chenchen."

Gu Chen rolled his eyes.

What do you mean more composed than me?

Is he already siding with outsiders?

Gu Xuan ignored him, his gaze falling on Huo Dongyuan, and he picked up his teacup and took another sip.

"I know why you've come."

Huo Dongyuan's heart tightened, and he was about to speak when Gu Xuan waved his hand.

"Bring your old Holy Master here too. I think he's nearing his end. With me, there's still hope."

After he finished speaking, the back mountain suddenly fell silent for a moment.

Huo Dongyuan's pupils constricted sharply, and he froze on the spot as if struck by lightning!

The elders behind him paled even more, some gasping in shock!

The Old Holy Master.

The Old Holy Master of the Azure Cloud Holy Land had been in seclusion for a thousand years, and no more than five people outside knew of this!

How did the Gu family patriarch know?

And how could he tell at a glance that the Old Holy Master was nearing the end?

"Patriarch, you…" Huo Dongyuan's voice trembled!

Gu Xuan glanced at him: "A Saint King at the third level, poisoned by the Soul-Devouring Powder, which recurs every hundred years."

Huo Dongyuan's legs went completely weak!

He knelt on the ground with a thud, his voice choked with sobs: "Patriarch, you have divine eyes! The Old Holy Master was indeed poisoned by the Soul-Devouring Powder, and has endured it for eight hundred years. Last time it flared up, he almost didn't survive. We've searched everywhere in the entire Eastern Region, but no one can cure him!"

"Please, Ancestor, save me!"

The elders behind him knelt down in unison, their kowtowing echoing loudly.

Gu Xuan put down his teacup, his tone calm: "The Soul-Devouring Powder isn't a difficult poison to cure. Once I bring him here, I'll cure him as well."

As well!

Cure him!

That's all!

Huo Dongyuan trembled, not from fear, but from excitement!

Eight hundred years of torment, the hope of the entire Holy Land, rested on these one sentence from the Gu family ancestor!

"Thank you, Ancestor! Thank you, Ancestor!" Huo Dongyuan kowtowed three times heavily, his forehead hitting the bluestone ground until blood flowed!

"From this day forward, the Qingyun Holy Land is willing to be subservient to the Gu family for generations to come, never to betray us!"

Gu Xuan glanced at him, didn't reply, but simply nodded.


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 42 What's with all this talk about the Western Emperor! Go talk to my ancestor if you have something to say!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Li Shuangxu stood to the side, watching his Holy Lord's face streaked with snot and tears, a complex mix of emotions welling up inside him.

To be honest, he had never seen Huo Dongyuan like this before.

The Gu family patriarch's words had saved not just one person, but the entire Holy Land!

Gu Tianhua sipped his tea nearby, his expression utterly calm!

He was used to such scenes; even if someone called Gu Xuan "father" on the spot, he wouldn't blink an eye!

Just then, hurried footsteps came from the foot of the mountain.

Gu Yushan, breathless, rushed to the back of the mountain, his face grim: "Patriarch! Something's happened!"

Gu Yushan gasped for breath and pointed outside the mountain gate: "A madwoman has come! A Saint King, chasing after eighteen people!"

Gu Xuan already knew about this group, but he hadn't expected them to head towards the Gu family.

With the cultivation level of a Great Emperor, even the slightest disturbance on this continent would be known to him!

"Jian Wuji, take Xiao Chenchen and deal with this."

"Yes!" Jian Wuji replied.

The people of Qingyun Holy Land then noticed a man standing behind Gu Xuan.

Just one look at him would terrify you!

They remembered the rumors circulating outside that someone from the Gu family had wiped out two Holy Lands with a single sword strike!

Hiss!!

This must be the man!

Outside the mountain gate.

A woman dressed in a golden suit, wielding a long whip, was chasing and lashing eighteen people!

The eighteen people were all covered in wounds and looked utterly disheveled!

But their cultivation levels were not low; they were all at the peak of the Saint realm!

Eighteen peak Saint realm cultivators were being whipped around like dogs by a woman—the scene was truly unsightly!

The onlookers had already retreated far away!

Not that they wanted to retreat, but they had no choice!

The woman's whip was too powerful; with each lash, a crack appeared in the ground.

The aftershocks were enough to kill even a King-level expert; who would dare approach?

"Aren't those people from the War Emperor's Palace of the Western Regions?" someone recognized the clothing and shouted!

"Yes!! That woman's attire is the mark of the War Emperor's Palace!"

"The War Emperor's Palace? That Eternal Realm power in the Western Regions?"

"That's it! I heard the War Emperor himself is an Eternal Realm expert, with countless Saint Kings under his command!"

"And who are those eighteen people? How did they offend the War Emperor's Palace? This is going to be interesting! I wonder if the Gu family will make a move!"

Someone recognized the eighteen figures fleeing in a disheveled state!

"It's the Righteous Gang! Those are the eighteen leaders of the Righteous Gang! They're all good people! They specialize in chivalrous deeds and have saved countless lives. How did they offend the War Emperor's Palace?"

"Good people? In this world, can being a good person avoid trouble?"

"Exactly! Everything is good these days, but being a good person is hard! And offending a power at the Eternal Realm level is a death sentence!"

Just then, the woman lashed out with her whip again!

It struck the slowest member of the Righteous Gang directly on the back!

The man screamed in agony, his back torn open, blood splattering everywhere, and he was sent flying!

The crowd watched in horror!

Hiss!!!

As the saying goes!

One whip shatters a saint's kindness! Two whips shatter a kind soul!

"Run? You think you're so fast?"

The woman sneered, her voice shrill.

"If you offend our War Emperor Palace, you'll die even if you run to the ends of the earth!"

The eighteen leaders of the Righteous Gang, supporting each other, retreated to the vicinity of the Gu Family Mountain Gate.

An old man gritted his teeth and said, "The War Emperor's Palace is going too far! We only rescued a few hostages, what's wrong with that?"

"Those hostages were goods our War Emperor's Palace wanted. Letting them go is suicide!"

There was nothing more to say!

The woman raised her long whip: "Today is your death anniversary!"

The Righteous Gang members looked towards the idyllic scene within the Gu Family Mountain Gate, a last glimmer of hope flashing in their eyes!

They had heard that the Gu Family wanted to take vassals, promising protection if they became vassals!

Although it was risky, it was their last chance to survive!

"Brothers, hold on!"

But the woman was getting closer and closer, her whip already swirling above their heads!

A middle-aged man from the Righteous Gang suddenly stopped, turned, drew his sword, and stood behind the others!

His name was Han Tiesheng, the third-ranking member of the Righteous Gang!

"Big Brother, you go first! I'll cover you!"

Another young man stopped, clutching a broken sword, covered in blood, but his eyes were resolute!

"Second Brother is right, we'll hold her off, Big Brother, lead the brothers and go first!"

The others also wanted to stop, but the leader, the elder Shen Qinghe, the head of the Righteous Gang, turned back, his eyes red-rimmed!

He and these brothers had fought their way through three hundred years, rising from nobody to the peak of Sainthood, relying not on cultivation, but on a spirit!

That spirit was righteousness!

"Bullshit!"

Shen Qinghe grabbed Han Tiesheng.

"We go together, we die together! When have I ever abandoned you?"

Han Tiesheng cried out, "Big Brother!"

"Shut up!"

Shen Qinghe shoved him back.

"You go first! Living is more important than anything! This is Big Brother's last order!"

The woman didn't care about any of that, and lashed out with her whip!

This whip carried the full power of a Saint King at the peak of the first level!

It struck straight at Shen Qinghe's face.

The instant the whip struck, Shen Qinghe closed his eyes!

Then, he didn't feel the pain!

A crisp crack rang in his ears!

He opened his eyes!

The long whip stopped in mid-air, breaking apart inch by inch from the tip, scattering into fragments!

It wasn't blocked, it shattered!

He wondered who had intervened?!

The woman watched the whip in her hand dissipate, her pupils shrinking sharply, her smile freezing on her face!

"Who is it?!" she demanded sharply.

A crack appeared in the great formation.

A blue dragon flew out, and riding on its back was Gu Chen!

Jian Wuji walked at the forefront, holding a black longsword, flying straight out.

Gu Chen, seated atop the Azure Dragon, looked at the woman. His voice, though not loud, was clear: "Causing trouble on my Gu family's territory, injuring innocents, disrupting order—this is Gu family territory, not a place for you to run wild. You have three breaths to get out!"

As the young master, Gu Chen naturally couldn't tolerate violence on his home turf!

The entire arena fell silent!

All the onlookers held their breath!

Those were people from the War Emperor's Palace, disciples of an ancient realm power, telling someone to get out just like that?

The Gu family was too powerful!

They hadn't expected the Gu family to intervene in this matter; it seemed there was going to be a good show!

Everyone watched this spectacle with bated breath!

The woman's face changed!

She hadn't expected anyone to dare speak to her like that in this remote, impoverished place!

This was truly outrageous!

"Do you know who I am? The Western Regions War Emperor's Palace! The War Emperor is an Eternal Realm expert! How dare you meddle in my affairs?"

Gu Chen remained silent!

What Western Regions, what War Emperor? Never heard of them!

None of that!

Go tell my ancestor what you have to say!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 43 The Gu Family! No Threats Allowed!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Suddenly, a figure stepped out from the crowd of the Righteous Gang.

Dressed in green, with delicate features and an outstanding air.

She walked out from behind Shen Qinghe, her steps light and graceful, as if she had known this moment would come!

Shen Qinghe's expression changed, and he reached out to stop her, but she gently pushed him away.

"Yao'er, you…"

"Big Brother! What's meant to happen will happen!"

Her name was Shen Yao, the eighteenth leader of the Righteous Gang!

She walked to Gu Chen's side, gave him a slight nod as a greeting, then turned to look at the woman.

"You say we ruined the War Emperor's Palace's plans? Those hostages are the goods you wanted?" Shen Yao's voice wasn't loud.

But every word was clear!

"Your War Emperor's Palace runs rampant in the Western Regions, forcibly taking women. Do you really think the whole world doesn't know?"

The woman's expression changed slightly.

Shen Yao continued, her voice growing colder: "This time you're chasing us not because of those hostages, are you! It's because the Western Heaven War Emperor has taken a liking to me, isn't it!"

The entire arena erupted in uproar!

The surrounding cultivators exchanged bewildered glances.

The Western Heaven War Emperor's reputation for womanizing was known even in the Eastern Region.

Rumor had it that he once took a fancy to a married woman whose husband was also an Eternal Realm expert. The Western Heaven War Emperor's attempt to abduct her failed, and he was severely injured, taking three hundred years to recover.

His lecherous nature hadn't changed, and now he had set his sights on this woman in green.

Shen Yao sneered: "The War Emperor's Palace, what a show of power! You take whoever you fancy, and if you can't, you wipe out their entire family! And you come up with this high-sounding reason? Don't you find it laughable?"

The woman's face darkened completely!

She hadn't expected this seemingly gentle woman to tear away the War Emperor's Palace's facade with just a few words!

"It's no use saying anything now! You're leaving today, whether you like it or not! If you know what's good for you, come back with me and serve the War Emperor well, and you'll suffer less!

Otherwise, you'll only face death!"

She glared at Gu Chen and threatened, "I advise you not to meddle! The affairs of the War Emperor's Palace are not your concern! Otherwise, if the War Emperor is enraged, your entire Gu family will be wiped out!"

The War Emperor's wrath can cause bloodshed for miles!

Clearly, they weren't taught a lesson enough by that Eternal Realm expert surnamed Lin!

Their nature is hard to change!

This time, they've set their sights on Shen Yao of the Righteous Gang!

They'll just find some excuse to bring her back!

Nothing else matters!

After all, the title of Western Heaven War Emperor isn't given lightly!

Gu Chen frowned.

"I said I'd give you three breaths to get out of here!"

Gu Chen's voice turned cold: "Not only are you not leaving, but you're threatening the Gu family?"

The woman sneered: "Threaten? You think you're worthy of my threats? I'll say it again, hand over the person!"

A family from a remote, impoverished area dares to threaten the War Emperor's Palace?!

Anyone who doesn't know better would think they're an imperial family!

"Once the Western Heaven War Emperor arrives in person, these people will know what a powerful expert of the Eternal Realm truly is!"

Hearing these threatening words repeatedly, Jian Wuji immediately became displeased!

Threats were one thing, but to dare disrespect the young master!

Jian Wuji raised his hand and drew his sword!

*Crack!*

In less than a breath!

The woman's body froze in place, her mouth still open, her expression frozen in the instant of her cold smile!

The next second, her body split open from the middle, turning into a cloud of blood mist!

Not even a scream!

In an instant!

The cultivators who had been anticipating this spectacle were completely stunned!

"Instant kill?! Instant kill?!"

"Damn, they really killed them!"

"Hiss! The Gu family is terrifying! They even dared to kill people from the War Emperor's Palace!"

"That's a member of the Western Heaven War Emperor! A Western Heaven War Emperor at the Eternal Realm!"

"Has the Gu family gone mad? No! The Gu family is completely fearless!"

"One sword strike, they didn't even see the sword clearly before they were gone! What is the cultivation level of that white-robed man from the Gu family?!"

"Who cares about his cultivation level? He seems much higher than a Saint King! I think the War Emperor's Palace has really bitten off more than they can chew this time!"

"Beater? This is steel!"

Outside the mountain gate, chaos erupted. Shock, fear, excitement, and those who loved a good spectacle—a mix of emotions swirled together.

Shen Yao was stunned!

Shen Qinghe was even more shocked!

The other members of the Righteous Gang never expected the Gu family to kill people from the War Emperor's Palace so readily!

Everyone thought the Gu family would at most drive away the War Emperor's Palace members!

But the man, without uttering a word, killed the War Emperor's Palace member with a single sword strike!

That was a Saint King!

He represented the War Emperor's Palace!

Killing him outright was undoubtedly an offense against the War Emperor's Palace!

The man didn't seem to care at all!

Jian Wuji sheathed his sword, expressionless: "The Gu family will not tolerate any threats! Threateners will only face death!"

These words were spoken softly, but everyone present heard them clearly!

Outside the mountain gate, tens of thousands of cultivators were deathly silent!

After an unknown amount of time, someone finally spoke from the crowd:

"The Gu family doesn't even respect the War Emperor's Palace!"

No one refuted this, because everyone had seen the white-robed man kill a Saint King without even lifting an eyelid!

What such power signified was clear to anyone with a modicum of sense!

"From today onwards, the rules of the Eastern Region will likely have to change," an old man muttered to himself.

"Change what? The Gu family is the rule!"

Amidst the murmurs, the attitudes of those forces that had been observing from the sidelines completely shifted!

Just moments ago, some hesitated about vying for the eighteen vassal positions; now, they practically wanted to rush into the Gu family and kneel down, calling them "father!"

With strength this great, being a vassal wasn't a humiliation at all!

It was practically a stroke of good fortune!

A sect leader from a second-rate sect gazed longingly at the celestial realm within the grand formation: "Do you think if we could become vassals of the Gu family, we could cultivate inside?"

A person beside him swallowed hard: "What are you thinking? Being a vassal is good enough; you want to cultivate inside?"

"What if? The Gu family is so generous; maybe..."

Before he could finish speaking...

A rogue cultivator nearby sneered, "Generous? Their generosity is their own ability. What do you have to offer to make them generous to you?"

The sect leader opened his mouth, but couldn't speak.

Yes, what?

With his meager wealth, even a single hair from the Gu family was worth more than their entire sect combined!

Shen Qinghe knelt on the ground, trembling all over!

Not from fear, but from shock!

He had lived for thousands of years and had never seen such a powerful figure!

Slaying a Saint King like killing a chicken, without even blinking an eye!

What shocked him even more was that the Gu family didn't take the War Emperor's Palace seriously at all!

That composure wasn't feigned!

Shen Yao also knelt down, her eyes red-rimmed: "Thank you so much, Gu family, for saving my life! The entire Yi Gang is willing to serve you like a slave for generations to come to repay your kindness!" Behind her, the seventeen leaders knelt down in unison, their kowtowing echoing loudly.

Gu Chen stepped forward and helped Shen Qinghe up, then helped Shen Yao up, his voice gentle: "Get up. The Gu family does not take advantage of others in times of distress. Everything is up to the Ancestor to decide."


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 44 The Mirror of Inquiry: A Beast Tide Without Warning!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Shen Yao stood up, gazing at Gu Chen's profile, a complex mix of emotions welling up within her.

This young man before her was merely a King-level cultivator, yet he rode a Azure Dragon, and his guard had slain a Saint King with a single sword strike!

This wasn't a matter of cultivation level; it was a matter of confidence!

Where did the Gu family's confidence come from?

She looked towards the depths of the grand formation!

Perhaps the answer lay there!

The news spread like wildfire!

In less than half a day, the entire Eastern Region knew that the Gu family had killed members of the War Emperor's Palace!

Some said the Gu family had gone mad, others said they were fearless.

But most people were asking the same question: Where was the Gu family? Would they still accept vassals?

If they didn't go now, it would be too late!

In a small sect in Qingyun Prefecture, the sect leader slammed his fist on the table: "Prepare gifts! Go to the Gu family! Even if we don't become vassals, absorbing a few breaths of spiritual energy outside the Gu family's mountain gate will be worth it!"

In a large power in a neighboring prefecture, the elders were in a heated argument!

The pro-war faction said the Gu family had offended the War Emperor's Palace and were doomed!

The pro-peace faction said the Gu family knew about the War Emperor's Palace but still dared to kill, so they must have a trump card!

After arguing for three hours without reaching a conclusion, the sect leader finally made the decision: "Go and see! No malice intended, just consider it making friends!"

Three days passed in the blink of an eye!

Outside the Gu family's mountain gate, a sea of ​​people thronged. From the foot of the mountain to miles away, it was densely packed with cultivators!

There were locals from Qingyun Prefecture, people who had come from neighboring prefectures, and even some who had crossed half a continent from the Western and Northern Regions!

Everyone was waiting, waiting for the Gu family to open its mountain gate.

But inside the Gu family, at this moment, there was an unusual calm.

Back on the mountain, Gu Xuan sat up from his recliner, a bronze mirror appearing in his hand.

The mirror was simple and unadorned, devoid of any patterns or spiritual energy fluctuations; it looked like an ordinary bronze mirror.

This bronze mirror was a "Heart-Testing Mirror," one Gu Xuan had obtained from previous sign-ins but had never used.

Today was perfect; he would use it to test the sincerity of those forces.

Gu Tianhua carefully took the bronze mirror. He could sense that this mirror was extraordinary, but he couldn't quite put his finger on why.

Anyway! It just felt different!

Perhaps, this was a case of aging and limited experience!

Gu Xuan explained, "This mirror can create illusions. Within these illusions are endless temptations and threats. If you can withstand them, it proves your sincerity is sufficient. If you can't, you're eliminated, no matter how high your cultivation level."

Gu Tianhua nodded. He hadn't expected such a small mirror to have such an effect!

This was truly eye-opening!

"I'll go prepare it now."

He turned to make the arrangements.

In the plaza, the Gu family members were all ready.

Gu Chen stood at the front, followed by Gu Tianhua and several other elders.

Jian Wuji stood to the side, holding his longsword, his face expressionless.

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue stood on the other side, their white robes flowing, like celestial beings.

As for Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua, they had disappeared again, riding their white tiger.

Ever since Gu Xuanhuan took the white tiger as their mount, they'd been restless, always running off and disappearing without a trace.

Gu Yushan paced the plaza three times, beads of sweat forming increasingly dense on his forehead.

He stopped a clansman and asked, "Have you seen Qingqing and Huahua?"

The clansman shook his head.

He stopped another, and they shook their heads again.

Those two girls, always riding their white tiger everywhere, never even a glimpse of them!

Gu Yushan stamped his feet anxiously, rushing to Gu Chen. "Young Master, have you seen those two little girls? Today is the opening of the mountain gates, such an important day. If they run off and something happens to them, what will I do?"

Gu Chen put down his sword, thought for a moment, and shook his head. "Elder, I haven't seen them since I woke up this morning. They've probably gone out for a ride on their tigers again. Don't worry too much; with the White Tiger with them, nothing will happen."

"How can I not worry? They're my own granddaughters!"

Gu Yushan paced anxiously, then turned and ran towards the back mountain.

Back mountain, Gu Xuan was reclining on a chair, drinking tea.

Gu Yushan rushed up, about to speak, when Gu Xuan raised a hand to stop him.

Gu Xuan looked at the distant horizon, his voice calm: "You're here to see Qingqing and Huahua? Don't worry, with the White Tiger, no one can touch them."

Gu Yushan opened his mouth, wanting to say something.

But the Ancestor hadn't lied, so he was relieved. However, those two little girls were too unruly!

Next time, he must keep a closer eye on them!

No sooner had Gu Yushan left than Gu Tianhua arrived!

His face was ashen, and he clutched a communication talisman in his hand!

"Ancestor! Something terrible has happened!"

Gu Xuan put down his teacup: "What is it?"

"A beast tide has erupted in the Demon Beast Forest! It's advancing towards the Eastern Region! Leading them are three high-level Eternal Realm demon beasts!"

Gu Tianhua's voice trembled.

He had seen records of the beast tide in the Northern Region ten thousand years ago, where six human Eternal Realm cultivators fought against three demon beast Eternal Realm cultivators!

Three died, two were severely injured, and the last one only survived thanks to a Quasi-Emperor artifact!

That battle devastated the Northern Region, leaving it barren and taking ten thousand years to recover!

And Gu Tianhua had only just broken through to the Saint King level; he was no match for the Eternal Realm cultivators!

Perhaps only the Ancestor could solve this!

Once the beast tide enters the Eastern Region, it will undoubtedly be a scene of utter devastation!

The Gu family will certainly suffer a severe blow!

Gu Xuan accepted the transmission talisman, scanned it with his divine sense, and his expression remained unchanged!

Three Eternal Realm demonic beasts, one demonic ape, one flood dragon, and one dark wolf.

Following them were tens of thousands of demonic beasts, leaving nothing but desolation in their wake!

The news reached outside the mountain gate, causing an uproar!

Those forces that had originally been waiting to become vassals of the Gu family were now in complete chaos.

"Three Eternal Realm demonic beasts! How can we stop them?"

"It's over! The beast tide is entering the Eastern Region, we're all going to die! What are we going to do!"

"The Northern Region suffered heavy casualties with only six Eternal Realm demonic beasts back then, how many can the Eastern Region muster? Two? Or three?"

"Gathering them would just be suicide! Demonic beasts' bodies are far stronger than humans', they could easily take on two at the same level!"

"Run! It'll be too late if we don't run now!"

"How can we run? Can you outrun an Eternal Realm demonic beast?"

Some people packed their belongings, some collapsed to the ground, some wept bitterly!

Despair surrounded the crowd!

Huo Dongyuan stood at the front of the crowd, his face grave!

He was a Saint King, but against an Eternal Realm demonic beast, he was nothing!

He turned to look at the celestial realm within the Gu family's mountain gate, a crazy thought surging in his mind: Could the Gu family stop them?

Perhaps only the Gu family could withstand them!

Shen Qinghe's expression was equally grave.

None of the eighteen members of the Righteous Gang retreated, but the fear in their hearts was real!

Eternal Realm demonic beasts—those are legendary beings!

A rogue cultivator from the Northern Region slumped to the ground, recounting the horrific scene he'd heard about ten thousand years ago: "Six Eternal Realm demonic beasts! All ancient monsters who had lived for tens of thousands of years. After that battle, three died, two were crippled, and one was bedridden for three hundred years before he could walk again!"

That battle was truly devastating!

The crowd grew even more desperate.

"Even the Northern Region couldn't stop them, how can we?"

"We can't even defeat those capable in the Northern Region, let alone us rogue cultivators!"

"The Gu family? No matter how strong the Gu family is, can they withstand three Eternal Realm demonic beasts?"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 45 Monsters Rampage! The Five Major Families Flee
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Meanwhile!

On the Eastern Border!

In the Demon Beast Forest, demon beasts periodically attack on the orders of the Demon King!

This time, however, the attackers are three Eternal Realm demon beasts!

They are incredibly powerful! They launch a fierce attack on the Eastern Region with unstoppable momentum!

Instantly! The Eastern Region trembles!

The Sword-Gazing Great Wall!

This majestic pass, standing for eight thousand years, is now soaked in blood!

Below the walls, the corpses of demon beasts pile up like mountains, but more demon beasts continue to surge forward!

King-level demon beasts roam the mountains, and Emperor-level demon beasts are countless!

Even Saint-level demon beasts are mixed in!

Every claw leaves deep claw marks on the walls!

The cultivators guarding the city have not slept for a day and a night!

"West City is in dire straits! Three Saint-level demonic beasts have breached the city walls!"

"East City's spiritual energy is exhausted! Requesting reinforcements!"

"We have no reinforcements! All reserves have been deployed!"

The shouts of battle, screams of agony, and the roars of demonic beasts mingled together, deafeningly loud!

On the city wall, an old man with white hair, covered in blood, held a long sword!

He was Murong Lie, the city lord of Sword Gazing City, a Saint-level expert at the eighth level!

Upon the outbreak of the beast tide, he immediately closed the city gates and sent messengers to various states for reinforcements!

This place was the first line of defense against the demonic beasts in the Eastern Region!

If the city fell, the gates of the Eastern Region would be opened!

The demonic beasts would then sweep across the states without restraint!

"Hold on even if it's difficult! I've already sent people to notify the major powers in each state! Reinforcements should arrive in half a day at most!"

Murong Lie raised his long sword, his spirits rising!

But he wasn't confident either. Facing three Eternal Realm experts leading a pack of demonic beasts, without the support of powerful forces, the city's fall was only a matter of time!

"Patriarch! You're injured, heal first! There are too many demonic beasts. If it gets too bad, let's retreat! We'll retaliate when those powerful forces arrive!"

A man covered in blood said to Murong Lie.

"It's alright! I can still fight! Go and defend the city walls, those beasts are coming again!"

Panic gripped the city. Everyone with any cultivation was conscripted to defend the walls, but some still packed up and left!

For them, it was tantamount to suicide!

Three Eternal Realm experts!

The history of the Northern Region ten thousand years ago had already provided the answer!

Most forces received the message Murong Lie sent out!

Including the Gu family!

The first to act were the families with Eternal Realm experts at their disposal: the Wang family, the Huo family, the Ming family, the Tian family, and the Barbarian Clan!

The five remaining pillars of the Eastern Region are all at the Eternal Realm!

But upon hearing of three Eternal Realm demonic beasts, they made the same decision!

Run!

Not fight! Run!!

They only wanted to protect themselves; they had no interest in the people of the Eastern Region!

Discretion is the better part of valor!

They weren't saviors!

They had no obligation to protect!

Inside the Wang family compound, the patriarch, Wang Zhentian, solemnly declared, "Pass down the order: the entire clan must relocate. Take everything you can, and depart immediately for the Central Continent!"

Not a single elder objected!

The Central Continent was the center of the Three Thousand Provinces, the home of the Imperial Clan, and home to countless Eternal Realm forces!

Only there would they be safe!

The imperial clan could easily suppress these demonic beasts by simply sending a few people!

There's no need for them to make unnecessary sacrifices!

When disaster strikes, everyone flees for their lives!

As for those people in the Eastern Region, their deaths are their own business; they don't care!

The Huo family, the Ming family, the Tian family, and the Barbarian Clan all made the same choice almost simultaneously!

The five great families, the five powerful figures at the Eternal Realm, not one of them stepped forward to say, "I will defend the Eastern Region!"

The news of the five great families' mass exodus shattered the last hope of the rogue cultivators!

"The Wang family ran away, the Huo family ran away, the Ming family, the Tian family, the Barbarian Clan, they all ran away!" A rogue cultivator slumped to the ground, his face ashen.

"They all have Eternal Realm experts, they can run faster than anyone else! What about us? Where can we run to?"

"Central Continent? That's the territory of the Imperial Clan, we don't even have the qualifications to go in!"

"Even if we could get in, could we outrun those Eternal Realm demonic beasts? They could kill a whole group of us with a single claw."

"Doomsday! It really is the end of the world!"

Despair weighed heavily on everyone's hearts like a mountain!

Gu Xuan saw all of this!

These Eastern Region forces are truly laughable. They act like kings and tyrants in peacetime, but when disaster strikes, they run faster than anyone else!

On the contrary, those small and medium-sized forces and rogue cultivators understand the principle of mutual dependence better!

Murong Lie has been able to hold on until now because of the courage of these people!

On the city wall of Jianwang, Murong Lie slashed back a Saint-level demonic beast with a single sword strike, retreating a few steps and panting heavily!

His wounds were bleeding, his spiritual energy was depleting, but he could not fall!

"Report!"

A messenger rushed forward, his face deathly pale!

"City Lord, the Wang family has replied!"

Murong Lie's eyes lit up: "What did they say?"

The messenger opened his mouth, his voice trembling: "The Wang family says they are too busy to help!"

Murong Lie's smile froze on his face.

Too busy to help?

The Wang family has an Ancient Realm expert in charge; they are the most powerful force in the entire Eastern Region!

What does "too busy to help" mean?

"What about the Huo family? What about the Ming family?"

The messenger lowered his head: "The Huo family says they have moved to the Central Plains and should not worry! The Ming family hasn't replied! The Tian family, the Barbarian Clan, none of them have replied!"

The five great families have all fled!

Murong Lie's longsword

Clang!

Falled to the ground!

He had lived for eight thousand years, guarding the Sword-Gazing Great Wall for three thousand years, never imagining that one day he would be abandoned!

Not defeated by demonic beasts, but abandoned by his own people!

"Lord!"

The lieutenant beside him had red eyes!

"Shall we retreat?"

Murong Lie looked up at the dense horde of demonic beasts below the city walls, then back at the cultivators still desperately resisting within the city.

Retreat?

Where to?

Behind them were billions of lives in the Eastern Region. If they retreated, who would defend?

"We cannot retreat!"

Murong Lie picked up his longsword, his voice resolute!

"Sword-Gazing Great Wall is the gate to the Eastern Region! If the gate falls, the demonic beasts will rampage in, and countless families will be destroyed! We have guarded it for three thousand years; we cannot lose the gate today!"

"But there is no support!"

"No support, then fight to the death!"

Murong Lie turned, facing the endless horde of demonic beasts below, and raised his longsword:

"Brothers of Sword-Gazing City! There is no support! The five great families have fled! No one is coming to save us!"

A deathly silence fell over the city walls!

"But we can't run! Behind us are our homes, our families, the millions of lives of the Eastern Region! As long as the Sword-Wang Great Wall stands, the gates of the Eastern Region stand! If the Sword-Wang Great Wall falls, the gates of the Eastern Region fall!"

"Tell me, are you willing to let those beasts trample over your corpses to slaughter your loved ones?!"

"No!!!"

Thousands of cultivators roared in unison, their voices shaking the heavens!

Murong Lie smiled!

"Then fight!"

"Fight!!!"

Despair transformed into a resolute will to die!

No one thought of retreating anymore, no one waited for rescue! Gripping their weapons, burning their spiritual power, they prepared for a final, desperate battle!

Just then, several streaks of light flew in from the horizon!

Not reinforcements, but several people!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 46: A Single Sentence! Mediating the War
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

An old man, accompanied by three Saint-level experts, flew in from the east and landed on the city wall!

The old man bowed to Murong Lie: "The Zhang family of Huyun Prefecture is late! We've brought three Saints, awaiting Lord Murong's orders!"

Murong Lie was stunned!

The Zhang family?

He had heard of them; a family in Huyun Prefecture, whose strongest member was this old man, a Saint-level expert!

He hadn't expected that even the Wang family wouldn't come, but they had!

"You!"

The old man smiled bitterly: "The five great families have fled, but there are more than just five great families in the Eastern Region! We small families, without our homes, have nothing. Guarding the Eastern Region is guarding our own homes!"

Just as he finished speaking, several more streaks of light flew in from the west!

A middle-aged woman, accompanied by two Saint-level experts, landed on the city wall: "The Li family of Yunzhou, coming to provide support!"

From the north: "The Zhao family of Cangzhou, coming to provide support!"

From the south: "The Zhou family of Lingzhou, coming to provide support!"

One after another, group after group!

There were no Eternal Realm cultivators, no Saint Kings, and many didn't even have Saints!

King Realm and Emperor Realm cultivators, their cultivation levels varied greatly, but every single one of them had a burning rage in their eyes!

Murong Lie stared at these people, his Adam's apple bobbing, speechless!

The five great families had fled, and all those who came were small and medium-sized forces!

They knew they were committing suicide, yet they still came!

"Good, good, good!" Murong Lie said three times, bowing deeply with his hands clasped!

"Murong Lie, on behalf of the people of the Eastern Region, thanks you all!"

With the addition of fresh troops, the defenses of Sword Hope City were temporarily stabilized!

But everyone knew that this was only temporary!

The three Eternal Realm demonic beasts hadn't even made a move yet; once they did, everyone present combined wouldn't be enough to withstand a single blow from them.

The news of the five great families' escape quickly reached the back mountain, and Gu Tianhua's face turned green with rage.

Gu Xuan, however, smiled, a very calm smile.

The various powerful families are sacrificing millions of lives to protect their own interests—it's truly laughable!
