"They ran away, so what? The Eastern Region doesn't need a few more."

"But Ancestor!"

"What's the rush?" Gu Xuan sat up, his divine sense sweeping across the area.

Gu Xuan's divine sense swept over the Eastern Region's border, his brows furrowing slightly!

The carnage in Sword Hope City, the escape of the five major families, the ferocity of the smaller forces—everything was laid bare before his eyes!

But at this moment, his attention was focused on something else: a hundred thousand miles to the southeast, a white tiger was carrying two little girls, flying at an incredible speed towards Sword Hope City.

"That stupid tiger!" Gu Xuan murmured, a hint of helplessness in his voice!

He wasn't worried about the two girls' safety!

The white tiger was a Great Emperor; let alone three Eternal Realm demon beasts, even thirty thousand wouldn't be a match for it!

He frowned because the two girls had no idea what a beast tide was!

In their eyes, the powerful energy fluctuations erupting from the Demon Beast Forest meant the birth of a rare treasure—something to behold!

They absolutely had to go see the spectacle!

And indeed, just as Gu Xuan had predicted.

Half an hour earlier, Gu Qingqing, perched on the white tiger's back, suddenly sat up and pointed southeast: "There's a huge commotion over there! Has some treasure been born?"

Gu Huahua also sensed it, her eyes sparkling: "It really is! That energy is so strong! It must be something good! White Tiger, White Tiger, take us there quickly!"

The white tiger sighed helplessly!

It knew, of course, that wasn't some rare treasure; it was a beast tide—three Eternal Realm demonic beasts leading tens of thousands of other beasts were attacking the Eastern Region's border!

But its two little darlings had spoken; could it refuse?

With it around, those beasts wouldn't cause much trouble!

So the White Tiger changed direction and flew towards the beast tide!

Gu Qingqing excitedly patted the tiger's head: "Hurry, hurry! Don't let someone else snatch it!"

Gu Huahua chimed in, "That's right! If you're too slow, it'll be gone!"

The White Tiger was bitter inside, but didn't say it aloud!

Looking towards the city wall, Murong Lie raised his sword again!

With the support of smaller forces, the defensive line was temporarily stabilized, but he knew in his heart that this was only a temporary fix!

The three Eternal Realm demonic beasts hadn't even made a move yet; once they did, everyone present combined wouldn't be enough to withstand a single blow from them!

"City Lord, look!" the deputy general suddenly pointed to the horizon!

A white light, swift as lightning, flew towards Sword Gazing City!

Murong Lie's heart tightened. Who was it?

Were they here to provide support, or just to watch the spectacle?

The white light drew closer and closer!

It wasn't a spirit boat, nor a celestial escape, but a gigantic white tiger!

Two little girls sat on its back!

Murong Lie was stunned!

He had lived for eight thousand years and had never seen such a scene—a white tiger with unfathomable power, carrying two little girls of eleven or twelve, flying towards the battlefield?

Were they here to fight?

Or for a picnic?

The cultivators on the city walls were also stunned.

"What is that?"

"A white tiger? And two little girls?"

"Could they have taken the wrong path?! This is a battlefield!"

The three Eternal Realm demonic beasts behind the beast tide also sensed it!

The demonic ape raised its head, the dragon raised its head, and the Nether Wolf pricked up its ears!

Then they sensed the White Tiger's aura!

A terrifying aura swept over them!

Divine beast bloodline! Emperor-level pressure!

The demonic ape's legs began to tremble!

The dragon's wings folded, afraid to open!

The Netherwolf's flames died down, and it lay motionless on the ground!

Tens of thousands of demonic beasts lay prostrate on the ground!

Trembling! Afraid to move!

The White Tiger hovered above Sword Gazing City, its golden pupils surveying the scene below, its pressure enveloping the entire battlefield!

Gu Qingqing leaned halfway out from the tiger's back and glanced down: "Huh? So many demonic beasts! Are they fighting?"

Gu Huahua also poked her head out: "Didn't they say there was treasure? Where's the treasure?"

The white tiger calmed itself and said, "My two little ancestors, this isn't the birth of treasure, it's a beast tide. Those demonic beasts are attacking human cities."

Gu Qingqing tilted her head: "A beast tide? Is it fun?"

The white tiger was silent for a moment: "No, it's not fun. A lot of people will die."

Gu Qingqing looked at the cultivators covered in blood on the city wall, then at the mountain of demonic beast corpses piled up below, and finally understood.

She patted the white tiger's head, stood up from its back, put her hands on her hips, and took a deep breath!

"Stop right there!!!"

Her voice was childish, but full of imposing force!

The white tiger released a burst of pressure at the opportune moment, matching her shout.

The three ancient realm demonic beasts simultaneously knelt down, burying their heads in the ground!

Tens of thousands of demonic beasts lay motionless on the ground, not daring to even breathe loudly!

Across the city walls, all was deathly silent!

Murong Lie's sword fell to the ground for the second time, but he didn't even notice!

The cultivators behind him gaped, speechless!

They had fought for a day and a night, thousands dead, so desperate they were ready to die, when then a little girl shouted, "Stop!"

Just like that?

"Then, who are those two little girls?" a young cultivator murmured!

A single sentence to mediate a war?!

"Immediately call the Special...mediator!"

This is too outrageous!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 47: Subduing Three Demonic Beasts Leaves the City Wall Crowd Stunned!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

No one could answer the young cultivator's question!

Everyone was still in shock!

After Gu Qingqing shouted, she waited a moment and found that the demonic beasts had indeed stopped moving!

She tilted her head, thought for a moment, and patted the white tiger's head: "Did you make them stop?"

The white tiger answered truthfully: "My aura has a suppressive effect on demonic beasts."

Gu Huahua's eyes lit up: "Then can you make them obey me?"

The white tiger replied: "Yes."

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua exchanged a glance and smiled simultaneously!

The smile was innocent and naive, but the white tiger inexplicably felt a chill run down its spine!

It felt like something bad was about to happen!

While the two little girls were plotting new games, the three Eternal Realm demonic beasts were already in an uproar!

The demonic ape struck first, its divine sense transmission filled with rage: "It's all your fault! You insisted on starting a beast tide! Now look what's happened, we've encountered a divine beast! A Great Emperor no less! What are we going to do?!"

The dragon chimed in: "Exactly! I told you not to be so high-profile, but you wouldn't listen! All the energy accumulated over ten thousand years was used to open the beast tide passage, and now look what's happened, the energy's gone, and you've even gotten yourself caught in the crossfire!"

The dark wolf was utterly aggrieved: "How was I supposed to know we'd encounter a Great Emperor? The Eastern Region hasn't produced a Great Emperor in millions of years! Not to mention the Eastern Region, even the Central Plains have very few. How can you blame me?"

"Who else is to blame but you? You sensed some weakening human fortune and said now was the best time to attack! You must know this is a Great Emperor! Not a Quasi-Emperor!"

"Can't I have been wrong? Didn't you all agree at the time?"

"Our agreement doesn't give you the right to shirk responsibility!"

The three demonic beasts argued fiercely, their divine sense transmission growing increasingly intense!

If it weren't for the White Tiger's oppressive aura, they would have almost come to blows on the spot!

The Dark Wolf grew increasingly nervous as it argued, its voice growing softer and softer.

It hadn't expected to be so unlucky—a Divine Beast of the Emperor level!

And of all people, they'd run into it!

Just then, footsteps approached.

The three demonic beasts simultaneously fell silent and looked up!

Two little girls jumped down from the white tiger's back and walked towards them.

Gu Qingqing walked in front, with Gu Huahua following behind; the two girls combined weren't even as tall as the white tiger's leg.

This was the white tiger in its shrunken form, showing how humble it was!

The three Eternal Realm demonic beasts looked at them with dread!

The white tiger stared at them with its golden pupils, its meaning clear: Make them happy, or I'll be unhappy!

Then I'll make them fly!

Gu Qingqing walked up to the demonic ape, looking up at it: "Can you transform into a human?"

The demonic ape paused, a flash of light appearing.

He transformed into a burly, dark-faced man over two meters tall, with broad shoulders and a muscular physique!

He knelt on one knee, head bowed, unable to look directly at her!

Gu Qingqing nodded in satisfaction: "Hmm! Looks quite honest! Just too dark."

The Demonic Ape: I am dark!

She then looked at the Flood Dragon.

A flash of light appeared on the Flood Dragon's body.

He transformed into a man in a green robe, with a cold and stern face and an effeminate air!

Gu Huahua commented: "He's alright looking, just a bit effeminate!"

The Flood Dragon's lips twitched, but he didn't dare utter a sound!

Finally, it was the Nether Wolf.

He transformed into a centenarian, his face full of wrinkles, his back hunched!

Gu Qingqing glanced at him and said to Gu Huahua, "This guy looks like Old Wang, the woodcutter from the next village."

Minglang almost lost his footing!

He was an ancient demon, having lived for ten thousand years. What did he mean by looking like Old Wang, the woodcutter from the next village?

This was utterly unreasonable!

Forget it!

What reason was there now?

The white tiger behind them was the reason!

"You three!"

Gu Qingqing put her hands on her hips. "From today onwards, you'll follow us. Any objections?"

The three demon beasts shook their heads simultaneously!

They had objections, but dared not speak them!

Even the White Tiger, a Great Emperor, had to appease these two little girls!

This showed how terrifying the person behind these two little girls was!

To a Great Emperor, they were merely slightly stronger ants!

If they were to say they were subordinates of a Great Emperor!

That would be far more prestigious than those old demons!

What was there to hold back!

"Alright!"

Gu Qingqing pointed at the Demonic Ape.

"From now on, you'll be responsible for carrying our things!"

The Demonic Ape nodded frantically!

Gu Qingqing then pointed at the Flood Dragon.

"From now on, you'll be responsible for scouting ahead for us!"

The Flood Dragon nodded frantically as well!

Finally, she pointed at the Dark Wolf: "Your job is to make us laugh."

The Dark Wolf paused for a moment: "Make us laugh? Make us laugh?"

"Just tell jokes. Aren't you wolves the best at barking? You can bark like a dog!"

The Dark Wolf opened its mouth, but no sound came out.

He was a Great Demon of the Eternal Realm, and you expect him to bark like a dog?

That's not right!

The White Tiger's pupils narrowed slightly!

The Dark Wolf immediately barked: "Woof!"

Gu Huahua clapped her hands: "Good dog! That's a nice bark! One more!"

The Dark Wolf felt utterly hopeless, but its mouth still honestly barked:

Woof!

Woof woof!

Woof woof woof!

Thus, tens of thousands of cultivators atop Sword Gazing City watched helplessly as three Eternal Realm demonic beasts were tamed and subdued by two little girls!

One carried a banner, one scouted ahead, and the other barked like a dog!

As for the demonic beasts, they all fled back to the Demonic Beast Forest!

They dared not look back!

“Am I dreaming?” one cultivator pinched his cheek!

“It’s not a dream! I saw it too!”

“Eternal Realm demonic beasts barking like dogs?”

“Don’t ask, I’ve never seen anything like it in my life! All the demonic beasts retreated! They were persuaded to retreat by two little girls with just a few words!”

Murong Lie took a deep breath, trying to calm himself down!

He was a Saint King, with eight thousand years of composure, and he almost collapsed at this moment!

Just then, a sharp-eyed cultivator suddenly pointed at Gu Qingqing’s waist!

His voice changed: “Look! What’s that?!”

Everyone looked in the direction he was pointing!

Gu Qingqing wore a small white jade waist tag with the single character "Gu" engraved on it!

Gu Huahua also wore an identical one!

"Gu! It's the Gu family! They're from the Gu family!"

The cultivator screamed, his voice booming across the city wall!

Boom!

The city wall erupted in chaos!

"The Gu family? The Gu family that destroyed the three holy lands?"

"The Gu family that dared to kill the War Emperor's Palace?"

"No wonder! No wonder! They're from the Gu family!"

"My God! Even two little girls from the Gu family are this powerful? How strong must the Gu family patriarch be?"

"Who cares how powerful he is? From today onwards, our lives are saved by the Gu family!"

Standing atop the city wall, Murong Lie swayed upon hearing the name of the Gu family, nearly losing his footing!

His adjutant quickly steadied him: "Lord, are you alright?"

Murong Lie waved his hand, took a deep breath, and tried to calm himself.

After the beast tide, he had sent a letter to the Gu family requesting reinforcements, but he hadn't expected them to actually send someone—and two young girls at that!

However, clearly two young girls were enough!

After all, three Eternal Realm experts were like three little dogs in their presence!

Was this really a family that had only recently risen to prominence?


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 48 We're just here to watch the show! This sword intent can protect you for ten thousand years.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Murong Lie took a deep breath, and, accompanied by his deputy and several personal guards, quickly descended the city wall to stand before the two young girls.

Behind him, thousands of cultivators followed in unison, a dense, dark mass!

"Murong Lie, the City Lord of Sword Hope City, on behalf of all the people of the city and the common people of the Eastern Region, thanks you two young ladies for saving my life!"

Murong Lie knelt on one knee, his voice hoarse but solemn!

This was the most sincere expression of gratitude to his saviors!

The thousands of cultivators behind him knelt in unison, their kowtows resounding!

"Thank you for saving my life, young ladies!"

Gu Qingqing was startled and quickly slid off the white tiger's back, frantically trying to help Murong Lie up: "Grandpa, get up! Don't kneel! We, we just came to watch the spectacle, not to save anyone!"

Murong Lie was stunned!

Comed to watch the spectacle?

He had lived for eight thousand years, and this was the first time he had ever heard of anyone coming to the beast tide battlefield to watch the spectacle?!

And they even saved the entire city, tens of thousands of cultivators, and the Eastern Region's gate?!

The cultivators behind them were stunned!

Were they here to watch the show?

What kind of mess is this?

Is this right?

This isn't right!

Gu Huahua jumped off the tiger's back and chimed in, "Exactly! We thought there were treasures here, that's why we came. If we'd known it was just a fight, we wouldn't have come. It's terrifying!"

The white tiger sighed silently, not revealing that the two little girls had been laughing more excitedly than anyone else on their way here!

They looked like they wanted to rush into the horde of monsters and search for treasures!

Murong Lie opened his mouth, wanting to say something, but swallowed it back!

He looked at the two innocent-looking little girls.

Then he looked at the three Eternal Realm monsters behind them, huddled together and already terrified!

Then he looked at the white tiger lying beside him, yawning!

He was both amused and exasperated!

How could he possibly respond?

“Ladies, whether you’ve come to watch the spectacle or search for treasure, a life-saving grace is a life-saving grace!”

He looked at the two young girls and said, “Ladies, you’ve had a long journey. If you don’t mind, please rest in the city. The beast tide has receded. We can’t repay your life-saving grace, but at least let us show you some hospitality.”

The two girls had never seen anything like this before.

Not knowing how to refuse, they reluctantly agreed.

“Then we’ll have to bother you?” Gu Qingqing smiled shyly.

Murong Lie was overjoyed: “Please!”

On the city wall, tables and chairs were quickly set up.

The cultivators brought out their best offerings: spirit fruits, spirit wine, and spirit beast meat, piling them high on the table!

Although these things were insignificant to the Gu family, they were the best Jianwang City could offer!

Gu Qingqing and Gu Huahua sat in the chairs, swinging their legs, eating with great relish.

Murong Lie stood by, personally serving them tea and fruit, courting their distinguished guest with utmost hospitality!

He was, in fact, their savior!

Just then, a crack suddenly appeared in the sky!

Everyone looked up simultaneously!

A figure emerged from the crack, dressed in green robes, a sword at his waist, his face expressionless!

It was Jian Wuji!

His gaze swept across the entire area, releasing no pressure, yet everyone held their breath!

The oppressive feeling emanating from the depths of their souls was even more terrifying than the aura of the White Tiger!

It felt as if the slightest movement would reduce them to ashes!

"Uncle Jian!" the two little girls cried out simultaneously, jumping off their chairs and running towards Jian Wuji.

Jian Wuji's expression softened, a slight smile playing on his lips. "The Ancestor sent me to take you home."

He knelt down in front of the two girls, his tone becoming serious. "The Ancestor said you two have been too unruly! If you run off again, he'll take the White Tiger away!"

Gu Qingqing quickly waved her hands. "Don't blame the White Tiger! Don't blame the White Tiger! We made it lead us out!"

Gu Huahua nodded in agreement. "Yes, yes, we forced it!"

Jian Wuji smiled and stood up. "The Ancestor isn't blaming you. He said this stupid tiger is too unruly, leading you to dangerous places and deserving punishment!"

The White Tiger, lying nearby, visibly stiffened upon hearing this!

So?

Its expression was even more interesting than Gong Baiwan's!

It was suffering inside!

"It was clearly these two little devils who wanted to go, so how come I'm the one who's to blame?"

The two little girls jumped for joy: "Grandpa didn't blame us!"

"Grandpa spoils you rotten." Jian Wuji turned to look at Murong Lie and the cultivators behind him.

His gaze swept over them, and everyone instinctively lowered their heads.

Saint Kings, Half-Saints, Emperors—without exception!

There was no hostility, no oppressive force in his eyes!

But that natural suppression made it difficult for them to even breathe!

Jian Wuji spoke, his voice calm: "I'm from the Gu family, here to take these two little girls home. They're just children and have caused you trouble."

Children who don't know any better?

This is considered trouble?

They had clearly saved them!

Murong Lie quickly cupped his hands in thanks: "Not at all! These two young ladies are the saviors of our entire city, and even more so, the saviors of the Eastern Region! How could you be causing us trouble!"

Jian Wuji nodded, saying nothing more.

He turned and glanced at the demonic beast forest outside the city walls!

Although the beast tide had receded and the three Eternal Realm demonic beasts had been tamed, no one could predict what would happen in the future.

He raised his hand, a wisp of sword intent condensing at his fingertips!

That wisp of sword intent, as thin as a hair, lightly traced a golden line in the air and disappeared into the city walls!

"This is a wisp of my sword intent, capable of guarding the city walls for ten thousand years." Jian Wuji withdrew his hand.

"For ten thousand years, no demonic beast will dare to approach this city."

On the city walls, utter silence reigned!

A wisp of sword intent, guarding for ten thousand years?!

What level of cultivation is this?!

They couldn't imagine!

Murong Lie opened his mouth, as if to say something, but swallowed it back!

He knelt down and kowtowed three times heavily!

The thousands of cultivators behind him knelt down in unison. No one spoke, but everyone trembled. This was too shocking for them!

Jian Wuji said nothing more.

He waved his hand, and with the two little girls and the white tiger, along with the three Eternal Realm demonic beasts, they disappeared into the horizon!

On the city wall, Murong Lie slowly stood up, looking at the empty sky, and murmured, "Sword intent! Just a wisp of sword intent! Yet it can guard for ten thousand years! Is this the foundation of the Gu family?"

The deputy general asked in a trembling voice, "City Lord, that wisp of sword intent!"

"Don't touch it!" Murong Lie turned to look at the deputy general, his voice solemn.

"Pass down the order: the area where the sword intent resides is to be designated a forbidden zone! No one is allowed to approach, touch, or discuss it! This is a priceless treasure bestowed upon Sword Hope City by the Gu family; anyone who touches it is a sinner against Sword Hope City!"

"Yes!"

Quickly!

The news spread like wildfire!

In less than half a day, the entire Eastern Region knew that the Gu family had sent two young girls, riding white tigers, to repel the beast tide with a single sentence and acquire three Eternal Realm demonic beasts as their followers!

While the news of the five great families' escape was still being scorned, the news of the Gu family's salvation had already spread!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 49: News spread, and people rushed to become vassals.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Before the news even reached the gates of the Gu family, Gu Tianhua was holding the Heart-Testing Mirror, ready to begin the test!

"Everyone, line up one by one, don't push! The Heart-Testing Mirror will test your sincerity. Those who pass will be qualified to become vassals of the Gu family! Those who don't, go back and cultivate for a few years before trying again!"

As soon as he finished speaking, a commotion erupted in the crowd!

Some hurriedly packed their belongings, others turned and left!

"Let's go! The beast tide is coming! Three Eternal Realm demonic beasts! If we don't leave now, it'll be too late!"

"Even the Northern Region couldn't stop six Eternal Realm beasts back then, what can the Eastern Region do? Even the Gu family, no matter how strong, can't withstand it!"

"Hurry up and leave, go to the Central State! Only the Central State is safe!"

The sect leader of a second-rate sect hesitated for a moment, then cupped his hands towards Gu Tianhua: "Clan Leader Gu, it's not that we're not sincere, it's just the beast tide! We'll definitely come back after the tide recedes!"

With that, he turned and ran with his men!

In less than half an incense stick's time, most of the people outside the mountain gate had left!

Less than 30% remained!

The Qingyun Holy Land hadn't left; Huo Dongyuan stood at the very front, motionless!

The Righteous Gang hadn't left either; Shen Qinghe, with his seventeen leaders, sat firmly in their seats!

A few smaller families and the loose cultivator alliance, though their legs trembled, remained still!

Someone couldn't help but ask Huo Dongyuan, "Holy Lord Huo, aren't you leaving?"

Huo Dongyuan glanced at the man and said calmly, "Leave? Where to? Zhongzhou? No matter how good Zhongzhou is, it's not home!"

"But there are three Eternal Realm demonic beasts!"

"The Gu family isn't panicking, why should we?" Huo Dongyuan interrupted him.

"Do you see the Gu family showing any sign of running away? They're eating and drinking as usual during the test; maybe they don't even care about the demonic beasts!"

He had witnessed the Gu family's strength firsthand; ginseng fruits and enlightenment tea were readily available, and ten-thousand-year-old pills were eaten like candy!

There were even quasi-emperor level cultivation techniques!

What does this mean?

It means the Gu family is at least an imperial clan!

For a few ancient-realm demons, wouldn't that be a piece of cake?

Heh!

What a bunch of country bumpkins who've never seen the world!

The man paused, glancing inside the mountain gate.

The Gu family was completely calm!

There was no panic, no retreat, not even a discussion about the beast tide!

"What trump cards does the Gu family have?" the man murmured.

Huo Dongyuan didn't answer.

He didn't know, but he knew one thing: the Gu family never played a card they weren't confident of winning!

Just as panic was rising outside the mountain gate, and most of the people had left, a streak of light flew in from the horizon!

A communication talisman, from Sword Gazing City!

The cultivator who received the talisman glanced at it, froze, and dropped the talisman to the ground, completely unaware!

"What happened? Sword Gazing City has fallen? Is this fate?!"

"Alas! Fate! Destiny is fickle! Like intestines wrapped around small intestines!"

"No! No! It's not fallen! The beast tide has receded!"

"What? Receded? Who did it? The Wang family? The Huo family? Or did an imperial clan intervene?!"

"It was the Gu family!!"

"The Gu family?! Who did the Gu family send?!"

The cultivator looked up, his face contorted with horror: "Two little girls! The two little girls riding white tigers outside the mountain gate a few days ago!"

The entire area fell silent!

"Say that again? Two little girls?"

"The letter says those two little girls made the demonic ape kneel, the flood dragon crawl, and the Netherwolf bark like a dog! Three Eternal Realm demonic beasts are now following behind those two little girls, carrying flags, scouting, and barking like dogs!"

No one spoke!

Everyone was processing this news!

What does "three Eternal Realm demonic beasts, carrying flags, scouting, and barking like dogs" mean?!

And!

The Gu family only sent two little girls?

Do they look down on Eternal Realm demonic beasts that much?

"What the hell?!" A cultivator who had just fled stood at a distance, not having gone far, and upon hearing this news, he was completely dumbfounded.

"Why did I run away?!"

The elder beside him looked even uglier than if he'd eaten shit: "You're asking me? You were the one who led the escape!"

Are the fleeing forces now caught in a dilemma?

They've completely lost face!

Not going back?

The Gu family can easily handle even a beast tide. Following the Gu family is better than going to Zhongzhou to be a lackey, isn't it?

Outside the mountain gate, those who remained were in an uproar!

Before long, everyone knew the news!

"The Five Great Families! Bah! They act like kings and tyrants all the time, but when trouble comes, they run away faster than anyone else!!"

"The Gu family is still reliable; they resolved the beast tide in one move!"

"They said those two little girls said they were just there to watch the show! Not to rescue anyone!"

"Watch the show? They rescued a city while watching the show? That's too Versailles!"

"What does Versailles mean?"

"Exactly! Sigh! Never mind, you wouldn't understand!"

"The Gu family's sword masters are truly terrifying! They've condensed a single wisp of sword intent to guard the Great Wall of Sword Gazing for ten thousand years!"

"A single wisp of sword intent, guarding for ten thousand years?! That's outrageous!"

Those forces that had turned back halfway back now slunk back to the mountain gate, lining up at the very back of the queue!

The gazes from the surrounding cultivators were like knives, but they dared not utter a sound!

They had chosen their path, and they would finish it, even if they had to crawl!

Gu Tianhua stood before the mountain gate, his expression unchanged, as if he had known this would happen all along!

He cleared his throat and said, "These demonic beasts are nothing to our ancestor!"

Another commotion erupted outside the mountain gate.

"Didn't they take it seriously? That's an Eternal Realm demon beast!"

"Didn't you understand? The Gu family doesn't even consider Eternal Realm beasts a threat! That shows the Gu family has the confidence and the strength!"

"No matter what it is, following the Gu family is the right thing to do!"

"I want to be tested! My sect wants to become a vassal!"

"Me too! Don't push! I was first! My clan wants to become a vassal too!"

Outside the mountain gate, a sea of ​​people surged, even more than before the beast tide!

Those forces that had fled returned, bringing even more people!

Everyone was shouting the same thing:

"I want to become a vassal of the Gu family!"

Gu Tianhua raised the Heart-Questioning Mirror, his voice echoing throughout the area: "Line up! One at a time!"

The Heart-Questioning Mirror hovered in mid-air, its surface glowing faintly!

The first to enter the illusion was the sect leader of the second-rate sect who had led the escape.

Less than three breaths after he entered, the Heart-Questioning Mirror flashed, and a figure flew out, crashing to the ground in a sorry state!

The mirror clearly showed his actions in the illusion: he saw the Gu family's mountain gate in ruins, disciples dead and wounded!

His first thought wasn't to rescue them, but to turn and run, stealing several cultivation manuals from the Gu family's cultivation manual hall before he fled!

The surrounding cultivators saw everything clearly!

"Tsk tsk tsk! Running away is one thing, but stealing is another!"

"With that kind of character, you think you can be a vassal of the Gu family? Dream on!"

The sect leader, his face flushed, scrambled to his feet and ran, not daring to look back.

Those people sneered at them, never expecting such people to exist. Truly ridiculous and disgraceful!

Pathetic! Lamentable!

Such people as vassals are nothing but parasites!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 50 Ugliness Revealed, Illusion Reveals True Nature!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Immediately afterward!

The leader of a rogue cultivator alliance entered in a grand procession of several hundred people!

He hadn't walked far in the illusion when a sly grin suddenly appeared on his face!

He muttered, "The Gu family has so many good things! Steal a few cultivation manuals! We'll be set for life! Anyway, the Gu family can't do anything about it now!"

"Hahaha! Alliance Leader, I stole a Saint King's cultivation manual! Hahaha! And a Saint King's weapon! And ten-thousand-year-old pills! Alliance Leader, we're rich! Rich!"

"What a pathetic family! All they have is trash! Not a single treasure? Oh no! The Western Heaven War Emperor is here! Run! Run! Run!"

"Is the Gu family crazy? How come they offend everyone? Those people beat me so badly! I must take revenge on the Gu family!"

"No! Don't kill me! I'll tell you everything, treasures, everything!"

The cultivators outside scoffed!

They never expected to find all sorts of people like this!

Those forces that spoke so righteously, look at them now!

They've truly revealed their ugliness!

Many forces entered the illusion one after another.

One after another, they were ejected.

Some saw the Gu family in distress within the illusion and turned to run away without even looking back!

Some feigned loyalty, but immediately revealed their true forms when demonic beasts attacked!

Even more ridiculous was the patriarch of a medium-sized family. After entering, he closed his eyes and walked forward, muttering, "I can't see, I can't see, I didn't see anything!"

After walking for a long time, he realized he was still in the same place, and he was sweating profusely with anxiety!

The scene displayed on the mirror made everyone laugh hysterically. The patriarch, after being ejected, covered his face and ran away!

A few people passed the test, but many more were eliminated!

A large sect with tens of thousands of members entered wave after wave, and in the end, the pass rate was less than 10%!

When the sect leader emerged, his face was ashen. His tens of thousands of subordinates—those who were cowardly, those who wanted to steal, those who were spies, and those with wicked intentions—had all been eliminated!

Especially those who, upon witnessing the Gu family's prosperity in the illusion, were consumed by greed!

They were thinking about how to escape unscathed and avoid being purged by the Gu family!

Such people were all clearly visible in the Inquiry Mirror!

Not a single one could escape!

Outside the mountain gate, Gu Tianhua looked at the data displayed in the Inquiry Mirror and shook his head.

Too much selfishness!

These people only thought about what benefits the Gu family could give them, never considering what they could do for the Gu family!

Being such vassals of the Gu family was nothing but parasites!

And they might even turn on you at a crucial moment!

Such forces cannot be allowed to remain!

Cannot be tolerated!

Even more unacceptable!

One force after another entered the Inquiry Mirror, and one after another, they were ejected!

Outside the mountain gate, laughter, boos, and curses rose and fell!

Gu Tianhua stood beside the Mirror of Inquiry, his face growing increasingly grim!

What was all this nonsense?

He held back, but finally couldn't take it anymore!

"Enough!" he roared!

"Look at yourselves! What kind of people are you?!"

The entire hall fell silent, everyone turning to look at him!

Gu Tianhua pointed to the scene still replaying on the Mirror of Inquiry!

His voice was filled with rage: "Running away! Stealing! Spying on your fellow disciples! Refusing to help those in need and introducing them to others for sale…"

"Is this your sincerity towards the Gu family? With your character, do you even deserve to be vassals of the Gu family?!"

In the crowd, some lowered their heads, some blushed, and some shrank back!

“We cultivators stand tall and upright! Clinging to life and fearing death, living a life of ignominy—that's what mortals do!”

“You've cultivated for hundreds, even thousands of years! Is this the kind of character you've cultivated?”

Gu Tianhua grew increasingly angry, pointing at the cultivator who had betrayed his fellow disciples in the illusion: “You! To save your own life, you pushed your junior brother out to block the demonic beasts! Does someone like that deserve to be in the Gu family?”

The man's face turned as white as paper, and he turned and ran!

“And you!” Gu Tianhua pointed to another.

“You kicked the Gu family when they were down in the illusion, stealing things faster than anyone else! The Gu family hasn't even fallen yet, and you're already dividing things up?”

The man knelt down with a thud: “Clan Chief Gu, I was wrong, please give me another chance!”

“Get out! The Mirror of Inquiry gave you a chance, but you didn't seize it!” Gu Tianhua said coldly.

Those people scurried away, not daring to look back!

A deathly silence fell outside the mountain gate!

Those forces that hadn't yet been tested were trembling!

Not out of fear, but out of trepidation!

They didn't know what they would become in the illusion, whether their hidden, sordid secrets would be exposed.

Gu Tianhua had utterly humiliated those forces!

He'd torn away their facade!

They were all a bunch of makeshift bandits!

They'd turned on their own people, let alone the Gu family!

They might betray you at any moment!

Just then, Huo Dongyuan stepped out!

He took a deep breath and, without hesitation, stepped into the Mirror of Inquiry!

In the illusion, the Gu family's mountain gate had been breached by demonic beasts, blood flowing like a river!

Huo Dongyuan didn't hesitate, drawing his sword and charging at the demonic beasts!

One sword slayed one head, two swords slayed two!

The disciples of the Azure Cloud Holy Land behind him didn't retreat either, charging forward alongside him!

The sounds of battle shook the heavens and earth!

Huo Dongyuan, covered in blood and with a severed arm, still refused to back down!

Only when the last demonic beast fell did he collapse, exhausted, his dying words still uttering, "The Azure Cloud Holy Land will live and die with the Gu family!"

The Mirror of Inquiry flashed, and Huo Dongyuan emerged!

He was soaked to the bone, but his eyes were resolute!

Gu Tianhua looked at the image on the mirror, remained silent for a moment, and then said, "The Azure Cloud Holy Land has passed!"

Huo Dongyuan bowed and silently walked to the side!

Behind him, one by one, the disciples of the Azure Cloud Holy Land entered the Mirror of Inquiry and passed through!

Not one ran away, not one betrayed, not one stole!

Everyone fought to the death!

Outside the mountain gate, those forces that hadn't yet been tested watched the Azure Cloud Holy Land's performance; some felt ashamed, some admired, and some remained silent!

Immediately afterward, the Righteous Gang entered!

Shen Qinghe led seventeen leaders into the Mirror of Inquiry!

The Righteous Gang had only eighteen members, but not one of them flinched!

In the illusion, they stood back-to-back in a small circle, facing the overwhelming horde of demonic beasts, fighting to the last breath!

Shen Qinghe died, the second-in-command died, the third-in-command, the fourth-in-command—all eighteen men perished.

Not a single one ran, not a single one betrayed, not a single one stole anything!

All eighteen passed!

Shen Qinghe emerged from the Mirror of Inquiry, covered in wounds!

Gu Tianhua looked at them, took a deep breath: "The Righteous Gang, passed!"

Outside the mountain gate, another commotion erupted!

"Qingyun Holy Land and the Righteous Gang are too powerful!"

"They genuinely treat the Gu family as their own home, while we're just opportunists!"

"Stop talking, it's getting more and more embarrassing!"

The test continues!

Some pass, some are eliminated!

Those large sects with tens of thousands of members have a pitifully low pass rate!

On the other hand, many small families and sects with dozens of members have all passed!

It's not about quantity! It's about loyalty!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 51: A Frenzied Summoning of Powerful Individuals, A New Mission Appears
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

As the sun set, the test finally drew to a close!

Eighteen spots—not one more, not one less!

The Qingyun Holy Land, the Righteous Gang, the Exploding Heaven Gang, and other forces that had demonstrated the utmost loyalty in the illusion were all selected.

Most were small sects with dozens of members or alliances of independent cultivators with hundreds!

On the contrary, the large sects with tens of thousands of members were completely wiped out—not a single one remained!

With a wicked heart, even a large number of people are a scourge!

Previously, they had intended to select a few who were pleasing to the eye, but now it seemed that those who had passed the Heart-Searching Mirror were already one in a million!

Gu Tianhua stood before the mountain gate, looking at the remaining eighteen representatives of various forces, and took a deep breath.

"From today onwards, you are all vassals of the Gu family!"

His voice resounded throughout the entire area!

"As long as the Gu family exists, they will protect you! But there's one condition: if the Gu family is in trouble, you must be the first to step in! Anyone who eats the Gu family's food and then stabs them in the back won't be blamed for the Gu family turning its back on them!"

The representatives of the eighteen forces bowed in unison, their voices perfectly synchronized: "We obey the Gu family's orders!"

Gu Tianhua nodded and waved his hand: "Go in! We'll arrange things for you later."

Many of the remaining forces felt utterly ashamed. The ugly side revealed in the Mirror of Inquiry made them want to flee immediately!

They didn't want anyone to see this scene!

Back Mountain!

The system panel appeared before their eyes!

【Subordinate Forces Mission: Completed!】

【Rewards Distributed!】

【Congratulations, Host, you have obtained: Saint Summoning Cards ×99!】

【Congratulations, Host, you have obtained: Random Summoning Cards ×3!】

Ninety-nine Saints, three random summoning cards!

Not bad, but not enough!

He raised his hand, and all the summon cards accumulated in his system inventory appeared before him!

Emperor Summon Card x4

Quasi-Emperor Summon Card x2

Eternal Realm Summon Card x3

Saint King Summon Card x3

Saint Summon Card x1

Adding the ninety-nine Saint cards he had just acquired, a total of 100 cards!

Plus three random cards, the summon cards were dazzling, covering the floor!

“System, synthesize them all. Upgrade them to whatever level you can.”

[Ding! Detected that the host possesses a large number of summon cards. Confirm that all will be used for upgrade synthesis?]

“Confirm!”

[Synthesizing!]

A flash of light, and the summon card emitted a dazzling light!

[Synthesis Complete!]

[Congratulations on obtaining: Eternal Realm Summon Card x1]

The light faded, and after using 100 Saint Summon Cards, only one Eternal Realm Summon Card had been synthesized!

After all that, only one Eternal Realm Summon Card!

Oh well!

Three more random summoning cards left, let's see if we can get anything good!

"Use random summoning cards, all three!"

[Ding! Random summoning cards in use!]

Three middle-aged men appeared in a corner of the back mountain!

The three men looked at each other, nodded to each other, and then came before Gu Xuan.

They knelt on one knee, clasped their hands in greeting, and said!

"Subordinate Bai Yujing greets Master."

"Subordinate Ye Wuhen greets Master."

"Subordinate Ding Jiangbo greets Master."

Two Great Emperors and one Quasi-Emperor!

Not bad luck, drew two gold ones!

Gu Xuan nodded, not bad, at least better than that Eternal Realm card!

The three men knelt on one knee in unison: "Greetings, Master."

Gu Xuan waved his hand, "Get up, stand over there."

The three men stood up.

Gu Xuan opened the system inventory.

He glanced at the remaining summoning cards: how many Saint King summoning cards were left? The Eternal Realm summoning card he had just synthesized was still unused. He also had two Quasi-Emperor summoning cards!

He thought for a moment.

The Gu family currently had enough top-tier combat power: Jian Wuji, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, Bai Yujing, and Ye Wuhen—five Great Emperors!

They had Ding Jiangbo as a Quasi-Emperor, plus the two Quasi-Emperor cards he hadn't summoned yet, making a total of three Quasi-Emperors!

The Four Divine Beasts were also Great Emperors, but they were only suitable for guarding the house; otherwise, they would just run off with their people like that stupid tiger!

If word got out that the Four Divine Beast Great Emperors were only suitable for guarding the house, countless people would be shocked!

The current mid-to-lower-level combat power was still insufficient; there were too few Saint King-level experts, and almost no Saint-level experts!

The family's overall strength needed to be improved!

"System, except for the Great Emperor Summoning Card, summon all other Saint Kings, Eternal Realm cultivators, and Quasi-Emperors."

[Ding! Summoning confirmed.]

Booms of light followed!

Three Saint King Summoning Cards activated first, and three figures appeared on the back mountain!

Two men and one woman, all middle-aged, knelt on one knee: "Greetings, Master!"

Gu Xuan nodded, gesturing for them to stand aside.

Four Eternal Realm Summoning Cards followed closely behind, golden light flashing one after another!

"Subordinate Tie Niu!"

"Subordinate Zi Linglong!"

"Subordinate Ku Mu!"

"Subordinate Xu Fan!"

"Greetings, Master!"

Four Eternal Realm cultivators stood in a row!

More and more people appeared on the back mountain, their auras becoming increasingly mixed.

Finally, two Quasi-Emperor Summoning Cards appeared!

The light faded, revealing two figures standing side by side, a man and a woman!

The man was tall and imposing, the woman petite and delicate, their auras equally unfathomable!

"Your subordinate, Qin Cangqiong."

"Subordinate Su Xiaoyan."

"Greetings, Master."

Gu Xuan glanced at them. Not bad!

At least they all looked presentable!

"Get up, all of you, stand to the side." He waved his hand, his tone casual.

A dense mass of people stood on the back mountain!

Jian Wuji, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, Bai Yujing, and Ye Wuhen, the five Great Emperors, stood at the very front!

Qin Cangqiong, Su Xiaoyan, and Ding Jiangbo, the three Quasi-Emperors, stood in the second row!

Tie Niu, Zi Linglong, Kumu, and Xu Fan, the four Eternal Realm experts, stood in the third row!

Finally, the three Saint Kings stood, barely daring to breathe!

What on earth is this?!

How did I get here?!

This is a place full of experts!

Is this really a place they can come to?

Just as Gu Xuan was about to speak, the system's mechanical voice suddenly rang in his mind!

【Ding! New mission issued!】

【Mission Objective: Within three months, raise the overall strength of the Gu family disciples to the Saint Realm! Simultaneously raise the overall strength of vassal forces to the Saint Realm!】

【Mission Rewards: Emperor Summoning Card x2, Random Summoning Card x2!】

Three months, the entire clan to Saint Realm?

Vast forces to Saint Realm too?

This mission is a pipe dream in the entire Eastern Region.

There are billions of cultivators in the Eastern Region, and the number of Saint Realm cultivators is roughly four figures.

To make thousands of people all Saint Realm in three months—no one would believe it.

But the Gu family has something others don't!

The Marrow Cleansing Array can reshape aptitude, Immortal Vein Spiritual Energy can increase cultivation speed a thousandfold, and Enlightenment Tea and Ginseng Fruit can be eaten like food!

Three months?

That's more than enough!

“Tianhua.” Gu Xuan's voice echoed outside the mountain gate.

Gu Tianhua was settling affairs with his eighteen vassal forces when he heard his patriarch's summons.

He immediately ran up the back mountain: "Patriarch, what is it?"

Gu Xuan pointed to the dense crowd standing on the back mountain: "These are the people from the Struggle Hall. Make arrangements for them, let them get to know their place and the people, and prevent them from fighting on their own territory. If there are any problems you can't handle in the future, you can ask the Struggle Hall for help."

Gu Tianhua looked at the rows of terrifying figures and swallowed hard.

Where did his patriarch get these people? Their auras are terrifying!

Even the weakest among them are people he can't afford to offend!

But so what?

He is the Gu family patriarch; they all listen to him!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 52 is truly a fairyland! The lowest-ranking Saint King in the Thug Hall.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Eighteen vassal forces entered the mountain gate.

The first to enter was Huo Dongyuan.

He took a deep breath, trying to appear calm!

After all, he had already experienced the ginseng fruit and enlightenment tea at the Gu family's place; he had already been thoroughly shocked!

He needed to remain composed!

But those behind him were far from calm!

“This! This spiritual energy!”

A middle-aged man's voice trembled!

“More than ten thousand times stronger than our ancestral land!”

“More than ten thousand times! Look at that waterfall, it's all flowing with spiritual liquid!”

Another man immediately squatted down and touched the stones on the ground!

“This is! Spiritual jade? For paving? Is this correct?”

Huo Dongyuan coughed lightly: “Mind your manners!”

Inwardly, however, he thought: They're all a bunch of bumpkins, never seen the world!

Those people tried to straighten their backs, but their eyes couldn't help but dart around!

The Righteous Gang followed behind.

Shen Qinghe had lived for eight thousand years; what hadn't he seen?

But the moment he stepped through the Gu family's mountain gate, he felt he had wasted those eight thousand years!

A waterfall of spiritual liquid cascaded from the sky, the spray not just mist,

but spiritual mist!

Nine mountain peaks pierced the clouds, their immortal veins meandering through them!

Even a random blade of grass by the roadside exuded the fragrance of millennia-old spiritual herbs, and every fruit on the trees was enough for outsiders to fight tooth and nail for!

And these were just a few inconspicuous trees planted by the Gu family!

Completely different from the powerful forces he had encountered before!

Shen Qinghe stopped in his tracks: "This! Is this a fairyland?"

None of the seventeen leaders behind him could answer!

Because they, too, were dumbfounded!

Second-in-command Han Tiesheng pointed to a distant mountain peak, "Big Brother! Look at that mountain! Aren't there ginseng fruit trees? Dozens of them?"

Shen Qinghe looked in the direction he was pointing, remained silent for a moment, then turned to the people behind him and said, "Stop looking! If you keep looking, you won't want to leave!"

The seventeen leaders nodded in unison!

The Exploding Heaven Gang followed behind.

The Exploding Heaven Gang, despite its imposing name, actually only had about thirty members, all independent cultivators. The gang leader, the most powerful among them, had just broken through to the Saint Realm.

He had only managed this breakthrough after frantically absorbing spiritual energy outside the Gu Family Mountain Gate!

Now, they were directly inside!

Gang Leader Zhao Tiangang stood motionless inside the mountain gate.

A disciple behind him whispered, "Gang Leader, what's wrong?"

Zhao Tiangang didn't answer.

Another disciple also whispered, "Is the Gang Leader scared out of his wits?"

Zhao Tiangang still didn't answer!

He was genuinely terrified. Having lived for over a thousand years, the largest power he'd ever visited was the Qingyun Holy Land.

Back then, he thought the Qingyun Holy Land was a paradise on earth!

Now, compared to the Gu family, the Qingyun Holy Land was just a village!

The Qingyun Holy Land?

Irrelevant!

There was also a small family, the Li family, whose patriarch, Li Tianming, was a young man at the first level of the Saint Realm!

He was the youngest patriarch among all the vassal forces.

He walked the slowest because he was constantly counting the various kinds of spiritual herbs along the roadside!

After counting to the fiftieth, he gave up!

Each one here!

was hundreds of times more precious than their Li family's prized herb!

How could anyone live like this?!

It was terrifying!

It was practically paradise!

Huo Dongyuan walked at the front, glancing back at the group behind him, a slight smile playing on his lips!

He understood that expression all too well!

A few days ago, when he first came in, he was even more exaggerated than them!

At least he didn't kneel on the floor and touch the ground!

That would be too embarrassing!

He's supposed to be a Holy Lord!

Gu Tianhua came down from the back mountain and saw these people. "Gentlemen, the Gu family's place isn't very big! But it's enough for all of you. I've already arranged accommodations. Follow me!"

Not big?

The crowd looked at this fairyland spanning a thousand miles, speechless!

Zhao Tiangang, the leader of the Exploding Heaven Gang, whispered to the person beside him, "He calls this a small place?"

No one answered him, because everyone was still in shock!

Gu Tianhua led the way, followed by representatives from eighteen forces.

Just then, three people suddenly walked out from the front.

Two men and one woman, all middle-aged, with restrained auras, looking quite ordinary!

After a brief exchange with Gu Tianhua, the three nodded, turned and left, their hurried steps disappearing in the blink of an eye.

Representatives from eighteen factions stood frozen in place.

They weren't intimidated by Gu Tianhua's words, but by the aura of those three individuals!

Although the three tried their best to conceal it, the aura of a peak Saint King still faintly emanated from them!

Huo Dongyuan's pupils constricted. He had only recently broken through to Saint King, and he could clearly sense that any one of those three could easily suppress him!

Yet Gu Tianhua treated them as casually as if they were underlings!

Zhao Tiangang, the leader of the Exploding Heaven Gang, couldn't help but ask, "Clan Leader Gu, who were those three just now?"

Gu Tianhua casually replied, "They're from the Thug Hall, a group of henchmen trained by our ancestor, responsible for handling matters that are inconvenient for them to handle personally."

Thugs?

Zhao Tiangang swallowed hard: "What's their cultivation level?"

Gu Tianhua said nonchalantly, "Saint King, I guess. They're the lowest-ranking members of the Thug Hall."

The entire audience instantly erupted in anger!

Saint Kings as thugs?

"Your cultivation level is considered the lowest?"

What does that mean?

Could it be that the Struggle Hall has others with even higher cultivation levels?

The smiles on everyone's faces froze!

Li Tianming cautiously asked, "Clan Chief Gu! The lowest level in the Struggle Hall is Saint King?"

Gu Tianhua nodded: "Pretty much! Actually, the Struggle Hall hasn't been established for long, and the Ancestor hasn't trained many people yet, only a few dozen at the moment. Those below Saint level are too embarrassed to even try to enter."

A few dozen?

Those below Saint level are too embarrassed to even try?!

Does that mean the Struggle Hall is full of Saints and above?!

Huo Dongyuan's mind was buzzing. He, the dignified Qingyun Saint Master, had just broken through to Saint King, a top-tier fighter in the Eastern Region, yet within the Gu family, he couldn't even touch the threshold of the Struggle Hall?

Shen Qinghe was only one step away from Saint King!

He thought he was someone of importance even within the Gu family.

Now he knew that in the Gu family, even a Saint at the peak was only fit to be a subordinate!

Just as everyone was still reeling from the shock of the lowest-ranking Saint King in the Strike Hall, a silvery laugh suddenly rang out from afar.

"Hurry up! Hurry up! You're too slow!"

Everyone looked in the direction of the voice!

Two little girls riding white tigers were galloping towards them!

Behind the white tigers were three little creatures.

Three Eternal Realm demonic beasts!

They were now happily trotting along behind the white tigers.

A demonic ape carried a flag, a dragon carried a flower basket, and a dark wolf had a red rope tied around its neck, the other end of which was held by Gu Qingqing!

Three ancient demonic beasts, each over ten thousand years old, were being led around like dogs by two young girls!

"Those? Aren't those three ancient demonic beasts from the beast tide?"

"It looks like it!"

"Why are they being led around by two young girls?"

"Didn't you see? That dark wolf has a rope around its neck."

Everyone fell silent!

Ancient demonic beasts, led around on ropes?

This is ten thousand times more outrageous than even the lowest cultivation level in the Strike Hand Hall, a Saint King!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 53 Three Months to Reach the Saint Realm? Gu Chen: I Have to Prove Myself
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Qingqing rode her white tiger to Gu Tianhua, "Uncle Clan Chief! We're training our pets! Look how well-behaved they are!"

Gu Tianhua looked at the three Eternal Realm demonic beasts huddled together, his lips twitching.

Gu Tianhua reluctantly said, "Not bad."

"But you two, don't just play around. The Ancestor said that if you don't reach the Saint Realm within three months, these three beasts will be chained to the mountain gate like stone lions!"

The two girls panicked, "Uncle Clan Chief, we're only at the King Realm now! How can we possibly reach the Saint Realm in three months!"

Gu Tianhua spread his hands, "The Ancestor said so. Go and argue with him. Anyway, I've delivered the message. If you don't reach the Saint Realm in three months, these three will be chained to the gate. Oh, and the white tiger has to be chained too!"

The white tiger froze!

What?!

Why am I included?

Can't the tiger be left unchained?!

"Uncle Clan Chief, please talk to the Ancestor! Three months is too short!"

Gu Tianhua was both amused and exasperated: "Don't talk to me, talk to the Ancestor."

He directly passed the problem on to Gu Xuan.

These two little girls are too troublesome. Let the Ancestor handle it!

The two girls exchanged a glance, then turned and ran towards the back mountain.

Their voices still drifted down the mountain path:

Ancestor! You're bullying us! How can we possibly reach the Saint Realm in three months!

Gu Tianhua turned around, looking at the eighteen vassal forces behind him.

Thousands of people stared blankly.

"Don't just stand there, let's go! I'll take you to your lodgings, though they're a bit simple." Gu Tianhua clapped his hands.

After a moment, as if he had thought of something, he spoke:

"Oh, right, you also have a mission. The Ancestor said that your overall strength must reach the Saint Realm within three months! Those who don't reach it by then, the Gu family won't spare!"

The entire place erupted in chaos!

Holy crap!

Reaching the Saint Realm in three months?!

“Several of our guild members are only at the King Realm! How could they possibly reach the Saint Realm in three months?!”

“From King to Saint in three months? That's suicidal!”

“The Gu family has so many resources, maybe they really can do it?”

“How could they do that? Even taking elixirs wouldn't be that fast!”

“Alright, alright, don't overthink it.” Gu Tianhua waved his hand.

Since they dared to make this request, they must have a way!

“Settle down first. Starting tomorrow, everyone will enter the Marrow Cleansing Array, drink Enlightenment Tea, eat Ginseng Fruit, and have all the cultivation resources you need! After three months, it will be difficult for you not to become Saints!”

The crowd quieted down!

Entering the Marrow Cleansing Array? Drinking Enlightenment Tea? Eating Ginseng Fruit?

Everyone swallowed hard: "Clan Chief Gu, you're giving us these things?!"

"What else? The Ancestor said, even vassals are members of the Gu family, and the Gu family can't be treated poorly!"

Their eyes reddened, and they knelt down with a thud: "The Gu family's kindness and virtue are unforgettable!"

The eighteen vassal forces knelt in unison.

Gu Tianhua quickly stopped them, urging them to rise: "Don't kneel, don't kneel. The Gu family doesn't do this. From now on, we're all family. What kind of behavior is this?"

The crowd stood up, their eyes still red!

From today onward, they had a new home!

A place called the Gu family!

Back mountain.

Two little girls clung to Gu Xuan's legs, acting coquettishly.

"Ancestor, three months is too short!"

Gu Xuan put down his teacup, looking at the two little girls: "Busy? Then tie the White Tiger to the door, so you won't run out every day."

The white tiger, lying nearby, stopped wagging its tail upon hearing this!

It even clenched its anus!

It was terrified that Gu Xuan would be angry and blame it!

The two little girls panicked; this wouldn't do!

"No, no! We'll cultivate!"

Gu Xuan smiled: "You mean it?"

"I mean it!"

"Then why aren't you going?"

The two little girls turned and ran, but halfway there they turned back. "Ancestor, if we reach the Saint level, can we keep a few more pets?"

Gu Xuan replied: "We'll talk about it when we get there."

"Alright!"

The two little girls rode the white tiger and disappeared in a flash.

Meanwhile!

Gu Chen sat in his room, growing angrier the more he thought about it!

Two little girls, riding a white tiger for a stroll, managed to quell the beast tide?

Now, the entire Eastern Region was abuzz with the heroic deeds of the two little fairies of the Gu family!

Gu Chen, the young master of the Gu family, had his limelight stolen by two little girls playing in the mud while riding tigers!

"No way!"

Gu Chen slammed his hand on the table and stood up!

"I have to prove myself! I am the young master of the Gu family! How can these two little girls steal my thunder?"

But how?

Gu Chen paced around the room three times, then suddenly remembered someone: Li Shuangxu!

The Holy Son of Qingyun, once the number one genius of Qingyun Prefecture, knowledgeable and surely intelligent!

Perhaps he could offer some advice!

Yes, he'd go find him!

Gu Chen turned and walked out.

At this moment, Li Shuangxu was standing at the foot of the back mountain, surrounded by Huo Dongyuan and several elders of the Qingyun Holy Land, with an elderly man with white hair in the middle!

His face was ashen, and his body exuded an aura of decay.

He was none other than Huo Tianqing, the old Holy Master of the Qingyun Holy Land!

A Saint King at the third level of the Heavenly Realm, poisoned by the Soul-Devouring Powder, had endured eight hundred years of suffering, and was now utterly exhausted!

Huo Tianqing stared in disbelief at the surrounding fairyland!

"This is the Gu family?" His voice was hoarse, filled with disbelief!

Huo Dongyuan nodded: "Yes, Ancestor!"

"Spiritual liquid? Spiritual jade? Ginseng fruit tree? Enlightenment tea tree?"

Huo Tianqing became increasingly agitated as he spoke!

"Why didn't you tell me sooner?!"

Huo Dongyuan smiled bitterly: "Ancestor, the Gu family has only been around for less than a month. I only found out a few days ago."

"I don't care! I've suffered for eight hundred years, where were you all this time?!"

Huo Dongyuan dared not reply!

The elders beside him lowered their heads, pretending not to hear!

Huo Tianqing waved his hand, trying to calm himself down!

"Alright, alright! Take me to see the Gu family patriarch! If he can cure my poison, I'll serve him like a slave in the Qingyun Holy Land!"

Huo Dongyuan's lips twitched. He was already a vassal, even more subservient than a beast of burden!

Just then, Gu Chen came walking down the mountain path.

He saw Li Shuangxu and his eyes lit up: "Shuangxu!"

Li Shuangxu turned around: "Young Master Gu."

Gu Chen pulled him aside and lowered his voice: "Shuangxu, I want to do something big. Can you give me some advice?"

Li Shuangxu was taken aback: "What big thing?"

Gu Chen briefly explained the matter.

The gist was that he wanted to prove himself and couldn't let those two little girls steal all the limelight!

After listening, Li Shuangxu was silent for a moment, then suddenly his eyes lit up: "Brother Gu, I've thought of a place that might make you famous!"

"What place?"

"Zhongzhou! The Genius Battle!"

Gu Chen was stunned: "The Genius Battle?"

Li Shuangxu nodded: "The Central Continent hosts a Genius Battle every hundred years, attracting geniuses from all three thousand provinces! There you'll find monsters nurtured by imperial clans, heirs of ancient realm forces, and even scions of legendary longevity families! Those who emerge victorious from the Genius Battle are all renowned prodigies!"

"For hundreds of thousands of years, many from the Eastern Region have participated in the Genius Battle, but none have ever broken into the top ten! If you can make it, not only the Eastern Region, but the entire continent will know your name!"

Gu Chen's eyes grew brighter and brighter, his heart burning with excitement!

Damn, this is awesome!

The Central Continent Genius Battle, where geniuses from all three thousand provinces gather, is far more valuable than those two little girls taming a few demonic beasts!

Besides, he could go out and train, without having to see those two demonic orbs every day!

A win-win situation!

Li Shuangxu wanted to say something more, but before he could finish, Gu Chen pulled him away!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 54 Like a God! Breaking through to the Emperor Realm while speaking?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Huo Dongyuan was leading the old Holy Master up the mountain when he saw Li Shuangxu being dragged away by Gu Chen. He quickly gave him a meaningful look!

That's our master!

Don't be negligent!

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan scanned the area with his divine sense, understanding their thoughts.

The Central Continent Genius Battle?

Interesting.

He glanced at the mission countdown on the system panel: three months.

Not a long time, not a short time.

Gu Chen's trip to Zhongzhou would take at least one or two months round trip.

Even if he didn't reach the Saint Realm upon his return, he could easily achieve it using time-manipulation and spatial magic treasures!

One day of cultivation was equivalent to several years for others!

Let him use these three months to hone his skills!

Soon, footsteps approached.

Huo Dongyuan, leading the old Saint Master and several elders, finally arrived at the back mountain!

Huo Tianqing walked unsteadily.

Not shocked by Gu Xuan's cultivation level, but because he simply couldn't see through it!

That feeling was indescribable!

The Soul-Devouring Poison had seeped into his bone marrow; he could only last for six months at most.

“Come here.”

Huo Tianqing stepped forward. Gu Xuan casually raised his hand, a wisp of spiritual light from his fingertip entering Huo Tianqing's brow.

Huo Tianqing shuddered. The eight-hundred-year-old Soul-Devouring Poison within him melted away like ice and snow, instantly dissipating!

His festering wounds healed at a visible speed!

His complexion regained its rosy hue, and even his white hair turned noticeably blacker!

Huo Tianqing was stunned!

Eight hundred years, seeking medical help and medicine, searching the entire Eastern Region, yet no one could cure him!

The Gu family patriarch simply raised his hand, and he was healed?!

This was too outrageous!

It was practically divine intervention!

“Thank you so much, Ancestor, for saving my life!” Huo Tianqing knelt down with a thud, tears streaming down his face!

Huo Dongyuan and several elders followed suit, kowtowing loudly!

Gu Xuan waved his hand: "Get up. The poison is gone. Go back and rest well. In three months, I want to see all of you at the Saint Realm. You, the old Saint Master, shouldn't be idle either; break through when you need to."

Huo Tianqing nodded repeatedly: "Yes, yes! This junior will certainly not fail the Ancestor's expectations!"

Huo Dongyuan helped the old Saint Master up, bowed deeply to Gu Xuan, and led his men away.

Getting all his disciples to break through to the Saint Realm in three months is a difficult task; he must go back and provide proper guidance!

Before leaving, Huo Tianqing glanced back at the white-robed figure lying on the chair, only one thought in his mind!

Having the Gu family in the Eastern Region is an immense blessing!

At the foot of the back mountain, Gu Chen had already led Li Shuangxu to the plaza.

"Shuangxu, you said the Genius Battle starts in a month?"

Li Shuangxu nodded: "It only happens once every hundred years. If you miss this one, you'll have to wait another hundred years. The journey from the Eastern Region to the Central State takes at least a month."

Gu Chen panicked upon hearing this: "Then what are we waiting for? Let's go now!"

Gu Chen grabbed Li Shuangxu: "Let's go! What are you standing there for?"

Li Shuangxu quickly stopped him: "Wait! Young Master Gu, do you know the cultivation threshold for the Central State Genius Battle?"

"Threshold? What threshold?"

Li Shuangxu looked at him helplessly: "The minimum is the Emperor Realm. You're currently at the peak of the King Realm, one major realm short. Even if you start your seclusion now, the Genius Battle will be over by the time you break through."

Gu Chen was stunned.

The Emperor Realm?

He looked down at his hands, then looked up at the sky.

"Is the Emperor Realm difficult?"

Li Shuangxu's lips twitched: "What do you think? There are billions of cultivators in the Eastern Region, and many are stuck at the peak of the King Realm their entire lives. Do you think breaking through is as easy as drinking water or eating?

Gu Chen didn't speak.

He closed his eyes and circulated his spiritual energy.

The Hidden Dragon Sword hummed, the Hidden Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword trembled within his dantian, and the Supreme Sword Dao True Dragon Emperor Technique circulated wildly!

Spiritual energy flowed through his meridians, like giant dragons charging towards the invisible barrier!

Boom!

The barrier of the peak King Realm shattered!

Gu Chen opened his eyes, a glint of light flashing within them: "I broke through!"

Li Shuangxu stood frozen, mouth agape!

What?!

Breakthrough?!

"You! What did you say?!"

"I said I broke through to the Emperor Realm!" Gu Chen flexed his wrist.

"It wasn't that hard, was it?"

Li Shuangxu was completely dumbfounded!

He had cultivated for decades, only breaking through to the fifth level of the King Realm!

He was already hailed as a once-in-a-century genius by the Azure Cloud Holy Land!

Gu Chen was only in his early twenties!

This talent was terrifying!

If he were to break through to the Emperor Realm himself, he would probably need to prepare!

Now, someone just said to him, "Is the Emperor Realm difficult?"

And then, *boom*, he broke through!

He truly broke through to the Emperor Realm with ease!

No pills, no seclusion!

He just stood in the plaza, facing the wind, and broke through!

"You! Are you even human?" Li Shuangxu's voice was bitter!

This person is definitely a jerk!

Are you kidding me?

This realm is yours for the taking!

Gu Chen patted his shoulder: "Let's go, don't waste time. Oh, and call Brother Qingshan too. What's the point of him always serving tea and water to the Ancestor? He should go out and see the world."

Li Shuangxu hadn't recovered from the shock of witnessing the breakthrough when he was dragged off to find Gu Qingshan!

In the side hall, Gu Qingshan was squatting in front of the tea stove, intently brewing tea.

Hearing that he was going to Zhongzhou to participate in the Genius Battle, the fan in his hand fell to the ground.

"Young Master, are you kidding me? Me? A third-level Emperor Realm cultivator, going to Zhongzhou to participate in the Genius Battle? I probably wouldn't even pass the preliminary rounds!"

"What are you afraid of? I'm here!" Gu Chen patted his chest and assured him.

Gu Qingshan looked at Li Shuangxu, who silently nodded. Although he didn't want to admit it, Gu Chen was indeed a freak!

From King to Emperor, breaking through like that, as easily as drinking water!

This is truly drinking water!

He had never seen a breakthrough as simple as drinking water!

"But Young Master, I think serving tea and water is safer!"

Gu Qingshan wanted to refuse, after all, wandering outside was nothing compared to staying by the ancestor's side!

He had broken through to the Emperor Realm before Gu Chen, though he still couldn't defeat Gu Chen.

But that didn't diminish the exhilarating feeling of rapidly increasing his realm!

"No! You have to go! The ancestor is entrusting you to me." Gu Chen didn't give him a chance to refuse!

He had to go; after all, they were good brothers, and he had to include him in this good deed!

But was it really a good thing?

Gu Qingshan was about to make another excuse when suddenly a familiar voice came from the void, "Qingshan, go. Don't just stay cooped up in the back mountain serving tea and water all day; you should go out and see the world."

"You three, come to the back mountain."

It was Gu Xuan's voice.

Gu Qingshan froze, swallowing back all the words he was about to say.

The ancestor had spoken; what could he say?

He had no choice but to go!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 55 Exclusive Protector, Are You Sure You're Not Raising Dead Servants?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The three walked towards the back mountain.

Li Shuangxu followed behind, his heart filled with mixed emotions.

On the back mountain, Gu Xuan lay in a recliner, with five people standing beside him!

Jian Wuji, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, Bai Yujing, and Ye Wuhen.

The five stood in a line, their auras restrained, but the invisible pressure made the air seem heavier!

Gu Xuan put down his teacup and looked at the three, "If the three of you are going out, you can't be without protectors. Jian Wuji, you go with Xiao Chenchen. The remaining four, you two each choose one!"

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu exchanged a glance.

They were being allowed to choose their protectors?

Those chosen by the patriarch himself must be incredibly powerful!

Moreover, they might encounter many troubles on the road; protectors were necessary.

Li Shuangxu naturally knew the identity of his protectors; when he was a Holy Son, he had a half-saint as his protector.

But the auras of the four before him were many times stronger than before!

He couldn't sense their auras at all!

Could this be the Gu family's hidden strength?!

Truly terrifying!

Li Shuangxu's gaze swept over the four, finally settling on Bai Yujing, dressed in white and holding a jade flute.

"Senior, this junior chooses you!" Bai Yujing nodded slightly and stood behind Li Shuangxu.

Gu Qingshan looked at the remaining three. Yue Qingshuang was aloof as frost, and Yue Qingxue was gentle as jade; neither of them were worthy of his attention!

Besides, taking such beauties out might only bring more trouble.

Such exquisite beauties should be left to the Ancestor!

Perhaps they'll become pregnant when they return?

That would be a happy ending for everyone!

After a moment's thought, Gu Qingshan said, "Senior, I choose you!"

He pointed to Ye Wuhen!

Ye Wuhen glanced at him, nodded slightly, and stood behind him, holding his sword.

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue exchanged a glance, feeling somewhat annoyed.

Why weren't they chosen?

Is being too beautiful a mistake?

Gu Xuan waved his hand: "You two stay here and guard the place!"

The two women had no choice but to nod and step aside.

Gu Xuan pointed to three packages beside him: "These are for you. Use them on the road. Spend what you need, especially you, Xiao Chenchen. Your cultivation is still shallow. Just do your best and don't worry too much about the rankings."

"Also, your protectors will only help you when you are in danger. On your journey, everything is up to you!"

Gu Chen took the packages; they were heavy. Opening them, he found them piled high with top-grade spirit stones!

Enough to buy four domains!

And many pills!

The bottles were labeled with various remedies, including healing, recovery, and breakthrough techniques—they had it all!

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu's were similar, except they each had an extra weapon!

These were specially chosen for them by Gu Xuan!

They would directly and maximally enhance their combat power!

"Don't worry, Ancestor, I will definitely do my best and not bring shame to the Gu family!" Gu Chen slung his pack over his shoulder!

Gu Xuan smiled and waved his hand: "Go! Don't bring shame to the Gu family while you're out there! Just do it!"

"Yes, Ancestor!"

The three bowed and turned to descend the mountain!

Outside the Gu family's mountain gate, Gu Tianhua was settling his eighteen vassal forces when he suddenly heard that his son was going to Zhongzhou to participate in the Genius Battle!

He hurriedly dropped what he was doing and ran towards the back mountain.

Halfway there, he saw three streaks of light fly across the sky—it was Gu Chen, Li Shuangxu, and Gu Qingshan.

He opened his mouth to call out, but the streaks of light had already disappeared into the horizon!

"That little brat!" Gu Tianhua stood rooted to the spot.

Gazing at the horizon, he finally managed to squeeze out a sentence after a long pause, "A boy is like a tree that grows up and can't be kept at home! Keeping him only breeds resentment! In the end, he belongs to someone else... No, that's not right, he's going to fight, not to find a wife!"

"He's so old and still hasn't found a wife! I wonder when I'll be able to hold my grandson!"

Gu Tianhua sighed, muttering,

"I wonder if he'll bring back a little girl, so I can hold my grandson, wouldn't that be wonderful?"

Just as he was happily thinking this, Gu Xuan's voice came from the void, "Tianhua, quickly go and arrange things with those forces. Explain the Gu family's rules clearly."

Gu Tianhua shuddered and ran off, "Yes! I'll go right now!"

Outer Mountain Square!

This was a place specifically designated by the Gu family for its vassal forces to reside in!

Originally, Gu Tianhua planned to let them return to their respective territories, but after these people came in, not one of them wanted to leave!

Not only did they not want to go back, they couldn't even be driven away!

A single breath of spiritual energy here is better than a whole day of cultivation outside. Anyone who leaves is a fool!

The plaza was packed with people. Some were meditating, some were sparring, and some were studying the cultivation manual issued by the Gu family!

When Gu Tianhua walked in, everyone simultaneously stopped what they were doing and stood at attention!

Gu Tianhua didn't waste any words and went straight to the point: "Everyone, now that you've become vassals of the Gu family, there are some rules that need to be clarified first!"

"First, once you become a vassal of the Gu family, you are a member of the Gu family. You can never betray them! Those who betray will be punished according to ancestral rules!"

"Second, fighting among vassals is forbidden. If there are conflicts, come to me for resolution. Anyone who dares to lay a hand on Gu family territory, don't blame me for being impolite!"

"Third, and most importantly, unity is essential! The Gu family members cannot fight amongst themselves!"

Everyone nodded.

They had already considered these rules; without rules, there is no order.

The Gu family, with its immense power, couldn't allow them to run rampant.

"Now that the rules are out, let's talk about the benefits!" Gu Tianhua's tone shifted, a smile playing on his lips.

Everyone perked up their ears!

"First, you can use the Gu family's cultivation facilities as you please, unlimited times, unlimited time."

The square erupted in commotion.

Unlimited times?

So, even something as valuable as the Marrow Cleansing Array can be used without limit?

The benefits weren't over yet!
