"Secondly, you may enter the Gu Family's Cultivation Techniques Hall and choose one of their supernatural abilities or cultivation methods. What you find depends on your luck and destiny! However, all cultivation methods and supernatural abilities are strictly forbidden from being passed on to outsiders! Anyone found doing so will be executed without exception!"

Even Huo Dongyuan was no longer calm!

He had personally witnessed the quasi-Emperor level cultivation methods in the Cultivation Techniques Hall!

Choosing just one would be enough to benefit them for a lifetime!

As for passing it on to outsiders, how could that be possible?

He would treasure it like a precious gem!

"Third, every month, the Gu family will distribute a suitable amount of cultivation elixir and pills to each vassal force. Not much, but enough for your needs! Cultivate diligently and strive to break through to the Saint Realm within three months!"

The entire hall was silent.

Is this really just a vassal relationship?

Are they sure they're not raising death servants?

Those resources mentioned earlier are treasures even the Holy Lands consider precious, things that even direct disciples can't easily obtain!

Now they can get them easily!

And they're going to cultivate everyone into a Saint!

The Gu family is reaching a whole new level!

In an instant, everyone was excited!

"Long live the Gu family!"

Someone shouted, and everyone joined in!

"Long live the Gu family!"

"Long live the Gu family!"

Gu Tianhua raised his hand, signaling everyone to be quiet: "Alright, alright, stop shouting. Hurry up and cultivate. Three months will pass in a flash. If anyone hasn't reached the Saint Realm by then, don't say I didn't warn you!"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 56 Arrival in Tianyuan Prefecture, News of the Genius Battle
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The three flew for half a day, the mountains and rivers beneath their feet becoming increasingly unfamiliar.

Gu Chen flew in the lead, full of vigor, wishing he could leap directly to Zhongzhou!

Li Shuangxu followed behind, silently calculating the route.

But why did it feel increasingly unfamiliar?

It was as if he had never been here before?

Gu Qingshan flew at the rear, looking around as he flew.

Suddenly, Gu Qingshan spoke, "Young Master, which direction is Zhongzhou?"

Gu Chen froze, stopping!

He turned to look at Gu Qingshan, opened his mouth, then looked around at the unfamiliar landscape.

He didn't seem to have asked!

Gu Chen turned to look at Li Shuangxu, who was also looking at him!

They stared at each other for three seconds!

???

"You don't know the way?" Gu Chen asked.

"I thought Young Master Gu knew!" Li Shuangxu said.

Another silence!

Gu Qingshan sighed, "So we've been flying for ages and still haven't figured out the direction?"

Gu Chen's lips twitched, and he coughed lightly, "Hey Shuangxu, aren't you the Holy Son of Qingyun? You should have been to Zhongzhou, right?"

Li Shuangxu shook his head, "No, but I should know the way."

He took out a yellowed map from his storage bag, unfolded it, looked at it, and then checked the direction.

"Our current location should be northeast of Qingyun Prefecture. To get to Zhongzhou, we need to go to Tianyuan Prefecture first. There's a cross-prefecture spirit ship there that can pass through a spatial rift directly to the border of Zhongzhou. Taking the spirit ship is faster than flying; it should save at least seven days."

Gu Chen's eyes lit up, "A spirit ship? Then what are we waiting for? Let's go!"

Li Shuangxu put away the map and turned around, "Follow me."

The three continued their journey, this time with Li Shuangxu leading the way.

Gu Chen flew to Li Shuangxu's side and patted him on the shoulder: "Shuangxu, I told you you're smart! For something like this, we really need you!"

Li Shuangxu smiled wryly. Why didn't this feel like a compliment?

"Brother Gu, you don't even know the way and you dare to run off? Aren't you afraid of flying into the Demon Beast Forest?"

Gu Chen patted the jade token given to him by Jian Wuji at his waist. "Afraid? Afraid of what? With this thing, I'm not afraid of flying anywhere!"

Li Shuangxu was speechless!

That makes sense!

With a protector chosen by the Ancestor, one really isn't afraid of flying anywhere!

Gu Qingshan followed behind, silently watching the two figures, and suddenly felt that this trip wasn't so scary after all!

Although his cultivation wasn't high in Zhongzhou, with a protector chosen by the Ancestor, what was there to panic about!

Just do it!

"Let's go!" Gu Chen sped up, flying quickly forward!

The three soared across the sky, flying towards Tianyuan Prefecture!

Behind them, the Gu family's mountain gate receded into the distance.

Ahead lay an unfamiliar world, an unknown journey, and the gathering place of geniuses from three thousand prefectures!

Led by Li Shuangxu, the three traveled for three days and three nights, finally arriving in Tianyuan Prefecture at dawn.

Tianyuan Prefecture, the largest prefecture on the outermost edge of the Eastern Region, beyond which lay the vast void!

This place was originally a desolate wasteland. Tens of thousands of years ago, a quasi-Emperor-level expert emerged, cleaving the void with a single sword strike, opening a spatial passage to the Central Region!

From then on, Tianyuan Prefecture became the most prosperous transportation hub in the Eastern Region, bustling with merchant caravans and cultivators!

The three entered the city.

The streets were wide, lined with shops selling spiritual herbs, magical treasures, and spirit beasts, their cries echoing throughout!

The streets were bustling with people—disciples of grand sects in splendid attire, weary cultivators from their journeys, and even a Saint-level expert mingled in the crowd, inconspicuous at all!

Gu Qingshan's eyes widened: "This! This is ten times more lively than the largest market in Qingyun Prefecture!"

Gu Chen calmly replied, "It's alright, but it's not quite as impressive as the Gu family's!"

Li Shuangxu's lips twitched. The Gu family lived in a celestial realm; could they compare?

He led the two into a small alley and found the spirit ship ticket office.

A long queue stretched before the window. Li Shuangxu squeezed in, asked around, and emerged with a grim expression.

"The next spirit ship to Zhongzhou departs in three days."

"Three days?" Gu Chen frowned.

“There’s nothing we can do. The spirit ships to Zhongzhou don’t run every day; the next one isn’t until three days from now,” Li Shuangxu said helplessly, putting away the map.

“Let’s find a place to stay first. Three days is a good time to rest.”

The three of them found an inn in the city and booked a quiet courtyard room.

Gu Chen wasn’t in a hurry to rest. He pulled Li Shuangxu to sit down at the stone table in the courtyard.

“Shuangxu, tell me in detail about the Genius Battle. What are the rules? Who participates? What should we pay attention to? Tell me everything!”

Since they were going to participate anyway, it was necessary to do some research beforehand!

Currently, only Li Shuangxu knew some things.

Just as Li Shuangxu was about to speak, a loud discussion suddenly broke out at the next table!

"Have you heard? In this Genius Battle, the Han family of the Imperial Clan has produced a monster named Han Zixuan! He broke through to the Emperor Realm at the age of thirty! It's said he was even bestowed with a pseudo-Emperor Artifact. I feel like he's aiming for the championship!"

"Really? A thirty-year-old Emperor Realm cultivator? And a pseudo-Emperor Artifact? What's the point of even competing?"

"Not just the Han family! It's said that the Ice Phoenix Clan also sent their Holy Maiden named Bing Xin, supposedly the purest Ice Phoenix bloodline in ten thousand years! She had extraordinary phenomena from birth! Her strength has already reached the peak of the fourth level of the Emperor Realm, and she possesses an ancestral pseudo-Emperor Artifact!"

"Wow! Han Zixuan! Bing Xin! How come this year's competition is so fierce? I've already heard the names of two Imperial Clans. Is this Genius Battle even going to work?"

"That's not all!" an old man said slowly.

"As far as I know, the Zhuge family, a prestigious clan known for its longevity, has also emerged. Zhuge Qingyun is proficient in formations and deduction. Although his cultivation isn't the highest, it's said that no one of his level can break his formations! There are also descendants of some reclusive powerful figures, all vying for this year's Genius Battle!"

"Why?" the young man asked, puzzled.

The old man lowered his voice: "Because the rewards this year are more generous than ever before! The top ten are said to receive fragments of quasi-Emperor artifacts, and the top three… hehe, I don't know either, but to attract so many imperial clans and longevity families, you can imagine!"

The conversation at the next table grew increasingly lively!

Suddenly, a young man slammed his hand on the table, his voice loud enough to be heard halfway down the street: "What you're saying is all outdated! I just received news that Kang Shen is also participating in this year's Genius Battle!"

"Kang Shen? Which Kang Shen?" someone asked.

"What other anti-seepage technology is there? It's the one that fights against people without taking any pressure, only eating braised chicken!"

"They say this guy was born a demon child, always getting into fights, which resulted in him developing incredible skills! For one thing, his resilience is unmatched among his peers! Others hit him like a tickle, he takes them down with a single punch."

"Really? He doesn't eat stress, he only eats chicken?"

"Absolutely true! My cousin's aunt's husband's neighbor's nephew used to be a henchman for Kang Shen, and he saw it with his own eyes! Before a fight, Kang Shen always eats a big braised chicken, and after eating it, he's like he's been injected with chicken blood, his fighting power doubles!"

The table erupted in laughter!

Some didn't believe it, some were skeptical, and some had already started asking how to make the braised chicken!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 57 Warships Set Sail, Confronting the Central Plains Imperial Clan
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The chatter at the next table continued, but Gu Chen was no longer paying attention!

He turned to look at Li Shuangxu, his expression somewhat strange.

"Breaking through to the Emperor Realm at thirty? Called a genius?" Gu Chen lowered his voice, puzzled.

"I'm in my early twenties, and I'm already at the Emperor Realm! All those imperial prodigies they're talking about, is this it?"

Li Shuangxu's lips twitched. He didn't know what to say for a moment, but he tried his best to remain calm.

Gu Chen was in his early twenties, at the first level of the Emperor Realm—in the Central Continent, that was indeed prodigious talent!

But his breakthrough process was too casual. He broke through just by standing in the square and feeling the wind. It didn't inspire any respect at all!

He just wanted to curse!

"I've given you your realm, don't say anything more!"

Li Shuangxu explained, “Brother Gu, the age limit for participating in the Genius Battle is no more than forty. Breaking through to the Emperor Realm at thirty is indeed considered genius in Zhongzhou! Not everyone is like you, breaking through as easily as drinking water!”

Gu Chen chuckled, “That’s true! But those geniuses from imperial clans and heirs of longevity families have quasi-Emperor artifacts and formations, which are quite troublesome to deal with. I only have a broken sword!”

Li Shuangxu almost choked on his saliva!

A broken sword?

He had heard that this was specially chosen for him by the Gu family patriarch. How could it be a broken sword?

If he accidentally shatters someone’s weapon during a burst of power, it would be difficult to explain!

Gu Qingshan, however, was thinking: This person who doesn’t eat stress but only eats braised chicken is quite interesting!

Not eating stress means his stress resistance is indeed strong!

As for eating braised chicken, it’s probably some kind of strange cultivation method!

There are many extraordinary people in this world; we can’t underestimate someone just because they like braised chicken!

However, he also realized that none of the participants in this Genius Battle were to be underestimated!

Even if their cultivation levels weren't high, they possessed numerous trump cards!

None of them should be taken lightly.

Over the past three days, rumors had circulated among all forces, large and small, including those at the Eternal Realm, Imperial Clans, Longevity Families, and reclusive powerhouses!

This year's Genius Battle was incredibly prestigious!

This fueled Gu Chen's burning desire to make a name for himself, to gain more prestige within the Gu family, and to showcase his abilities before the patriarch!

Thinking of this,

it further solidified Gu Chen's determination to make a name for himself!

Three days passed in the blink of an eye!

During these three days, Gu Chen did nothing but eat, sleep, and practice his sword!

Jian Wuji taught him three moves: the Sky-Piercing Sword Draw Slash, the Returning to One Sword, and a third move that remained unnamed!

Jian Wuji only said one thing: This sword strike kills without leaving a trace! After the sword is drawn, the enemy won't even know how they died!

Gu Chen practiced for three days, barely managing to grasp the basics!

Still far from mastery, but he felt it should be sufficient!

Three days of cultivation had solidified his sword intent, causing the Hidden Dragon Sword to tremble violently, seemingly urging him to unleash his power!

On the morning of the third day, the three headed towards the spirit ship dock!

The dock was already packed with people—those going to Zhongzhou, those seeing them off, and those just watching the spectacle—it was incredibly crowded!

The enormous spirit ship was docked at the shore, its powerful aura deterring lower-level cultivators from approaching!

Just as the three reached the end of the line, a fragrant breeze wafted by!

A veiled woman walked past them, dressed in snow-white robes, emanating a faint, pleasant fragrance!

Perhaps this was a woman's natural scent!

So fragrant!

Gu Qingshan sniffed, "It smells so good!"

Gu Chen was also drawn by the fragrance, and judging from the girl's demeanor, she seemed quite extraordinary!

It seemed no one on this ship was simple!

The woman walked to the front of the line, followed by two maids, both exuding considerable power!

Just then, a commotion arose behind them!

Several people swaggered over, led by a young man in a long golden robe.

His face was handsome, but his eyes were arrogant, and his chin was held high!

"Make way! Make way!" His attendants shoved the people in line!

Some people turned around in displeasure, but upon seeing the token at the golden-robed man's waist, they immediately shut up and made way!

They dared not say a word, seemingly aware of the man's identity!

The attendants pushed through the line, and one oblivious individual directly confronted Gu Chen and his companions, raising his hand to shove Gu Qingshan aside.

Before Gu Qingshan could react, Gu Chen had already reached out and stopped the attendant's hand!

Gu Chen's voice wasn't loud, "Line up properly! What are you doing cutting in line?"

The attendant paused, sizing up Gu Chen and his companions!

The three were dressed plainly, without tokens or markings; they clearly weren't from any powerful faction!

Yet they spoke so arrogantly!

Were they being disrespected?

The attendant's tone was contemptuous: "Boy, what's your problem? Do you know who we are? The Zhao family! The Zhao family, the imperial clan of Zhongzhou!"

The surrounding crowd instantly fell silent, then erupted in commotion!

"The Zhao family? The imperial Zhao family of Zhongzhou?"

"Such a big shot? Could that guy in the golden robe be Zhao Ritian?"

"The Great Sun Holy Body! The Zhao Ritian who has the potential to become an emperor in the future?"

"This is going to be good! Daring to challenge the Zhao family? Are they courting death?"

"I wonder who these three are? What a pity!"

Li Shuangxu's expression changed slightly!

These past few days, he'd heard a lot about the imperial clans, and Zhao Ritian's name appeared frequently!

The Great Sun Sacred Body, a prodigy nurtured with all his might by the Zhao family, an imperial clan, a third-level Emperor Realm cultivator, and possessing a quasi-Emperor artifact!

He'd thought he'd only encounter these imperial clan geniuses in the Central Continent, but unexpectedly, he'd run into them right here in Tianyuan Continent!

Gu Chen remained unfazed, his grip tightening, and said calmly, "I don't care who you are! Go line up, understand?"

Zhao Ritian, who had already walked past, stopped upon hearing this and turned back.

He looked at Gu Chen.

He laughed!

That laugh was filled with condescending arrogance!

Zhao Ritian crossed his arms. "Interesting! In my thirty years of life, this is the first time I've encountered someone in this wretched place who dares to reason with me!"

"Wretched place?" Gu Chen released his attendant's hand and looked at Zhao Ritian.

"You're standing at the Spirit Ship Dock in Tianyuan Prefecture. Everyone's queuing, but you're not? I thought the Imperial Clan knew all the rules, but you're practically a savage from the forest!"

The entire place fell silent!

No one dared to speak; even breathing became quieter!

Savage?

Calling the young master of the Zhao family, an Imperial Clan, a savage?

Is he tired of living?

Zhao Ritian's smile froze on his face, his eyes flashing with cold light!

His entourage stepped forward in unison, their aura menacing, their spiritual energy surging—the lowest being at the peak of the Emperor Realm!

Some had even reached the Saint Realm!

Whispers arose from the crowd!

"Who are those three? Daring to challenge Zhao Ritian?"

"I don't know, never seen them before! Maybe a small force from the Eastern Region?"

"A small force dares to be so arrogant? Aren't they afraid of being wiped out by the Zhao family?"

"Who knows? Maybe they're a ruthless character! Let's keep our voices down!"

The atmosphere instantly became tense!

This also attracted the attention of the woman in white.

She felt that angering the Imperial Clan was a very unwise thing to do!

She wondered how these three would escape.


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 58 First Steps into Zhongzhou, Hidden Dangers
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Suddenly, someone in the crowd recognized Gu Chen.

"That young man looks like he's from the Gu family! The Gu family who dealt with the Eastern Region beast tide a few days ago!"

Everyone was startled, their gazes turning to Gu Chen!

The Eastern Region beast tide, with three Eternal Realm demonic beasts being driven back by two young girls from the Gu family riding white tigers—this news had already spread throughout the entire Eastern Region!

And the young man before them had the character "Gu" engraved on the waist tag!

"He really is from the Gu family!"

"I heard the Gu family even dared to tame Eternal Realm demonic beasts, no wonder they dared to challenge Zhao Ritian!"

"A Central State imperial family, a rising star from the Eastern Region, this is going to be interesting!"

The murmurs grew louder!

Zhao Ritian frowned slightly upon hearing the name "Gu family."

The Gu family?

When did the Eastern Region produce a family that dared to challenge an imperial family?

The atmosphere became tense, the air at the spirit ship dock seemed to freeze!

Just then, a deep voice came from the spirit ship, "Zhao Ritian, this is Tianyuan Prefecture, not Zhongzhou. If you want to cause trouble, go back to your Zhao family! The spirit ship is about to depart; if you don't leave, wait for the next one!"

Everyone looked up!

Standing at the bow was an old man, his aura powerful—a master of the Eternal Realm!

The guardian of Tianyuan Prefecture, backed by more than one imperial clan, even the Zhao family had to show him some respect!

Zhao Ritian's expression changed slightly. He stared at the old man for a few seconds, then coldly snorted, "Fine! I'll give you face today!"

He turned to Gu Chen, his eyes even colder than before, "Boy, you're lucky this time! Next time we meet, I won't be so polite!"

He then looked at the old man at the bow, "I'll remember what happened today!"

With that, he boarded the spirit ship with his entourage and headed towards the second-floor private room!

The moment the door closed, his face turned completely grim!

No one had ever dared to speak to him like that before!

Today, he'd finally met someone fearless!

The attendants didn't dare breathe, serving him with utmost care!

A personal guard stepped forward, making a beheading gesture!

"Young Master, let's send someone to deal with those three reckless fools!"

"No rush!" Zhao Ritian interrupted him.

"Those three won't escape! Once we get to Zhongzhou, we'll find an opportunity to deal with them!"

He spoke casually, having done this many times before!

As for the Gu family, haha! Never even heard of them!

Completely unnecessary to consider!

Besides, his Great Sun Holy Body should suppress everything in the world!

There's no need to put anyone or any power before him; it wouldn't affect his Great Sun Holy Body's mindset, nor would it be good for his path to becoming an emperor!

The next instant, his brows suddenly furrowed!

A faint, delicate fragrance drifted through the air, almost imperceptible!

Yet, it inexplicably stirred his Great Sun Sacred Body!

His blood accelerated, his spiritual energy surged!

It was as if he had encountered some kind of innate affinity!

It could actually cause his Great Sun Sacred Body to stir so violently!

Zhao Ritian followed the fragrance, his gaze landing on the white-clad woman at the front of the line!

She wore a veil, obscuring her face, but her slender figure and that unique fragrance suggested she was exceptionally beautiful!

And what's more, it could cause his Great Sun Sacred Body to stir even more intensely!

If he were to unleash the Great Sun!

Perhaps his Sacred Body would reach its full potential!

“Interesting!” Zhao Ritian withdrew his gaze, a playful smile playing on his lips.

“My Great Sun Sacred Body has actually reacted to a woman! If I could get my hands on her, dual cultivation to complement my Sacred Body might just lead to its full potential!”

The guard understood and was about to reply.

Zhao Ritian continued on his own, “With the Sacred Body fully developed, becoming a Quasi-Emperor is just around the corner! Perhaps even reaching the legendary Great Emperor realm isn't impossible! That woman definitely holds a secret!”

The guard nodded: “I'll go investigate that woman's background right away!”

“Investigate carefully, don't alert her!” Zhao Ritian closed his eyes.

“As for those three fools who don't know their place, let them live a few more days. Once they're off the ship, find some excuse to kill them!”

On the deck!

Li Shuangxu lowered his voice, "Young Master Gu, wasn't that too impulsive? You've offended a force of this caliber right from the start. We might have a hard time when we get to Zhongzhou!"

Gu Chen replied calmly, "We're here for training. We're going to fight anyway. What difference does it make who we fight? So what if it's an imperial clan? Does that mean they can't be reasonable?"

Li Shuangxu opened his mouth, but found he couldn't refute him.

But this world isn't about reason; it's about might and connections!

The imperial clan has bigger fists and deeper connections than you. Are you going to reason with them?

"But!"

Gu Chen patted his shoulder. "No buts! Shuangxu, you're good in every way, just too cautious. If you're going to fight, then fight! The patriarch said to just do it!"

Li Shuangxu fell silent!

The Gu family seems to have never known what cowardice is!

Are all the Gu family members this fierce?

What he didn't know was that Gu Chen even dared to beat up his father, Gu Tianhua, let alone a stranger?

"Just go for it!"

"We're already here! Let's just do it!"

At this moment, Gu Qingshan patted the jade pendant at his waist, "We still have protectors! If we can't handle it, we'll talk to the ancestor!"

Li Shuangxu's lips twitched, completely giving up on persuasion!

That makes sense. No matter how big the problem, the Gu family ancestor will handle it!

The spirit ship sailed through the void rift for more than ten days, the journey relatively smooth, before docking at a pier on the border of Zhongzhou.

Gu Chen and the other two stepped onto the land of Zhongzhou, finding themselves in a completely unfamiliar world.

This was the first time the three of them had ever been here.

In the distance, cities stood in rows, each more prosperous than the largest city in Qingyun Prefecture!

Li Shuangxu took out a map, "The registration point for the Genius Battle is in Tianque City, three days' journey from here."

Gu Chen and Gu Qingshan nodded.

The three of them took to the air and flew in the direction marked on the map!

Behind them, on the spirit ship dock, the woman in white also disembarked!

She gazed in the direction where Gu Chen and the other two had disappeared, remained silent for a moment, then followed with two maids!

From the shadows of the dock, Zhao Ritian slowly emerged!

A cold smile played on his lips, like a hunter watching its prey slowly walk into a trap!

“Is everything ready?” he asked.

The guard beside him nodded: “The men I sent out have already caught up with those three reckless fools! As for that woman, I’ve also investigated her background!”

“Speak!”

The guard lowered his voice: “That woman in white’s surname is Ru, her name is Ru Shuang, she’s from the Ru family! And she’s ranked third on the Beauty Ranking!”

“Which Ru family?”

"The Ru family of Qitian Prefecture once produced a Quasi-Emperor, but later declined and is now merely a second-rate family! However, she herself possesses a special physique: the Taiyin Spiritual Body!"

"This physique is extremely rare. If one can cultivate with her, regardless of one's physique, one can comprehend deeper levels of the laws of heaven and earth! Simultaneously, one's cultivation will increase!"

Zhao Ritian's eyes grew brighter, and his smile deepened!

The Taiyin Spiritual Body?

And ranked third on the Beauty Ranking!

No wonder it caused such a reaction from my Sacred Body!

If I could cultivate with the Taiyin Spiritual Body, my cultivation would at least increase by two minor realms!

With a fully developed Sacred Body, a Quasi-Emperor is within reach!

Even a Great Emperor is not impossible!

"Chase her!"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 59: Ru Shuang's unparalleled beauty kills Zhao Ritian.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Thousands of miles away, Gu Chen and his companions were flying towards the heart of the Central Plains.

Gu Qingshan glanced back, seeing nothing, but a sense of unease lingered.

"I have a feeling someone's following us."

Gu Chen also glanced back.

A vast sea of ​​clouds, empty and desolate, nothing at all!

Gu Chen said, "Maybe you're just imagining things, but it's always good to be careful!"

The three accelerated, transforming into three streaks of light and disappearing into the horizon!

Thousands of miles behind, several men in black were in hot pursuit!

The leader was a Saint Realm Eighth Level expert!

He held a bronze mirror in his hand, its surface displaying three points of light—the spiritual energy fluctuations of Gu Chen and his companions!

The leader put away the mirror. "Chase! Young Master Ri said he wants to see them alive or dead!"

Six men in black sped off!

Meanwhile, in another direction, another group was chasing after Ru Shuang!

Taiyin Spiritual Body, Great Sun Saint Body, dual cultivation complementing each other!

This was something Zhao Ritian was determined to have!

As for Gu Chen, he was nothing more than an ant blocking his path to becoming an Emperor, easily crushed!

The three were discussing whether Zhao Ritian would take this opportunity to retaliate against them!

Suddenly, a chilling voice exploded from above, "You can't escape!"

The three looked up simultaneously!

Six men in black descended from the sky, surrounding them!

The leader was an elderly man at the eighth level of the Saint Realm, the other five were all at the peak of the Emperor Realm!

The auras of the six intertwined, like an invisible net, suffocating them!

Gu Chen glanced at the six men, disdainfully, "You were sent by Zhao Ritian, weren't you?"

The leader didn't answer, only sneered and waved his hand!

The six men approached simultaneously, their spiritual energy surging, their killing intent chilling!

Just then, a whooshing sound came from the sky on the other side!

Ru Shuang, accompanied by two maids, flew in from another direction, followed by several men in black!

Upon seeing Gu Chen and his companions, their expressions changed slightly, and they flew over and landed beside Gu Chen.

The two groups of men in black merged, surrounding the seven people!

"Heh! Let's see where you can run to!"

Zhao Ritian landed expressionlessly, wielding a battle halberd that radiated the terrifying pressure of a quasi-Emperor weapon!

He hadn't expected to encounter such fearless individuals in this Genius Battle!

But what surprised him most was encountering the Taiyin Spirit Body, a great tonic for him!

The Great Sun Sacred Body would soon be fully formed!

Although his mood was dampened by these three fearless individuals, overall, he had still gained something!

Moreover, he was tired of the servants his father had rewarded him with; having the Taiyin Spirit Body as a servant would be a good way to relieve his frustrations!

It wasn't a bad choice!

After all, the people on the Beauty Ranking were all exceptional!

With a wave of his hand, a gust of wind howled forth, heading straight for Ru Shuang's face!

Ru Shuang had no time to dodge; her veil was swept away by the wind and drifted down into the air!

Everyone froze simultaneously!

The face beneath the veil was flawless, with skin as smooth as jade, exquisitely beautiful features, and eyes as clear as autumn water. Standing in the wind, she was like a fairy descended to earth!

Gu Chen was mesmerized!

In his twenty-odd years of life, he had never seen a woman like this!

It was an indescribable beauty!

Not just stunning, but the kind of beauty that made you unable to look away once you saw her!

The other two were also stunned; the girl was even more beautiful than the two women beside the patriarch!

"So beautiful!"

Zhao Ritian was also momentarily stunned by Ru Shuang's beauty, but quickly recovered!

Third on the Beauty Ranking, truly living up to her reputation!

Only such a beauty is worthy of his Great Sun Sacred Body!

"Would you be willing to follow me, to serve me as my maid! After I become Emperor, I can make you Empress!"

Zhao Ritian stared at Ru Shuang, asking expressionlessly!

Suddenly, Ru Shuang felt nauseous!

Looking at Zhao Ritian's arrogant face, Ru Shuang felt her stomach churn!

Empress?

Who cares!

She subconsciously took a step back, her palms sweating profusely!

Two maids stood in front of her, but their legs trembled; after all, the difference in strength was too great!

Just then, a frail, aged voice suddenly echoed in Ru Shuang's mind, "Girl, don't be afraid! There should be others behind him. Grandpa will control your body and take you away from here! Afterwards, Grandpa might fall into a deep sleep, so be careful!"

That's right!

There was a soul within Ru Shuang's body!

And this soul was none other than her grandfather!

The reason Ru Shuang was here was because her father wanted her to marry a quasi-Emperor level expert who had been around for ten thousand years!

The reason given was that her physique would be helpful to that expert!

But it would sacrifice her, a girl in her twenties!

That's way too old!

How could she possibly agree to such a thing!

She sent her grandfather to reason with him, but unexpectedly, her grandfather had also been imprisoned by her father, and only a wisp of his soul remained, attached to the jade pendant within her body!

She had no desire to be with this eternally old man!

She fled her family overnight, carrying her grandfather's remnant soul, to participate in the Genius Battle!

All she wanted was to win the reward, so her grandfather could rebuild his physical body!

But luck hadn't smiled upon her; she'd encountered Zhao Ritian, who coveted her physique!

She knew Zhao Ritian was a notorious womanizer; how could such a person be worthy of the man of her heart!

But the current situation was far from optimistic for her!

However, she noticed the three people beside her showed no sign of nervousness. Did these three have some hidden trump card?

"Grandpa, control will be yours soon!"

"Hmm! I'm waiting for my chance!"

Ru Shuang took a deep breath, suppressing her surging emotions, and coldly looked at Zhao Ritian: "Dream on!"

Zhao Ritian's smile froze on his face, a flash of anger in his eyes. "You won't listen to reason, will you?"

He raised his battle halberd, the oppressive aura of a quasi-Emperor weapon washing over him. "I'm giving you face, but you don't appreciate it! Then don't blame me for being impolite!"

The men in black behind him stepped forward in unison, their spiritual energy surging, their killing intent palpable!

Ru Shuang's face paled, her teeth clenched as she stared intently!

In her mind, her grandfather's voice echoed again: "Girl, get ready!"

Meanwhile! Gu Chen and his two companions moved!

Gu Chen gripped his sword and charged forward, actively seeking to fight Zhao Ritian!

“Want to die? This young master will grant your wish!” Zhao Ritian sneered, brandishing his halberd!

The black-clad men behind him were about to step forward when Zhao Ritian raised his hand to stop them: “Don’t move! This young master will personally play with him and show him what the Great Sun Sacred Body is all about!”

The black-clad men retreated, and Ru Shuang’s grandfather also called out to her in her mind, his aged voice carrying a hint of surprise, “Wait, this kid is not simple.”

Boom!

The halberd clashed with the longsword, a terrifying surge of spiritual energy exploded outwards, and the surrounding trees were cut in half!

In these few exchanges, Zhao Ritian hadn't gained any advantage!

Zhao Ritian's expression changed slightly!

He was a third-level Emperor Realm expert, possessing the Great Sun Sacred Body, and wielding a Quasi-Emperor weapon, yet he hadn't gained the upper hand?

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu exchanged a glance and charged forward!

"Great Dragon Claw Hand!"

Gu Qingshan suddenly extended a giant hand, slamming it towards Zhao Ritian's head!

Zhao Ritian hurriedly defended!

But it wasn't over yet; another attack followed!

It was Li Shuangxu!

"Damn ant!"

Zhao Ritian roared, "Sun!"

The Great Sun Sacred Body unleashed its full power!

Gu Chen didn't give him time to think, directly unleashing the Myriad Swords Returning to Origin technique taught to him by Jian Wuji!

"Swords, come!"

A deluge of swords attacked, making it difficult for him to defend for a moment!

He drew his battle halberd, intending to withstand the attack with his quasi-Emperor weapon, but to his surprise, Gu Chen shattered it with a single sword strike!

It shattered just like that!!

Boom!

Where the sword light passed, it was as if meteors were falling!

“Enough!”

Boom!

An incomparably powerful aura appeared!

It was a peak Eternal Realm expert!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 60 Calling for help? I'll call too! My heart is fluttering with spring fever.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Zhao Ritian's protector emerged from the void—a peak Eternal Realm expert!

His oppressive aura enveloped the entire world!

He glanced down at the unconscious Zhao Ritian, his face darkening!

"You will all die for damaging our young master's Dao heart!" the man's voice was chilling!

He looked at Gu Chen, his gaze icy, "Especially you, I will make you wish you were dead!"

Ru Shuang's expression turned grim. Her grandfather's voice echoed in her mind, "Peak Eternal Realm! In my prime, I could put up a fight, but now..."

Now she was merely a remnant soul, her strength reduced to less than a tenth!

Even escaping from a peak Eternal Realm expert was unlikely!

"Grandpa, then don't come out!" Ru Shuang cried out anxiously!

Grandpa was already weak; now it seemed he could only face his fate!

Grandpa was silent for a moment, "If those three brats don't have any trump cards, I'm afraid none of us will escape!"

Gu Chen and the other two, however, remained calm!

It wasn't an act; he was genuinely calm!

With the patriarch backing him up, what was there to panic about?

Call for help?

Anyone could do that!

Gu Chen's lips curled into a smile, his voice soft but brimming with confidence, "Uncle Jian!"

In the sky, three figures instantly appeared!

The three stood in a line!

They didn't release any pressure, but the oppressive aura emanating from the Great Emperor caused the heavens and earth to change color!!

The elder at the peak of the Eternal Realm's pupils dilated, his legs went weak, and he knelt on the ground with a thud!!

It wasn't that he wanted to kneel; his body knelt uncontrollably!

It was an instinctive reaction from the depths of his soul!!

It was something a cultivator of his level couldn't possibly comprehend!

Jian Wuji looked at Gu Chen, his brows furrowing slightly: "Young Master, you're injured!"

Gu Chen was stunned for a moment, then looked down to check his entire body; there was nothing wrong!

He wasn't injured!

"I'm not injured?!" Gu Chen asked, puzzled.

Jian Wuji stepped forward, reached out, and wiped the sweat from Gu Chen's forehead.

The movement was gentle, the expression serious.

Gu Chen's lips twitched.

Sweating counts as an injury?

But he didn't dare say anything, because Jian Wuji's expression was far too serious!

"The Ancestor said, fighting is allowed, but injury is not. You've already sweated."

The other two were also stunned. This is acceptable?

I was the one who was scared!

In Ru Shuang's mind, her grandfather's voice rang out again, this time with deep shock: "Girl, these three people! I can't sense their cultivation!"

Ru Shuang was stunned. Her grandfather couldn't sense them?

Her grandfather was at the peak of the Eternal Realm in his lifetime; even a Quasi-Emperor could be sensed by him!

But her grandfather couldn't sense these three!

"Those three are beyond my comprehension! Even at my peak, I probably couldn't withstand a casual strike from them! Could they be at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm?!"

Ru Shuang's heart skipped a beat!

Peak Quasi-Emperor!

That's a top-tier imperial clan!

With no Great Emperors appearing, peak Quasi-Emperors are the strongest!

But this Ancient Desolate Continent hasn't produced a Great Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years!

"When did such a family emerge in the Eastern Region?" Grandpa muttered to himself.

The Gu Family? The Gu Family?

I've never heard of this family in the Central Continent!

Could they be a reclusive family?

Ru Shuang stared at Gu Chen's retreating figure, silent!

She didn't know the Gu family's background, but a family that could have three suspected Quasi-Emperor peak experts as protectors couldn't be an ordinary family!

The peak Eternal Realm cultivator didn't even dare to raise his head, because he knew that any one of these three could obliterate him!

Jian Wuji finally looked at him, his voice calm: "So, you're the one who wanted to kill our young master?"

The man opened his mouth, but no sound came out!

Jian Wuji glanced at the man and said nothing more!

He raised his hand and casually swung his sword!

There was no sword light, no sword energy, not even any fluctuation of spiritual power!

But the peak Eternal Realm elder, along with the unconscious Zhao Ritian on the ground, and the black-clad men who hadn't yet run far away!

They were all frozen!

Unable to resist!

Then! They began to dissipate!

From head to toe, they turned into countless tiny fragments, drifting into the air!

In the blink of an eye, the peak of the Eternal Realm, the Saint Realm, and the Emperor Realm were all reduced to nothingness!

As if they had never existed!

The entire arena was deathly silent!

Ru Shuang couldn't believe her eyes; her lips trembled slightly!

In her mind, her grandfather's voice had completely changed, "One! One sword? Peak of the Eternal Realm, gone in one sword strike? How is that possible? Those were peak of the Eternal Realm! These three are probably truly peak Quasi-Emperors!"

He had witnessed a peak Quasi-Emperor unleash a devastating attack, annihilating everything around them with a single strike!

Only a peak Quasi-Emperor could possess such terrifying power!

Jian Wuji sheathed his sword, his tone returning to its usual calm, "Young Master, next time you fight, don't sweat."

Gu Chen's lips twitched.

Bai Yujing and Ye Wuhen only made a brief appearance.

Then, the three figures gradually faded away, disappearing into the void, as if they had never existed!

The suffocating oppression still lingered in the air, but no one could feel it anymore!

Because those who could feel it were all gone!

Gu Chen turned to look at Ru Shuang, opened his mouth as if to say something, but found his heart pounding!

It was the feeling of being moved!

A flutter of spring fever!

Not because of the battle just now, but because of the person before him!

The veil was gone, and her face was so beautiful that it was impossible to look away!

He had lived for over twenty years and had never seen a woman like her!

The person before him possessed an indescribable beauty.

“Miss…Miss.”

Gu Chen spoke, his voice slightly unnatural!

A hint of nervousness in his voice!

“May I ask your surname, Miss?”

Ru Shuang looked at him, a slight smile playing on her lips. She softly said, “Ru Shuang.”

Gu Chen blushed. This young man in his early twenties was speaking to such a beautiful girl for the first time, and he was so nervous he didn't know what to do with his hands!

“Miss Ru, where…where are you going? We…we’re also going to Zhongzhou for the Genius Battle, if it’s on our way.”

Before she could finish speaking, her ears were already bright red!

Gu Qingshan, watching from behind, whispered to Li Shuangxu, “Gu Chen’s blushing!”

Li Shuangxu nodded: “I noticed!”

Gu Qingshan added, “I’ve never seen Gu Chen like this before.”

Li Shuangxu thought for a moment: “It’s normal for the first time!”

People might lie, but her second brother wouldn’t!

Ru Shuang looked at Gu Chen’s blushing face and suddenly found it somewhat amusing.

This was the first time she’d seen a young man like this!

She was completely at a loss!

Completely different from those frivolous men.

Ru Shuang replied, "I'm also going to Zhongzhou to participate in the Genius Battle."

Gu Chen's eyes lit up: "Then... then together?"

Ru Shuang nodded: "Okay."

Gu Chen turned around, trying to appear calm, but he couldn't suppress the smile on his lips!

"Let's go, or we'll miss the registration deadline."


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 61 My Son's in Love? The Zhao Family's Fury
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan was reclining on a deck chair, sipping tea, his divine sense casually sweeping across the Central Plains. Suddenly, he smiled.

He saw Gu Chen standing before Ru Shuang, his face flushed, looking completely flustered, like a naive young boy!

It was the first time he'd ever seen this kid like this!

"Interesting!" Gu Xuan put down his teacup.

Gu Tianhua was standing to the side reporting on the cultivation progress of the vassal forces. Seeing his ancestor suddenly smile, he was taken aback. "Ancestor, what's making you so happy?"

Gu Xuan glanced at him, his tone calm, "Your son is in love!"

Gu Tianhua froze, stunned for a moment before reacting. "What? My son? Gu Chen? In love?"

His voice grew louder and louder, until it was almost a shout!

"Really? Ancestor, you're not lying to me, are you?"

Gu Xuan took a sip of tea. "When have I ever lied to you?"

Gu Tianhua's hands trembled with excitement. He grabbed Gu Xuan's clothes. "Ancestor! Tell me quickly, what's that girl like? Which family is she from? What's her cultivation level? How does she look? Is my Chen'er worthy of her?"

It wasn't about whether the girl was worthy or not!

It was about whether he was worthy!

Gu Tianhua thought to himself how difficult it was to find a wife these days!

And anyone who could make his son's heart flutter must be extraordinary!

Gu Xuan looked at him, speechless!

The dignified head of the Gu family, about to reach the peak of the Saint King realm!

Hearing that his son was in love, he was so excited, completely losing his composure!

"She's quite beautiful, a Taiyin Spiritual Body, more than enough for your little Chenchen!"

Gu Xuan added after speaking.

"Third on the Beauty Ranking."

Gu Tianhua was shocked!

What?!

It was a beauty ranking list! A ranking of beauties from across the entire continent! Every woman on that list was breathtakingly beautiful!

Third place... just how beautiful must she be? He started imagining it in his mind, and as he thought about it, his mouth almost watered!

Wait a minute!

That's my daughter-in-law!

What am I doing?!

Gu Tianhua immediately slapped himself!

“Ancestor, when is that girl coming to the Gu family? I need to quickly have the courtyard prepared, the betrothal gifts ready, and find someone to choose an auspicious date!”

“Stop!”

Gu Xuan raised his hand to stop him.

“The girl hasn't even agreed yet, and you're already preparing the betrothal gifts?”

Gu Tianhua chuckled, “It's only a matter of time! My Chen'er is handsome, highly skilled, has excellent swordsmanship, and is the young master of the Gu family. What girl could refuse him?”

Gu Xuan ignored him, leaned back in his recliner, and closed his eyes to rest!

Gu Tianhua was still rambling on, already planning who to invite to the wedding!

He needed to invite the Qingyun Holy Land, the Righteous Gang, all eighteen vassal forces, the surrounding villages and towns, and even the forces from Zhongzhou…

Wait, he hadn't even dealt with those forces yet!

Then he wouldn't invite them.

After talking for a while, Gu Tianhua suddenly remembered the important matter and quickly reported, “Ancestor, the cultivation progress of the vassal forces has been very smooth this past month. Resources have been supplied generously, and those people are cultivating like madmen!”

“There was an old guy stuck at the peak of the Emperor Realm who broke through to Half-Saint in just three days after entering the Marrow Cleansing Array! At this rate, in another month or two, everyone will be Saints!”

Gu Xuan nodded: “Don't be stingy with resources. Give them as much as they want, as long as they can break through.”

Anyway, he had plenty of resources; completing the mission was the priority!

Gu Tianhua sighed, "Those people are now completely devoted to the Gu family. They'd be willing to be slaves, let alone vassals!"

"Yesterday, the head of a small family came to me, saying he wanted to erect a longevity tablet for the Gu family and offer sacrifices day and night. I said it wasn't necessary, but he insisted, and I couldn't stop him!"

Gu Xuan smiled and said nothing.

With resources like that, even a pig could fly. How could they not worship?

As long as the resources are right, cultivation can increase ten thousandfold in a day!

Gu Tianhua muttered a few more words, then turned to get busy.

As he walked out of the back mountain, he was still muttering to himself:

Taiyin Spiritual Body, third on the Beauty Ranking!

Just how beautiful must this girl be?

He took a few more steps and stopped!

"No, I have to go and prepare. If the girl comes, I can't let her stay in a shabby place!"

A moment later, Gu Tianhua disappeared in a flash!

Being a father really makes you worry yourself sick!

The Gu family is thriving!

Meanwhile!

The Zhao family!

The Zhao family's main hall!

The Zhao family patriarch, Zhao Wuji, sat in the main seat, holding a shattered life tablet in his hands, his fingers trembling!

Zhao Ritian's life tablet was broken!

His son was dead!

That was the Zhao family's most valued genius!

The Great Sun Sacred Body, once fully developed, at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, was comparable to an Emperor!

He was destined to lead the Zhao family to an even more prosperous future!

Now, all of that had vanished like bubbles!

Zhao Wuji was a Quasi-Emperor level three expert, having lived for tens of thousands of years, he had seen all kinds of storms!

But at this moment, looking at the pile of fragments in his hands, his eyes were bloodshot, and his entire body radiated a terrifying killing intent!

"Who did it?"

An elder below spoke up, "Patriarch, the young master went to Tianyuan Prefecture on a spirit ship to participate in the Genius Battle. He was accompanied by his protector, Uncle Zhao, a peak Eternal Realm cultivator, and numerous followers. With such a entourage, he should be able to roam freely in Zhongzhou. How could this happen…?"

Zhao Wuji suddenly looked up, "You mean the opponent was stronger than a peak Eternal Realm cultivator?"

The elder dared not speak.

A peak Eternal Realm cultivator was already top-tier combat power in the Zhao family, second only to the two Quasi-Emperor elders!

The person who could kill Uncle Zhao was at least a Quasi-Emperor!

But there were quite a few Quasi-Emperor experts in Zhongzhou. Who would dare to touch a member of the Zhao family?

Everyone knew that Zhao Ritian was Zhao Wuji's son; to touch him was to break ties with the Zhao family!

Most imperial clans only had one Quasi-Emperor, but the Zhao family had three!

Even if they encountered a Quasi-Emperor, seeing it was Zhao Ritian, they wouldn't act rashly!

Zhao Wuji stood up, his aura as a Quasi-Emperor of the Third Heaven erupting forth, causing the entire hall to tremble!

"Investigate! Find out who killed my son! No matter who he is, no matter who stands behind him, I will kill him!"

"Yes!"

Half a day later, news returned.

At the spirit ship dock in Tianyuan Prefecture, someone witnessed Zhao Ritian clashing with several young men.

The other party claimed to be from the Gu family, a clan from the Eastern Region.

After the spirit ship reached the border of Zhongzhou, Zhao Ritian led his men in pursuit, and then never returned!

Zhao Wuji looked at the intelligence report in his hand, furious: "The Eastern Region? A small clan from the Eastern Region dares to touch my Zhao family!"

Zhao Wuji sneered: "Send word to summon the two Quasi-Emperor elders to accompany me to Zhongzhou! I want to see just what this Gu family is capable of!"

"Patriarch, the Genius Battle is imminent, and forces from all sides of Central Continent are gathering. To start a large-scale war at this time is probably unwise."

"Please, Patriarch, take care of yourself! Take care of yourself!"

Zhao Wuji roared angrily, "Easier said than done! You didn't lose your son!"

"Guards, kill his son! Let him accompany the young master on his journey to the Yellow Springs!"

The man collapsed to the ground!

How could he have been so talkative!

The news quickly spread throughout the Zhao family!

The major forces of Central Continent were all discussing Zhao Ritian's death, killed by a family called the Gu family!

Who was the Gu family?

A small, unknown family from the Eastern Region!

But to kill a peak Eternal Realm protector, could they be an ordinary family?

The name Gu family slowly began to appear in the sights of the major forces of Central Continent!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 62 A Battle of Geniuses Among Billions of Cultivators! Auction
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Meanwhile, the group was flying towards Tianque City!

Gu Chen's face hadn't returned to normal the entire way!

He secretly glanced at her several times, his heart racing each time!

It was the feeling of being smitten!

Tianque City was now close at hand, its city walls soaring into the clouds!

Two city guards stood at the city gate, actually Saint Kings—at least regional overlords in the Eastern Region, yet here they were only guarding the gate!

"Zhongzhou is indeed different!" Li Shuangxu exclaimed.

The group arrived at the registration area, a sea of ​​people stretching as far as the eye could see!

Gu Chen stood in the middle of the line, absent-minded, his gaze constantly drifting towards Ru Shuang.

Gu Qingshan, watching from behind, shook his head.

"If you keep staring like that, your eyeballs will pop out!" Gu Qingshan whispered.

Gu Chen finally snapped out of his reverie, pretending not to be looking.

At the testing platform, one young man after another stepped forward to have their cultivation level and age tested.

The Emperor Realm was the threshold; those who passed received a token, while those who failed left dejectedly.

The old man in charge of the tests was expressionless, announcing numbers like chanting: "Emperor Realm, Second Heaven, 29 years old, passed."

"Emperor Realm, First Heaven, 42 years old, two years short of passing."

"Emperor Realm, Third Heaven, 37 years old, passed."

...

After waiting for a long time...

It was Gu Chen's turn. He stepped onto the testing platform and placed his hand on the crystal ball.

The crystal ball lit up, and the old man looked up. "Emperor Realm, First Heaven, 21 years old, passed."

His tone was fair, and he even gave Gu Chen a second look.

A twenty-one-year-old Emperor Realm expert—in the Central Continent, that was considered monstrous talent!

Gu Qingshan continued the test: "Emperor Realm, Third Heaven, 24 years old, passed."

Ru Shuang stepped forward, attracting the attention of many around her!

A light flashed, and the old man glanced at her, unusually adding a few words: "Second level of the Emperor Realm, twenty-one years old, Taiyin Spiritual Body, not bad."

Whispers arose from the crowd!

"Taiyin Spiritual Body? No wonder she's so beautiful."

"Isn't that Ru Shuang, third on the Beauty Ranking?"

"That's her!"

"I heard her father is going to marry her off to a ten-thousand-year-old Quasi-Emperor. She probably escaped!"

"Really? A ten-thousand-year-old Quasi-Emperor, how old must he be?"

Gu Chen frowned slightly upon hearing these discussions.

A ten-thousand-year-old Quasi-Emperor?

Married to an old man?

His hand trembled involuntarily!

As for Li Shuangxu, his realm was stuck at the peak of the King Realm, just one step away from the Emperor Realm, but he wasn't disappointed.

He was just here to be a bystander and lead the way.

He couldn't even defeat the monstrous geniuses of the Eastern Region, let alone the monstrous geniuses of the Central State!

He didn't want to suffer, otherwise it would damage his Dao heart!

This will be detrimental to your future cultivation!

The group received their tokens and left the registration point.

The plaza was still packed with people, filled with young cultivators, gathered in small groups, discussing amongst themselves.

Li Shuangxu held a competition schedule booklet in his hand, reading it as he walked, his expression growing increasingly grave!

“Young Master Gu, this elimination round is a bit brutal!”

Li Shuangxu handed him the booklet.

“The first round of elimination will directly eliminate 99% of the participants! There are hundreds of millions of cultivators participating, divided into countless battlefields, each with 10,000 participants, leaving only one winner!”

Gu Qingshan gasped: “Only one out of 10,000? Isn’t that just a free-for-all?”

"Yes! Those who are killed will be teleported out; they won't truly die, but their spot will be gone!"

Li Shuangxu turned to the next page. "The second round, the third round, elimination rounds upon elimination, until only a thousand people enter the main competition! Those who can break into the top thousand among billions of geniuses are monsters among monsters!"

Gu Chen was also shocked to hear about billions of cultivators; so many people were participating.

These people had all reached the Emperor Realm before the age of 40!

In their respective regions, they would be considered geniuses among geniuses, but here, it's merely the threshold!

The first round of elimination was unexpectedly fierce!

Ru Shuang also felt immense pressure, but she was determined to win the reward in this genius battle and then help her grandfather rebuild his physical body!

To completely break free from her father's control and avoid marrying that eternal monster!

Suddenly, Ru Shuang heard her grandfather's voice in her mind, weaker than yesterday.

“Girl, when we entered the city, I saw an auction in the east. I sensed the aura of Soul-Nourishing Wood there. If I can get it, my soul can last for a few more months.”

Ru Shuang’s heart tightened. Her grandfather probably wouldn’t last much longer.

Ru Shuang silently calculated. She hadn’t taken many spirit stones when she fled the family, but it should be more than enough to buy a piece of Soul-Nourishing Wood. She just hoped nothing unexpected would happen at the auction.

As she was thinking, Gu Chen’s voice came from beside her, “Miss Ru, where are you going next?”

Ru Shuang turned to look at him, “I heard there’s an auction in the east tonight. I’d like to go see it. Would Young Master Gu be interested?”

It was as if she were inviting Gu Chen.

Gu Chen couldn’t resist!

His heart pounded, and his voice almost trembled, “Yes! We’re free anyway, let’s go see it together!”

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu exchanged a glance behind them and sighed simultaneously!

Gu Qingshan muttered under his breath, "He's completely smitten!"

Li Shuangxu nodded slightly, a hint of helplessness in his voice, "He hasn't been normal since he started blushing!"

But that girl was indeed beautiful; they would have fallen for her too!

However, they wouldn't have a chance!

The group continued walking towards the east of the city.

Gu Chen walked beside Ru Shuang, pretending to be nonchalant.

He secretly planned that if Ru Shuang took a fancy to anything at the auction, he would definitely help her bid for it!

After all, the ancestor had given him plenty of spirit stones, and the ancestor had personally instructed him to spend them as needed!

He should be able to find a girl, right?

Anyway, he had more spirit stones than he could ever use!

For Gu Chen, the most important thing right now wasn't the elimination rounds, but the auction that evening!

East of the city, Tianque Auction House.

Gu Chen and his companions reached the entrance, where a waiter greeted them. "Gentlemen, are you attending tonight's auction? The entrance fee is one thousand high-grade spirit stones per person!"

One thousand high-grade spirit stones was essentially the annual cultivation expense for a rogue cultivator at the Emperor Realm!

But Gu Chen didn't even blink, pulling out a handful of spirit stones and handing them over before entering.

The hall was already quite full, with groups of three or five people whispering amongst themselves.

Gu Chen glanced around, not heading towards the main hall, but going straight to the front desk and knocking on the counter!

"Do you have any VIP boxes?"

The old man behind the counter looked up, sizing up Gu Chen.

VIP boxes were reserved for imperial families and longevity clans; ordinary forces wouldn't even dare to ask!

But judging from the young man's confident demeanor, it didn't seem like he was faking it!

The old man smiled broadly, "Yes, yes, yes, what a coincidence! Just the last room left! But the price is a bit high!"

He held up five fingers!

"Five million high-grade spirit stones!"

Li Shuangxu gasped!

Five million high-grade spirit stones for just one room?

But Gu Chen didn't even blink, taking out spirit stones from his storage bag and placing them on the counter.

This was just the tip of the iceberg in his storage bag!

Let alone five million, he could easily pull out five hundred million from his storage bag!

This was just high-grade spirit stones; the storage bag Gu Xuan gave him contained countless top-grade spirit stones!

Gu Chen didn't even care about this amount of money!

The old man took the storage bag, checked the contents, and smiled broadly. He then bowed and scraped, "Honored guest, this way please. I'll personally escort you upstairs!"

Ru Shuang stood behind Gu Chen, watching his retreating figure, a strange light flashing in her eyes.

The money he spent on a private room was already an unattainable fortune for her!

Men who spend money are indeed very handsome!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 63 Showcasing Wealth, Leaving No One Unpunished
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

As evening fell, the auction commenced as scheduled.

The Heavenly Auction House was brightly lit and bustling with activity.

The hall was packed, and even the aisles were overflowing with people!

The lights were all on in the top-tier private boxes on the third floor, their curtains protected by special shielding arrays, making it impossible to discern the identities of those inside.

Those who could sit in the top-tier boxes were either from imperial families or long-established clans, or at the very least, descendants of reclusive powerful figures!

In the third-tier box, Ru Shuang sat next to Gu Chen, her gaze fixed on the auction stage below through the crystal window!

Li Shuangxu was still lamenting the five million spirit stones he had spent, muttering, "Five million high-grade spirit stones just to rent a room, this auction house is making a killing!"

"It's nothing," Gu Chen said casually, picking up his teacup.

After all, it really was nothing!

Besides, it was worth it for picking up girls!

The patriarch certainly wouldn't say a word to him if he found out!

Li Shuangxu's lips twitched!

Five million high-grade spirit stones was a pittance?

He didn't know the true depth of the Gu family's wealth, but judging from Gu Chen's nonchalant expression, it was probably just a drop in the ocean!

Envious of a day in the life of the rich!

Beside Gu Chen, Ru Shuang felt a surge of emotion she had never experienced before!

This man was unlike any other scion of a noble family she had ever met!

He spent money without batting an eye, not to show off, but with an innate confidence!

It was as if these spirit stones were truly just numbers to him!

For Gu Chen, it was indeed just a number!

He'd never earned spirit stones, but spending them was certainly enjoyable!

Suddenly, his grandfather's voice rang in his mind, carrying a hint of amusement, "Girl, this lad is good! Rich, powerful, and well-connected! A good choice!"

Ru Shuang's face flushed instantly. "Grandpa! What are you saying!"

Grandpa smiled, saying nothing more, quietly waiting for the auction to begin.

Downstairs, a white-haired elder walked onto the auction stage, exuding an extraordinary air!

His voice resonated throughout the hall, "Welcome everyone to the Heavenly Palace Auction House! As usual, the highest bidder wins! Tonight's auction items are all exquisite, with the grand finale being a treasure rarely seen even once in ten thousand years!

"Ladies and gentlemen, prepare your spirit stones!"

As soon as the white-haired elder finished speaking, the atmosphere in the hall ignited!

All eyes were focused on the stage, anticipating the debut of the first item!

"First item up for auction: the Flowing Cloud Sword, a Saint King level weapon, custom-made for a woman! Its blade is light and graceful, its spiritual energy flowing like clouds and water. Starting bid: 800,000 high-grade spirit stones!"

A waiter carried a silver-white longsword onto the stage. A faint light shimmered on its surface; it was clearly no ordinary weapon!

In the third VIP box, Ru Shuang's gaze was drawn to the sword. She leaned forward slightly, a hint of surprise flashing in her eyes!

She hadn't expected the first item to be such a suitable Saint King weapon for a woman, and a sword at that!

Her fingers twitched slightly, as if she were thinking.

Gu Chen noticed her slight movement and immediately raised his paddle, "One million."

Li Shuangxu was taken aback. "Young Master Gu, why are you buying a sword? Don't you already have one?"

"To give as a gift," Gu Chen said casually.

Ru Shuang turned her head, looking at him with some surprise: "Young Master Gu, I…"

"A small amount!"

Gu Chen interrupted her, his tone casual.

Someone in the hall placed a bid.

"One million one hundred thousand."

"One million five hundred thousand."

Gu Chen raised his paddle again!

The other party fell silent!

While Saint King weapons were rare, one million five hundred thousand was already a significant premium; it wasn't worth going head-to-head with someone from a top-tier VIP box!

The auctioneer brought down the gavel, and the Flowing Cloud Sword went to Gu Chen!

Ru Shuang opened her mouth, wanting to say something, but ultimately remained silent.

She was actually only surprised by the sword's quality; she didn't actually want it.

But Gu Chen had already bought it, saying it was a gift.

Gift to whom?

The answer was self-evident!

Ru Shuang's face flushed slightly, but she didn't speak!

Gu Chen hadn't originally planned to bid on the next few items.

But every time Ru Shuang showed the slightest reaction—a slight lean forward, a gentle twitch of her fingers, or a glint of light in her eyes—he would immediately raise his paddle!

The third item, a ten-thousand-year-old snow lotus, elicited a soft "Eh!" from Ru Shuang. Gu Chen bid two million and won it!

The fifth item, a scroll of ice-attribute cultivation techniques, caught Ru Shuang's eye for a moment. Gu Chen bid three million and won it!

The seventh item, an ice silkworm armor, made Ru Shuang subconsciously touch the hem of her garment. Gu Chen bid five million and won it!

In total, Gu Chen had spent over twenty million high-grade spirit stones!

Li Shuangxu and Gu Qingshan sat in the back, their initial shock turning to numbness, and now to confusion!

They exchanged a glance and sighed simultaneously!

"Why is Young Master bidding on these things?" Gu Qingshan asked softly.

Li Shuangxu thought for a moment: "Wasteful! Probably giving them away?"

He glanced at Ru Shuang, then at Gu Chen.

"But that girl didn't seem to say she wanted it!"

"Did Gu Chen misunderstand something?"

"I don't know, but as long as Young Master Gu is happy! Anyway, the patriarch has money!"

In Ru Shuang's mind, her grandfather's voice rang out again, this time with deep surprise, "Girl, why is this kid bidding on everything? Did you just touch it?"

Ru Shuang helplessly said in her heart, "Grandpa, I just thought that soft armor was pretty, I took a second look! I didn't want it!"

Grandpa was silent for a while, "Then he's already bid! And he spent five million!"

Ru Shuang opened her mouth, wanting to tell Gu Chen not to bid, but every time the words were on the tip of her tongue, Gu Chen had already raised his paddle!

She didn't even have time to speak!

What was she supposed to do?

The auction continued!

The eighth item, the ninth item, the tenth item—Gu Chen was like he had entered a shopping spree mode. Every time an item was put up for auction, no matter what it was, he would raise his paddle!

He bid on pills, spiritual materials, magic weapons, even an ordinary ten-thousand-year-old Ganoderma lucidum!

The number plate for VIP box number three was barely lowered!

The people in the hall were already losing interest!

Discontent was also rising in the other VIP boxes on the third floor!

In VIP box number five, someone's face grew increasingly grim!

He had come to the auction with a refining material in mind, but Gu Chen had snatched it!

In VIP box number two, a white-haired young man sneered, "Who is this guy? Why does he steal everything?"

In VIP box number six, an aged voice said calmly, "Investigate the background of VIP box number three! There aren't many families in the entire Central Continent who dare to spend money like this!"

But these sounds were all isolated within their respective private boxes; Gu Chen couldn't hear them!

He only knew that every time Ru Shuang moved, he would bid on an item!

Ru Shuang finally couldn't hold back any longer and said softly, "Young Master Gu, you don't need to bid on every single item, do you?"

"It's just small change."

Gu Chen turned to look at her and smiled.

Ru Shuang was speechless!

Looking at Gu Chen's profile, an indescribable feeling welled up inside her.

The way this man spends money is really handsome!

Men who spend money are the most handsome!

By the latter half of the auction, Gu Chen had already spent nearly one hundred million high-grade spirit stones!

Li Shuangxu silently did the math in his mind, then decided not to bother!

Anyway, the Gu family's money had nothing to do with him!

Ru Shuang's grandfather was completely silent!

He had lived for nearly ten thousand years and seen many spendthrifts, but he had never seen anyone so wasteful!

What's even more terrifying is that after this kid squandered all his money, he didn't even blink an eye, as if he'd spent stones instead of spirit stones!

Is that right?


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 64 The Soul-Nourishing Wood is Acquired, the Elimination Rounds Begin
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The auction had finally reached the moment Ru Shuang had been most eagerly anticipating!

When the Soul-Nourishing Wood was brought out, her breathing became incredibly cautious!

The starting price was three million, and each bid made her heart tremble!

When the price climbed to ten million, she was almost in despair!

The Soul-Nourishing Wood usually cost around eight or nine million; the current price was far beyond her budget.

If she couldn't obtain the Soul-Nourishing Wood, her grandfather's soul probably wouldn't last much longer, not even until the end of the Genius Battle!

"Twenty million."

Until Gu Chen raised his paddle, finally acquiring the Soul-Nourishing Wood for twenty million!

Ru Shuang's lips moved, her eyes slightly red: "Young Master Gu, this is too precious, I…"

"A small amount."

Gu Chen interrupted her, smiling, "Besides, this isn't for you, it's for the soul within you."

What?

He actually knew about her grandfather!

Ru Shuang was stunned!

In her mind, her grandfather's voice froze, "How did he know I existed?"

Gu Chen didn't explain, but simply turned to look at the auction stage.

Ru Shuang's grandfather remained silent for a long time, countless thoughts churning in his mind.

He remembered the three protectors who emerged from the void, remembered their unfathomable aura!

Perhaps back then, those three had already seen through his existence!

Even some quasi-Emperor level experts couldn't see through his disguise, yet now his identity had been discovered!

It seemed they simply didn't care, or rather, disdained it!

A remnant soul couldn't cause any trouble, naturally not worth a second glance!

"No wonder!"

Ru Shuang's grandfather's voice was somewhat hoarse.

"No wonder he wasn't surprised, nor did he question it! It wasn't that he didn't know, it was that he simply didn't care!"

Ru Shuang bit her lip, looked at Gu Chen's profile, and softly said, "Young Master Gu, I will definitely return the spirit stones for the Soul-Nourishing Wood, but it might be a little later."

A little later was hard to say!

Gu Chen turned his head. "No need, it's nothing serious. Consider it a gift for our first meeting."

Ru Shuang was about to say something when her grandfather's voice suddenly rose in her mind, "Girl, listen to your grandfather, you don't need to return these spirit stones!"

Ru Shuang was stunned, wondering why.

This wasn't just two thousand spirit stones!

That was twenty million spirit stones! And top-grade ones at that!

Her grandfather's tone became meaningful. "This kid is incredibly generous, throwing twenty million spirit stones around without batting an eye. That means he's not short of money at all!"

"And his three protectors, have you forgotten? They could see through my presence but remained calm, which shows they have the backing!"

Wealth can buy anything, power can intimidate!

"The Gu family's resources are probably deeper than we thought! If your father's men come after you, you can go hide with them for a while; they're a nice guy! You can also cultivate a relationship!"

Ru Shuang's face flushed crimson. She thought to herself, both anxious and embarrassed, "Grandpa! What are you saying!"

Who would spend twenty million spirit stones as a gift?!

He sold himself for twenty million spirit stones? What is Grandpa thinking?!

How embarrassing!

Grandpa smiled but didn't say anything more, though his meaning was clear!

However, Ru Shuang's grandfather was somewhat worried.

This kid spends money like crazy! He's bought so much stuff, it's already made a lot of people envious.

After the auction, someone might cause trouble. I wonder if her protector can handle it?

After all, Central Continent is full of hidden talents, and there are countless Emperor-level experts.

Seeing Ru Shuang suddenly blush, Gu Chen assumed she was still upset about the spirit stones. "Really, you don't need to pay it back. If you really feel bad, just treat me to a meal after the Genius Battle."

Ru Shuang looked up at him and nodded gently: "Okay."

Gu Chen didn't bid again in the subsequent auctions.

The competition in the other private rooms became fierce, with several final items fetching astronomical prices!

The auction ended quickly.

As they left, many people looked towards the No. 3 private room, some seemingly harboring ill intentions!

The group walked out of the auction house, Gu Chen walking beside her, an arm's length apart, neither speaking.

“Young Master Gu.” Ru Shuang suddenly stopped.

Gu Chen also stopped and looked at her.

“Thank you!”

Ru Shuang’s voice was soft, yet earnest. “Thank you, not only for the Soul-Nourishing Wood, but also for saving me before.”

Gu Chen’s face flushed slightly. “No need to thank me. The elimination rounds start in a few days. Take care of yourself!”

Ru Shuang looked at his profile. “You too.”

The few days of rest passed in the blink of an eye!

The Genius Battle elimination rounds had finally arrived!

Outside Tianque City, a vast, dark mass of people stretched as far as the eye could see!

Millions of cultivators, under forty years old and above the Emperor Realm, gathered here!

Each person held a token, marked with their respective battlefield number!

Millions of cultivators, 99% to be eliminated in the first round!

Each battlefield had 10,000 participants, leaving only one winner!

The elimination was fierce!

Gu Chen glanced down at his token, battlefield number sixty-two!

Gu Qingshan's token was for Battlefield Number 78!

Ru Shuang's token was for Number 98.

The three weren't together!

Li Shuangxu stood on the outer edge, without a token.

His cultivation wasn't high enough to register, making him the most relaxed person.

Ru Shuang stood not far away, her gaze fixed on Gu Chen. Her lips moved, but she said nothing.

She seemed shy or embarrassed.

The girl's naiveté was the same as the boy's!

In the sky, an aged voice echoed across the wilderness, majestic as the heavens!

"The Genius Battle, the elimination rounds, begin now!"

Tokens lit up simultaneously, and the figures of countless cultivators vanished in the light, teleported into their respective battlefields!

Gu Chen's vision blurred, and he found himself standing on a barren plain!

All around him were bare hills, and above him was a gray sky!

He glanced down at the token at his waist; the numbers on it began to change!

Each decrease in the number meant someone had been eliminated!

Flashes of light appeared around him, and cultivators were teleported in one after another!

Some drew their swords as soon as they landed, some surveyed the terrain, and some had even begun forming alliances!

Gu Chen stood still, his hand on his sword, eyes closed in meditation!

In a battlefield of ten thousand, there can only be one victor.

Just fight your way out!

The teleportation light from the Genius Battle elimination rounds gradually faded, and the crowds on the open field were now empty.

Li Shuangxu stood on the outskirts, looking at the competition schedule in his hand, then turned and walked towards the city center!

Tianji Pavilion, the largest gambling den in Tianque City!

At this moment, it was bustling with noise. A huge screen scrolled through the names of the participants and their odds. The hall was packed with people—cultivators placing bets, independent cultivators watching the spectacle, and spies from various forces!

At the very top, a line of large golden characters stood out prominently:

"Genius Battle Championship Hot Picks."

Han Zixuan, odds 1 to 3!

Bing Xin, odds 1 to 4!

Zhuge Qingyun, odds 1 to 5!

Kang Shen, odds 1 to 8!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 65 I've staked everything on my wife! Sweeping across the battlefield.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Following them was a long list of names, all descendants of renowned geniuses, imperial families, longevity clans, and reclusive powerhouses from Central Continent!

Each name was legendary!

As for the odds they'd never heard of before, they were all 500 to 1!

After all, the first round was meant to eliminate 99% of the participants. Why bet on the strong? Betting on strangers?

Obviously not!

Li Shuangxu squeezed to the counter, pulled out his storage bag, and slammed it on the table. "I'm betting on Gu Chen! He wins the first round! One million high-grade spirit stones!"

The old man behind the counter looked up, adjusted his glasses, and sized Li Shuangxu up and down. "Gu Chen? Which Gu Chen? Never heard of him."

Li Shuangxu pointed to the bottom. "That's Gu Chen."

The old man looked at him, then at Li Shuangxu, his lips twitching. "Young man, are you sure? Gu Chen's odds are high, but in the first round of elimination, only one out of ten thousand will survive!"

"An unknown contestant like that will most likely be eliminated in the first round. You'd be better off betting your spirit stones on Han Zixuan or Bing Xin; at least you'd earn some pocket money!"
