"I'm betting on Gu Chen." Li Shuangxu didn't hesitate.

That's their young master! Who else should they bet on?

A young man in brocade robes leaned over, glanced at Li Shuangxu's betting slip, and scoffed, "Bet on some nobody? Are you out of your mind? Look at the odds, 500 to 1, obviously just filling a spot! Who would bet on someone like that?"

His voice wasn't quiet, and several people around looked over.

Some laughed, some shook their heads, and some looked at Li Shuangxu like he was an idiot!

Betting on a nobody to win was simply impossible!

This guy must be here to do charity!

He's practically throwing money away!

"Exactly, with spirit stones, you should bet on Han Zixuan and Bing Xin, it's a sure win!"

"Maybe he's just here to give away spirit stones, who cares?"

"Five hundred to one? That's outrageous! I'd eat shit if that guy could win!"

Li Shuangxu ignored the gossip, put away his betting slip, and turned to leave the Heavenly Mystery Pavilion.

Behind him, the laughter of the young man in brocade robes still echoed, "There are always fools, but this year there seem to be more than usual!"

"Stop talking and drink! I think he's just obsessed with money!"

Li Shuangxu walked down the street, a slight smile playing on his lips.

Fools?

Heh!

Wait until the elimination rounds are over, then you'll see who the fools are!

Our young master can even shatter a quasi-Emperor weapon with a single sword strike. A battlefield of ten thousand?

It's questionable whether that's even enough for our young master to warm up!

He glanced up at the hazy teleportation area on the horizon, silently pleading, "Young Master, don't scare me!"

I've staked my entire savings on this!

I need to put in the effort!

Otherwise, a million high-grade spirit stones will be wasted!

Meanwhile, Gu Qingshan nearly tripped and fell flat on his face when he landed.

He'd been eating too well these past few days; he needed to get some exercise!

He steadied himself and surveyed the surrounding desolate mountains, rocks, and overgrown weeds.

The sounds of fighting were already approaching—screams, clashing weapons, and explosions of spiritual energy all blended together!

The thought of surviving only one out of ten thousand was daunting!

Gu Qingshan swallowed hard and gripped his battle sword tightly!

This sword was a gift from his ancestor; he didn't know its level, but it was of a high grade, so it should be decent!

He'd never had a chance to use it, and today he could finally try!

"Boy, hand over the token!"

Suddenly, a tall, thin man sprang out from behind the rocks, wielding a spear. He was a third-level Emperor Realm expert, the spear tip pointing directly at Gu Qingshan!

Gu Qingshan instinctively raised his sword to block!

Clang!

The spear snapped in two. The tall, thin man froze, staring at the broken spear shaft in his hand, his face turning deathly pale!

What the hell?

How could a single touch destroy my spear?

Gu Qingshan was also stunned, looking at his battle sword. There wasn't even a mark on the blade!

The ancestor gave him; it was indeed no ordinary item!

"You, your sword! How is this possible!" The tall, thin man's voice trembled.

He actually launched a sneak attack! Unforgivable!

Gu Qingshan didn't give him a chance to speak, slashing down with his sword!

The tall, thin man was teleported away.

Instantly eliminated!

Gu Qingshan looked at the battle sword in his hand, a slow smile spreading across his lips!

He might not be able to defeat Gu Chen, but he wasn't afraid of these people!

Besides, this sword cut people down like vegetables!

One slash, one kill!

Fighting! So exhilarating!

"Come on!" Gu Qingshan charged into the crowd, sword in hand, like a tiger pouncing on a flock of sheep!

One slash, one kill; two slashes, two kills!

His cultivation wasn't the highest, but his sword was superior!

Those people's prized weapons were like paper before him, shattering at the slightest touch!

"Run! Someone's charging at us with a machete!"

"Heh! I'll kill him with one sword!"

"Let him taste my spiked club!"

"Hey! Brothers, why are you being slaughtered like pigs? Are you two cosplaying as pigs?"

...

Ru Shuang's situation was far more difficult than Gu Qingshan's.

She landed in a dense forest, surrounded by towering trees, the light dim.

A man at the second level of the Emperor Realm, seeing Ru Shuang's face, his eyes turned frivolous. "Miss, all alone? Want your brother to protect you?"

Ru Shuang didn't speak, but thrust her sword forward!

Caught off guard, the man was struck in the shoulder by the sword light, screaming as he vanished in a flash of white light!

But this strike also revealed her location!

In less than half an incense stick's time, four people surrounded her.

Two men and two women, all at the third level of the Emperor Realm, worked together seamlessly, clearly a team!

The competition only stipulated that there could only be one winner, so teamwork didn't violate the rules.

"Taiyin Spiritual Body? Third on the Beauty Ranking?"

The leading woman sneered.

"What good is good looks? Here, might makes right."

The four attacked simultaneously.

Ru Shuang fought four against one. Her cultivation was no lower than her opponents, but her combat experience was too lacking, and she quickly fell into a disadvantageous position.

"Girl, the sword of that man on the left is veering off course, dodge to the right."

Ru Shuang dodged to the right. Her grandfather, a peak Eternal Realm cultivator in life, still possessed keen eyesight. Although he couldn't fight, his guidance helped Ru Shuang defuse many crises.

However, Ru Shuang still managed to defeat these individuals thanks to her own strength and her grandfather's help.

She managed to stabilize herself on this battlefield!

On the other side, Gu Chen was quite relaxed, killing dozens of people with a single sword strike!

This attracted many people who came in droves, thinking they needed to drive away such powerful individuals first!

A group of ranged cultivators attacked from afar, bombarding her with various techniques, spiritual treasures, and hidden weapons!

Gu Chen didn't even look, unleashing a single sword strike that shattered the attacks in mid-air with invisible sword energy!

"His sword is strange! Don't get close to him!" someone shouted!

Gu Chen immediately stepped forward and killed the attacker. Everyone knows I'm strong, but nobody wants to fight me!

This won't do!

These people are too weak; they can't even be considered opponents!

Gu Chen continued searching for opponents worthy of his attention.

An hour later, the number on Gu Chen's token jumped to over three thousand, and he alone had killed more than a thousand!

Some people were devastated, wondering why he didn't seem to get tired!

He'd killed over a thousand people; it seemed like his spiritual energy was inexhaustible!

Another hour later, the number jumped to six thousand.

Of the ten thousand people on the battlefield, nearly three thousand were eliminated by Gu Chen alone!

The remaining people either cowered in corners, trembling, or fought amongst themselves for second place!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 66 The Imperial Clan is nothing special; that sword is an ultimate imperial weapon!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Finally, someone couldn't hold back any longer!

A young man clad in black battle armor stepped out from the crowd, wielding a spear—a peak fourth-level Emperor Realm expert!

Behind him were a dozen or so followers, all at least third-level Emperor Realm experts!

"You're interesting!" the man said coldly, looking at him.

"The Imperial Clan, Nangong Family, Nangong Lie."

The man revealed his family name, letting Gu Chen know who he had lost to!

Gu Chen's eyes finally lit up when he heard the words "Imperial Clan"!

Ha!

So that's why there wasn't a decent opponent!

Turns out, all that fighting was against weaklings!

This broken sword is practically rusty!

It's time to consecrate it!

Gu Chen sized up Nangong Lie and nodded.

Nangong Lie was the first to move, his spear held horizontally, the pressure of a quasi-Emperor weapon erupting forth!

His dozen or so followers stepped forward, surrounding Gu Chen!

"You think I'm one of those trash?"

Nangong Lie thrust out his spear, a black dragon phantom materializing on it as it roared.

"Black Dragon Break!"

Gu Chen swiftly drew his sword.

The Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword let out a dragon's roar!

The sword light collided with the black dragon, cleaving it in two!

Nangong Lie felt his fingers go numb, the spear nearly slipping from his grasp!

His expression changed, before he could even react.

Gu Chen's second sword strike arrived.

Nangong Lie quickly parried, forced back three steps by the impact!

His followers behind him paled and immediately attacked.

A dozen attacks rained down on Gu Chen!

Damn it!

What was supposed to be a one-on-one duel turned into a group attack!

But Nangong Lie hadn't exactly said he wanted a one-on-one fight!

Gu Chen hurriedly unleashed three sword strikes!

The three swords swept out, their light spreading, tearing apart the dozen attacks in mid-air!

Three people were eliminated on the spot!

Nangong Lie was shocked. He thought he was the hunter, but now he realized the prey was never the person before him!

He gritted his teeth, channeling all his spiritual power into the spear, and desperately tried to block!

With a clang!

The spear shattered. A quasi-Emperor level spear couldn't withstand the Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword, breaking into more than a dozen pieces scattered on the ground!

Nangong Lie held the broken spear, stunned!

That was a quasi-Emperor level weapon!

How could it be broken!

"You! Who exactly are you?" Nangong Lie felt incredulous!

Gu Chen's tone was calm, "The Gu family, Gu Chen."

The Gu family?

Could it be the Gu family that killed Zhao Ritian?

The Zhao family, the Gu family that offered a bounty for Gu Chen's head with a quasi-Emperor level weapon?

He hadn't expected that the person the Zhao family was looking for was right in front of him.

"So it was you!"

Gu Chen didn't give him a chance to speak, striking with his sword!

The genius of the Nangong family, a peak fourth-level Emperor Realm cultivator, was eliminated!

"The Imperial Clan is nothing special! How come they couldn't withstand my sword?"

It's just a broken sword, isn't it?

Is it that powerful?

The remaining followers turned to run, but Gu Chen didn't let them escape.

Two swings of his sword, and they were all wiped out!

He wouldn't let them escape, lest he have to search for them again!

It's a waste of time!

It's time to clear the field!

Half an hour later, on Battlefield 62, only Gu Chen remained!

A voice rang out in the sky, "Battlefield 62, the winner is Gu Chen."

The Heavenly Mystery Pavilion!

Gu Chen's name was updated again!

The entire hall erupted in cheers!

"Gu Chen? That Gu Chen with the 1 to 500 odds?"

"Holy crap! That guy really won? He's struck it rich!"

"I'm so damn stupid! Why didn't I bet on him?!"

"Five hundred times the odds! How much did he bet?"

"One million! And it's high-grade spirit stones!"

"What? That's five hundred million?!"

The old man behind the counter was trembling!

One to five hundred!

Someone even bet a million!

In its five thousand years of operation, Tianji Pavilion has never seen such a huge bet!

Five hundred million!

How many years would it take to earn that back?!

The young man stood in the crowd, his face turning from white to ashen!

The person next to him patted him on the shoulder and whispered, "Wasn't it you who said you'd eat shit if he won?"

The young man turned and ran, faster than a rabbit!

Eat shit?

I'd never eat shit in my life!

Li Shuangxu sat in the corner, staring at the name on the screen!

He was completely speechless!

Five hundred million!

Five hundred million!

He rushed to the counter with his betting slip, slamming it on the table, "Claim your prize!"

The old man, his hands trembling, paid him the spirit stones!

Li Shuangxu walked out of the Heavenly Mystery Pavilion with his storage bag, ecstatic!

Five hundred million high-grade spirit stones—enough for a hundred wives, let alone a dowry!

The winners of Battlefield 78 were also announced: Gu Qingshan.

Battlefield 98: Ru Shuang.

All three names advanced!

The teleportation area flashed!

Gu Chen, Gu Qingshan, and Ru Shuang appeared almost simultaneously in the wilderness!

Meanwhile, outside the teleportation array of Battlefield 62!

Nangong Lie walked out with a grim face!

He had prepared for so long, even bringing a quasi-Emperor artifact, only to be eliminated by an unknown nobody!

What infuriated him most was that the seemingly rusty, broken sword had easily shattered his quasi-Emperor level spear!

This was definitely not normal!

“Third Uncle!”

Nangong Lie turned to the gray-robed elder beside him and said.

“That kid’s sword is problematic! I request the family elders to come and take a look!”

The gray-robed elder was an outer sect elder of the Nangong family, a peak Saint King!

He frowned.

“What good sword could a junior at the Emperor level have?”

Nangong Lie gritted his teeth, “My spear is a quasi-Emperor level weapon, and he shattered it with a single strike! Do you think that’s normal?”

The gray-robed elder’s expression changed!

A quasi-Emperor level weapon being shattered with a single strike meant either the opponent’s sword was far superior to a quasi-Emperor level weapon, or the opponent’s cultivation was far superior to an Emperor level weapon!

But Gu Chen’s cultivation couldn’t be faked; he was clearly at the first level of the Emperor level!

Then there was only one possibility: the sword was problematic!

"I'll immediately send a message to the patriarch!"

The gray-robed elder took out a jade talisman, infused it with spiritual power, and sent the message back to the Nangong family!

Less than half an incense stick's time later, a crack appeared in the void!

An elder dressed in a purple Daoist robe emerged from the crack—a Quasi-Emperor!

Moreover, it was the Nangong family's Great Elder, Nangong Yuan!

"Where is the sword?" Nangong Yuan asked bluntly.

Nangong Lie pointed in the direction Gu Chen had disappeared, "That's the young man, his name is Gu Chen. His sword shattered my Quasi-Emperor weapon with a single strike!"

Nangong Yuan didn't ask any further questions, chasing after the sword and taking a look from afar!

Just one look!

Nangong Yuan felt a jolt run through his body!

The composure on his face vanished instantly, replaced by unbelievable shock!

"It's an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon!"

His voice trembled!

"And it's an Emperor's natal weapon, of extremely high grade, far surpassing ordinary Emperor weapons!"

Nangong Lie and the gray-robed elder simultaneously gasped!

An Ultimate Emperor weapon—that's a weapon used by Emperors; there are only a handful in the entire Central Continent!

Each one is enough to drive any imperial clan mad!

And that young man actually carries such a treasure with him, and even uses it openly in the elimination rounds!

"Elder, we..." Greed flashed in Nangong Lie's eyes.

Nangong Yuan raised a hand to stop him, "No rush! This boy dares to openly use an Ultimate Emperor weapon; he's either ignorant or fearless. There might be a powerful figure behind him!"

"Then find out his origins. As for that sword, since it's appeared in the Central Continent, don't think you can take it away easily!" Nangong Lie sneered.

Gu Chen, you wait!

A common man is innocent, but possessing a treasure is a crime!

The allure of an Ultimate Emperor weapon is enough to make all the imperial clans in the Central Continent restless!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 67: All sides covet it, but none are enough to fight it.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

By the time Gu Chen and his companions emerged from the teleportation array, it was nearly dark.

The plaza was less crowded than during the day, now filled with victors from various battlefields, each bearing the marks of battle.

Gu Qingshan, carrying his battle sword, walked with a swagger.

That was the feeling!

"That battlefield was so much fun! One slash, one kill, like cutting melons and vegetables!"

Gu Chen chuckled. "Brother Qingshan, I told you not to stay in the back mountains all the time, now you know better."

"That's different! The Ancestor had it easy!"

Gu Chen hadn't expected his brother Qingshan, who used to always be at the forefront, to become so laid-back!

He wondered what benefits the Ancestor had given him.

To make him like this, but he didn't care. After all, he was the young master, and everyone had to listen to him from now on!

Ru Shuang walked at the very back, her steps unsteady.

Her battlefield had been the most difficult, and she had only managed to survive thanks to her grandfather's mental guidance.

Her white robes were covered in dust, and her hair was somewhat disheveled.

But her face was still so beautiful that it was impossible to look away.

Gu Chen turned to look at her. "Are you injured?"

Ru Shuang shook her head. "No, just a little tired."

Gu Chen took out a pill from his storage bag and handed it to her. "Take this pill and you'll recover quickly."

Ru Shuang took the pill, glanced at it, and her hand trembled!

"This is?!"

An Emperor-rank healing pill!

Her grandfather felt a chill run down his spine when he saw it!

How come this kid has everything?!

That's an Emperor-rank healing pill, and he doesn't seem to care at all. What's going on with this?

Could it be that his family specializes in alchemy?

No wonder he's so rich, no wonder he's so generous!

"Take this pill and sleep it off."

Ru Shuang opened her mouth, wanting to say something, but ultimately didn't.

She swallowed the pill, and a warm spiritual energy spread from her stomach!

A wave of energy instantly washed over her, sweeping away all her fatigue!

She looked at Gu Chen, the inexplicable feeling in her heart intensifying.

Just how many more treasures did this man possess?

…

Tianque City, Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

Gu Chen had booked the top floor; opening the window revealed half the city.

Ru Shuang's complexion had improved considerably after taking the pill. Sitting by the window, her gaze unconsciously drifted towards Gu Chen.

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu were in the lobby downstairs, inquiring about the Genius Battle main event from the waiter.

Everything seemed peaceful.

But outside the city, things were anything but peaceful!

The three Quasi-Emperors of the Zhao family had arrived!

Zhao Wuji personally led the team, followed by two white-haired elders. The three formed a triangular formation, guarding a valley thirty miles outside the city!

A spatial sealing array had been set up; within a radius of a hundred miles, not even a fly could escape!

"Patriarch, that brat is at the Drunken Immortal Pavilion now." Zhao Kun returned from the city and knelt on one knee.

Zhao Wuji opened his eyes. "Keep a close eye on him. The moment he leaves the city will be his death!"

"Yes."

Zhao Kun withdrew.

In the valley, Zhao Wuji closed his eyes to rest, waiting for the best opportunity.

He didn't act immediately, as fighting within the city would inevitably harm innocent people.

Many descendants of imperial families lived within the city. If innocent people were harmed, those forces would likely pursue the Zhao family relentlessly.

Facing numerous powerful enemies was certainly not a good thing, especially since the Genius Battle was being held outside the city; he couldn't stay hidden inside forever.

Zhao Wuji's aura, faintly emanating from the third level of the Quasi-Emperor realm, seemed to solidify the surrounding air.

The two elders behind him remained silent, also waiting.

Waiting for their prey to be caught!

Once outside the city, the three would lock onto their target and ambush him!

With three Quasi-Emperors attacking simultaneously, even with his protectors, he wouldn't have time to save himself!

Across from the Drunken Immortal Pavilion, on the second floor of a teahouse!

Several ordinary-looking middle-aged men sat by the window, their gazes occasionally sweeping towards the entrance of the Drunken Immortal Pavilion!

They were spies from the Nangong family!

Nangong Lie sat in a corner, holding a jade talisman, his expression grim.

"The Grand Elder said, we won't act for now," a gray-robed elder beside him said in a low voice.

Nangong Lie was puzzled. "The Zhao family is already preparing to act, why aren't we? Once that brat dies at their hands, the Supreme Emperor Weapon will be theirs!"

"What's the rush?"

The gray-robed elder narrowed his eyes.

"The Zhao family has three Quasi-Emperors. Do you think that brat can leave the city alive? Even if he dies, the Supreme Emperor Weapon won't just disappear into thin air. We can just seize it then!"

"But!"

"No buts!" the gray-robed elder interrupted him. "Just keep an eye on it, don't alert them!"

Nangong Lie remained silent.

He was willing!

But he dared not disobey the Grand Elder's orders!

After all, the three Quasi-Emperors of the Zhao family were not to be trifled with. If he rashly attacked, forget about seizing it!

The two families might even start fighting!

It wasn't just the Nangong family.

In the inn across from the teahouse, several other groups were sitting!

All eyes were fixed in the same direction.

Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

That young man, at the first level of the Emperor Realm, was currently the hottest target in the entire Heavenly Palace City.

Some wanted his life, some wanted his money, and some wanted his sword.

After all, he had made such a splash at the auction; many people saw him as easy prey!

"That kid is a fat sheep waiting to be slaughtered," a middle-aged man laughed in the inn.

"Tsk tsk, this kind of entourage, even imperial clans don't have this much!"

A person next to him chimed in, "Who says otherwise? A country bumpkin from the Eastern Region, thinking he's something special just because he has a few stinking coins. Now he knows, right? In the Central Continent, money without strength is just a death sentence!"

"It's a pity about that girl ranked third on the Beauty Ranking; she'll suffer along with him."

"Do you think that girl will die?"

"Die? The girl is so beautiful; maybe those people will keep her for themselves."

"Hahaha, that makes sense!"

Laughter echoed in the inn; no one thought Gu Chen would leave alive.

A mere first-level Emperor Realm cultivator facing three Quasi-Emperors from the Zhao family!

How could he possibly survive?

...

Top floor of the Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

Gu Chen sat by the window, a teacup in his hand, gazing at the distant horizon.

He knew everything.

Not guessed, but told to him by Jian Wuji!

“Thirty miles outside the city, three Quasi-Emperors, they’ve set up a spatial sealing array.”

Jian Wuji’s voice echoed in his mind, his tone completely calm.

Gu Chen asked, “Can we fight them?”

“Yes.”

“How many can we fight?”

“All of them.”

Jian Wuji’s answer was brief, but Gu Chen understood.

All of them?

Couldn’t even they all be enough for Uncle Jian to handle?

What kind of monsters did the Ancestor choose for him?

He smiled!

“The Ancestor is right, let’s just do it!”

Looking at his smile, Ru Shuang couldn't help but ask, "Young Master Gu, what are you smiling about?"

Gu Chen turned to look at her. "Nothing, just thinking tomorrow's competition should be quite interesting."

Ru Shuang was stunned.

Tomorrow's competition?

What is this person thinking?

In her mind, her grandfather's voice echoed, "Girl, this kid is no simpleton. I sensed a quasi-Emperor level expert outside, yet he's not running or in a hurry, and even has the mind to drink tea. He's either a fool, or he has a trump card."

Ru Shuang asked in her mind, "Grandpa, what do you think he is?"

Grandpa was silent for a moment, "I think he's a fool with a trump card."

Ru Shuang: "..."

That was as good as saying nothing at all.

...


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 68 The prize pool is still piling up; the allure of quasi-Emperor artifacts.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The next morning.

Before dawn, the gates of Tianque City opened.

Today was the day of the Genius Battle main event. The martial arts arena, a hundred miles outside the city, had already been set up, and people from all walks of life were setting off from Tianque City towards the arena!

Gu Chen got up very early; Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu were already waiting at the gate.

Ru Shuang also came out of her room, having changed into clean white clothes, her hair tied up, looking much more energetic.

“Let’s go,” Gu Chen said, taking a step forward.

The four of them walked out of the Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

In that instant, countless gazes fell upon them simultaneously.

Across the street in the teahouse, behind the windows, several pairs of eyes narrowed at the same time!

Since Gu Chen’s name appeared yesterday, everyone was astonished to discover that he was the person the Zhao family was looking for!

The Zhao family offered the temptation of a quasi-Emperor artifact, which naturally everyone wanted to obtain!

Everyone stared intently at Gu Chen; he moved around like a fat sheep!

Meanwhile, people were buzzing with discussion about the Gu family. Who was this Gu family? They dared to participate in the Genius Battle after killing Zhao Ritian!

Didn't they fear death?

"Look, that's Gu Chen!"

"The one who killed Zhao Ritian?"

"Yes, that's him! I heard the Zhao family offered a quasi-Emperor artifact for his head, and he still dares to participate?"

"He's incredibly bold. I heard three quasi-Emperors from the Zhao family are waiting outside the city. Isn't he just courting death?"

"It's already quite impressive that a small family that appeared out of nowhere has made it this far!"

The discussions surged from all directions, like waves, one after another.

Some were shocked, some were confused, some were gloating, and some were simply there to watch the spectacle!

"I heard Zhao Wuji is personally leading the team, with a Quasi-Emperor of the Third Heaven, plus two Quasi-Emperor elders. With three Quasi-Emperors guarding the outside, this kid has no chance of escape!"

"Then why is he even here for the competition? Is he courting death? Shouldn't he run?"

"Who knows? Maybe he thinks he's going to die anyway, so he might as well die with some dignity on stage!"

"Dignity? Dying anywhere is the same, what's the difference? As for escaping, do you think he can outrun three Quasi-Emperors?"

From the crowd, a young man sneered, "I think he's just courting death. He thinks he's something special just because he won one round of eliminations, but little does he know that in front of Quasi-Emperors, he's less than an ant!"

The people next to him echoed, "Exactly, no one the Zhao family wants to kill has ever left Zhongzhou alive!"

"It's a pity about that girl, she's going to suffer the same fate!"

"What girl?"

"The one ranked third on the Beauty Ranking, Ru Shuang, who came with Gu Chen. Didn't you see her yesterday?"

"Oh, that one, what a pity! Such a beautiful girl!"

Ru Shuang heard all these words clearly!

She felt speechless!

Not out of fear, but because of those people's faces!

They clearly knew nothing, yet they spoke with such authority, as if Gu Chen were already dead!

She turned to look at Gu Chen.

Gu Chen sat beside her, wiping his sword, completely oblivious to the stares of those around him.

"Young Master Gu," Ru Shuang couldn't help but speak.

"Hmm?"

"Aren't you afraid?"

Gu Chen looked up at her. "Afraid of what?"

"They say there are three quasi-emperors outside the city!"

"Oh, that?" Gu Chen smiled. "Are they really that afraid?"

Afraid?

Uncle Jian said he could fight them all!

He shouldn't be the one running away!

Ru Shuang opened her mouth, wanting to ask him why he wasn't afraid, but swallowed the words back!

If it weren't for Gu Chen's appearance, she probably would have been taken away by Zhao Ritian.

She remembered what her grandfather had said last night: he was a fool with a trump card.

But what exactly was that trump card?

Meanwhile, Nangong Lie, in a corner, kept his eyes fixed on Gu Chen.

His face was grim, his fingers trembling incessantly!

That was an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon!

He desperately wanted it!

"The Grand Elder said we won't act today, let's see the Zhao family's reaction," the gray-robed elder said in a low voice.

Nangong Lie gritted his teeth, "Can those three Quasi-Emperors from the Zhao family take him down?"

"It's hard to say," the gray-robed elder said, narrowing his eyes.

"That kid's protector is no ordinary person. Someone who could kill Zhao Bo of the Zhao family must be at least a Quasi-Emperor. The Zhao family has three Quasi-Emperors; let them fight it out first!"

"What if the Zhao family loses?"

"Even better if they lose! Then we'll have a chance!"

Nangong Lie took a deep breath, suppressing his resentment!

Alright, then let's wait!

Let's see how the Zhao family ends up!

Let's see how Gu Chen dies!

In the distance, representatives of several imperial clans sat together, conversing in hushed tones.

"The Zhao family has really gone all out this time. Three Quasi-Emperors, even Zhao Wuji himself has come."

"What's that kid's background? Why is the Zhao family taking him so seriously?"

"I heard he killed Zhao Ritian."

"No wonder the Zhao family is going crazy. Three Quasi-Emperors, even Zhao Wuji himself has come."

"You can't say that. He dared to participate in the competition, which means he has confidence."

"Confidence? What kind of confidence can withstand three Quasi-Emperors?"

The man opened his mouth, unable to answer.

"Yes, what gives him the confidence to withstand three Quasi-Emperors?

Unless someone stronger than a Quasi-Emperor stands behind him!

But someone stronger than a Quasi-Emperor is a Great Emperor!

A Great Emperor, unseen for hundreds of thousands of years, how could that be possible?

The stands buzzed with discussion; no one believed Gu Chen had a chance!

Everyone thought he was doomed!

Before the match even started, Gu Chen had already become the target of everyone's anger!

Ru Shuang walked behind Gu Chen, her palms sweating.

Her grandfather's voice in her mind grew tense, "Girl, if a fight breaks out later, stay far away. A battle between Quasi-Emperors can kill you from the aftershocks!"

"Grandpa, what about Young Master Gu?!"

"Don't worry about him. His protector is no ordinary person. If a fight really breaks out, the outcome is uncertain. We'll only affect him."

Ru Shuang bit her lip and remained silent.

Her strength was too weak; she couldn't help at all. Even with her grandfather controlling her body, she was no match for a quasi-emperor!

She might even be discovered.

It was all because of her own weakness!

She secretly vowed to become stronger!

In the inn, the restaurant, and on the street, more than a dozen groups of people were simultaneously watching Gu Chen.

Some clutched communication jade talismans, ready to send messages at any moment.

Some had their fingers on their storage bags, containing Immortal-Binding Ropes and Spirit-Sealing Array Plates, ready to act at any time!

Others followed him directly, maintaining a distance of a hundred feet!

Gu Chen was like a fat sheep walking among wolves!

Countless gazes fell upon Gu Chen.

The Zhao family offered a bounty for a quasi-Emperor artifact, dead or alive!

That price was enough to tempt anyone!

Everyone was watching him!

Everyone wanted him dead!

Gu Chen walked down the street, his expression unchanged, completely unconcerned!

Of course he knew these people were watching him!

Jian Wuji had already told him!

They were all insignificant figures, incapable of causing any trouble!

Gu Chen ignored them!

Insignificant figures, not worth his attention!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 69 The Zhao Family's Grand Formation Locks Them Down, Little Doom is Imminent
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Thirty miles outside the city, in a valley.

Zhao Wuji stood at the center of the formation, a vast, dark mass behind him!

Three Quasi-Emperors, twelve Eternal Realm experts, thirty-six Saint Kings, and over a hundred Saints!

This was the entire force his Zhao family could mobilize.

To kill a mere junior at the Emperor Realm, he had brought out the entire Zhao family's reserves!

"Patriarch, everyone is here!" Zhao Kun stepped forward, kneeling on one knee!

Zhao Wuji opened his eyes. "Where is that brat?"

"He just left the Drunken Immortal Pavilion and is heading out of the city!"

"Keep a close eye on him!"

"Yes."

Zhao Kun withdrew.

Zhao Wuji looked up at the distant horizon. He had been waiting for days!

Ever since Zhao Ritian's life tablet shattered, he had gone mad!

Zhao Wuji murmured, "Ritian! Father will avenge you today!"

Behind him, two white-haired elders remained silent, eyes closed in meditation.

Twelve Eternal Realm experts stood on either side, their auras incredibly powerful!

Over a hundred Saints stood in neat formation, their spiritual energy surging!

This was the entire fighting force the Zhao family had accumulated over the years!

In the Central Continent, it would be enough to crush any force below the Imperial Clan!

Even an Imperial Clan would have to think twice before confronting them head-on!

Now, all of them were being used against a young man at the first level of the Emperor Realm!

It was a display of immense prestige!

"Even if that kid had three heads and six arms, he'd die here today!" an Eternal Realm expert whispered.

The person beside him nodded. “Three Quasi-Emperors, twelve Eternal Realm experts—this lineup would make even a fifth-level Quasi-Emperor think twice!”

“What can a junior at the Emperor Realm use to withstand this? His life? His life isn't worth as much as the young master’s!”

“I heard he had a protector. He killed Uncle Zhao? This person is probably not simple!”

“Uncle Zhao? Just a peak Eternal Realm expert. Can he compare to a Quasi-Emperor?”

The person shut up.

Yes, a peak Eternal Realm expert and a Quasi-Emperor are worlds apart, not even in the same league!

Uncle Zhao’s death only proves that the kid’s protector was a Quasi-Emperor!

But can one Quasi-Emperor withstand three?

Not to mention twelve Eternal Realm experts and hundreds of Saints!

The spatial sealing array has been activated; within a hundred miles, not even a fly can escape!

No escape!

"Report!"

Zhao Kun returned, his face grave. "Patriarch, that brat has left the city!"

Zhao Wuji stood up, his eyes flashing coldly!

"Everyone, listen up!"

"Yes!"

"The moment that brat appears, attack with all your might, leave no survivors!"

"Yes!"

The voices were perfectly synchronized, echoing through the valley.

Zhao Wuji stepped out of the valley, his gaze fixed on the direction of Tianque City.

In the distance, four black dots were slowly approaching.

Zhao Wuji's pupils contracted slightly.

Those were the men who killed his son.

A first-level Emperor Realm cultivator, in his early twenties, carrying a rusty sword!

That's?!

Zhao Wuji's pupils contracted sharply!

That wasn't an ordinary sword!

A faint halo flowed around the sword, invisible to ordinary people, but his third-level Quasi-Emperor Realm eyesight saw through it instantly!

It's an ultimate imperial weapon!

Moreover, it's a top-grade natal weapon, of extremely high quality, far surpassing ordinary imperial weapons!

Zhao Wuji's breathing quickened!

He had lived for tens of thousands of years and seen many imperial weapons, but this was the first time he had ever seen an ultimate imperial weapon in person!

The oppressive aura emanating from the depths of the sword made even his quasi-Emperor body tremble slightly!

“An Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon!” Zhao Wuji murmured, his killing intent replaced by greed!

His son was dead, and he was heartbroken!

But his son's growth was too slow—decades, centuries, even millennia—before he could become a Quasi-Emperor!

But an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon was different!

This thing could be used immediately upon acquisition, and after high-level integration, it could even contend head-on with Great Emperors in the future!

If the Zhao family possessed an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon, not only the Central Continent, but the entire continent would have to defer to the Zhao family!

“Patriarch!”

A white-haired elder behind him opened his eyes, his gaze also fixed on the sword, his voice trembling as well.

“That’s an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon?!”

“Yes!” Zhao Wuji nodded.

Another elder also opened his eyes, his gaze gleaming, “And its grade is not low, at least high-grade!”

The three exchanged glances and smiled simultaneously!

"Congratulations, Patriarch, on acquiring an Supreme Emperor Weapon!" The two elders bowed in unison.

"Congratulations, Patriarch!"

The Eternal Realm experts behind them also reacted and offered their congratulations.

"Hahahaha!" Zhao Wuji laughed loudly, his laughter echoing through the valley!

He lost a son, but gained an Supreme Emperor Weapon!

This deal was definitely worth it!

"Who is that brat? A mere first-level Emperor Realm cultivator, and he dares to wield an Extreme Emperor Weapon?" Zhao Wuji sneered.

"This thing should belong to my Zhao family!"

"The Patriarch is right!" the white-haired elder echoed.

"That brat killed the young master; he deserved to die. Consider the Extreme Emperor Weapon compensation!"

"Compensation?" Not enough! His life for my son's life, plus an Extreme Emperor Weapon, barely enough!" Zhao Wuji shook his head.

His tone was nonchalant, as if Gu Chen were already dead!

The Eternal Realm experts behind him laughed along!

"A mere junior at the Emperor Realm, swaggering around with an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon—isn't that courting death?"

"Exactly! 'A common man is innocent, but possessing a treasure is a crime.' Doesn't he know this principle?"

"Maybe he's just a bumpkin from some small family, completely ignorant of what an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon is!"

"Who cares what it is? After today, that sword belongs to the family head!"

Laughter rose and fell; no one believed Gu Chen would leave alive.

A mere first-level Emperor Realm cultivator was less than an ant before three Quasi-Emperors.

As for his protectors, so what if they killed Zhao Bo?

Zhao Bo was only at the peak of the Eternal Realm, a whole realm below a Quasi-Emperor.

Could one Quasi-Emperor defeat three?

Besides, there were twelve Eternal Realm cultivators and hundreds of Saints.

Zhao Wuji's smile vanished, his voice icy. "After that brat appears, kill his men first, then take his sword."

"Yes!"

Zhao Wuji added, "Don't damage the Supreme Emperor Weapon; it's mine."

"Yes!"

The valley was filled with killing intent!

Zhao Wuji stood in the void, his eyes filled with unwavering determination.

The Supreme Emperor Weapon, he was determined to have it!

That brat's life, he was determined to take it too.

"Young Master Gu," Ru Shuang suddenly spoke up.

"What is it?"

"Aren't you really afraid?"

Gu Chen turned and glanced at her, smiling.

"No."

"Why?"

Gu Chen turned his head, looking at the dark mass of people ahead, his tone calm.

"Because the one who should be afraid isn't me."

Ru Shuang was stunned for a moment.

She didn't understand what Gu Chen meant.

However, Gu Chen didn't seem afraid at all. She couldn't understand why Gu Chen was so confident.

And he didn't seem nervous at all, even a little excited?

Could this person really be fearless?

Soon, the four of them walked to within a hundred meters of the group!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 70 Quasi-Emperor's Killing Move: I Slay Three with One Sword
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Zhao Wuji stood in the void, followed by two white-haired elders. The three of them stood firmly in the air, locking Gu Chen and his companions in the middle.

The spatial sealing array had been activated, freezing the space within a hundred miles. Forget flying, even teleportation was impossible!

They were trapped!

Zhao Wuji looked down at Gu Chen, his eyes icy cold!

“You’re from the Gu family?”

Gu Chen looked up at him, disdainful. “Are you Zhao Ritian’s father?”

Zhao Wuji’s face darkened!

“You killed my son, and you dare speak to me like that?”

“Your son attacked first, he deserved to die! I just dealt with him, what, you’re not satisfied?”

“If you’re not satisfied, come and fight me!”

Gu Chen’s voice wasn’t loud, but everyone heard it clearly.

The crowd behind him gasped!

This kid, in front of three quasi-emperors, said their son deserved to die?

And he even asked them if they were satisfied!

"Brother, you killed someone's son! Anyone who didn't know better would think your own son was beaten to death!

Is this courting death?

Do you want to die even faster?!

Well, after all, three Quasi-Emperors are here. A little verbal sparring before death makes this life worthwhile!

Zhao Wuji laughed angrily, "Very good! Very good! Very good indeed! You have guts!"

He raised his hand, and the spiritual power of a Quasi-Emperor at the third level condensed in his palm, transforming into a blinding light.

"Then take your guts and die!"

A burst of light exploded, transforming into a gigantic hand that blotted out the sky, slamming down towards Gu Chen and his companions.

"Boy, today is your death day!"

The crowd behind them gasped!

"They've really made their move! People are going to die!"

"Three Quasi-Emperors, this kid is finished!"

"What a pity. If he had time to grow, he might have really amounted to something."

"No time left. Today is his end! Such is fate!"

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu weren't nervous at all; it was just that the old man talked too much.

He'd already killed his son and he still talked so much!

Next time, they'd just send it to him directly!

Let him be furious!

Let him be anxious to death!

Ru Shuang bit her lip, her hand on her sword, desperately trying not to retreat.

Gu Chen stood motionless.

The wind gently fluttered the hem of his clothes.

He watched the giant hand fall, a slight smile playing on his lips.

"Uncle Sword!"

A sword light flashed in the void!

It didn't come from any direction; it descended from the sky, like a bolt of lightning cleaving the heavens and earth!

The giant hand was pierced by the sword light and shattered with a deafening roar!

Boom!!!

Shards of spiritual energy scattered and flew everywhere, turning into a rain of light that fell upon the earth!

Zhao Wuji's pupils shrank sharply, and he instinctively retreated!

But the sword light didn't stop; it continued its descent!

The two elders behind him attacked simultaneously, unleashing two quasi-Emperor level attacks towards the sword light.

Boom!!!

The sword light collided with the two attacks, unleashing a terrifying surge of spiritual energy. The ground within a hundred miles instantly exploded, sending rocks flying!

The cultivators who had followed to watch the spectacle were thrown about by the aftershocks, some even vomiting blood on the spot!

This was no longer a battlefield they could witness!

Weak cultivators were instantly killed by the spiritual energy!

"How is this possible!" Zhao Wuji's expression changed!

He sensed a powerful sword intent!

It was beyond his imagination!

He looked into the void!

In the void, a figure slowly emerged!

A sword hung at his waist, his face expressionless!

It was Jian Wuji.

Behind him were two others!

Bai Yujing and Ye Wuhen.

The three stood in a line before Gu Chen!

Zhao Wuji's pupils contracted!

He couldn't sense the cultivation levels of these three!

How could this be!

It wasn't that he couldn't see through them, it was that he simply couldn't sense them!

He had lived for tens of thousands of years, yet he couldn't sense the aura of these three before him!

He was a Quasi-Emperor Level 3 expert!

"You, who are you?"

Jian Wuji didn't look at him, turning to Gu Chen.

"Young Master, step back."

Gu Chen obediently took a few steps back.

Jian Wuji turned his head to look at Zhao Wuji!

Zhao Wuji's expression completely changed!

Not out of fear, but because he sensed that aura!

Not a Quasi-Emperor!

Not a peak Quasi-Emperor!

It was an Emperor.

He had only glimpsed that aura in fragments of his clan's ancient texts!

Above all ages is the Emperor, the master of heaven and earth, the source of all laws!

He had lived for tens of thousands of years and had never seen a true Emperor!

Today he had!

And not just one!

Three!

“Impossible!” Zhao Wuji’s voice trembled!

Before he could finish speaking,

Bai Yujing took a step forward.

“Jian Wuji, let me and Ye Wuhen handle this time.”

He stretched, his tone casual.

“You always rush to attack, we don’t even have a chance to stretch!”

Ye Wuhen also took a step forward, holding his sword, expressionless.

“That’s right, last time that peak Eternal Realm cultivator from the Zhao family, you killed him with a single sword strike, we didn’t even have to lift a finger.”

Jian Wuji glanced at them, then at the three members of the Zhao family opposite him.

“Alright, one each.”

Bai Yujing and Ye Wuhen nodded.

Then Jian Wuji added, “I can take three with one sword, no problem!”

Bai Yujing was stunned.

“What did you say?”

“You can each take one, I can take three with one sword, no conflict!”

The two were stunned!

They thought they had misheard.

Jian Wuji ignored them, his hand swiftly resting on his sword!

Bai Yujing panicked!

“Wait! We agreed on one each!”

He quickly raised his hand, a ball of white light condensing in his palm, its aura terrifying!

Ye Wuhen also moved, his hand on his blade, the blade not yet drawn, its intent already tearing through the void!

The two attacked simultaneously!

One move each!

Bai Yujing's palm strike shattered space inch by inch, creating a direct rift in the void!

Ye Wuhen's blade strike arrived instantly, its intent already present, formless and invisible, yet causing the heavens and earth to change color!

Jian Wuji drew his sword too!

One sword strike!

Three people, three attacks, simultaneously crashing down on the three members of the Zhao family.

Zhao Wuji was terrified!

He wanted to run, but his body wouldn't move!

It wasn't spatial confinement, but the oppressive force of the three attacks that pinned him to the spot!

He wanted to shout, but no sound came out!

He wanted to block, but he couldn't raise his hands!

He was like a helpless husband!

Unable to move, unable to speak, he could only watch helplessly!

His cultivation at the third level of Quasi-Emperor was, at this moment, a joke!

The two elders behind him were even worse off, their faces deathly pale, their bodies trembling!

The cultivation at the second and first levels of Quasi-Emperor were less than ants before these three attacks!

Boom!!!

The three attacks landed simultaneously!

Boom!

Feeling despair, the three slowly closed their eyes and completely gave up resisting!

Immediately afterwards, they were utterly crushed into dust by the three terrifying pressures!

They were completely obliterated from this world!

Zhao Wuji glanced at Gu Chen one last time, his eyes filled with resentment!

He hadn't obtained the Supreme Emperor Weapon!

He hadn't avenged his son!

The Zhao family's millennia-old foundation was destroyed in his hands!

He wanted to say something, but his mouth was already closed!

Three breaths later, the three Quasi-Emperors!

Even the Eternal Realm experts, Saint Kings, and Saints behind them were all obliterated by these three powerful pressures!

Completely vanished!

Not even ashes remained!

The entire arena was deathly silent!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 71 Three Quasi-Emperors at Peak? And a Little Insect
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The cultivators who had followed to watch the spectacle were completely dumbfounded!

What on earth was going on?!

Those were three Quasi-Emperors!

How could they be as fragile as paper?!

Before these three, the three Quasi-Emperors didn't even have time to deploy their defenses before being killed!

The strength of these three was terrifyingly powerful!

"Three...three Quasi-Emperors, gone just like that? This is outrageous!"

"Those three! Each only made one move!"

"Just one move! The three Quasi-Emperors couldn't even put up a fight, they were gone!"

"And those Eternal Realm cultivators, Saint Kings, Saints, all gone! How terrifying must that power be to do that?"

"That was the Zhao family's entire foundation, gone just like that?"

"What's the background of this Gu family? I've never heard of them before!"

"I don't know! I just heard about them recently, a small family from the Eastern Region!"

"Dude? A small family wiped out an Imperial Clan? And three of them? Are you serious?"

No one could answer!

Everyone was still in shock!

They had cultivated for decades, even centuries, and had never seen anything like this!

Quasi-Emperors were already top-tier combat power on the Ancient Desolate Continent, existences at the very pinnacle!

Yet, before those three, they couldn't even withstand a single move!

No! It wasn't that they couldn't withstand it!

There was simply no time to react!

Three attacks landed, and the three Quasi-Emperors didn't even have a chance to react before being wiped out of this world!

What did this mean?

They had no idea!

"These three are at least at the sixth level of Quasi-Emperor! Otherwise, they couldn't have killed three Quasi-Emperors in one move!"

"Sixth level Quasi-Emperor? You underestimate them."

"I've seen a seventh level Quasi-Emperor in action, and they weren't this terrifying! These three are at least at the peak of Quasi-Emperor!"

"Peak Quasi-Emperor? That's even more outrageous! How many peak Quasi-Emperors are there in the entire Central Continent? The Gu family sent three at once?"

Whispers rose and fell, but everyone kept their voices low, not daring to speak loudly!

Because the three people in the sky were still standing!

Those three faint but powerful auras still enveloped this world!

They dared not speak loudly, dared not move rashly, and even dared not look up!

"I've cultivated my whole life, and I haven't even reached the Saint level!" A middle-aged cultivator slumped to the ground. "But this guy has three peak Quasi-Emperors as protectors?!"

"Don't compare, you can't!" Someone patted him on the shoulder, their tone complex.

"Some people are born at the finish line, we haven't even found the starting line!"

"Just what kind of background does this Gu family have?" someone asked.

"Sending three peak Quasi-Emperors as protectors all at once? Each Quasi-Emperor is a top-tier fighter in their family! They wouldn't send them out so easily, unless…"

"Unless what?"

"Unless the Gu family has so many peak Quasi-Emperors they don't care!"

The man opened his mouth, speechless.

So many peak Quasi-Emperors they don't care?

What kind of family would that be?

An Imperial Clan?

Impossible, even Imperial Clans aren't that arrogant!

An Imperial Clan is lucky to have two or three Quasi-Emperors, peak Quasi-Emperors are extremely rare.

Do they really think peak Quasi-Emperors are wholesale?!

To suddenly field three protectors isn't just arrogant, it's terrifying!

"Stop guessing. From today onwards, nobody should mess with the Gu family!"

Everyone nodded silently.

This principle was self-evident; no one needed to explain it.

Of course, those who weren't afraid of death could try!

Ru Shuang stood there, completely stunned!

That scene just now—instantly annihilating three Quasi-Emperors?!

Did she see that right?

Ru Shuang asked, "Young Master Gu, were those three seniors at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm?"

Gu Chen thought for a moment, "Probably, I didn't ask."

Ru Shuang fell silent.

Probably?

Shouldn't they be at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm?

Then what were they?

She dared not think about it!

In her mind, her grandfather's voice rang out, filled with deep shock!

"Girl, this Gu family is not simple! I used to think they were just an ordinary imperial family, but now it seems they are more than that!"

Ru Shuang asked in her heart, "Grandpa, what did you see?"

"I didn't see anything, I couldn't see anything at all. I've seen quite a lot of things."

"These three are very likely at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm!"

Ru Shuang's heart skipped a beat.

Grandpa was at the peak of the Eternal Realm in his lifetime; even he couldn't see through him!

What exactly were the cultivation levels of those three protectors?

She looked at Gu Chen, wondering what his background was.

He actually had a Quasi-Emperor peak protector!

In the distance, the Nangong family's spies stood at the very back of the crowd, their faces deathly pale!

"Quick! Go back and report!" The lead spies' voice trembled!

"Tell the patriarch, under no circumstances should he make a move! The Zhao family's three Quasi-Emperors were wiped out in one strike! The Gu family's three protectors were at least Quasi-Emperors peak!"

"Yes!"

The spies turned and ran, faster than rabbits!

But they had only taken a few steps when a voice came from the sky!

It was calm, yet it sent chills down everyone's spines.

"There's another little bug, come out!"

Jian Wuji stood in the void, his gaze fixed on a spot at the back of the crowd!

There was no one there!

But Jian Wuji's gaze was fixed in that direction!

"Hidden for so long, aren't you tired?"

The entire arena fell silent.

Everyone followed Jian Wuji's gaze, but saw nothing.

But the next moment, the void distorted!

A figure emerged from the void!

The man's face was ashen, his eyes filled with terror!

A Quasi-Emperor, First Level.

The First Elder of the Nangong Family, Nangong Yuan!

He had been hiding very well, concealing all his aura, concealing himself in a void crevice, preparing to seize the Supreme Emperor Weapon!

He had witnessed the entire process of Jian Wuji and the other two making their move!

In that instant, only one thought occupied his mind.

Run!

But before he could run, he was discovered!

"It's someone from the Nangong family!"

"Nangong Yuan, the Grand Elder of the Nangong family! A Quasi-Emperor at the first level!"

"He also wants to steal from the Gu family?"

"Of course! An Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon, who wouldn't want that?"

"But he hid it so well, how was he discovered?"

"Didn't you hear? They called him a little bug. A Quasi-Emperor is just a little bug in their eyes!"

The whispers in the crowd made Nangong Yuan's face even uglier.

He had lived for tens of thousands of years. The Grand Elder of the Nangong family, a Quasi-Emperor at the first level, when had he ever been called a little bug?

But now, he dared not utter a sound.

Because standing before him were three people, three people he couldn't afford to offend.

Nangong Yuan spoke, "Senior, I was just passing by, I meant no harm."

Jian Wuji looked at him, expressionless.

"Passing by? Passing by in the void?"

Nangong Yuan's face turned completely pale.

"You've been watching my young master since yesterday, hiding outside the city for a whole day and night, just waiting for your chance."

Jian Wuji's voice was calm, but every word pierced Nangong Yuan's heart!

"What you want is the sword in my young master's hand, right?"

Nangong Yuan opened his mouth, but couldn't speak.

Because what Jian Wuji said was entirely true!

He did indeed want that supreme imperial weapon.

From the moment Nangong Lie sent back the message, he had been planning!

The Zhao family made a move, he waited for his opportunity.

Gu Chen died, he would seize the sword.

Gu Chen was crippled, he would still seize the sword.

But he didn't expect that Gu Chen wasn't dead or crippled, but the three quasi-emperors of the Zhao family were dead!

Even more unexpectedly, despite hiding so well, he was still discovered!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 72 The Quasi-Emperor Begs for Mercy, Who's Getting Married?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"Senior, please spare my life!" Nangong Yuan knelt down with a thud!

A Quasi-Emperor level one expert, kneeling on the ground, trembling all over!

"Please, senior, spare my life! Please, senior, spare my life!"

The entire arena was deathly silent.

A Quasi-Emperor! That's a Quasi-Emperor!

In Zhongzhou, that's being at the very pinnacle!

The contrast was too great!

Is this still the high and mighty Quasi-Emperor?

"Young Master, how should we deal with this person?"

Swish!

All eyes turned to Gu Chen.

To see what he would do.

Gu Chen raised the sword, examined it, and didn't find anything particularly special about it!

But!

This was a gift from his ancestor!

That was its most special quality!

Now someone actually wanted to steal the sword his ancestor had given him—that was absolutely unforgivable!

“If you want my sword, you'll have to be alive to take it!”

Jian Wuji nodded and looked at Nangong Yuan.

One strike!

Nangong Yuan's body froze.

Three breaths later, the Nangong family's Great Elder, a Quasi-Emperor at the first level.

vanished instantly!

Hiss!!

The entire arena fell silent once more.

Those who were just discussing were now speechless.

Another Quasi-Emperor.

Gone!

Also with a single sword strike!

"This is the fourth Quasi-Emperor!"

"Three from the Zhao family, one from the Nangong family, four Quasi-Emperors, all gone in less than the time it takes for an incense stick to burn!"

"Not just as if, in their eyes, Quasi-Emperors really are no different from chickens!"

"Killing Quasi-Emperors like killing chickens, only they could do that!"

Everyone saw it!

From their appearance to their attack, from beginning to end, those three people didn't make a single unnecessary movement, didn't waste a single drop of spiritual energy!

Clean and efficient!

"The millennia-old order of Zhongzhou has likely been shattered today."

"The Zhao and Nangong families are finished. From now on, the Gu family will become the new elite of Zhongzhou!"

"How can they be just new elites? They're practically overlords!"

"Three peak Quasi-Emperors as protectors—which imperial clan can do that? Do you think peak Quasi-Emperors are just mass-produced?"

The crowd was speechless!

Yes, which imperial clan could do that?

Not a single one!

"Today, we've witnessed history! Four Quasi-Emperors have fallen—we saw it with our own eyes, not just hearsay."

"We can brag about this for the rest of our lives!"

"Bragging? Do you dare tell anyone? The Zhao and Nangong families may be finished, but their remnants are still around. Don't you want to live?"

Ru Shuang stood beside Gu Chen, looking at his calm profile, and the indescribable feeling in her heart intensified.

This man remained unfazed in the face of three Quasi-Emperors, showing no excitement even after killing them, as if everything was perfectly natural.

Or is this kind of thing commonplace in the Gu family?

That can't be, can it?

Her grandfather's voice echoed in her mind.

"Girl, this Gu family is deeper than we think. The more you think about it, the more terrifying it becomes."

Her grandfather's voice was heavy.

"Three protectors at least at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, serving as bodyguards for a young master at the Emperor realm—what does that mean? It means that in the Gu family, the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm is nothing."

"Then what about the Gu family's patriarch?"

"Don't guess, you can't figure it out."

"Grandpa! You're making fun of me again."

Ru Shuang's grandfather suddenly blushed!

At the same time, Gu Chen patted his clothes, now only thinking about the competition!

He'd be late if he waited!

"Let's go! We're going to be late for the competition."

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu quickly followed.

Ru Shuang hesitated for a moment, then followed as well.

...

Meanwhile, a tremendous change was taking place within the Gu family!

Gu Tianhua stood in the plaza, looking at the sea of ​​people before him, his smile almost splitting his jaw!

“Breakthrough! Another breakthrough! The seventeenth!”

A disciple from a vassal force emerged from the Marrow Cleansing Array, his aura surging, reaching the peak of the Half-Saint realm, just one step away from becoming a Saint!

“Clan Chief Gu, I broke through too!”

Another person rushed out, his face filled with excitement and joy!

This feeling was absolutely exhilarating for him!

“Good, good, good!”

Gu Tianhua grinned from ear to ear.

For the past month, the Gu family's cultivation resources had been supplied without restraint; the spiritual energy of the immortal veins was so dense it seemed to condense into water.

The resources were inexhaustible!

When they ran out, they arrived again in the next second; restocking was more effective than talking to one's own father!

You might hesitate and be reluctant to ask your father for money, but in the Gu family, if you say you're out of resources, they'll be delivered in the next second!

Those disciples of the vassal forces were cultivating like madmen!

Today you break through to Half-Saint, tomorrow I'll break through to Saint.

The day after tomorrow?

The day after tomorrow, everyone will be a Saint!

“Clan Chief Gu!”

Huo Dongyuan walked over, his demeanor composed, a Saint King at the fifth level.

Behind him followed the disciples of the Azure Cloud Holy Land, the lowest being at the peak of Half-Saint, with Saints making up a third!

“At this rate, in another half month, everyone in the Azure Cloud Holy Land will be a Saint!”

Gu Tianhua nodded, “That’s right. The Ancestor said it should be achieved within three months. You still have time.”

Huo Dongyuan smiled bitterly.

Three months?

At this rate, one month would be enough.

But he didn’t say that.

Because the Gu family’s resources were simply terrifying!

Marrow Cleansing Arrays, Immortal Veins, Enlightenment Tea, Ginseng Fruit, Emperor-grade pills, and all sorts of other things—it was like they were free!

These things, if left out in the open, would cause a bloodbath among imperial clans.

But within the Gu family, use them freely!

Unlimited!

Self-service!

“Clan Chief Gu.”

Huo Dongyuan lowered his voice, “What exactly was the Ancestor’s cultivation level?”

Gu Tianhua smiled.

“I don’t know.”

Huo Dongyuan’s lips twitched.

You’re the clan chief, and you don’t even know?

Who would believe that?

Gu Tianhua patted his shoulder, “Don’t think about it, anyway, he’s higher than you!”

Huo Dongyuan smiled wryly.

Isn’t that obvious?

He was about to ask again when Gu Tianhua turned and left.

“Alright, you keep an eye on things. I have something to do.”

“What is it?”

Gu Tianhua didn’t turn around. “Prepare the bride price.”

Huo Dongyuan was stunned.

Bridal price?

Who’s getting married?

…


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 73 Demonic Aura, Gu Chen vs. Han Zixuan
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Tianhua's room was piled high with things.

Gu Tianhua picked up a hairpin, entirely green, emitting a faint spiritual light.

This was a Great Emperor-level spiritual treasure, specifically for women.

Of course, Gu Tianhua couldn't tell the difference, but he knew it was definitely a good item, and the ancestor had instructed him to prepare it.

It must have been tacit approval, so it would naturally work!

"And this, this, this!"

He picked up a Rainbow Feather Robe, also a Great Emperor-level defensive spiritual treasure, as thin as gauze, yet impervious to blades and spears!

"This little girl would definitely look beautiful in it!"

Gu Yushan stood at the door, watching Gu Tianhua bustling around, speechless!

"Clan Chief, the girl hasn't agreed yet! You've already prepared the betrothal gifts?"

Gu Tianhua didn't turn around. "It's only a matter of time."

"How did you know?"

This wasn't preparing betrothal gifts; it was like opening a general store!

Preparing so many things, making such a fuss, I thought a major battle was about to break out!

But this father is doing a decent job!

Preparing so many things.

"Qingqing and Huahua have emerged from seclusion."

Gu Tianhua was stunned. "They broke through?"

Gu Yushan's lips curled into a wide smile, harder to suppress than Ak's. "They broke through! The two little girls, both of them, broke through to the Saint Realm!"

They're only eleven or twelve years old!

They actually broke through to the Saint Realm!

"So fast?"

"Of course, with the resources personally given by the Ancestor, they could have easily reached that level."

Gu Tianhua nodded.

That's true.

In the Gu family, breaking through to the Saint Realm doesn't seem so difficult!

Saints used to be rare, now they're everywhere!

"Where are they?"

"They went to the back mountain to find the Ancestor."

The back mountain!

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, resting with his eyes closed.

The two little girls, one on each side, squatted beside him, their eyes sparkling.

"Ancestor, Ancestor, we've broken through!"

"Saint Realm! We've reached the Saint Realm!"

Gu Xuan opened his eyes and glanced at them.

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue.

Yue Qingshuang's lips curled slightly upward, but she didn't speak.

Finally, I can go outside!

Yue Qingxue smiled. "Let's go."

Then, the two little girls rode off on their white tigers and disappeared in a flash.

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue followed behind, unhurriedly.

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, his eyes closed.

But his divine sense had already drifted beyond the Gu family compound, heading into the distance.

He sensed an aura.

Very faint, almost imperceptible.

But Gu Xuan was a Great Emperor.

A Great Emperor's divine sense covered the entire continent.

That aura could not escape his notice.

The Demon Race, and even other different races.

All from different continents.

Hundreds of thousands of years ago, a Great Emperor of the Desolate Ancient Continent wiped out these invading alien species to ensure the development of humanity!

Hundreds of thousands of years have passed, and the powerful figures of those great worlds seem to have sensed the fall of that Great Emperor and are plotting their return!

Hundreds of thousands of years have passed, and those things are about to emerge again!

…

The martial arts arena is bustling with noise.

The first round of the Genius Battle's main competition has concluded, and the matchups have been announced.

Gu Chen stands in the contestant area, looking at the names on the list, his expression unchanged.

Han Zixuan.

A hot contender, a genius from the Han family of the Imperial Clan, a peak fourth-level Emperor Realm expert!

Ru Shuang whispered beside him, "Young Master Gu, be careful of his equipment. Han Zixuan's set, although it's a pseudo-quasi-Emperor weapon, the whole set—helmet, armor, boots, weapon—is stronger than a true quasi-Emperor weapon."

Gu Chen glanced at Han Zixuan, who was warming up in the distance.

Clad in silver-white armor, his helmet covered most of his face, revealing only his eyes.

Holding a silver spear, he stood there like a silver war god.

"It's quite intimidating," Gu Chen smiled.

Seeing his nonchalant attitude, Ru Shuang couldn't help but add, "He's really not weak. Last year in the Central Continent Youth Competition, he came in third!"

Gu Chen asked, "Third? Then who are first and second?"

"First is Bing Xin of the Ice Phoenix Clan, second is another member of the Imperial Han Clan, Han Zixuan's cousin, but he's too old this year and didn't come."

Gu Chen nodded and didn't ask further.

The competition will reveal the true strength!

"First match, Gu Chen versus Han Zixuan!"

The announcer's voice echoed throughout the arena.

Gu Chen stepped onto the arena, and Han Zixuan walked up from the other side!

He gripped his spear tightly, spiritual energy flowing across it.

"Your protector is strong, that's your protector's business. In the arena, the battle depends on yourself!"

"Well said."

The two exchanged a glance, the air freezing for a moment!

"Begin!"

The words fell.

Han Zixuan moved.

The speed was incredible!

So fast it was impossible to see!

The speed of a peak fourth-level Emperor Realm expert exploded at full power, the spear transforming into a silver lightning bolt, aimed straight at Gu Chen's face.

The enhancement from his complete set of equipment allowed his speed, strength, and reaction time to reach the pinnacle of the Emperor Realm!

Gu Chen sidestepped and unleashed a sword strike!

Clang!

Sword and spear clashed, sparks flying.

Gu Chen took a step back, Han Zixuan took two steps back.

Han Zixuan glanced at the spear in his hand; a shallow mark had appeared on its shaft.

His expression changed in shock!

His spear had actually been struck?!

The two clashed.

Spears and swords clashed, spiritual energy exploding.

Light flashed on the arena, figures weaving through the crowd, their speed so fast that those below couldn't see them clearly.

"So strong! Neither of them is weak!"

"Han Zixuan's equipment is incredible, a complete set of pseudo-quasi-Emperor artifacts, increasing his combat power by at least 50%."

"Gu Chen isn't weak either, only at the first level of the Emperor Realm, he's fighting Han Zixuan to a standstill."

"His swordsmanship is a bit strange, every strike carries an indescribable sharpness!"

"It's not his swordsmanship that's strange, it's his sword intent. His sword intent is very strong."

Beside the arena, several representatives of the imperial clans sat together, watching the battle on the stage, their expressions solemn.

"That Gu Chen, his sword intent has already touched the threshold of the Dao."

"He's only at the first level of the Emperor Realm, how is that possible?"

"I don't know, but his sword intent is indeed extraordinary. It's said that Han Zixuan's equipment could withstand a quasi-Emperor's attack, but under his sword, cracks are already appearing!"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 74 Sweeping Through the Battlefield, Entering the Finals
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

On the stage, Han Zixuan grew increasingly alarmed with each strike!

Was the person before him truly only at the first level of the Emperor Realm?

He had absolutely no ability to resist, not even the will to!

Gu Chen's sword intent grew stronger and stronger!

At first, he could exchange blows, but later, he could only defend!

Each sword strike was faster and heavier than the last!

An outsider would have thought it was Seven Wolves!

The blows grew heavier and heavier!

Han Zixuan couldn't block them at all; even his hands ached from the force of the attacks!

"Draw Sword Slash!" Gu Chen attacked!

One sword strike.

The sword light flashed like a shadow, tearing through the void.

Han Zixuan's pupils contracted, and he raised his spear to block.

Clang!!

Han Zixuan was sent flying backward, crashing onto the edge of the stage.

He looked down at the broken spear in his hand, then at the cracked armor on his body, his face turning deathly pale.

What the hell?

His entire set of pseudo-quasi-Emperor level weapons, how did they all shatter?

Did this guy's weapon have armor-piercing capabilities?

All the equipment I painstakingly collected is gone!

"I…" He opened his mouth, wanting to say something, but no words came out.

Forget it, he couldn't beat the person in front of him, and he couldn't afford to offend the person behind him!

Han Zixuan lay on the arena, looking at the gray sky, silent for a long time.

"I concede."

The entire arena erupted in uproar.

"Han Zixuan conceded?"

"Gu Chen, just what kind of monster is this guy?"

Han Zixuan got up from the arena, watching Gu Chen's retreating figure, and gave a bitter smile.

This person is too strong.

Not in terms of cultivation level, but in terms of sword intent.

He had never seen that kind of sword intent even in his family's Quasi-Emperor ancestor!

Even his family's ancestor didn't possess such sword intent. He truly didn't know how his successors had taught him.

Terrifying!

Han Zixuan murmured, "Gu Chen, you're very strong. I'll remember you. We'll spar again sometime!"

"Okay, I'll wait for you."

The contestants' area.

Ru Shuang watched Gu Chen walk back, her heart still racing.

Her grandfather's voice echoed in her mind.

"Grandpa, you mean!"

Meanwhile!

On the border of Zhongzhou, a void rift.

Two little girls rode white tigers, flying directly out of the rift.

What void?

It doesn't exist!

The white tigers carried them across the void!

Unlike Gu Chen, who had a spirit ship to travel on!

"But the spiritual energy is far inferior to our home! I feel like I'm going to suffocate!" Gu Huahua commented.
