The white tiger roared, transforming into a streak of white light and flying southeast.

Behind it, Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue followed.

Meanwhile.

Tianque City, Martial Arts Arena.

Gu Chen sat in the contestant area, waiting for the next round's match list.

He suddenly sneezed.

"Who's talking about me?"

A sense of foreboding washed over him, a vague unease rising within him!

In the following matches, Gu Chen swept through the competition!

In the second round, his opponent was a rogue cultivator at the fourth level of the Emperor Realm, defeated with a single sword strike!

In the third round, his opponent was a descendant of an imperial clan, at the fifth level of the Emperor Realm, defeated with three sword strikes!

In the fourth round, his opponent was a disciple of the Zhuge family, a family of immortals, skilled in formations, who had covered the arena with array patterns!

Gu Chen stepped in, cleaved the array's core with a single sword strike, and then sent his opponent flying with another!

Two sword strikes!

The entire arena fell silent!

"Is he even human?"

"Wasn't it said that the Zhuge family's formation was unbreakable by anyone of the same level? He cleaved it in two sword strikes? Isn't that cheating?"

"It's not that the formation was weak, it's that his sword was too powerful. That sword intent is simply not of the Emperor Realm."

"What is his cultivation level? Is he really only at the first level of the Emperor Realm?"

No one could answer that question!

Because every match Gu Chen played ended too quickly!

So quickly that no one had time to analyze his strength before the match was over!

Several rounds in a row ended swiftly!

Gu Chen fought his way into the finals!

His opponent in the finals was Bing Xin of the Ice Phoenix Clan, the biggest favorite to win, a peak fifth level Emperor Realm cultivator with Ice Phoenix bloodline, wielding a quasi-Emperor weapon!

As dusk fell, the crowd in the martial arts arena slowly dispersed.

The finals begin tomorrow.

But the news had already spread throughout the entire Heavenly Palace City!

"Gu Chen's in the finals! He's going to fight Bing Xin!"

"A kid from the Eastern Region has made it to the finals of the Genius Battle? How many years has this been?!"

"Tens of thousands of years! The last time someone from the Eastern Region made it into the top 100 was 30,000 years ago, and this time he's gone straight to the finals!"

"Just who is this Gu Chen? His protector is a peak Quasi-Emperor, and he can fight on his own. Is he even letting anyone else live?"

The discussions echoed through the streets and alleys. From this day forward, Gu Chen's name was truly renowned throughout the Central Continent.

He didn't rely on his protector; he relied on himself.

One sword strike broke the formation, two sword strikes defeated his enemy, sweeping all the way to the finals!

No one dared to underestimate his strength!

But while some rejoiced, others grieved.

Ru Shuang sat in her room at the Drunken Immortal Pavilion, looking at the night outside the window, her heart filled with mixed emotions!

She lost.

She lost in the sixth round.

Her opponent wasn't particularly strong, only at the third level of the Emperor Realm, but she still lost.

Too little practical experience, too slow reaction time. Her grandfather's guidance could help her temporarily, but not permanently.

She looked down at the token in her hand, on which was written her ranking.

242nd.

This ranking didn't even reach the edge of the rewards!

At most a few bottles of pills, a little spirit stone—a drop in the ocean for her grandfather's recovery!

"Girl, don't be sad."

Ru Shuang's voice choked slightly, "Grandpa, I'm so useless."

Grandpa's tone was gentle, "You've already tried your best. When you escaped from the Ru family, you were only at the second level of the Emperor Realm. To have reached this point in just a few months, Grandpa is very proud."

"But Grandpa, your remnant soul..."

“It can hold on for a while longer,” Grandpa interrupted her. “The Soul-Nourishing Wood can last for a few more months, no rush.”

Ru Shuang didn’t speak; she knew Grandpa was lying.

The Soul-Nourishing Wood could only delay the dissipation of the remnant soul, not cure it.

In a few months, at most half a year, Grandpa’s remnant soul would completely dissipate.

Then, she would truly be all alone.

Grandpa comforted her, “There will be a way; there’s always a way out!”


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 75 The Finals! Gu Chen vs. Bing Xin
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Qingshan lay on the bed, secretly pleased.

Top 32.

He stopped at the top 32.

He lost to an opponent at the fifth level of the Emperor Realm. His opponent's cultivation was two minor realms higher, and their weapon was equally formidable. In the end, exhausted of spiritual energy, he was knocked off the stage in a single move.

Just that one crucial point.

It was a matter of talent.

His talent couldn't compare to those others, nor could his cultivation time.

He reached the top 32 thanks to the sword given to him by his ancestor, the resources of the Gu family, and the experience honed through countless deaths in the Locking God Tower.

But all of that was insufficient against absolute strength.

However, it was enough for him. He hadn't held out much hope to begin with, and making it into the top 32 was a respectable achievement.

Anyway, he was only there to observe.

Getting into the top 32 while he was at it seemed pretty good!

"Brother Qingshan." Gu Chen's voice came from outside the door.

Gu Qingshan sat up. "Gu Chen?"

The door opened, and Gu Chen walked in, carrying two jugs of wine.

"Want a drink?"

Gu Qingshan hesitated for a moment, then smiled.

"Come on!"

The two sat by the window, facing the night outside, each with a jug of wine.

Gu Qingshan took a large gulp and let out a long sigh.

"It's a pity I only got this far."

Gu Chen glanced at him. "You're in the top 32, that's already very strong."

"But I want to be in the top 16."

"Next time."

"Next time will be a hundred years."

Gu Chen was silent for a moment. "Then wait a hundred years."

Gu Qingshan smiled bitterly. This competition had an age limit; he wouldn't be able to participate for another hundred years.

He actually told him to wait a hundred years!

He's so good at comforting people!

"You're good at everything, except comforting people."

Gu Chen laughed too. "Isn't this how I comfort you?"

"You're just giving me the runaround."

They looked at each other and laughed simultaneously.

"Good luck in the finals tomorrow."

Gu Chen nodded. "Yeah."

Gu Chen smiled. "Okay."

They clinked their wine jugs and drank it all in one gulp.

On the other side of the Drunken Immortal Pavilion, Li Shuangxu sat in the lobby, holding a competition schedule booklet.

He hadn't registered; he'd only come to watch the spectacle from the start.

But seeing Gu Qingshan stop at the top 32 and Ru Shuang eliminated, he felt a pang of sadness.

"This is Zhongzhou! Geniuses are everywhere, and champions are as common as dogs."

In Qingyun Prefecture, he was a Holy Son, a genius, the number one.

In Zhongzhou, he didn't even have the qualifications to register.

Cruel, but true!

"Forget it, I don't want to think about it anymore." He stood up and went upstairs.

The finals are tomorrow, I need to go to bed early.

I still have to bet on the young master to win tomorrow!

To earn some more for my wife!

The next day.

Before dawn, the martial arts arena was already packed.

The finals!

Gu Chen vs. Bing Xin!

One was a dark horse from the Eastern Region who had suddenly appeared, sweeping all the way to the finals!

The other was the biggest favorite to win, the Holy Maiden of the Ice Phoenix Clan, a peak fifth-level Emperor Realm expert!

This finals attracted the attention of the entire Central Continent!

In the stands, representatives from various imperial clans, longevity families, and reclusive powerhouses gathered together!

"Who do you think will win?" someone asked.

"It's hard to say. Bingxin has high cultivation, a strong bloodline, and even a quasi-Emperor weapon."

"Gu Chen isn't weak either. You all saw his sword intent; he even cleaved through the Zhuge family's formation with a single strike."

"But Bingxin isn't from the Zhuge family. With her Ice Phoenix bloodline fully unleashed, her chilling aura can freeze space. No matter how fast Gu Chen's sword is, it's useless once frozen."

"That makes sense!"

"However, I'm more optimistic about Bingxin. After all, the Ice Phoenix Clan has a history of hundreds of thousands of years; their strength is definitely extraordinary!"

Discussions rose and fell, but few favored Gu Chen.

After all, Bingxin's strength was undeniable: peak of the fifth level of the Emperor Realm, Ice Phoenix bloodline, a quasi-Emperor weapon, and rich combat experience.

Although Gu Chen had swept through the competition, no one dared to say whether he could win against a truly top genius.

Bingxin sat in a corner, eyes closed, meditating, surrounded by several elders of the Ice Phoenix Clan.

"Holy Maiden, Gu Chen's sword intent is very strong. You must be careful," an elder whispered.

Bingxin opened her eyes. "I know."

"His sword intent is strong, and behind him stands an unfathomable family."

The elder nodded. "So..."

Bingxin interrupted her. "So I won't underestimate him, but I won't be afraid either!"

She stood up and glanced at Gu Chen. "Just a genius who suddenly emerged from the Eastern Region. He's strong, but not invincible!"

The elder opened his mouth, unsure what to say.

This Gu Chen is indeed a dark horse; defeating him won't be easy.

However, with the foundation of the Ice Phoenix Clan spanning hundreds of thousands of years, Bingxin, carefully nurtured, will not be easily defeated!

This is the confidence and power of a top-tier imperial family!

Bingxin said calmly, "I will win! The championship is mine."

Her voice wasn't loud, but it was firm.

The elders around her exchanged glances, all seeing reassurance in each other's eyes.

The Holy Maiden is still the Holy Maiden—confident, composed, and unaffected by anyone.

That's enough.

If she lacked even this much confidence, she wouldn't deserve to be the Holy Maiden of the Ice Phoenix Clan!

Absolute confidence is what matters!

On the other side.

Gu Chen looked up at the arena, his eyes filled with unwavering determination!

“Let’s go.”

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu followed behind.

Ru Shuang also followed, but her face was noticeably pale.

Gu Chen turned to look at Ru Shuang. Jian Wuji had told him that the soul entity wouldn't last long, at most six months, before dissipating.

He also told her that the soul entity seemed to be someone important to him.

However, Gu Chen didn't know what to do. Perhaps only the Ancestor had a solution.

But directly bringing her back to the Gu family seemed a bit inappropriate, after all, she was still a young woman!

He could only wait until after the competition to ask for her opinion.

Now that the finals had begun, his focus had to be on this final!

The stands were filled with a cacophony of voices.

"Gu Chen! Gu Chen! Gu Chen!"

"Bing Xin! Bing Xin! Bing Xin!"

The shouts rose and fell, deafeningly loud.

Bing Xin stepped onto the arena first, standing to one side, her long hair flowing!

The bloodline of the Ice Phoenix Clan surged within her, chilling the surrounding air!

In the stands, the elders of the Ice Phoenix Clan nodded in satisfaction.

"The Holy Maiden's Ice Phoenix bloodline is becoming increasingly pure!"

"This championship is a sure thing!"

"A guaranteed win!"

Gu Chen walked up from the other side, his rusty sword still at his waist.

A murmur of discussion arose in the stands.

"That's Gu Chen? He doesn't look that impressive! Is he really as amazing as you all say?"

A newly arrived rogue cultivator inquired.

"You wouldn't believe it, his strength is absolutely top-tier! And his cultivation is only at the first level of the Emperor Realm!"

"The first level of the Emperor Realm, to reach the finals is already a miracle!"

"The miracle is over! Bing Xin definitely won't give him a chance! After all, I'm betting on Bing Xin to win!"

Gu Chen walked to the center of the arena and stood opposite Bing Xin.

The two were fifty feet apart.

Their eyes met.

"The Genius Battle, the finals, now begins!"

Bing Xin's Ice Phoenix bloodline grew increasingly violent, its chill intensifying!

"Genius Battle, Champion: Gu Chen!"

"This year's champion's prize is a remnant of an Emperor." The old man's voice was calm, but the entire arena erupted in shock!

That aura wasn't that of a Quasi-Emperor, nor even a peak Quasi-Emperor.

It was that of an Emperor!

"This jade pendant is a remnant left behind by an Emperor hundreds of thousands of years ago!"

"A portion of his legacy? A portion of an Emperor's legacy?"

"If a peak Quasi-Emperor obtains it, they might have a chance to become a Great Emperor!"

On the stands, representatives from the various imperial clans couldn't sit still.

A Great Emperor's inheritance?!

Even a partial inheritance is enough to drive the entire Central Continent mad!

Obtaining it at the peak Quasi-Emperor level might really allow one to step into the Great Emperor realm!

That's a Great Emperor! A Great Emperor who hasn't appeared in hundreds of thousands of years!

Chapter 77 Follow Me Back to My Hometown, Old Man Cangwu
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Chen took the wooden box and glanced at the jade pendant inside.

An Emperor's Inheritance!

Not only would it prove his worth, but it would also please his ancestor!

His ancestor had seen everything, but surely he'd never seen something like an Emperor's Inheritance before?

Wait, his ancestor seemed to have everything!

Whatever, he'd keep it for now!

On the stands, countless eyes were fixed on the wooden box!

An Emperor's Inheritance!

Even a partial inheritance is still an inheritance!

If a Quasi-Emperor at the peak obtained it, perhaps he could truly step into the Great Emperor realm.

Some swallowed hard, some had greed flashing in their eyes!

But no one dared to move!

The corpses of the three Quasi-Emperors of the Zhao family weren't even cold yet, and the ashes of the Nangong family's Great Elder were still fluttering in the wind!

Behind Gu Chen stood three peak Quasi-Emperors!

Who would dare touch him?

They wouldn't risk their lives by touching that thing!

The Gu family ancestor!

"Girl, agree to him. There's no better choice now."

Elder Cangwu was the old man Ru Shuang's father wanted her to marry!

His strength was at the ninth level of Quasi-Emperor!

Elder Cangwu didn't belong to any faction, he was a lone wolf, and his strength was immense!

Although he was only at the ninth level of the Quasi-Emperor realm, Elder Cangwu had killed countless young girls.

Ru Shuang didn't want to be killed at such a young age!

"What if Elder Cangwu comes after us?"

A ninth-level Quasi-Emperor is still just a ninth-level Quasi-Emperor. The Gu family has three peak Quasi-Emperors, and who knows if there's an even stronger force behind them!

Besides, it was the young master of the Gu family who invited him. If Elder Cangwu dared to come to the Gu family, they certainly wouldn't let him act recklessly!

Ru Shuang thought for a moment!

Yes, three peak Quasi-Emperors!

No matter how strong Elder Cangwu was, he was only at the ninth level of the Quasi-Emperor realm.

In front of three peak Quasi-Emperors, he wouldn't dare utter a sound!

A complete Quasi-Emperor's inheritance, in exchange for a Taiyin Spiritual Body.

Three Quasi-Emperors peak.

He had already given Ru Tianming half of the complete Quasi-Emperor inheritance; the other half would be given after Ru Shuang obtained it.

He wouldn't give it away for nothing!

"What's the background of the Gu family?"

"We investigated, but found nothing. It's like they appeared out of thin air."

A cold glint flashed in the old man's eyes.

"Interesting!"

Chapter 78 Demon Emperor Pearl, what kind of person is the Gu family patriarch?

Pervert!

was not human.

So, it was one of their own!

Clearly, the old man Cangwu had sided with the Demon Clan, solely for the sake of acquiring greater power!

He was practically obsessed with power!

Mo Yuan smiled. “I heard the reward for this Genius Battle is a portion of an Emperor’s inheritance.”

Hearing this, a glint of greed flashed in the old man Cangwu’s eyes.

A portion of an Emperor’s inheritance?!

He only knew that Ru Shuang was in the Genius Battle, but he didn’t know what the reward was.

An Emperor’s inheritance, even a portion, was enough to drive any Quasi-Emperor mad!

“Add to that little girl’s Taiyin Spiritual Body, it’s a great tonic. The combination of the two, becoming an Emperor isn’t impossible!”

The old man Cangwu’s breathing quickened.

An Emperor!

That was a realm he wouldn’t even dare to dream of!

Now, there was an opportunity?!

But he quickly calmed down!

If he had heard Ming say that the kid had three peak Quasi-Emperors behind him, he probably wouldn’t be a match for him.

"To try and steal it would be a losing proposition!"

Mo Yuan smiled.

"Since you've already pledged allegiance to our Demon Clan, we'll naturally give you something good!"

A black bead appeared in Mo Yuan's hand.

The bead emitted a strange aura!

"What is this?"

The old man Cangwu stared at the bead.

Mo Yuan said, "It's my Demon Clan's Demon Emperor Bead, which can temporarily make you a Great Emperor!"

The old man Cangwu's pupils dilated!

Temporarily become a Great Emperor?!

"And there won't be any side effects," Mo Yuan added.

"It won't put any burden on your body! You can experience the power of an Emperor beforehand!"

The old man Cangwu stared at the black bead, his eyes flashing with greed!

The power of an Emperor!

That was something he had always dreamed of!

Even if it was only temporary, it was worth it!

He wanted it so badly!

"What's the price?" the old man Cangwu asked.

"Smart!"

His smile vanished, his voice turning icy.

"When the rift opens, that will be the time for our demon army to invade. These humans will be food for us, and you can use this opportunity to increase your strength. At that time, not only will you be at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, but a true Emperor won't be impossible!"

The old man Cangwu was silent for a moment.

He knew, of course, what the demons' purpose was.

Invading the Ancient Desolate Continent, slaughtering humans, plundering resources.

But he didn't care!

What he cared about was only his own strength!

The Taiyin Spirit Body, the Emperor's inheritance, the power of the demons!

What did he care if humanity perished, as long as these things allowed him to become a Great Emperor?

This was why he joined the Demon Clan!

Simply to gain greater power!

“Deal!” The old man Cangwu reached out and took the black bead.

Immediately, a strange power surged into his body.

His spiritual energy began to boil, his blood began to circulate!

The feeling was like he was about to break through!

“This is only one percent of the Demon Emperor Bead’s power. When you truly use it, you will feel the complete power of a Great Emperor!”

The old man Cangwu gripped the bead tightly, a mad glint in his eyes.

A Great Emperor!

He had waited for thousands of years, and finally, he had waited!

Tianque City, Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

When Gu Chen and his companions returned to Drunken Immortal Pavilion, it was almost dark.

Tonight was their last night in Tianque City.

Tomorrow morning, they would set off back to the Eastern Region!

Gu Chen ordered a table full of dishes.

Gu Qingshan ate meat heartily, while Li Shuangxu drank wine in large gulps.

Ru Shuang sat by the window, gazing at the night view outside.

Gu Chen sat opposite her.

“Miss Ru,” Gu Chen spoke.

“Hmm?”

“We’ll set off first thing tomorrow morning.”

Ru Shuang nodded. “Okay.”

Gu Chen looked at her, then paused for a moment.

“Your grandfather’s matter can’t be delayed any longer.”

Ru Shuang’s hand tightened; she knew Gu Chen was right.

Her grandfather’s remnant soul couldn’t hold on much longer. A few months—not a long time, not a short time!

The sooner they reached the Gu family, the greater her grandfather’s hope!

Ru Shuang said softly, “I know, thank you, Young Master Gu.”

Gu Chen smiled. “You’re welcome.”

Ru Shuang looked at him, a feeling she couldn’t quite describe welling up inside her.

This man, despite being young himself, always acted like an adult.

He couldn’t speak well, yet he always tried to comfort others.

“Young Master Gu,” Ru Shuang suddenly spoke.

“Hmm?”

“What kind of person was your ancestor?”

Gu Chen thought for a moment, “A very powerful person.”

Ru Shuang was taken aback. “That’s all? Nothing else?”

Ru Shuang was speechless!

How could this person not even describe his own ancestor?!

That was their ancestor!

The strongest person!

Gu Qingshan couldn’t help but speak up from the side.

“Miss Ru, don’t ask him. He doesn’t know anything.”

Gu Chen smiled awkwardly.

“Our ancestor… he was like a god.”

Gu Qingshan put down his wine cup, his spirits lifting.

“Let me tell you,” he said, “our ancestor went into seclusion for five hundred years. When he emerged, he destroyed the Tianming Sect, then the Zixiao Holy Land, and then the Yuheng and Tianxuan Holy Lands!”

“Even the three Quasi-Emperors of the Zhao family wouldn’t dare utter a sound in front of our ancestor!”

“And that beast tide—three Eternal Realm demonic beasts! Our ancestor sent two little girls riding white tigers, and they were driven away with a single word!”

“And what else?” Ru Shuang couldn’t help but ask for more information.

“There’s a lot more. You’ll find out when you get there.”

Ru Shuang looked at Gu Chen.

Gu Chen didn’t speak, everything seemed so ordinary.

She suddenly felt a sense of anticipation.

What kind of place was that?

What kind of person was that ancestor?


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 79 The Extraterrestrial Demons Come to See Their Never-Before-Seen Sister-in-Law
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Deep within the void of the Ancient Desolate Continent lies the neighboring world of the Myriad Prisons Demon Realm.

Both worlds are adjacent, and both are ruled by Great Emperors as their supreme power!

The Myriad Prisons Demon Realm.

This is a world completely different from the Ancient Desolate Continent!

The sky is a dark crimson glow, and the earth is black!

The air is thick with demonic energy; an ordinary person here wouldn't survive a single incense stick's time!

However, this is a paradise for demons!

The denser the demonic energy, the stronger they are.

In this world, demons are the sole rulers!

There are no other races, no humans, no demons, only demons!

For countless years, they have conquered the heavens, plundering the essence of world after world.

With each world plundered, the Demon Realm expands, and the demonic energy thickens.

As a result, their overall strength far surpasses that of the Ancient Desolate Continent!

Moreover, they are ruled by a supreme ruler, the most powerful Demon Emperor in the land!

If they disobeyed, their people would have no reason to live!

Absolute loyalty was paramount!

Deep within the Demon Realm, a colossal black palace stood, and here resided the Demon Emperor!

This was the gathering place of the Demon Clan's powerful warriors.

The Demon Clan numbered in the hundreds of millions, producing countless powerful figures and countless demon soldiers, and nurturing an unparalleled number of powerful beings!

Inside the palace, a dozen figures stood on either side.

Each figure exuded the aura of a quasi-Emperor.

And at the very top, five figures sat side by side.

Five Great Emperors.

The five Demon Kings of the Demon Clan.

Each one was a supreme powerhouse at the Great Emperor realm.

Seated in the very center was the most powerful Demon King, the Annihilation Demon King.

He stood several meters tall, clad in black armor, and only a pair of dark golden eyes were visible!

Those eyes had no pupils!

Only endless darkness and demonic energy remained!

"Has the passage opened?" the Demon Emperor of Annihilation asked in a deep and authoritative voice.

Below, a demon at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm stepped forward and knelt on one knee.

"Your Majesty, the passage is still under repair. Hundreds of thousands of years ago, during the battle, the Great Emperor of the Ancient Desolate Continent sealed the passage before his death. It took us a full hundred thousand years to find the weak point in the seal!"

"How much longer?" the Demon Emperor of Annihilation asked.

"At most three months."

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation nodded, remaining silent.

Another Demon Emperor spoke up, "What's the situation on the Ancient Desolate Continent now? That Great Emperor has been dead for hundreds of thousands of years. The human race should have declined by now, right? Without that Great Emperor's fortune, they're just lambs to the slaughter!"

The demon at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm quickly replied, "Reporting to the Heavenly Demon Emperor, according to our spies, the Ancient Desolate Continent now has very few even at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, let alone a single Great Emperor!"

The Heavenly Demon Emperor laughed loudly, "Hahahaha! Not a single one? Doesn't that mean they're at our mercy?"

"We've already infiltrated some forces on the Ancient Desolate Continent. One of them, a Quasi-Emperor peak cultivator named Elder Cangwu, has already submitted to us."

The Heavenly Demon Emperor raised an eyebrow, "Elder Cangwu? Quasi-Emperor peak?"

"Yes, he recently broke through. He only wanted the Taiyin Spirit Body and that Great Emperor's inheritance. We gave him a Demon Emperor Pearl, allowing him to temporarily possess the power of a Great Emperor and help us break the seal!"

The Heavenly Demon Emperor nodded in satisfaction, "Not bad. Anything else?"

"He told us the champion of the Genius Battle is a young man named Gu Chen, from the Gu family of the Eastern Region. He has three Quasi-Emperor peak protectors, but they are nothing to fear!"

The Annihilation Demon Emperor finally spoke, "The Gu family of the Eastern Region? What's their background?"

"We can't find anything about them, they seem to have appeared out of thin air."

The Annihilation Demon Emperor was silent for a moment.

"Never mind that." He stood up, his dark golden eyes sweeping over everyone in the hall.

The powerful aura they felt was beyond their reach!

This was absolute power!

"Hundreds of thousands of years ago, that Great Emperor of the Desolate Ancient Continent slaughtered countless of our people, severely weakening our Demon Realm."

Everyone felt that killing intent.

They remembered the history of the Demon Race; the Demon Race was all about revenge!

Regardless of right or wrong, if they believed it was right, then it was right!

Now that Great Emperor had fallen, they shouldn't be blamed for their ruthlessness!

"Now, it's time for revenge!"

Inside the hall, all the demons stood up simultaneously, shouting in unison.

"Revenge! Revenge! Revenge!"

The shouts shook the palace.

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation raised his hand, signaling for silence.

"Three months later, the passage will open."

"At that time, our army will press in, slaughtering humanity and plundering our life force! Let our demon race usher in a new era of prosperity!"

"I will make the Ancient Desolate Continent a second Demon Realm."

"Yes!" all the demons responded in unison.

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation sat down again, a cold glint flashing in his dark golden eyes.

"The Ancient Desolate Continent, hundreds of thousands of years of blood debt, must be repaid!"

Meanwhile!

Gu Chen and Ru Shuang sat by the window, a table separating them, not too close!

But the moonlight streaming in cast their shadows close together on the floor!

Just then!

Bang!

The door was kicked open.

"Brother Gu Chen!"

Two little girls rushed in, one on each side like cannonballs.

Before Gu Chen could react, Gu Qingqing had already jumped onto his back, and Gu Huahua burrowed into his arms.

"Why are you two here?!" Gu Chen was breathless from being squeezed.

Gu Qingqing said with a grin, "We came to find you! We've broken through, so we came to play with you!"

Gu Huahua nodded in agreement.

Gu Chen finally managed to get the two little girls off him and turned to look at the door.

The White Tiger lay at the doorway, yawning, looking utterly resigned!

These two little devils are really hard to please.

The tiger can't even be majestic!

Luckily, they weren't here to play, otherwise it would be the one getting scolded!

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue followed behind.

"The two older sisters have arrived too," Gu Chen quickly stood up and bowed.

Yue Qingshuang nodded, remaining silent.

Yue Qingxue smiled, "The Ancestor was worried about the two of them, so he sent us along."

Gu Chen understood.

These two little girls were indeed a bit worrisome!

If they were, they wouldn't have come.

Gu Qingqing jumped off Gu Chen's back, her gaze shifting to Ru Shuang.

Ru Shuang sat by the window, dressed in white, the moonlight illuminating her face, making her seem almost unreal!

Gu Qingqing's eyes lit up instantly.

You think the two little girls came to see Gu Chen?

Wrong!

They weren't here to see Gu Chen. They had often seen Clan Chief Gu Tianhua preparing the betrothal gifts while they were cultivating!

Then they heard that Gu Chen was getting married!

Isn't that Gu Chen, their older brother?

Hearing about the wedding, the two little girls were incredibly excited!

They both wanted to see what their sister-in-law looked like!

Coming to see Gu Chen?

It was just an excuse to avoid being allowed to go out freely by the patriarch!

Gu Chen, irrelevant!

They were just coming to see their sister-in-law, whom they had never met before!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 80: Just Friends! Hidden Dangers
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"Wow!"

She pulled Gu Huahua along, the two little girls huddled together, staring intently at Ru Shuang!

"So beautiful! So beautiful!"

"Even more beautiful than a person in a painting!"

"Brother Gu Chen, who is this?"

Gu Chen's face flushed instantly. "This is Miss Ru, my friend."

"Friend? Just a friend?" Gu Qingqing tilted her head, looking at Gu Chen, then at Ru Shuang.

"What friend?"

"Just a friend."

Gu Qingqing didn't believe her, moving closer to Ru Shuang and scrutinizing her from head to toe.

"Sister, what's your name?"

Ru Shuang felt a little uncomfortable under the two little girls' gazes. "Ru Shuang."

Gu Qingqing was very sweet-talking. "Sister Ru Shuang! What are you to Brother Gu Chen?"

Ru Shuang's face flushed instantly. "A friend."

Gu Qingqing blinked. "A friend? But Uncle Clan Chief said Brother Gu Chen is getting married!"

Gu Chen almost choked on his saliva!

What the heck?

"How come I didn't know?"

"What's that unreliable old man up to now?

Could he have found me a blind date? What will happen if Miss Ru finds out?!"

"What marriage? Who said that?"

Gu Qingqing looked innocent. "Uncle Clan Chief said it! He said you brought your wife back and asked us to see what she looks like!"

Gu Huahua nodded in agreement. "Yes, yes, Uncle Clan Chief even prepared a lot of dowry, it filled the whole room!"

Gu Chen's face turned bright red!

What?! Isn't that Miss Ru Shuang?

Okay then!

"Don't talk nonsense! Miss Ru is just a friend!"

"Why are friends so close?" Gu Qingqing pointed to the shadows of the two people on the ground.

They were very close, almost touching.

Gu Chen looked down and immediately went to the side and whistled.

Ru Shuang saw it too and quickly moved aside.

The two little girls exchanged a glance and burst into laughter.

“Sister-in-law is shy!”

“No, sister-in-law! It’s a friend!” Gu Chen exclaimed urgently, reaching out his hand.

“Why would a friend blush?”

“I’m hot!”

“It’s autumn now.”

Gu Chen fell silent.

Ru Shuang, sitting beside him, also blushed deeply.

Just then, her grandfather’s voice echoed in her mind, “Girl, those two little girls! They’re actually saints!”

Ru Shuang was stunned.

What?

“And they’re only eleven or twelve years old! Have you ever seen a saint who’s eleven or twelve?”

Ru Shuang had never seen one.

Not only had she never seen one, she’d never even heard of one!

How could this be!

“The Gu family is indeed extraordinary.”

Ru Shuang looked at the two little girls, who were chattering excitedly around Gu Chen, pulling his hair and tugging at his clothes, having a great time!

Eleven or twelve-year-old saints!

In Zhongzhou, that would be a prodigy among prodigies!

No! It's impossible for anyone to reach that level!

But in the Gu family, it seems quite ordinary?

Why is that?

She didn't understand!

"Sister-in-law!" Gu Qingqing suddenly leaned over and took Ru Shuang's hand.

"We're not sisters-in-law..." Ru Shuang tried to explain, but was immediately interrupted.

"Sooner or later!" Gu Qingqing said with a grin, "Uncle Clan Chief said that any girl Brother Gu Chen sets his sights on won't escape!"

Ru Shuang was suddenly at a loss for words.

They really are just friends…

Gu Chen was getting impatient. “You two, stop messing around! If you keep this up, I’ll send you back!”

Gu Qingqing retorted confidently, “Ancestor said we can’t run around, but we can follow you! Wherever you go, we’ll go!”

Gu Chen had a headache.

Screw it!

Last time in the secret realm, they promised to follow him, and then they ran off everywhere!

Killing, killing, killing!

Now these two little girls, he couldn’t hit them, couldn’t scold them, and couldn’t chase them away.

But now it wasn’t a matter of fighting or not; these two little girls were already saints, and Gu Chen couldn’t beat them!

“Fine, we’ll leave first thing tomorrow morning. Don’t sleep in.” Gu Chen sighed.

“No, no!” the two little girls said in unison.

Ru Shuang looked at them and couldn’t help but smile.

These two little girls, though mischievous, were very cute.

Moreover, she didn't feel any resentment when they called her "sister-in-law."

"Girl, the Gu family is more complex than we thought! An eleven or twelve-year-old saint—just think how many resources the Gu family must have needed to create such a person!"

Ru Shuang remained silent; she dared not imagine.

How vast must the resources be to create a saint!

Grandpa's tone suddenly lightened. "This is a good thing! The stronger the Gu family, the greater Grandpa's chances of recovery!"

Ru Shuang nodded; a powerful Gu family was ultimately a good thing.

Even if it meant...

She looked at Gu Chen, who was arguing with the two little girls, his face flushed and neck red.

This man, fearless and invincible outside, was completely dominated by the two little girls at home.

She suddenly felt that the Gu family must be a very interesting place!

She felt a little anticipation, but what she hoped for even more was Grandpa's recovery!

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu were also laughing heartily beside her; the two grown men were not good at expressing themselves.

However, the two little girls didn't care about location, speaking coolly and casually.

This troubled Gu Chen greatly, but it was ultimately a good thing!

He had to let Miss Ru Shuang know that Gu Chen liked her!

The next day, the group left the Drunken Immortal Pavilion.

Many people watched their departing figures, but they dared not make a move. Although they possessed the inheritance of the Greedy Emperor, they still wanted to live.

Three Quasi-Emperors at their peak were no joke!

And so, the group began their journey home!

But in the distance, the Cangwu Elder stood atop the mountain, looking at the eight black dots, his brows furrowing slightly.

Several more people had appeared.

He smiled, a hint of disdain flashing in his eyes.

"It doesn't matter, just a few more blood offerings!"

However, his gaze fell upon the two white-clad women at the very back!

Yue Qing Shuang and Yue Qing Xue.

The Cangwu Elder's pupils contracted slightly.

He wasn't astonished by the two women's cultivation level.

It was their striking good looks that excited him.

"So lustrous! Even more lustrous and larger than that spirit body!" the old man murmured.

"Interesting!"

Without further thought, he averted his gaze. "Follow them and see where the Gu family is!"

He strode forward and followed.

He maintained a steady pace, keeping a distance of about a hundred miles.

A family capable of producing three peak Quasi-Emperors must possess unfathomable resources!

Their resources must be countless!

Spirit stones, pills, cultivation techniques, spiritual treasures, and so on!

Any one of these would be enough for him for hundreds of years!

"Once I obtain the Taiyin Spiritual Body, the Great Emperor's inheritance, and then use the Demon Emperor's Pearl to gain the Great Emperor's power," the old man Cangwu's eyes flashed with greed.

"Then, the Gu family's resources will be mine too!"

He licked his lips and quickened his pace.

"Three kills in one! No, four kills!"

"The Taiyin Spiritual Body, the Great Emperor's inheritance, the Gu family's resources, and those two little girls!"

"With these, let alone a Quasi-Emperor peak, even a true Great Emperor isn't impossible!"

The more he thought about it, the more excited he became, and his pace quickened.

But after reaching a hundred miles, he slowed down again.

He couldn't get too close; those three Quasi-Emperor peak protectors weren't pushovers.

He couldn't get too far either; if he lost them, it would be a huge loss.

The old man Cangwu smiled, "Take your time! You can't run away anyway!"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 81 First Visit to the Gu Family, Everyone's a Saint
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

The Gu family home.

Decorated with lanterns and streamers.

Large red lanterns hung from the mountain gate all the way to the back mountain, and three or four festive tents made of spirit wood were erected!

Red ribbons fluttered above the Spirit Liquid Waterfall, announcing that good news was on the horizon!

Anyone who didn't know better would think the Gu family was celebrating a wedding today!

Gu Tianhua stood at the mountain gate, gazing into the distance for what seemed like the umpteenth time!

Gu Yushan, unable to bear it any longer, said, "The patriarch said they won't be back for a few days. Don't stand here like a statue waiting for her husband! You're the clan leader, can't you have some dignity?"

Gu Tianhua didn't even turn his head. "What do you know? This is my daughter-in-law's first visit; I have to see her as soon as possible!"

"She hasn't agreed yet?"

"It's only a matter of time."

Gu Tianhua stroked his chin. "Besides, didn't you see all the things I prepared? That girl will definitely like them!"

Gu Yushan was speechless. "You call that a dowry? That's more like a general store! You brought out all sorts of random things!"

Gu Tianhua waved him away. "Go away, go away! Go keep an eye on those vassal forces and make them cultivate faster. When Chen'er comes back, we have to let that girl see the prestige of our Gu family!"

"Everyone's a Saint! How prestigious that is! My son is the young master; his status is top-notch."

Gu Yushan shook his head and left. He couldn't persuade him!

Stubborn as a mule!

That old rascal!

Stubborn!

This old geezer, his son hasn't even returned yet, and he's already bragging!

He doesn't care about any of that; bragging isn't illegal anyway!

But saying everyone's a Saint gives off a very powerful vibe!

I wonder if those imperial clans are as powerful as the Gu family!

Back mountain.

Gu Tianhua ran up again.

"Ancestor, Ancestor, how many more days until they arrive?"

Gu Xuan opened his eyes, glanced at him, and sighed!

These past few days, this old guy hasn't acted like a clan leader at all!

He's always thinking about his son, leaving all the matters of cultivation and supplementation to the Grand Elder!

It's no good to leave him idle!

I'll have Gu Chen teach him a lesson when he returns!

I'll put him in the God-Locking Tower and let the two of them fight one-on-one!

“Three days.”

“Three days? That’s great!”

Gu Tianhua rubbed his hands together. “My daughter-in-law will be here in three days. I need to go and make preparations, and reinforce those wedding tents, just in case it’s windy or rainy!”

“She’s a King Realm expert; she’s not afraid of wind or rain.”

“She still needs reinforcement, just in case?”

Gu Xuan was too lazy to pay him any attention; he’d already given up.

He was considering whether they should replace him as clan leader!

He felt this kid had gone crazy over his son’s affairs!

But Gu Chen’s judgment was really good!

He still needed to praise him and say what he needed to say!

The old man was unreliable, but the son was reliable!

Gu Tianhua rambled on for a long time, and seeing that the ancestor wasn’t paying attention, he reluctantly left.

Having reached the mountainside, he encountered the Second Elder, Gu Kong.

Gu Kong was sitting on a rock, looking at the distant scenery, his face full of melancholy.

“Brother, what’s wrong?” Gu Tianhua asked.

Gu Kong sighed, "My Qingshan has been away with the young master for so long, I wonder how he's doing."

"Your son? He's doing great, he made it into the top 32 of the Genius Battle."

Gu Kong was taken aback, "Top 32? That kid made it into the top 32?"

"Of course! The young master took him out, how could he be bad? Just wait and enjoy the good life!"

Gu Kong sighed again, "But the young master brought a wife back, while my Qingshan hasn't even shown up."

Gu Tianhua chuckled, "Well, there's nothing I can do about it, your son's a disappointment."

Gu Kong glared at him, "Your son's a disappointment?"

"Of course! The girl my son's eye is third on the beauty list!"

Gu Tianhua puffed out his chest!

"Go ask around the entire Eastern Region, whose son has that kind of ability?"

This guy, his son isn't even married yet, and he's already showing off?

It left Gu Kong speechless!

"Alright, alright, I'm not talking to you anymore."

With that, Gu Tianhua disappeared in a flash!

Gu Kong sat on a rock, looking at the distant horizon, "Qingshan! You should bring one back too! Dad doesn't care, as long as it's a woman!"

Dad doesn't care, anyway, it's not mine!

Three days later.

Gu Chen and his group were mostly gone!

The spirit ship dock in Tianyuan Prefecture was just ahead; beyond the dock lay the Eastern Region.

Two more days' walk, and we'd be home.

Gu Chen walked at the very front.

Gu Qingqing, riding her white tiger, kept running ahead and then back, making a ruckus!

Ru Shuang walked beside Gu Chen, gazing at the distant horizon, her heart filled with both anticipation and trepidation.

"Miss Ru, we're almost there," Gu Chen said.

Ru Shuang nodded. "Yes."

"Don't be nervous, my family is all well."

Ru Shuang glanced at him. "How did you know I was nervous?"

"Your hands are shaking again."

Ru Shuang looked down; her hands were indeed trembling.

She quickly hid them behind her back.

Gu Chen smiled but didn't speak.

A hundred miles away.

The old man of Cangwu stood atop a mountain, watching the direction of the group, his eyes turning sinister!

"I'll make my move once you reach the Gu family! Then I'll wipe them all out!"

He licked his lips, his eyes filled with greed.

The Taiyin Spiritual Body, the Great Emperor's inheritance, the Gu family's resources, and those two women in white.

They're all his.

The spirit ship quickly docked at the Tianyuan Prefecture pier!

The moment she stepped onto the Eastern Region's soil, Ru Shuang clearly felt the spiritual energy was much thinner.

It couldn't compare to the Central Region!

She didn't say this aloud, because she hadn't reached the Gu family yet.

Gu Qingqing rode a white tiger, leading the way.

"Sister-in-law, hurry! We're almost there!"

Ru Shuang had given up trying to correct him.

Let him call her that!

It's pointless to change his mind anyway!

Two days later.

The group finally saw the Gu family's mountain gate.

Ru Shuang stopped.

She thought she was mentally prepared.

Gu Qingshan had told her about the Gu family, and Gu Chen had also mentioned it; the two little girls had boasted about it all the way!

But when she saw it with her own eyes, she was still stunned!

The mountain gate wasn't some magnificent structure, just two old trees with a stone between them!

The stone was engraved with the single character "Gu"!

Very ordinary!

Behind the mountain gate lay a world she had never seen before!

Nine mountain peaks pierced the clouds, shrouded in spiritual mist, with immortal veins meandering through them like luminous dragons!

A waterfall cascaded down from the highest peak!

The spiritual mist solidified, flowing slowly through the valleys like rivers!

Below the waterfall was a pool of spiritual liquid.

The spiritual liquid was crystal clear, shimmering with a faint spiritual light.

Trees grew beside the pool, laden with fruit.

Ginseng fruit.

Ru Shuang had seen it in ancient texts; it blossomed every three thousand years, bore fruit every three thousand years, and matured every three thousand years!

One tree can add 47,000 years to one's lifespan!

There's a whole grove of them here!

Not just one, but a whole grove!

And there are Enlightenment Tea Trees nearby!

Planted right by the roadside, like ordinary landscaping trees!

The path is paved with spirit jade, soft underfoot, spiritual energy flowing into your body, making you feel completely comfortable!

"This!"

Ru Shuang opened her mouth.

Your home really looks like this?!

"Girl, I've never seen anything like it in all my years!"

"You've been to those imperial clans in Zhongzhou, have you ever seen any imperial clan with such extravagance?!"

Ru Shuang shook her head, she hadn't seen it!

Not a single one!

This can't even be described as luxurious, it's downright extravagant!

Extravagant!

Grandpa looked to the other side, utterly shocked. "Look at those people! They're all Saints!"

Ru Shuang looked at the people cultivating in the square.

A dense, dark mass, at least several thousand people.

Each one exuded the aura of a Saint.

Several thousand Saints.

What does that mean?

Perhaps all the Saints in the entire Central Continent combined wouldn't even equal the number of Saints the Gu family possessed!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 82 Take your time, there's no rush! There's something strange about this jade pendant!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

"Sister-in-law, what are you standing there for? Come on!"

Two little maids pulled her hands.

Ru Shuang snapped out of her daze and followed them inside.

She hadn't gone far when she stopped again.

Because she saw what was planted by the roadside.

Ten-thousand-year-old spiritual herbs, planted like weeds by the roadside.

Not just one or two, but patches of them.

"These are all spiritual herbs?!"

These are probably even older than her grandfather!

"Yes, the ancestor said they take up too much space, so he planted them by the roadside," Gu Huahua said casually.

Too much space, planted by the roadside?

That's an exaggeration!

"Sister-in-law, look over there."

Gu Qingqing pointed to a mountain peak in the distance.

On the mountainside stood a palace, emitting a faint spiritual light.

"That's the Cultivation Technique Hall, there are hundreds of Quasi-Emperor level cultivation techniques inside!"

Ru Shuang's mind was buzzing.

Quasi-Emperor level cultivation techniques?

And hundreds of them!

There are probably fewer than a hundred Quasi-Emperor level cultivation techniques in the entire Central Continent, and the Gu family has hundreds?!

“Sister-in-law, there’s another one over there.”

Gu Huahua pointed to another mountain peak.

“That’s the Pill Hall. There are mountains of Emperor-level pills inside. The Ancestor said we should eat them like candy!”

Eating Emperor-level pills like candy?

Is this person even speaking human language?

“Sister-in-law, why aren’t you saying anything?”

Gu Qingqing turned to look at her.

Ru Shuang took a deep breath. “I’m digesting.”

Gu Qingqing didn’t understand. “Digesting what?”

Ru Shuang didn’t answer!

These two little girls are really innocent!

She didn’t know how to answer!

She couldn’t very well say, “I’m digesting how outrageous your Gu family is, could she?!”

Ru Shuang asked in her heart, "Grandpa, have you ever seen a family like this?"

Grandpa's answer was crisp, "No! I've never even heard of one, let alone seen one."

"Those imperial families of Zhongzhou aren't even worthy of carrying the Gu family's shoes!"

This was the truth. He had seen so many imperial families, but he had never seen such an extravagant family!

"The young master is back!"

A shout rang out from the square.

Immediately afterward, the entire Gu family erupted in cheers!

"The young master is back!"

"The young master is back!"

The voices rose and fell, echoing from the square to the mountainside.

From the mountainside to the summit, from the summit to the back mountain!

Gu Tianhua was chatting with his ancestor on the back mountain when he heard the shouts. He sprang to life like a spring.

"He's back?!"

He turned and ran, faster than a spirit ship.

From the back mountain to the mountain gate, a distance of dozens of miles, he covered in mere breaths!

Gu Tianhua landed breathlessly at the mountain gate, his eyes immediately falling on Gu Chen!

Then his gaze was fixed on her!

Not on Gu Chen, but on the woman in white beside him!

Gu Tianhua's mouth gaped open.

"Father,"

Gu Chen called out.

Gu Tianhua didn't react.

"Father!"

Gu Tianhua snapped out of his daze, shoved Gu Chen aside, and walked up to Ru Shuang.

"You're Miss Ru Shuang?"

Ru Shuang was startled by this display. "Yes, yes."

Gu Tianhua looked her up and down, increasingly pleased.

"Good! Good! Good!"

He said "good" three times, beaming from ear to ear.

"Miss, I'm Gu Chen's father, Gu Tianhua."

Ru Shuang quickly bowed. "Uncle, hello."

Gu Tianhua was even more delighted. "Good, good, good! Miss, you must be tired from your journey? Are you hungry? Thirsty?"

"I'm alright."

“No, you must be tired,”

Gu Tianhua turned around and called to those behind him,

“Someone! Prepare tea and snacks! Bring up all the good things I prepared!”

“Uncle, really, no need.”

“Yes, yes!” Gu Tianhua interrupted her.

“It’s your first time here, young lady, we can’t neglect you.”

He looked around and said, “This isn’t the place to talk. Come on, I’ll show you around the Gu family estate.”

“Our Gu family estate isn’t very big, but the scenery is quite nice.”

Ru Shuang looked at Gu Chen.

Gu Chen nodded, “Go ahead.”

“Sister-in-law, hurry up!” Gu Qingqing pushed her.

“Yes, yes, Uncle Clan Chief prepared so many things!” Gu Huahua chimed in.

Ru Shuang was pushed forward.

Gu Tianhua walked ahead, introducing the various things as he went.

"This is the Spirit Liquid Waterfall; we usually get our drinking water from here."

"This is the Ginseng Fruit Tree; eat the ripe fruit as you like, like dessert."

"This is the Enlightenment Tea Tree; steeping it in water can help one achieve enlightenment; we drink it like tea."

"This is the Cultivation Technique Hall; there are hundreds of quasi-Emperor level cultivation techniques inside. Miss, you can go in and take a look if you're interested."

"This is the Pill Hall; Emperor-level pills are piled up like mountains. Miss, take whatever you need."

...

As Ru Shuang listened, she became numb.

Drinking it like water, eating it like snacks, eating it like candy, and so on.

These were words she had only ever seen in storybooks before.

Is this a storyteller telling me stories?

Is this the world storytellers speak of?

But it seems real!

"Girl, in this life, Grandpa has truly seen the light."

Ru Shuang felt the same way!

"And have you noticed? The entire Gu family is really welcoming to you."

Ru Shuang noticed.

Everyone who saw her smiled broadly, as if she were their own daughter-in-law!

Although a little embarrassed, she was undeniably charmed!

“Uncle,” Ru Shuang began.

“Hmm?”

“Young Master Gu, he…”

“Young Master Gu? You mean Chen’er? Miss, don’t call him Young Master Gu, just call him Chen’er, it’s more intimate.”

Ru Shuang’s face flushed instantly.

“Uncle, Young Master Gu and I are just friends.”

“Friends? That’s even better!” Gu Tianhua didn’t care at all, “Let’s start as friends, take it slow, no rush.”

Ru Shuang was instantly embarrassed.

This father is even less reliable than his son!

Gu Chen didn’t go with them to tour the Gu family estate.

He turned and walked towards the back mountain.

“Ancestor,” Gu Chen approached and knelt on one knee.

Gu Xuan opened his eyes and glanced at him.

"You're back?"

"Yes, I'm back."

Gu Chen took the wooden box from his storage bag and presented it with both hands.

"Ancestor, this is the champion's reward for the Genius Battle: the inheritance of an Emperor."

Gu Xuan took the box and opened it.

A jade pendant, emitting a faint light.

It was the aura of an Emperor!

Gu Xuan glanced at it, closed the box, and casually placed it aside.

Gu Chen was stunned.

"Ancestor, aren't you going to take a look?"

"It's quite ordinary."

The inheritance of an Emperor, ordinary?

If this got out, the imperial clans of Central Continent would be furious.

Gu Chen didn't say anything. He knew that in his ancestor's eyes, this thing was probably really quite ordinary.

Gu Xuan glanced at him, "But you did well this time! The champion has brought honor to the Gu family!"

Gu Chen was secretly pleased, but tried to appear calm.

“Of course.”

Gu Xuan added, “That girl is quite nice too. I’ll help you with her matter.”

Gu Chen’s face flushed instantly. He hadn’t expected the Ancestor to bring up that matter so readily.

It seemed that the soul body within Ru Shuang really wasn’t a problem for the Ancestor.

“Ancestor, Miss Ru and I are just friends.”

“Hmm, friends, girlfriend, is that right?” Gu Xuan smiled.

Gu Chen stood awkwardly for a moment, then turned to leave.

“Wait.”

Gu Xuan called after him.

Gu Chen turned back.

Gu Xuan picked up the wooden box and opened it again.

The jade pendant was still the same jade pendant.

Gu Xuan’s brow furrowed slightly.

Not because of the jade pendant itself.

It was because of what was inside the jade pendant.

The Great Emperor’s inheritance was real, but there was something else inside.

A faint aura.

So faint that even a Quasi-Emperor couldn’t detect it.

This jade pendant is strange!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 83 The Carefree Emperor, the Age of Dharma Decline?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Xuan's divine sense entered the jade pendant!

Deep within, there was a remnant soul!

So faint it was almost dissipating!

That aura... it was that of a Great Emperor!

The Great Emperor from hundreds of thousands of years ago.

He wasn't truly dead; a wisp of his remnant soul remained within the jade pendant!

However, a wisp of black energy swirled around his remnant soul!

It was demonic energy!

Gu Xuan frowned!

Not because the jade pendant contained demonic energy, but because this Great Emperor's remnant soul seemed to have been possessed by a demon!

Gu Xuan withdrew his divine sense and placed the jade pendant aside.

"Ancestor, what's wrong?" Gu Chen asked疑惑地问道.

Gu Xuan interrupted him. "More than enough time. Don't come out until you break through!"

Gu Chen opened his mouth, was he really pouting?

The Ancestor had spoken; it seemed there was no room for negotiation!

If the three of them didn't break through, they wouldn't receive the reward, and they would be the only ones not Saints!

Gu Xuan had no choice but to push them!

"Yes."

He took two steps, then turned back and asked, "Ancestor, what about Miss Ru's matter?"

"I will handle it myself."

Great!

She will definitely thank me!

I'll reward myself with a flying kiss!

After Gu Chen left, Gu Xuan picked up the jade pendant.

Crack!

A wisp of black energy shot out from the jade pendant!

The demonic energy was so dense it was almost solidified!

Gu Xuan raised his hand and gently pressed down!

Easily suppressed!

The black energy was forced back, along with the remnant soul, frozen in mid-air.

The remnant soul cried out.

"Kill...kill me...I'm going to become a demon..."

Gu Xuan ignored it and waved his hand.

A golden light shot into the remnant soul!

Then the demonic energy rapidly dissipated!

A moment later, the demonic energy vanished, and the remnant soul quieted down.

A transparent phantom hovered in mid-air.

White-haired, its eyes much clearer.

It looked around blankly, somewhat bewildered.

"Where...where am I?"

Its voice was very weak.

Gu Xuan looked at him. "The Ancient Desolate Continent."

The man was stunned. "The Ancient Desolate Continent? I'm still alive?"

"Your remnant soul is about to dissipate."

The man looked down at his hands; they were transparent, almost invisible.

He gave a bitter laugh. "Yes, my remnant soul is about to dissipate."

He looked up at Gu Xuan, utterly shocked!

Because he couldn't see through the person before him.

Not that he couldn't see through his cultivation level, but that he couldn't sense him at all!

It was as if the person sitting before him wasn't a person, but the very universe itself!

"Who are you?"

"The patriarch of the Gu family."

The man was silent for a moment.

The Gu family?

He'd never heard of them.

They must be a new power; it's normal not to know them.

Then, he cupped his hands in thanks. "Thank you. I am Xiaoyao."

"The Great Emperor who repelled the demons and sealed the passage hundreds of thousands of years ago?"

"Yes."

The phantom of the Xiaoyao Great Emperor appeared in mid-air.

He looked down at his hands and gave a bitter laugh.

"Hundreds of thousands of years have passed. I never thought I'd wake up again."

Gu Xuan leaned back in his recliner. "Tell me, what happened back then?"

Xiaoyao remained silent for a moment before slowly recounting the story.

Years ago, the demon race launched a massive invasion, with five Demon Emperors, countless Quasi-Emperors, and a tidal wave of demonic soldiers!

The imperial clans, holy lands, and rogue cultivators of the Ancient Desolate Continent all went to the battlefield!

After a century of fighting, ninety percent perished!

His voice was calm, but suddenly a light shone in his eyes.

It was the light of war!

“Back then, I alone faced five Demon Emperors! I fought for seven days and seven nights! I killed three, severely wounded two, and drove them back to the Demon Realm!”

“But I was also severely injured in the process; demonic energy invaded my soul!”

Here, the demonic energy pierced by the Demon Emperor's claws seeped into the wound, and I can't get rid of it no matter what I do.”

Gu Xuan nodded.

That wound wasn't caused by ordinary demonic energy; it was the Demon Emperor's primordial demonic energy, deeply ingrained in the soul, extremely difficult to remove.

Gu Xuan realized this; without a powerful physique and sacred medicine, it would be impossible to control.

"I originally intended to go into seclusion to heal my injuries, but I discovered the Age of Dharma Decline is approaching!"

Xiaoyao sighed. "The spiritual energy of heaven and earth is beginning to decline, the laws of the Great Dao are beginning to conceal themselves, becoming an Emperor is increasingly difficult, even a Quasi-Emperor is difficult, and as for the path to Immortality, it's even more impossible!"

"Back then, I calculated that in at most tens of thousands of years, the Ancient Desolate Continent would enter the Age of Dharma Decline!"

"At that time, let alone Great Emperors, even Saints will be hard to find!"

He looked at Gu Xuan, "And you are the strangest person I've ever met."

Gu Xuan raised an eyebrow, "Strange?"

"In the Age of Dharma Decline, you actually became an Emperor!"

Xiaoyao stared at him, looking at something that seemed impossible!

"Moreover, the aura on your body is stronger than mine at my peak!"

This is illogical!

Gu Xuan smiled, offering no explanation.

There was no need to discuss the system with a remnant soul.

Xiaoyao didn't press further, continuing to recount the events of that year.

He knew the Demon Clan would definitely return!

So he reinforced the passage's seal, but it could only last for a few hundred thousand years. After that, the seal would loosen, and the demons would return!

He couldn't let the Ancient Desolate Continent be without a Great Emperor.

Therefore, he left behind this jade pendant, sealing away a portion of his inheritance and separating a wisp of his uncorrupted remnant soul!

When the Dharma-ending Age passed and spiritual energy revived, a destined person would obtain the jade pendant, and his remnant soul would awaken, bestowing upon him the inheritance!

Even if he couldn't become a Great Emperor, a peak Quasi-Emperor would be enough to protect the continent!

This was his worst-case scenario plan!

He just hadn't expected that even his supposedly uncorrupted remnant soul would be corrupted by the demons!

He sighed. So many years of planning had almost ruined him, and also the person who obtained the jade pendant!

He dared not imagine that the destined person who obtained the jade pendant wasn't receiving the inheritance, but rather courting death!

He also hadn't expected the demons' actions to come so quickly!

He looked at Gu Xuan.

"Thank you."

Gu Xuan waved his hand. "You've guarded the Ancient Desolate Continent for hundreds of thousands of years; you've done your best."

Xiaoyao stood up, looking at the distant sky.

"The seal is about to break. I can feel the aura from the Demon Realm growing stronger!"

"The passage will open in three months at most."

He turned to Gu Xuan. "What do you plan to do?"

Gu Xuan took a sip of tea, dismissively saying, "Wait for them to come."

Xiaoyao was taken aback.

What do you mean, wait for them to come? Wouldn't it be over once they arrive?

"Wait? You're not going to take the initiative?"

"Taking the initiative is too tiring. Let's wait for them to come, then wipe them out in one fell swoop, saving time and effort."

He had lived for hundreds of thousands of years and had never seen such an arrogant person.

You're pretty arrogant, aren't you?

"You alone? Can you take on five?" Xiaoyao asked.

Gu Xuan thought for a moment, "Easy."

Xiaoyao: "?"

Easy?

That's as if taking on five Demon Emperors is as easy as eating and drinking!

What a load of bull!

"There are many powerful beings in the Demon Realm. Can you handle them all by yourself?"

Gu Xuan glanced at him, "Who said I'm alone?"

Xiaoyao was stunned.

Gu Xuan raised his hand and pointed into the distance.

Jian Wuji, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, Bai Yujing, and Ye Wuhen were feeding fish by the pond.

The four divine beasts were lying on the back mountain, basking in the sun!

Xiaoyao's divine sense swept over!

"Five Great Emperors?!"

His voice changed.

"There are four more divine beasts, also Great Emperors."

Xiaoyao was completely speechless.

Nine Great Emperors!

What kind of concept is that?!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 84: Recovering His Body, the Old Man of Cangwu Attacks the Mountain Gate
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

And each of them possessed an aura stronger than his!

Stronger than just a little!

Bor, I was blind to their true power!

Ten Great Emperors!

A full ten!

If they don't beat the Demon Race to a pulp, it'll be a disaster!

Everyone present possessed an aura stronger than his!

Back then, he was a ruthless character who could take on five opponents at once, these people before him were probably even more ruthless!

Three punches to shatter the Demon Race's soul, sir, I'm just a nice guy!

Ten Great Emperors had emerged in the Age of Dharma Decline!

What did that mean?

He didn't know.

But he knew one thing.

The Ancient Desolate Continent was safe.

This is a guaranteed win!

It's a sure thing!

Let the Demons come!

"You've guarded it for hundreds of thousands of years; it hasn't been easy," Gu Xuan suddenly said.

One word, spoken casually!

That was a legendary technique, something even he couldn't do at his peak!

Could this person before him be an immortal?!

Could this person truly be able to do it?

Gu Xuan waved his hand, "Don't thank me. Thank that child; he brought you back."

Xiaoyao straightened up, looking towards the distant Locking God Tower.

Gu Chen was in seclusion inside the tower, his sword intent soaring to the heavens.

"That child is indeed remarkable," Xiaoyao said.

Gu Xuan put down his teacup and used his divine sense to send a telepathic message to Gu Tianhua.

“Stop playing around, bring that girl over here.”

Meanwhile, Gu Tianhua was showing Ru Shuang around the pill hall, enthusiastically talking about a single Emperor-grade pill.

“Look, Miss, this is an Emperor-grade healing pill. Take one if you're injured, and you'll be fine immediately.”

“This is an Emperor-grade breakthrough pill. Take one when you break through, and your success rate will double.”

“This is an Emperor-grade aphrodisiac…”

Ahem!

Gu Tianhua realized he had taken the wrong pill and immediately took out another one from beside him.

“This is an Emperor-grade soul-nourishing pill. Your grandfather probably needs one for nourishing his soul.”

Ru Shuang took the bottle of Emperor-grade soul-nourishing pills, her hands trembling.

This stuff, one of it outside could cause a war among imperial clans!

The Gu family just casually gave her a whole bottle?

"Uncle, this is too precious!"

Gu Tianhua waved his hand, "Not precious at all, we have plenty at home to eat like candy."

Just then, Gu Xuan's voice rang in his mind.

Gu Tianhua shuddered. "Miss, let's go. The Ancestor wants to see you."

Ru Shuang's heart raced.

The Gu family patriarch.

That unfathomable person!

Finally, she was going to see him!

Back mountain.

Gu Tianhua led Ru Shuang up.

"Ancestor, the person has been brought."

Gu Xuan nodded, looking at Ru Shuang.

Ru Shuang stiffened, feeling as if she had been seen through.

A golden fruit, radiating a rich life force!

The other was a pure white herb, its leaves glistening with dew-like spiritual sap!

Xiaoyao's pupils constricted sharply!

That was...!

"The Nine-Turn Resurrection Fruit? The Primordial Soul-Sealing Grass?"

He had only heard of these two things in ancient legends.

The Nine-Turn Resurrection Fruit: blooms every ninety thousand years, bears fruit every ninety thousand years, and matures every ninety thousand years!

One fruit can reshape the physical body, bringing the dead back to life and healing even the most grievous wounds!

The Primordial Soul-Sealing Grass: a divine object from the beginning of time.

A single leaf can reunite a remnant soul; a complete plant can even revive the dead!

This Primordial Soul-Sealing Grass was actually a complete plant!

Either of these two things would be enough to drive the entire continent mad!

Even at his peak, he only dared to dream of them; he had never seen them in person!

How could this person have everything?

"You actually have something like this!"

Gu Xuan glanced at him. "Is this thing very precious?"

Gu Xuan was joking, wasn't he?

Very precious?

That's as if these two things are as common as cabbages!

There's a limit to jokes!

Gu Xuan ignored him and waved his hand.

The Nine-Turn Rejuvenation Fruit flew towards Ru Feng, Ru Shuang's grandfather.

The Primordial Soul Grass flew towards Xiao Yao.

Two beams of light shone simultaneously!

The golden light enveloped Ru Feng, and his remnant soul began to solidify.

The white light enveloped Xiao Yao, and he too began to solidify.

After an incense stick's time, the light dissipated.

Ru Feng stood on the ground, solidified!

He looked down at his hands, touched his old face, and tears streamed down his face.

He never thought this day would come!

Xiao Yao also stood on the ground, clenching his fists, feeling the spiritual energy flowing again within his body!

Her strength had recovered to the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, but her physical body was still at the Great Emperor level!

“Grandpa!” Ru Shuang rushed forward and hugged her grandfather.

Ru Feng hugged her back, “Alright, alright, don’t cry, Grandpa’s fine.”

Gu Tianhua stood to the side, his eyes also reddening.

Gu Xuan glanced at him, “Your son hasn’t had a tough time either.”

Gu Tianhua chuckled, “That’s true! But she’s so beautiful, this kid’s lucky!”

Ru Feng hugged Ru Shuang and cried for a while, then let go of her, walked to Gu Xuan, and bowed deeply.

“Thank you for saving my life, Ancestor.”

Gu Xuan waved his hand, “You are Ru Shuang’s grandfather, so you are an elder of the Gu family. No need to be so polite.”

Ru Shuang blushed, “Grandpa, it’s not what you think…”

Ru Shuang's face turned even redder.

That was tacit agreement!

Gu Tianhua grinned from ear to ear beside her.

At this very moment, outside the Gu family's mountain gate, an attack struck the protective array.

Boom!

The attack exploded, but the protective array remained unmoved.

The Cangwu Elder stood outside the mountain gate, gazing at this paradise before him, his eyes filled with greed.

Spiritual liquid, ginseng fruit, enlightenment tea, spiritual jade!

A single one of these would be enough for him to use for hundreds of years.

"Good stuff, all good stuff." He licked his lips.

He raised his hand and launched another attack.

Boom!

Chapter 85 Nine Emperors Assume Defense, Forming a Standoff

A true Great Emperor!

Below a Great Emperor, all are ants. Even if there are many peak Quasi-Emperors, they are nothing more than a slap in the face of a Great Emperor!

A peak Quasi-Emperor, and you all go for it?

He's targeting the honest!

White Tiger: "I was the one who spotted him first, I should go first!"

Now that he'd come all the way home, they wouldn't be so lenient!

Azure Dragon: "What's the use of you finding him? One breath from my dragon breath and it's over."

Black Tortoise: "Stop arguing, all of you! My shell is hard, he can't hurt me, one bump and he's done for!"

That quasi-Emperor at the ninth level who forced her father to give her away.

But the Gu family was unlike anything he had ever seen!

Ten Great Emperors, ruling over a region!

It wasn't fearlessness, it was disdain!

A fierce tiger wouldn't fly into a rage because an ant bit it.

When the ant crawled in front of it, it would just slap it dead!

This was Gu Chen's wife; they couldn't be allowed to get hurt. He had finally found a wife.

It wasn't easy!

Jian Wuji walked in front, Bai Yujing and Ye Wuhen followed behind, with Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue on either side.

Xiaoyao followed at the very back.

These were all Great Emperors; his prestige was completely gone!

Outside the mountain gate.

The old man from Cangwu stood in the void, arms crossed, staring at the celestial realm within the protective array.

Waiting for the Gu family to emerge!

Behind him followed a man and a woman, dressed in snow-white robes.

Further behind were four colossal creatures:

A White Tiger, a Azure Dragon, a Black Tortoise, and a Phoenix.

The old man from Cangwu's pupils constricted sharply.

Divine beasts?

And their aura?!

He couldn't sense them!

Not hidden, but completely undetectable!

But then, he smiled.

Not out of fear, but out of disdain!

So what if they were divine beasts? So what if they were at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm? He possessed the Demon Emperor Pearl; once its power was fully unleashed, he would be an Emperor!

Below the Emperor, all are ants!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 86 The clamoring Cangwu, his life was betrayed!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Terrifying beyond their comprehension!

Anyway, they could just stay in the back!

Growing up healthily!

Hahaha!

They didn't take him seriously at all!

Half-step Great Emperor…

Great Emperor Level One!

That was a Great Emperor!

It disintegrated into countless fragments, dissipating into the air!

The old man's pupils constricted sharply!

Could this thing be fake?

Then wouldn't he be ganged up on and killed?!

Impossible!

They remained standing there, neither attacking nor even looking at him.

The old man felt it; their auras had locked down this entire area!

His Emperor-level power was being suppressed!

Not suppressed by one person, but suppressed by all nine simultaneously!

He could only unleash less than one percent of his power!

"You!"

The old man's expression completely changed.

"Trying to play dirty tricks on me?"

No one paid him any attention.

The old man of Cangwu gritted his teeth and activated the Demon Emperor Pearl once more!

The black light surged, and his aura soared again!

But the nine streams of energy also intensified!

No matter how much he increased, it was suppressed!

He could only ever unleash less than one percent of his full power!

"Damn it!"

The old man of Cangwu went completely mad!

He refused to believe it and activated the Demon Emperor Pearl, drawing all its power outwards!

His body began to swell, his blood vessels bulged, his skin cracked, as if it were about to burst!

But he couldn't care less about that now; he had to take down these people today!

The nine streams of energy also intensified!

No matter how much he increased, it was suppressed!

He could only ever unleash less than one percent of his full power!

"Damn it! Damn it! Damn it!"

He was furious!

But the crater's diameter was less than three meters!

He was stunned.

His punch, delivered by a Great Emperor, couldn't even create a crater?!

The old man Cangwu's expression completely changed!

These people's cultivation wasn't just at the peak of Quasi-Emperor level!

They were Great Emperors!

True Great Emperors!

Nine Great Emperors?!

Only in this way could they suppress him!

It's over!

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 87 Destroying Cangwu, Gu Xuan: Your cultivation is too low.

The old man from Cangwu felt as if a mountain was pressing down on him!

The Demon Clan!

Hundreds of thousands of years ago, the Demon Clan launched a massive invasion, causing widespread devastation and killing ninety percent of the population!

In the end, a Great Emperor single-handedly repelled the Demon Clan and sealed the passage. That Great Emperor paid the ultimate price!

He could even turn against his own people!

This kind of person deserves to die!

Ru Shuang didn't waste any words. She raised her sword, showing no mercy to such a person!

His eyes were filled with resentment and confusion!

"You!"

Before he could finish speaking!

The sword light surged, its immense power instantly engulfing his body!

Leaving no trace behind!

The old man of Cangwu's mouth still moved, but no sound came out!

He went from anger to despair, yet he couldn't change anything. Then, his eyes vanished!

Three breaths later, the Cangwu Elder completely disappeared!

Not even ashes remained; he turned into thin air!

Ru Shuang stood there, her hands trembling. The wind blew her hair, dispersing the lingering black aura in the air.

Ru Feng patted her back.

"However, it's not truly over yet."

Ru Shuang looked up, her eyes blurred with tears as she looked at her grandfather.

"The Cangwu Elder was just a dog. The real wolf that's going to bite hasn't come yet!"

The Demon Clan!

The Demon Clan that nearly destroyed the Ancient Desolate Continent hundreds of thousands of years ago.

They're coming!

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan nodded, showing no reaction.

He spoke casually, showing no concern whatsoever.

If they dare to come, then we'll fight them again!

"Don't worry."

Ru Feng glanced at the people on the back mountain, then at the unfathomable ancestor before him, and understood!

Indeed, there was nothing to worry about!

Ru Shuang nodded as well, turning to leave.
