Gu Xuan waved his hand, saying nothing more.

Ru Shuang left with her grandfather.

Xiao Yao stood still, not moving.

Gu Xuan glanced at him. "Is something wrong?"

Xiao Yao was silent for a moment. "I want to stay in the Gu family."

Gu Xuan raised an eyebrow.

Xiao Yao smiled bitterly. "My power from back then was gone hundreds of thousands of years ago. Now, in the Desolate Ancient Continent, probably very few people even know me. I have nowhere to go, and besides..."

He glanced at the Great Emperors on the back mountain.

"This place is quite nice, it has everything."

He just wanted to get these resources for free!

After all, he wasn't this extravagant back then; this place has everything!

The resources are excellent, and the spiritual energy is abundant; returning to his peak is only a matter of time!

Gu Xuan smiled, "Alright, then stay. The Strike Force Hall is short-handed."

Xiaoyao was taken aback. "Strike Force Hall?"

"Yes, a place specifically for handling troublesome matters. From now on, you'll be a member of the Strike Force Hall."

Xiaoyao's lips twitched.

At his peak, he was a seventh-level Great Emperor, a Great Emperor who saved the Ancient Desolate Continent hundreds of thousands of years ago. Now he's a henchman for the Strike Force Hall?

But it doesn't seem like a bad deal!

Here, there are Great Emperors as protectors, Great Emperors as divine beasts, and Great Emperors as henchmen!

He was one of them!

Moreover, the resources here are abundant. Forget recovering to his peak, even reaching a higher level is entirely possible!

“Thank you, Ancestor,” Xiaoyao said, cupping his hands.

Gu Xuan glanced at Xiaoyao, his tone casual, “However, your cultivation is too low.”

Xiaoyao’s lips twitched.

He was a peak Quasi-Emperor, a Body Emperor level expert—he could still roam freely outside!

But in front of this group in the back mountain, he was indeed no match!

“You need to recover quickly; these people are all stronger than you were at your peak.”

This was the truth, though he didn’t want to admit it!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 88 Resources Smash in My Face, Xiaoyao: From Now On, I'm the Gu Family's Dog!
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Xuan raised his hand and started pulling things out of the system's inventory.

A pile of items lay on the ground, lumped together like trash!

He'd received so many things from the system's daily check-ins that Gu Xuan had only taken out a handful!

Xiao Yao looked at them and was dumbfounded!

Nine Revolutions Breakthrough Pill, Emperor-rank, high-grade, used to break through bottlenecks!

He'd searched for thousands of years and hadn't found a single one!

Yet there were 180 of them here!

Primordial Soul Pill, Emperor-rank, high-grade, used to strengthen the soul!

There were 288 of them in one bottle!

Myriad Laws Return to Origin Pill, Emperor-rank, high-grade, used to enhance comprehension of laws!

He'd been fortunate enough to obtain one years ago, which not only greatly increased his law power but also allowed him to break through to a higher realm!

There were a full 1001 of them here!

And all sorts of miscellaneous pills, all Emperor-rank, high-grade, with at least a hundred of each!

Wait, are they really going to eat them like candy?!

Even if he were to absorb them all, it would probably take over a thousand years!

Why not just take pills and reach the Great Emperor realm without cultivating?!

But it's not over yet!

Gu Xuan raised his hand again, and dozens of stones flew out!

Enlightenment Stones, Time-Space Stones, Chaos Stones!

Each one exuded a suffocating aura!

Enlightenment Stones allow you to comprehend the Dao as easily as drinking water!

Time-Space Stones allow you to manipulate time within a specific space!

Chaos Stones allow you to cultivate countless laws at will!

Each stone is extremely rare; even Great Emperors struggle to obtain them!

And Xiaoyao had never seen a single one!

The Gu family patriarch casually produced dozens at once!

Gu Xuan raised his hand again, and a beam of light flew out, transforming into a spear!

A faint imperial aura flowed over the spear!

Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon, High-Grade!

*It's on par with Gu Chen's Hidden Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword!*

Xiaoyao's mind went blank!

He'd seen this before!

Finally, something he'd seen before!

"This is...? Are these all for me?"

Gu Xuan glanced at him. "Otherwise what? I have no use for them. I have a whole bunch of these things."

These things were indeed useless to Gu Xuan!

Fighting and killing all day is so boring. Isn't it much better to drink tea in this back mountain?

Besides, he had so many subordinates. Why not just send them out?

The Ancestor was second only to the Emperor. He could just assign tasks to his subordinates!

Xiaoyao's understanding was completely shattered!

He'd lived for hundreds of thousands of years and had never seen anything like this!

No! He'd never even heard of it!

A single item from these things could drive the entire continent crazy!

They were piled up all over the place, and this guy said they were useless.

Are you kidding me, buddy?!

And he even said he had a whole bunch more?

Does that mean this is just a small amount?!

Suddenly, he felt utterly defeated as an emperor.

He knew everything, yet had seen nothing?

Why did others have everything, while he had nothing?

What caused this?

Was it because he hadn't tried hard enough?

For a moment, Xiaoyao felt like the sky had fallen!

“Who exactly are you?”

Gu Xuan said calmly, “The Gu family patriarch who drinks tea in the back mountain.”

Xiaoyao didn't want to ask anymore. He knew it was pointless.

This kind of reclusive master was beyond his comprehension!

Doing his own thing was more important than anything else!

He crouched down, staring at the pile of things on the ground, his hands trembling, but his face beaming with a wide grin!

It smells amazing!

These resources are piled up like a mountain!

A single item would be enough for him to work his whole life for, and here it is, a whole floor full!

Never mind!

Once I'm in this family, I'm a member of the Gu family!

It smells amazing!

With so many resources, even a pig could cultivate to the Great Emperor level!

Xiaoyao: From now on, I'm the Gu family's dog!

People always say I'm not hardworking enough. If so many resources were thrown in my face, why wouldn't I be willing to be their slave?

Xiaoyao took a deep breath, stood up, looked at Gu Xuan, and bowed deeply, "Thank you, Ancestor."

Gu Xuan waved his hand, "No need to thank me. From now on, you're a member of the Gu family. The Handmaid Hall is still short-staffed; go fill the vacancy."

Xiaoyao nodded. "Okay."

He paused. "What does the 'Handsome Hands Hall' do?"

"It's a place that deals with trouble. If someone comes to attack, you go. If someone bullies the Gu family, you go. If demons come, you go too."

Xiaoyao nodded. Oh!

I understand!

They just use their hands to beat them up!

Beat them hard!

There are ten Great Emperors here, an endless supply of Emperor-level pills, inexhaustible cultivation resources, and Supreme Emperor Weapons to take at will!

If he had had all this back then, he could have fought ten Demon Emperors, let alone five!

Gu Xuan waved his hand, "Alright, go soak in the Spirit Liquid Pool first and raise your cultivation to the Great Emperor level."

Xiaoyao nodded, "Okay!"

Ten Thousand Prisons Demon Realm.

Inside the black palace, the Demon Emperor of Annihilation sat on the highest throne, his eyes sweeping over everyone present.

"Any news from Elder Cangwu?"

Below, a demon at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm stepped forward and knelt on one knee. "Reporting to Your Majesty, there is none yet."

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation frowned. "Not yet? He said yesterday he had found the Gu family's location."

The demon lowered his head. "This subordinate can no longer sense Elder Cangwu's aura."

The palace fell silent.

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation narrowed his eyes. "Can't sense it? What do you mean?"

The demon's voice trembled. "This subordinate suspects that Elder Cangwu may already be dead."

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation abruptly stood up, unleashing the oppressive aura of an eighth-level Great Emperor, causing the entire palace to tremble!

If you're going to find someone, at least find them properly!

"Dead? A peak Quasi-Emperor, just like that?"

The demon lay prostrate on the ground, not daring to raise his head.

Originally, they planned to coordinate an inside attack, but it seems they've chosen an unreliable ally!

"This subordinate doesn't know either, but I certainly can't sense his aura anymore."

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation was silent for a moment, then sat down again.

"Investigate! Find out for me what happened to Elder Cangwu!"

He paused, his voice icy, "The great battle is about to begin, and I don't want any accidents!"

"Yes!" The demon withdrew.

Another Demon Emperor spoke, his voice sharp, "Could it be that the Ancient Continent discovered our plan?"

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation shook his head, "Impossible, the passage hasn't opened yet, they can't detect it!"

"How could Elder Cangwu be dead?"

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation was silent for a moment!

He didn't know either.

But he had a bad feeling.

However, he couldn't worry about that now; invading the Ancient Continent was imperative!

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation stood up. "Never mind! The passage will open in two months! When it does, our army will press in, slaughtering humans and plundering their essence. Whether Elder Cangwu lives or dies is unimportant!"

He looked at all the demons in the hall. "The Ancient Desolate Continent must be taken."

"Yes!"

All the demons responded in unison.

The Demon Emperor of Annihilation sat down again, a cold glint in his eyes.

"No matter what's over there, nothing can stop my demon army!"


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 89 Demon Vanguard, Imperial Alliance
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Meanwhile, in the far north of the Desolate Ancient Continent!

A rift opened in the void, neither long nor wide.

But wide enough for a few people to pass through, enough for some demons to enter!

Several black figures squeezed through the rift!

Entirely shrouded in black aura, only their scarlet eyes were visible, their bodies wreathed in demonic energy!

Leading them was a demon at the first level of Quasi-Emperor realm, followed by several demon soldiers at the Saint King level!

The leader took a deep breath, "This is the Desolate Ancient Continent? The spiritual energy is so thin, but that's fine! This is a good opportunity for us demons!"

He waved his hand, "Kill! Leave no human alive!"

The demon soldiers scattered, rushing towards the nearest village!

Immediately following, screams, cries for help, and sobs mingled together!

Flames soared into the sky, and blood stained the snow crimson!

Some people escaped, covered in wounds, scrambling and crawling towards the nearest city!

The soldiers guarding the city paled at the sight of the man's gruesome state!

"The Demons! The Demons are coming!"

The news spread like wildfire throughout the entire continent!

"The Demons have appeared!"

"In the far north, they've already killed many!"

"They say the Demon Emperor is coming. The Great Emperor of the Ancient Continent has long since fallen; no one can stop them!"

"What? The Demons are coming? What are we going to do? The Demon Emperor is the strongest among the Demons, historically comparable in strength to the Great Emperor!"

A sense of unease gripped the nation.

Representatives from the major imperial clans, longevity families, and reclusive powerhouses of Zhongzhou gathered together!

Representatives from the Ice Phoenix Clan, the Han Family, the Zhuge Family, and several imperial clans sat in the main hall, their expressions grave!

This matter had caused a huge uproar; it jeopardized the interests of their respective families, and they could not remain uninvolved!

They were well aware of the Demon Clan's cruelty and bloodshed; leaving such a significant threat unchecked was not something they could afford to be complacent about!

The Ice Phoenix Clan's Grand Elder, Bing Yun, spoke first, "Demons have appeared in the far north! The news has been confirmed; our clan's quasi-Emperor elder witnessed it firsthand!"

Han Tianze, the Han family head, frowned. "Weren't the demons sealed away hundreds of thousands of years ago? How have they reappeared?"

Zhuge Ming, the Zhuge family head, guessed, "The seal has probably already weakened! Hundreds of thousands of years—even the strongest seal couldn't withstand that!"

"Then what do we do? How many demons are there? What are their cultivation levels?"

Bing Yun shook his head. "Currently, only an advance team has appeared. There was only one quasi-Emperor level demon, who has already been killed by our clan elder."

He paused.

"But those demons said before they died that their Demon Emperor was about to arrive, and then the Ancient Desolate Continent would be theirs!"

A moment of silence fell over the hall.

No one had expected that the demons, after hundreds of thousands of years, still wouldn't give up, and now they were directly announcing the Demon Emperor's arrival!

This was utterly disregard for them!

Moreover, upon hearing of the Demon Emperor's existence, everyone gasped!

That was an Emperor! Their Ancient Desolate Continent hadn't produced a Emperor in hundreds of thousands of years!

If the Demon Emperor of the Demon Clan led an attack, the Ancient Desolate Continent would be utterly powerless to resist!

For a moment, a heavy weight pressed on everyone's hearts.

Just then, Han Tianze said in a deep voice, "What about that Emperor's inheritance? Wasn't the Genius Battle champion won?"

"We got it, but it's only a partial inheritance. Even with the complete inheritance, it's impossible to cultivate a Great Emperor in a short time!"

"Then what do we do? Wait for the Demon Clan to arrive, and we're just waiting to die?"

"Then make him hand it over!" Han Tianze suddenly spoke, his voice echoing throughout the hall.

"The Great Emperor's inheritance is a waste in his hands. It's better to hand it over to a Quasi-Emperor peak expert on our continent! If someone can use it to break through to Great Emperor, we'll still have a glimmer of hope!"

Han Tianze's words fell silent!

Everyone turned to look at him!

Their first reaction was that he was insane!

And seriously insane!

Didn't he know the power of the Gu family?

An elder stood up, looking at Han Tianze, "Are you crazy? The Gu family's three protectors are all Quasi-Emperor peak experts. What do you have to offer them to make them hand over their inheritance?"

Han Tianze's expression changed. He had acted impulsively and forgotten about this!

The three protectors of the Gu family are all at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm!

Why would they give it to them?!

Even if there were an inheritance, it would be for their own people, not for someone else to inherit the legacy of an Emperor!

Anyone who didn't know better would think the Han family was going to clash head-on with the Gu family!

"Then what do we do?"

Han Tianze's voice lowered.

"The Demon Clan is coming, the Demon Emperor is coming, we can't just wait to die, can we?"

For a moment, everyone was at a loss for words.

At this moment, Bing Yun stood up, "Everyone, now is not the time to argue. The Demon Clan hasn't launched a full-scale invasion yet, we still have time!"

He looked at everyone,

"I propose that we form an alliance to jointly fight against the Demon Clan. All the major imperial clans, longevity families, and reclusive powerful figures, those willing to join, let's discuss countermeasures together! Let's fight the Demon Clan together!"

Some nodded, others remained silent.

Could they really resist?

Those are Emperors! The Ancient Desolate Continent hasn't had an Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years, how can they fight against the Demon Clan?

Bingyun continued, "I'll personally go to the Gu family. If they're willing to join, the alliance's strength will increase significantly!"

"Will they join?"

Bingyun shook her head. "I don't know, but we have to try."

Han Tianze frowned. "The Gu family will agree? They don't even care about the affairs of Zhongzhou, why would they care about the Demon Clan?"

Bingyun glanced at him. "The Demon Clan isn't just a matter for Zhongzhou, it's a matter for the entire continent. No matter how strong the Gu family is, they're still on this continent. If the Demon Clan comes, the Gu family can't escape."

He paused. "Moreover, the young master of the Gu family won the Genius Battle and obtained the Great Emperor's inheritance, which shows that the Gu family isn't completely indifferent to external affairs!"

Everyone nodded!

Bingyun looked around. "Then it's settled. I'll personally go to the Gu family!"

The murmurs in the hall gradually subsided!

Some began discussing the alliance, some began assessing their family's strength, and some began preparing for the worst!

As for the worst!

They planned to join the Demon Clan to ensure their own interests weren't harmed!

Those people didn't show it!

Everyone in the hall had their own ulterior motives; only a small minority truly cared about the peace of this continent!

No one but the Great Emperor could be truly selfless; in their eyes, only their own interests mattered. For those who had knelt for so long, the arrival of the Demon Race was merely a change of masters.

But for some, this continent is different; it's their roots, their home!

They won't allow outsiders to invade, even at the cost of their lives!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 90 I'm getting married? How come I didn't know? Let's have the wedding first.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

When the news reached the Gu family, Gu Tianhua was preparing!

The back mountain!

Gu Xuan opened his eyes, "Hmm, I know."

Gu Tianhua shut up.

He said too much. He should have known better!

It seems the ancestor knows everything.

"Don't worry about it. Let them cause trouble. The passage won't open for another two months. What's coming now are just small fry. The imperial clans of Zhongzhou can handle them."

Using a sledgehammer to crack a nut is a waste of resources!

They're just small fry. Let the imperial clans of Zhongzhou handle them!

Gu Tianhua nodded and turned to leave.

"Wait."

Gu Xuan called after him.

Gu Chen's seclusion had reached its most crucial moment.

His strength had already reached the peak of the Half-Saint realm, just one step away from becoming a Saint.

Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu were also close to reaching the Saint realm!

Three days later.

Gu Qingshan let out a low roar, his spiritual power surging forth like a tidal wave!

A Saintly aura soared into the sky!

Gu Qingshan broke through, reaching the first level of the Saintly Realm!

At the same time, another Saintly aura soared into the sky!

Li Shuangxu also broke through to the first level of the Saintly Realm!

He opened his eyes and let out a long breath, "Finally, I won't be a burden anymore!"

Immediately afterward, another Saintly aura soared into the sky!

More than ten times stronger than Gu Qingshan and Li Shuangxu!

The rust on Gu Chen's Hidden Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword completely fell off, and a dragon's roar exploded, resounding throughout the entire Gu family!

This drew everyone's attention to the direction where Gu Chen had been in seclusion!

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner and opened his eyes.

He smiled, "Breakthrough, all three of them broke through."

Then, the system panel appeared before his eyes!

[Congratulations, Host! The entire family's Sainthood mission is complete.]

[Rewards being distributed—2 Emperor Summoning Cards, 2 Random Summoning Cards.]

Gu Xuan glanced at the rewards and casually put them away.

Then the system beeped again.

[Ding! New mission issued!]

[Detected that the Demon Race is preparing to invade the Ancient Desolate Continent! As the strongest family, protecting our base is essential! The Ancient Desolate Continent is the root of the Gu family, and naturally, it cannot be invaded by others!]

[Mission Requirements: Counterattack the Demon Race and ensure the invaders never return!]

[Mission Reward: 5 Emperor Summoning Cards (Cannot be upgraded)]

Gu Xuan raised an eyebrow and smiled.

Five Emperor Summoning Cards!

Adding the two from before, that makes seven.

Although they can't be upgraded, it seems pretty good!

After all, it's exactly what I needed to deal with!

A convenient thing!

"The system understands me! I was just worried about not having enough henchmen, and here they are!"

Adding the previously upgradable Emperor cards and the two he just completed the mission with, he now has six!

Summoning an immortal is just around the corner!

He looked towards the distant horizon, where the aura of the Demon Realm grew increasingly intense!

However, Gu Xuan felt a surge of anticipation. This was truly a case of choosing the path to hell when heaven offered it!

He put down his teacup and closed his eyes.

No rush. Let the imperial clans of Zhongzhou keep busy for a while.

Once they're done, he'll make his move!

Take it all in one fell swoop!

The Locking God Tower.

Gu Chen stood up, gripping the Abyss Emperor Dragon Sword tightly!

The rust on the sword had completely fallen away, revealing the flowing spiritual energy beneath!

Sword intent circulated within him, the aura of a first-level Saint surging endlessly!

"Emerging from seclusion!"

He stepped out of the Locking God Tower.

Gu Family Square was brightly decorated, with large red lanterns hanging high!

Gu Chen nodded, asking in confusion, "Is the Gu family holding a celebration? For whom?"

The disciple hesitated. "Young Master, you didn't know?"

"Know what?"

Gu Chen was taken aback. "I was in seclusion and knew nothing, how come it seems like I know everything now?"

Could this be mine?

The disciple scratched his head. "Weren't you getting married?!"

Gu Chen's mind went blank. "What?!"

Married?

How come I didn't know?

What the hell?

What happened?

Can anyone tell me?

The disciple was startled by his reaction. "The clan leader said so! He said the wedding would be held as soon as you came out of seclusion. The dowry is all prepared, piled up in the room!"

Gu Chen's face flushed red. He weakly asked, "Who am I marrying?"

The disciple looked blank. "Miss Ru Shuang, isn't she your wife?"

Gu Chen opened his mouth, speechless.

That's wonderful!

His unreliable father has finally been reliable!

He won over Miss Ru Shuang while he was in seclusion!

Love you, Dad!

In the distance, Gu Tianhua walked over. Seeing Gu Chen, his eyes lit up. "Chen'er! You've come out of seclusion?"

Gu Chen looked at his father. "Dad, what's going on?"

Gu Tianhua chuckled. "What's going on? You've already brought your wife back, what are you waiting for?"

"Miss Ru Shuang and I are just friends, how could I possibly accept this…"

Although he said that, he was secretly overjoyed!

Gu Tianhua asked in confusion, "Friend? You brought her back as a friend? You let her stay at your house as a friend? You let her meet the Ancestor as a friend?"

Gu Chen was speechless with embarrassment.

Isn't this what friends are like?

Gu Tianhua patted his shoulder, "Alright, stop pretending! Father has everything prepared, just waiting for you to come out of seclusion! The wedding will be held in three days!"

"Three days?!" Gu Chen exclaimed anxiously, "Isn't that too hasty?"

"What's the rush? Father has been preparing for almost two months, just waiting for this day! And the girl hasn't said anything, what's the rush?"

Gu Tianhua turned and left, "I'm going to tell the Ancestor that you've come out of seclusion."

Gu Chen stood there, blushing deeply. What did he mean by the girl agreeing?

Ru Shuang agreed, so what else could he say?

He gave it to her!

In the distance, Ru Shuang was walking towards the Spirit Liquid Pool!

She saw Gu Chen, paused, and their eyes met.

Gu Chen opened his mouth, wanting to say something, but couldn't utter a single word.

Ru Shuang's face also flushed.

The two stood there, neither speaking.

A breeze blew, and the large red lanterns swayed gently.

Back Mountain.

Gu Tianhua ran up, "Ancestor, Chen'er has emerged from seclusion."

Gu Xuan opened his eyes, "Hmm."

"The wedding will be held in three days, is that alright?"

Gu Xuan nodded, "Alright."

He paused, "Let's ignore the Demon Clan for now, let's get the wedding done first!"

Gu Tianhua smiled, "That's what I was thinking too!"

It's time for the Gu family to have some good fortune!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 91 Two more Great Emperors added, the Gu family now has Great Emperors?

For the past month, she had cultivated frantically, breaking through from the peak of the fifth level of the Emperor Realm to the sixth level, wanting to fight him again!

She thought the gap shouldn't be too large!

Bing Yun glanced back at her. "Xin'er, we're going to ask the Gu family for help, not to fight. Put away that competitive look."

Although she didn't want to admit it, Gu Chen was indeed exceptionally talented, and he wielded an ultimate imperial weapon—a weapon fused into himself!

It was normal that their Holy Maiden couldn't beat him!

She wasn't too concerned; this time they were asking for help, not to fight!

Of course, she probably wouldn't win in a fight either!

Although the Ice Phoenix Clan had a long and illustrious history, and their patriarch was a peak Quasi-Emperor!

But compared to the Gu family's three peak Quasi-Emperors, they were simply no match!

Bing Xin nodded. "I know, Elder."

She paused. "But a friendly spar, surely?"

Bing Yun shook his head helplessly, remaining silent.

This girl, all she could think about was fighting!

But that was a good thing; having fighting spirit meant she had a goal!

They wouldn't give up because of one failure.

The three flew for three days and entered the Eastern Region!

The spiritual energy was becoming increasingly thin, making them all feel somewhat uncomfortable.

Bing Xin frowned, "This is the Eastern Region? The spiritual energy is far inferior to that of the Central Continent!"

Bing Yun nodded, "The Eastern Region is inherently a desolate place. It's already a miracle that a family like the Gu family could emerge from here."

He was extremely curious that someone could actually win the Genius Battle in such a place!

Moreover, the existence of an Extreme Dao Emperor Weapon, yet not a single piece of information about the Gu family has surfaced—this is utterly unbelievable!

What kind of family is this?!

He grew increasingly curious about the Gu family; they even had three peak Quasi-Emperors protecting them!

Just what was the cultivation level of the strongest ancestor of such a family?

He knew he had to go. He could only wonder; he'd only know once he saw it for himself!

After flying for another half day...

Suddenly, Bing Yun stopped!

The two Quasi-Emperor elders behind him also stopped, their expressions changing simultaneously!

Bing Xin was startled, "Elder, what's wrong?"

Bing Yun didn't answer, his gaze fixed intently ahead.

That direction was the Gu family!

"Those two auras!"

Bing Xin asked, puzzled, "What?"

"Those two auras just now were even stronger than the Clan Chief! Could they be Great Emperors?!" one of the Quasi-Emperor elders added, his voice trembling.

Bing Xin's mind went blank!

A Great Emperor?

The Gu family has a Great Emperor?

Not just a Quasi-Emperor at the peak, but a Great Emperor?

"Could it be that the Demon Clan struck first?" Bing Xin's expression changed!

After all, there hadn't been any news of Great Emperors on this continent for so long; the only news was that the Demon Emperor of the Demon Clan was a Great Emperor.

Could it be that the Demon Clan, fearing the Gu family's strength, had already begun their attack?

Bing Yun shook her head, "I don't know, but those two auras don't seem to belong to the Demon Clan. The Demon Clan's aura carries demonic energy, but those two auras are very pure, more like human auras?"

"Humans? But the Ancient Desolate Continent hasn't produced a Great Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years..." Bing Xin was shocked!

If the elder's aura was stronger than their clan leader's, then it could only be a Great Emperor!

Bing Yun was silent for a moment, "Let's go, let's go take a look."

The three of them accelerated, flying towards the Gu family!

The closer they got to the Gu family, the stronger the oppressive feeling became!

Bing Xin's heart leaped into her throat.

She recalled the day of the Genius Battle finals, when Gu Chen cleaved her Ice Phoenix Annihilation Blade in two with a single sword strike!

And the three protectors behind Gu Chen had killed three Quasi-Emperors with a single sword strike!

She had always thought those three were peak Quasi-Emperors, but now she suspected they weren't peak Quasi-Emperors, but Great Emperors!

Outside the Gu family's mountain gate, Bing Yun landed and took a deep breath!

Refreshing!

The aura outside the mountain gate alone was even denser than the spiritual energy of their Ice Phoenix Clan's ancestral land!

This was truly a blessed land!

"Bing Yun of the Ice Phoenix Clan requests an audience with the Gu family's patriarch."

The voice wasn't loud, but it resonated throughout the entire Gu family.

A crack appeared in the protective array.

Gu Yushan emerged from within, sizing up Bing Yun and the others, his face expressionless.

"And who are you all?"

Bing Yun cupped his hands in greeting. "Bing Yun, the Grand Elder of the Ice Phoenix Clan, has come to request an audience with the Gu family's patriarch on the orders of the clan leader."

Gu Yushan nodded. The patriarch had already informed him of their purpose via telepathy.

"Come in, but let's clarify a few rules first. First, no fighting within the Gu family! Second, no loud noise! ​​Third, today is our young master's joyous occasion. Guests are welcome; please eat and drink well. We can discuss other matters tomorrow!"

Bing Yun was taken aback. "Joyful occasion? Gu Chen is getting married?"

Gu Yushan glanced at him. "Yes, today."

It seemed inappropriate for them to discuss fighting the demons while others were celebrating a wedding, but since they were already there, they couldn't just turn around and leave.

"Shall we attend the wedding first?" Bing Yun suggested.

Gu Yushan nodded and led the group into the protective array.

Then Bingyun froze.

Not just a little stunned, but completely stunned!

Spiritual liquid, ginseng fruit tree, enlightenment tea, spiritual jade, and a terrifying boost of spiritual energy!

She had a faint premonition of breaking through!

The two quasi-emperor elders behind Bingyun were also stunned; it was the first time they had ever seen such a scene!

Bingxin's eyes widened, unable to believe what she was seeing!

She knew the Gu family was powerful, but she hadn't expected them to be this powerful!

Any one of these things, even in the Central Continent, would be enough to make imperial clans fight tooth and nail!

"These are all from the Gu family?"

Gu Yushan didn't even turn his head. "Yes, just some unwanted things planted randomly."

What a fine way to put it!

Planted randomly!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 92 May a son be born soon, and may they be sent to the bridal chamber.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Passing through the path lined with spiritual herbs, the scene before him suddenly opened up!

The Gu Family Square was brightly decorated, with lanterns hanging high and countless spirit-wood tents erected for celebration!

Hundreds of tables were set up across the square, offering spirit beast meat, ten-thousand-year-old spiritual fruits, and Emperor-grade pills as snacks—eat and take as you please!

Thousands of people sat around the tables, chatting and laughing, creating a lively atmosphere!

Bing Yun's steps froze on the spot!

In all his years, he had never seen such a spectacle!

It wasn't the sheer scale of the event, but the food on the tables!

It was all Emperor-grade pills and ten-thousand-year-old spiritual fruits!

Eat as much as you want! Consume as much as you want!

What did this mean?

Even at their Ice Phoenix Clan's best banquets, while Emperor-grade pills were served, there were only one or two. Here, it was like they were free!

Their thousand-year-old spiritual fruits were only brought out for banquets, and ten-thousand-year-old ones were reserved as rewards for their own geniuses.

But here, eat and consume as you please!

They weren't afraid of the cost at all!

Compared to the Gu family, they weren't even worthy of carrying their shoes!

The patriarch was waiting for his report; he didn't have time to waste with these people!

They're all adults now, they can't possibly get lost!

Well, he wouldn't care. Even if they got lost, he wouldn't be afraid of not finding them.

Bing Yun stood there, looking at the table full of food, unsure whether to sit or stand!

This was too difficult!

The two quasi-emperor elders were even more at a loss. They had lived for tens of thousands of years and had never seen such a scene!

Emperor-grade spiritual wine, mellow on the palate, spiritual energy flowing through her body!

Her cultivation level instantly broke through from the sixth to the eighth level!

And she showed no signs of intoxication!

She put down her wine cup, picked up a high-grade Emperor-level pill, examined it, and then put it back.

“Won’t you try some?” an elder asked.

The temptation was too great; ordinary people simply couldn’t resist!

The two elders fell silent!

They couldn’t respond to that!

The Holy Maiden actually had such awareness!

But this stuff smelled too good!

They were having trouble resisting!

Bing Yun sighed. “They’re getting married, and we haven’t prepared anything…”

After all, what they brought was just a pile of scrap metal, a world of difference compared to this!

Gu family disciples served dishes and wine, their steps light and their faces beaming with joy.

Huo Dongyuan didn't reply, but he was thinking about it too.

Gu Chen possessed the Hidden Dragon Sword Body, and Ru Shuang possessed the Taiyin Spirit Body. The talent of their child was truly unimaginable!

Bingxin sat in a corner, listening to the surrounding chatter. She recalled the day of the Genius Battle finals, when Ru Shuang waited for her in the contestant area. Perhaps they were already together then.

“Xin’er,” Bingyun suddenly spoke.

“Hmm?”

“This time when you come to the Gu family, don’t mention the sparring!”

Bingxin nodded, “I know!”

Bingxin was obedient. Besides, today was someone’s wedding day; to spar with them would be disruptive.

Back Mountain.

Gu Xuan got up from the recliner, changed into clean white clothes, tied his white hair back, and looked much more energetic!

His son had really made something of himself; he went out and brought back a wife!

Gu Xuan nodded, “Let’s go.”

Ru Feng stood beside Ru Shuang.

"Girl, from now on you're a member of the Gu family."

Ru Shuang nodded. "Grandpa, I'll be good."

Gu Xuan walked onto the stage and stood before Gu Chen and Ru Shuang.

He looked at the two of them and smiled.

Just four words.

This left the newlyweds completely bewildered!

Gu Xuan turned and walked off the stage, taking his seat in the main seat.

Gu Chen and Ru Shuang turned and bowed to Heaven and Earth.

Gu Tianhua beamed, while Gu Xuan nodded.

"Husband and wife bow to each other!"

The two faced each other and bowed deeply.

"Send them straight to the bridal chamber!"

The entire audience cheered!

This was the show they most wanted to see!

Why drink wine? Why serve tea?

It didn't matter!

They had prepared several teams to eavesdrop tonight!

Tonight was definitely going to be exciting!

Gu Chen, holding Ru Shuang's hand, turned and walked towards the bridal chamber.

Gu Qingshan stood below the stage, laughing the loudest. "Young Master, go for it! Just do it!"

Gu Chen turned and glared at him, but Gu Qingshan pretended not to see!

Bing Yun sat beside the banquet table, watching all this, her heart filled with mixed emotions!

He had never seen a wedding like this before—not because of its grand scale, but because of its warm hospitality!

There was no scheming or calculating backstabbing here, only genuine blessings!

On the other side, Ru Feng sat at the head table, flanked by Gu Yushan, Gu Kong, and several other elders.

He picked up his wine glass, took a sip, and was filled with emotion.

These past few days at the Gu family had truly broadened his horizons!

He couldn't sense the aura of those people in the back mountain at all!

Peak Quasi-Emperor?

No.

He guessed they were probably Great Emperors!

Great Emperors, a phenomenon unseen for hundreds of thousands of years, and the Gu family had more than one!

He didn't make a fuss. The stronger the Gu family, the better they would treat his granddaughter!

This was a good thing!

These past few days, he'd also consulted those people on the back mountain. A few casual words of advice from them were enough to loosen his bottleneck!

Yesterday, he broke through from the peak of the Eternal Realm to the first level of Quasi-Emperor. He never imagined that in his lifetime he could actually break through to Quasi-Emperor!

Looking at Gu Chen and Ru Shuang on the stage, Ru Feng's eyes reddened again. "Girl, you've found a good family."

…


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 93 Fallen petals are not heartless things, paying respects to the Gu family patriarch.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

After several rounds of drinks, a quasi-Emperor elder of the Ice Phoenix Clan, holding a wine cup, glanced inadvertently towards the direction of the bridal chamber. Suddenly, his hand trembled, and he spilled wine.

“What’s wrong?” another elder asked.

The elder didn’t answer, but instead asked in confusion, “Is Gu Chen getting married today? The Gu Chen from the Genius Battle?”

Bing Xin nodded, “Yes.”

The elder looked incredulous, “How can his cultivation be a Saint?”

Bing Xin was stunned, completely bewildered by what her elder meant!

“What?”

“A Saint, Gu Chen is a Saint.”

The elder’s voice trembled.

He clearly remembered the Genius Battle had just ended, and at that time, Gu Chen’s cultivation was only at the first level of the Emperor Realm.

It’s only been a few days?!

"How long has it been since the Genius Battle ended? He was only at the first level of the Emperor Realm back then, and it's only been a few days? He's already at the Saint Realm?"

Bing Xin's mind went blank. She was still wondering why she couldn't sense Gu Chen's cultivation level earlier!

She thought he had cultivated some kind of technique to conceal his cultivation, but now it seemed it wasn't concealment; it was simply beyond her perception range!

Saint Realm, in just a few days, from the first level of the Emperor Realm to the Saint Realm, skipping the peak of the Emperor Realm, the Half-Saint, directly to the Saint Realm!

"How is this possible?" Bing Xin exclaimed in disbelief!

This cultivation speed is ridiculously fast!

Even the top geniuses of Central Continent might not be able to do what he did!

At the same time, Bing Yun spread her divine sense.

Everywhere she went, there were Saints. She dared not explore the back mountain of the Gu family; there were several auras there that she didn't even dare to touch!

Soon, she withdrew her divine sense, picked up her wine cup, looked at the Emperor-grade spiritual wine inside, and gave a bitter smile!

"Don't even think about it. With this concentration of spiritual energy and these resources, it's not impossible for someone to cultivate to the Saint level in such a short time."

She paused. "Besides, there must be a master guiding him!"

The two Quasi-Emperor elders fell silent.

They didn't want to admit it, but the facts were undeniable!

The Gu family was beyond their comprehension!

Inside the bridal chamber, the lights were bright!

Gu Chen sat on the edge of the bed, Ru Shuang sat beside him, a fist's distance between them!

Neither spoke.

Firecrackers crackled outside, the noise from outside gradually fading away!

Gu Chen took a deep breath and turned to look at Ru Shuang, "Miss Ru."

Ru Shuang also looked up at him.

Gu Chen opened his mouth, his face flushing, "Um, I..."

Ru Shuang looked at his stammering and couldn't help but laugh, "What?"

Gu Chen took a deep breath and took Ru Shuang's hand. "From now on, I will be good to you!"

Ru Shuang lowered her head and whispered, "Okay."

The two fell silent again!

The red light shone on Ru Shuang's face, making her look unreal.

Gu Chen looked at her, his heart pounding like a drum!

"Miss Ru," he spoke again.

"Hmm?"

"May I call you Shuang'er?"

Ru Shuang didn't answer, only nodded.

"Shuang'er."

Gu Chen took a deep breath and gently lifted Ru Shuang's chin.

Ru Shuang's eyes were watery as she looked at him. Their eyes met, their faces drawing closer.

Suddenly, a soft sound came from outside the window.

Gu Chen frowned!

"You've ruined my plans!"

"Did you hear that?" Gu Qingshan asked softly.

"No, it was too quiet," Li Shuangxu shook his head.

"Closer."

The group pressed their ears even closer.

Gu Chen walked to the window and abruptly pushed it open!

Outside, several heads were huddled together, ears pressed against the windowpane. Gu Qingshan, Li Shuangxu, and several other Gu family disciples held their breath, afraid to make a sound!

The moment the window opened, they lost their balance and tumbled to the ground with a thud!

They hadn't expected to be discovered even after trying to hide!

Gu Chen stood at the window, looking at the people on the ground, expressionless. "What are you doing?"

Gu Qingshan, lying on the ground, looked up at Gu Chen and chuckled. "We were just passing by."

"Passing by? Passing by my window?"

Gu Qingshan fell silent.

"Passing by my window, and with your ears pressed so close?"

Li Shuangxu scratched his head awkwardly. "Um! Young Master, we're here to learn!"

Gu Chen's lips twitched. "Learn? Learn what?"

Gu Qingshan glanced at Li Shuangxu, and Li Shuangxu glanced at Gu Qingshan. They both spoke simultaneously, "Learning how to be a groom."

Gu Chen's face darkened. "Get out! Get out! Get out!"

Those were some rather vulgar words!

The group scrambled to their feet and scurried away. After a few steps, Gu Qingshan turned back. "Young Master, good luck! Go all out!"

Gu Chen picked up a smelly shoe and threw it!

Gu Qingshan ducked; the shoe grazed his forehead and hit a tree. The group ran off quickly!

Gu Chen closed the window and turned back to the bedside.

Ru Shuang looked at him and couldn't help but smile. "Is it them?"

Gu Chen sat down beside her and sighed. "Yeah, these guys need a good beating."

Ru Shuang smiled.

Gu Chen looked at her smile, and the tension in his heart dissipated considerably. He took her hand again.

"Shuang'er."

"Yes."

"Tonight we…"

Ru Shuang's face flushed again. She lowered her head and remained silent.

Gu Chen took a deep breath and blew out the candle.

The night was gentle.

Outside the window, the moon hid behind the clouds.

Fallen petals are not heartless things…

The next morning.

When Gu Chen woke up, Ru Shuang was still asleep.

He looked at her face, a feeling he couldn't quite describe welling up inside him!

This person would be his wife from now on!

He gently kissed her forehead, then got up and dressed.

Ru Shuang opened her eyes, looking at him with a contented expression.

"Good morning," Gu Chen said.

Ru Shuang sat up, head bowed. "Good morning."

Gu Chen walked to the window, opened it, and the morning light streamed in. He turned to look at Ru Shuang and extended his hand.

"Let's go, it's time to pay our respects to the Ancestor."

Ru Shuang nodded, took his hand, and got out of bed.

Sunlight shone on them, casting their shadows side by side on the ground, very close.

The banquet in the square had already been cleared away!

Gu family disciples were cleaning up when they saw Gu Chen and Ru Shuang, and greeted them with smiles!

"Good morning, Young Master! Good morning, Young Mistress!"

Ru Shuang blushed, but still nodded.

Back on the mountain, Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, watching the two approach, and smiled, "You've arrived?"

Gu Chen nodded, "Ancestor."

Gu Xuan glanced at them, "Did you sleep well last night?"

Gu Chen blushed, "Very well."

Gu Xuan smiled, not asking further, "Go on, spend more time with your wife."

Gu Chen nodded, and pulled Ru Shuang away.

Meanwhile, Bingyun, accompanied by Bingxin and two quasi-Emperor elders, headed towards the back mountain under Gu Yushan's guidance!

The back mountain!

Bingyun paused!

Several people stood in a row before them!

Bingyun couldn't sense their presence. Her palms were sweaty, but she didn't stop. She walked to Gu Xuan and bowed deeply.

"Bingyun, the Great Elder of the Ice Phoenix Clan, pays respects to the Gu Family Ancestor."

Gu Xuan opened his eyes and glanced at her. "Sit."

Bingyun straightened up and sat on the stone bench beside him!

Bingxin and the two elders stood behind him, not daring to breathe!

It wasn't that they didn't want to sit, it was that they dared not!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 94 The Gu Family Agrees, Each with Their Own Thoughts
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Xuan picked up his teacup and took a sip. "Speak," he said, "what is it you wish to discuss?"

Bing Yun took a deep breath. "Senior," she said, "the demon race has appeared in the far north. They claim their Demon Emperor is about to arrive! When that happens, the demon army will press in, and the Ancient Desolate Continent will be in imminent danger!"

He paused. "The major imperial families and longevity clans of the Central Continent have formed an alliance to jointly fight against the demons. I have come here to invite the Gu family to join the alliance and defend against this external enemy!"

Gu Xuan listened without reacting.

He already knew about this!

Bing Yun's heart sank. She thought the Gu family was unwilling to intervene.

But she still wanted to try. After all, the Gu family's participation would definitely elevate their strength to a higher level!

This would increase their hope of fighting the demons, preventing them from feeling utterly hopeless!

“Senior, the demon race is cruel and bloodthirsty. Hundreds of thousands of years ago, they nearly destroyed the continent. Now the seal is loosening. If the demon army is allowed to enter, the ancient continent will be devastated. Even with your strength, Senior, you cannot remain unscathed.”

Gu Xuan raised his hand, interrupting him. “I understand.”

Bing Yun was taken aback, unsure what to say.

She wondered what this Gu family patriarch was thinking. When the demons truly invaded, no force could remain unaffected!

She still hoped the Gu family would join them!

Gu Xuan glanced at him. “You should go back first. I will send someone over in a few days.”

Bing Yun was stunned. He had thought the Gu family would refuse or make some demands.

He hadn't expected them to agree so readily!

“Thank you, Senior!” Bing Yun stood up and bowed deeply.

Gu Xuan waved his hand, saying nothing.

Bing Yun, along with Bing Xin and the two elders, turned and left.

After leaving the back mountain, Bing Xin let out a long sigh. Her back was soaked with cold sweat!

“Elder, the Gu family patriarch…”

Bingyun shook her head. “Don’t ask.”

Bingxin remained silent.

She knew the Elder meant the Gu family patriarch’s cultivation level was beyond her comprehension!

Bingyun quickened her pace. “Let’s go back to Zhongzhou first.”

Then, the group left the Gu family.

The news quickly reached Zhongzhou!

In a grand hall, representatives from various imperial clans and longevity families gathered, awaiting news from Bingyun!

“He’s back!” someone shouted.

Bingyun entered the hall, and everyone looked at him.

“How is it? Did the Gu family agree?” Han Tianze was the first to speak.

Bingyun nodded. “They agreed.”

A sigh of relief filled the hall, but soon someone asked, “How many people did they send? What are their cultivation levels?”

Bingyun glanced at the person. “That senior said he would send people in a few days, but didn’t say who they were or what their cultivation levels were.”

The hall suddenly fell silent, then murmurs arose.

"A few days? The demons could arrive at any time, why would they wait a few more days?!"

"Exactly! They're already at our doorstep, and they're still dragging their feet!"

"Send a few people? Are they just sending some low-level thugs to fob us off?"

"After all, it's not their own business, they don't care."

This instantly sparked discontent and discussion among some people, who felt the Gu family wasn't taking it seriously enough!

Some even accused the Gu family of selfishness!

Some even questioned the Gu family's strength!

The demons were practically at their doorstep, yet they showed no sign of tension, which greatly displeased some!

Bing Yun glanced at them, offering nothing more.

The Gu family would send people in a few days; who they were and their cultivation levels would become clear then!

What's the point of arguing here now?

Does arguing here somehow produce an Emperor?

Or will it make them stronger?

Neither!

Bing Yun ignored them. Their comments about the Gu family were simply a ploy to get them to send more powerful experts—in other words, they wanted the Gu family to foot the bill.

Bing Yun had witnessed the Gu family's power firsthand; for these people, it was nothing more than protecting their own interests!

Bing Yun ignored them.

These people wanted the Gu family to contribute the lion's share while they themselves conserved their strength!

The battle hadn't even begun, and they were already plotting against others!

She had witnessed the Gu family's power firsthand. Any one of the auras emanating from the back mountain of the Gu family could crush everyone present into dust!

Yet these people were still arrogant, believing themselves to be even more powerful than the Gu family!

Did they really think those three peak Quasi-Emperor experts were just a joke?

Once the Gu family sends someone, these people will realize how ridiculous they are!

There's no need to expose their hypocrisy here!

"Disperse," Bing Yun said, turning to leave.

Suddenly, a white-haired elder stood up. "Elder Bing, wait."

Bing Yun turned back, looking at the man.

It was Zhuge Yun, the patriarch of the Zhuge family, a family of immortals.

Having lived for tens of thousands of years, a seventh-level Quasi-Emperor, one of the most powerful people present!

Zhuge Yun surveyed his surroundings. "Now that the demons are invading, a matter of life and death for the continent, the alliance, now established, must have a supreme leader. Only with one person in charge, to unify command and control, can orders be strictly enforced during wartime, preventing deserters from taking advantage!"

Discussions erupted again within the hall.

Some agreed with this view; a supreme leader was essential, as it would greatly facilitate resource allocation!

It would make the work of those fighting on the front lines much easier!

It would also instill fear in those who deserted!

Han Tianze was the first to speak, "I recommend the Li family. The Li family has a million-year history, having produced a Great Emperor. Their foundation is profound. Their ancestor is a peak Quasi-Emperor, highly respected and virtuous, more than capable of holding the position of supreme leader!"

As his words fell, some echoed his sentiments, while others shook their heads!

At this moment, a representative from another imperial clan stood up. "While the Li family is strong, in terms of foundation, the Ice Phoenix Clan is no less powerful. The Ice Phoenix Clan has also produced a Great Emperor, and Elder Bingyun is at the eighth level of Quasi-Emperor. Furthermore, their clan leader's cultivation is at the peak of Quasi-Emperor, no less than the Li family's ancestor. I recommend the Ice Phoenix Clan!"

Another representative stood up. "You're all wrong. In terms of strength, foundation, and prestige, the Qi family is the most suitable. The Qi family has two peak Quasi-Emperors, produced a Great Emperor a million years ago, and their lineage has never been broken. If the Tian family ancestor is willing to come out of seclusion, what is there to fear from the Demon Clan?"

The three families argued, each with its supporters, the debate raging on!

Some supported the Han family, some supported the Ice Phoenix Clan, some supported the Tian family, and some supported the Gu family!

After all, the Gu family had three peak Quasi-Emperor experts; such a family might have an even more terrifying existence behind it!

But these people only believed in historical fact, and besides, the Gu family had only recently emerged.

Their foundation and heritage certainly can't compare to families that have existed for so long!

Bingyun stood to the side, silent.

He glanced at the arguing crowd, then turned and walked out of the hall.

She didn't want to get involved in these matters!

She didn't care who became the leader!

What she cared about was how to stop the demons after they arrived!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 95 All-Out Effort, the Throne of the Supreme Ruler
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Behind the mountain.

Gu Xuan lay on a recliner, Jian Wuji standing beside him. After a moment, Jian Wuji asked, "Master, when do we depart?"

Gu Xuan opened his eyes. "Three days later. You lead the team and send those Great Emperors on a trip. Gu Chen should go too, let him see the world and let them see the strength of the Gu family."

This time, Gu Xuan mobilized everyone, aiming to eliminate the demon race as quickly as possible!

As for the Gu family, not only do they possess a defensive array capable of withstanding immortals, but Gu Xuan himself is a Great Emperor!

Anyone who dares to attack the Gu family will face certain death!

Jian Wuji nodded.

Gu Xuan looked at him. "While you're out there, don't tarnish the Gu family's reputation! You can handle anything detrimental to the Gu family, including those who damage its prestige."

The strongest family must be powerful and inviolable!

Not even the slightest threat can be tolerated!

Jian Wuji nodded again, asking no further questions.

He understood his master's meaning: anyone who dared disrespect the Gu family should be killed!

He had killed countless people; killing a few more wouldn't be advisable!

Jian Wuji turned and left.

Back mountain, Bai Yujing, Ye Wuhen, Yue Qingshuang, Yue Qingxue, Tian Gang, and Tian Sha stood in a line!

The four divine beasts also lay nearby!

Xiaoyao stood at the very back, his aura steady, a first-level Great Emperor!

He broke through from the peak of Quasi-Emperor to Great Emperor in just a few days, not because of exceptional talent, but entirely due to abundant resources!

He ate Emperor-grade pills like food, used Enlightenment Stones, Time-Space Stones, and Chaos Stones at will, and since he was already a Great Emperor, his insights remained; the breakthrough was simply a natural progression!

Ru Feng stood beside Xiaoyao, a second-level Quasi-Emperor.

He hadn't broken through to Great Emperor, but he was already satisfied.

Reaching Quasi-Emperor in his lifetime was something he had never dared to dream of before!

Jian Wuji looked at everyone, his tone calm, "We'll depart in three days. The young master will go too."

Xiaoyao nodded, offering no objection.

He had no goodwill towards the demons, and this time he was determined to make them pay a heavy price!

Although he was likely just there to make a cameo appearance!

Rufeng nodded, also offering no objection. His granddaughter, Rushuang, had just married Gu Chen, and the Gu family's affairs were his affairs!

Besides, the demons were utterly detestable; he could only do his best to contribute!

When the news reached the front mountain, Gu Chen was taking a walk with Rushuang.

The two walked hand in hand beside the Spirit Liquid Waterfall.

Just then, Gu Tianhua approached. "Chen'er, you'll go to Zhongzhou in three days to represent the Gu family in joining the alliance."

Gu Chen was taken aback. "Me too?"

"The Ancestor said it's to let you go out and see the world."

Gu Tianhua patted his shoulder, lowering his voice, "And take your wife to Zhongzhou for a visit, like a honeymoon!"

Staying at the Gu family home all day, with so many people from the vassal states around, it's too embarrassing!

Wait, it'll make both of them feel awkward!

Gu Chen glanced at Ru Shuang.

Gu Tianhua smiled, turned, and left.

Ru Shuang lowered her head. "Me too?"

Gu Chen took her hand. "Let's go on our honeymoon together."

Ru Shuang nodded.

Suddenly, two little girls appeared out of nowhere, each grabbing one of Gu Chen's legs!

"Brother Gu Chen, are you going to Zhongzhou? We want to go too!"

Gu Chen had a headache. "What are you going there for?"

These two little girls never seem to be up to anything.

It wasn't that he didn't want to, but he couldn't keep an eye on those two little girls!

He himself wanted to go out and have fun with his wife!

"Play! It's so boring at the Gu family. Cultivating every day is making us stupid!"

Gu Qingqing tilted her head back, her eyes sparkling, looking quite pitiful!

Gu Huahua nodded in agreement, "Yes, yes, we haven't been out in ages. We didn't even have a proper time when we went to Zhongzhou last time!"

Ru Shuang smiled, "Take them with you."

The two little girls cheered and ran towards the back mountain.

"Ancestor, Ancestor, we're going to Zhongzhou too!"

Back mountain, Gu Xuan watched the two little girls run up and smiled.

Anyway, with so many people coming this time, two of them could keep an eye on them!

"Go on, stay with your brother Gu Chen, don't run around."

"We won't run around! We won't run around!" the two little girls said in unison.

Gu Xuan looked at Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue, "You two follow them."

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue nodded.

Three days later.

Outside the Gu family's mountain gate, a group of people were ready to depart. Jian Wuji stood at the front, with three on each side behind him.

Gu Chen and Ru Shuang sat on the Azure Dragon, while the two little girls rode the White Tiger!

The Phoenix stood on the tortoise shell, while the Black Tortoise couldn't stick its head out!

Xiaoyao and Rufeng stood to one side.

"Let's go." Jian Wuji turned and flew towards Zhongzhou.

The group followed behind, a grand and imposing procession.

In another grand hall!

The strongest figures from many forces appeared, including 18 peak Quasi-Emperor level experts!

Each one was incredibly powerful.

They argued for three days without reaching a conclusion.

The issue was the position of supreme ruler!

The Li family, the Ice Phoenix Clan, the Qi family, and a rogue cultivator at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm were arguing heatedly!

None would concede to the others.

Li Cangqiong, the head of the Li family, slammed his fist on the table and stood up. "The Li family has a lineage of Great Emperors spanning millions of years! In terms of foundation, strength, and prestige, the Li family is undoubtedly the rightful heir!"

Qi Tian, ​​the head of the Qi family, sneered, "The Li family has produced Great Emperors, and so has my Qi family! How many years has your Great Emperor passed away? My Qi family has two peak Quasi-Emperors under our command; does your Li family have any?"

Li Cangqiong's face darkened, and he was about to retort.

Bing Ru, the patriarch of the Ice Phoenix Clan, raised her hand. "Enough."

The two looked at her.

Bing Ru's tone was calm. "You've been arguing for three days already, haven't you had enough? The Demon Clan is coming soon, and you're still arguing about who should be the leader?"

Li Cangqiong snorted coldly, "Then tell me, who should be?"

Bing Ru did not answer.

She looked at Bingyun, then at Bingxin.

She pondered their descriptions after returning from the Gu family, and came to a conclusion!

That is, the Gu family has Great Emperors!

And more than one!

But she didn't say it in this setting, because no one would believe her!

A place that hadn't produced a Great Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years, suddenly having several appear—who would believe that?

“I recommend the Gu family,” Bingru said.

The hall was silent for a moment, then erupted in chaos!

“The Gu family? That small family from the Eastern Region?”

“Just a nouveau riche, what makes them worthy of being the leader?”

“Clan Chief Bing, are you kidding me?”

Li Cangqiong's face turned ashen. “Bingru, what do you mean? My Li family, the Qi family, which one isn't stronger than the Gu family? You're recommending an unknown nouveau riche?”

To them, three peak Quasi-Emperors were nothing more than nouveau riche!

Without the legacy of an Emperor, their foundation is significantly weaker!

They can only rely on the Emperor's legacy as their saving grace!

In this era of declining Dharma, breaking through to the Emperor level is difficult; this is their last vestige of dignity!

Qi Tian frowned, "Clan Chief Bing, although the Gu family has three peak Quasi-Emperors, in terms of foundation, legacy, and history, they are far inferior to my Qi family. Besides, you know the Gu family's attitude towards the Demon Clan—they just sent a few people to appease them. Can such a family be entrusted with anything?"

Bing Ru remained silent; their prejudices were entrenched!

These families are still stuck in traditional perceptions, judging people based on their past!

Even if someone else's young master won first place in this genius battle!

While they consider someone a genius, who in their era wasn't a genius?

Only those who survive can be called geniuses!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 96 This position is mine, Gu family's. Who can compete with it?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Li Cangqiong sneered, "So what if he has three peak Quasi-Emperors? My Li family has them, his Gu family has them, and my Li family has them too. Why should he be the leader?"

Qi Tian spoke calmly, "We don't deny the Gu family's strength, but you've all seen their attitude towards the Demon Clan—arrogant, indifferent. They don't even know how many people they'll send, and who knows when they'll arrive. Can you really entrust your lives to such a family?"

Some nodded, others echoed.

"Exactly, their attitude is too arrogant."

"They don't value the Demon Clan. How can we trust someone like that to be the leader?"

"They won't even show their face, yet they act more important than a Great Emperor!"

The murmurs grew louder.

Bing Ru raised her hand, stopping the discussion.

"The position of supreme ruler will be decided once everyone is present. The Gu family hasn't arrived yet; we'll discuss it when they do."

Li Cangqiong was displeased. "Wait? How long? The Demon Clan doesn't wait for anyone."

Bingru glanced at him. "We've waited three days already, what's a few more?"

Li Cangqiong snorted coldly and remained silent.

But discontent continued to spread.

Those dissatisfied with the Gu family were still whispering amongst themselves, but Bingru ignored them. It seemed they could only wait for the Gu family to appear!

Meanwhile, Jian Wuji and his group had arrived outside the city.

He concealed his aura, and the six people beside him followed suit.

The auras of the seven Great Emperors were suppressed to a minimum; outsiders couldn't sense them, they just appeared to be ordinary people!

The four divine beasts also shrunk in size, looking like ordinary demon beasts.

The group entered the city and headed towards the main hall.

Gu Chen walked in the middle, holding Ru Shuang's hand, with two little girls riding white tigers behind them.

Inside the hall, Bingyun suddenly looked up; she sensed that aura!

It was the aura of the Gu family.

“Clan leader, the Gu family has arrived.”

Bingru stood up. “Let’s go, greet them.”

Li Cangqiong frowned. “Greet them? Such high and mighty attitude?”

Bingru ignored him and walked out of the hall, Bingyun and Bingxin following behind.

Outside the hall, a group of people were walking towards them.

Seventeen people?

Bingru paused.

She had expected the Gu family to send many, at least a hundred, since other forces often sent thousands or even tens of thousands, all elites!

These were the top experts of those forces, each nurtured with countless resources!

Unexpectedly, the Gu family only sent seventeen.

Her heart sank; this would likely cause dissatisfaction among some!

She stepped forward, her gaze sweeping over the seventeen people.

Four Saints, one Quasi-Emperor at the Second Heaven, and she couldn't discern the remaining twelve!

Her divine sense couldn't even get close to those twelve!

She swallowed hard, unable to show her disbelief, and walked up to Gu Chen, bowing respectfully. "Young Master Gu, you've had a long journey."

Gu Chen was somewhat taken aback. "It's alright, it's alright."

Bing Ru led him into the hall.

Inside the hall, all eyes were on Gu Chen and his group.

Seventeen people, entering a hall that could hold tens of thousands, seemed remarkably ordinary.

Li Cangqiong frowned as he looked at the four Saints. "Just these few people?"

A person nearby whispered, "Four Saints, one Quasi-Emperor, and the other dozen or so look unremarkable! Is the Gu family just giving us the runaround?"

"The Ice Phoenix Clan even recommended them as their leader? Aren't they playing with our lives?"

At this moment, Li Cangqiong looked at Gu Chen and launched the first attack, "Young Master Gu, is this all the people the Gu family sent? Did they bring their wives here for their honeymoon?"

He continued, "My Li family has mobilized a large force this time, including three Quasi-Emperors. The Qi family and the Ice Phoenix Clan have also sent their elites. Not to mention the other forces, large and small. In contrast, your Gu family has only seventeen people. Are you here to fight or to sightsee?"

Gu Chen didn't understand what this meant.

After all, his ancestor hadn't told him anything before they came.

Why did this man's words feel like they were targeting the Gu family?

And the looks and whispers from those people made him very uncomfortable!

Could it be that the Gu family had done something wrong?

Wasn't it said that someone had invited them?

This has completely confused him.

Why are they acting like this?

At this moment, Bing Yun's voice rang in his ears, audible only to him.

"Young Master Gu, these past few days, the various families have been arguing fiercely over the position of Chieftain. The clan leader recommended the Gu family, but they feel the Gu family isn't qualified. They complain about your slow arrival, the small number of people you sent, and your arrogant attitude."

Gu Chen understood immediately!

They just think the Gu family isn't worthy, right?

He glanced at Li Cangqiong, then at Qi Tian. The two were looking down on him with condescending eyes, as if he were an ignorant junior!

Just then, Jian Wuji's voice rang in his mind, "Young Master, do you want to be the Chieftain?"

Gu Chen was stunned for a moment, then turned to look at Jian Wuji.

They're practically behaving themselves; why should they tolerate this?

Especially this guy, speaking so arrogantly. With Uncle Jian here, do you think I'm afraid of you?

The Ancestor said, don't tarnish the Gu family's reputation when you're out in the world!

The Gu family doesn't cause trouble, but it's not afraid of trouble either!

Besides, this time the people were all arranged by the Ancestor; what problems could possibly arise?

"Do they really think the Gu family is easy to bully just because they haven't shown their teeth?"

"Yes," Gu Chen said.

His voice wasn't loud, but everyone in the quiet hall heard him.

Li Cangqiong frowned. "What did you say?"

Gu Chen stood up and looked at him. "I said, our Gu family will be the ruler."

The hall erupted in chaos.

"You're a saint, what makes you think you can be the ruler?"

"The Gu family only sent so few people, and they're already worthy of being the ruler? Are you kidding me?"

"Young man, this isn't child's play, this is war! People will die!"

Li Cangqiong sneered, "Young Master Gu, you haven't grasped the situation. The position of ruler isn't something anyone can just take. You need strength, you need to be respected! Your Gu family has three peak Quasi-Emperors, which is indeed not weak, but which family here doesn't have peak Quasi-Emperors? What makes you think you can?"

"What makes me think I can?"

Gu Chen didn't speak, but Jian Wuji stepped forward.

All eyes fell on him!

This man, with a sword at his waist, was expressionless and looked utterly unremarkable!

He possessed none of the aura of a powerful figure.

Jian Wuji looked at Li Cangqiong, "You just said the Gu family only has three peak Quasi-Emperors?"

Li Cangqiong nodded, "What? Did I misspoke?"

Jian Wuji released a wisp of his aura—not his entire aura, but just a wisp!

A wisp of the aura of a Great Emperor at the peak of his power!

Then, like a mountain, it pressed down on the three peak Quasi-Emperors of the Li family!

The three of them immediately collapsed to the ground like dogs, unable to lift their heads!

Li Cangqiong's expression instantly darkened!

He sensed that the aura wasn't that of a Quasi-Emperor, but that of a Great Emperor!

This time, they'd really bitten off more than they could chew!

No! Not just iron, but steel!

He never expected that an obscure family would possess an Emperor!

No one like that had appeared on this continent for hundreds of thousands of years. He wondered how it could possibly be possible, but the aura didn't lie!

An Emperor was indeed an Emperor!

Not just a peak Quasi-Emperor, but someone far beyond that!

The numerous forces in the hall dared not speak, nor dared they speak up for the Li family!

With an Emperor appearing, who could contend with them?


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 97 Who's in favor? Who's against? We should kill a couple to whet our appetites.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Inside the hall, everyone else also sensed that aura.

Qi Tian's face turned ashen, and he staggered, kneeling uncontrollably!

Although his ancestors had produced a Great Emperor, that was history, only enough to intimidate those who didn't understand!

Now, a living Great Emperor stood before him—how could he not be afraid!

A Great Emperor could kill a peak Quasi-Emperor as easily as killing a chicken!

This Great Emperor had probably heard his words, and if he were to pursue him, he would truly be held accountable!

Everyone bowed their heads before that aura.

Those who had been disrespectful earlier were all kneeling on the ground.

And those who had laughed or whispered earlier were now speechless!

They didn't even have the courage to raise their heads.

Jian Wuji withdrew his aura.

He looked at the people kneeling on the ground and slowly spoke. "You mere Quasi-Emperor Peak cultivators dare to disrespect my Young Master?"

"My master said you are just cannon fodder. Your so-called alliance is nothing but a bunch of chickens and dogs!"

He paused, his gaze sweeping over the kneeling people, his eyes filled with no pity, only disdain!

A bunch of weaklings!

Come out and have a go!

"Those who are not convinced, come out and fight me one-on-one!"

No one dared to move!

Those Quasi-Emperor Peak cultivators didn't even dare to raise their heads!

One-on-one? Isn't that courting death?

Their cultivation level was no match for a Great Emperor; it was just a matter of killing them!

Gu Chen stood behind Jian Wuji. He knew Jian Wuji was strong, but he hadn't expected him to be this strong!

A mere wisp of his aura was enough to force a peak Quasi-Emperor to their knees!

Jian Wuji, who was usually quiet, suddenly seemed imposing!

He turned to look at Bing Yun. Bing Yun was still standing; Jian Wuji's aura wasn't directed at her, and her face only showed shock!

Bing Ru had guessed that the Gu family had Great Emperors, but she hadn't expected so many, let alone to be this strong!

Just what was the background of this Gu family?

Gu Chen looked around, his tone calm, "The position of the supreme ruler belongs to the Gu family. Who agrees? Who disagrees?"

Inside the hall, no one spoke, and no one dared to speak!

Gu Chen looked around, his gaze landing on Li Cangqiong, who was prostrate on the ground.

The once arrogant patriarch of the Li family was now groveling on the ground like a dog, trembling uncontrollably, too afraid to even raise his head!

Gu Chen's tone was indifferent, "Old man, do you still object?"

Li Cangqiong trembled, his voice shaking, "No, no objection! I, I wholeheartedly support the Gu family becoming the head of the family!!"

Gu Chen looked at him with interest, "No objection? Then why are your legs shaking? Do you have some grievance against my Gu family? You can say it!"

But you can't actually say it!

Li Cangqiong's face turned deathly pale, cold sweat dripping from his forehead onto the ground, "No, no, I, I'm just... just old, my old leg pain is acting up!"

"Then you'll have to get it treated properly, lest it become a chronic condition. If it doesn't work, come to my Gu family, our ancestor will help you cure it!"

Gu Chen's words sounded kind, but were full of threat!

Li Cangqiong naturally understood and quickly refused, "No, no! Thank you for your kindness, Young Master Gu!"

Gu Chen didn't look at him again, turning to look at Qi Tian!

Qi Tian's expression was even more unpleasant than Li Cangqiong's. Just now, he had looked at Gu Chen with such a gaze, as if he wanted to devour him; now he didn't dare to even breathe loudly!

Gu Chen stared at him. "What did that look in your eyes mean? Did you want to eat me alive?"

Qi Tian froze, his lips trembling for a long time before he managed to squeeze out a few words, "No, I wouldn't dare!"

"Wouldn't dare?" Gu Chen took a half-step forward.

Qi Tian instinctively shrank back.

"You weren't like this just now. You said the Gu family was arrogant and cold, sending a few people to fob you off. Why don't you say that now?"

Qi Tian was speechless!

He hadn't expected the other man to know everything, repeating every word of their conversation!

But what could he say?

Say the Gu family was indeed arrogant?

That would be suicide!

Say the Gu family wasn't arrogant?

That would be slapping himself in the face!

Their once-proud heritage and foundation shattered into pieces!

They never expected that this person wouldn't care about their heritage or foundation at all; this person was a true Great Emperor!

A living Great Emperor!

“My Gu family's actions are none of your business!”

Then, he turned to Jian Wuji, “Uncle Jian, what did the Ancestor say before he left?”

Jian Wuji took a step forward, standing beside Gu Chen, his gaze sweeping over the people kneeling on the ground.

“The Master said that the Gu family will not tolerate any threats when it is out in the world! Anyone who dares to disrespect the Gu family or threaten its interests can be shown what cruelty truly means!”

These words were meant to make an example of them!

Who was the chicken and who was the monkey here? It was obvious!

For a moment, those peak Quasi-Emperors dared not speak!

They had just been enjoying their moral blackmail, but they hadn't expected this person to be so ruthless!

With the appearance of a Great Emperor, they all fell silent!

After a while, Gu Chen spoke calmly, "Our Gu family is here to fight the Demon Clan, not to bully you. If anyone has any objections, feel free to speak up. Of course, I hope you have no objections!"

"If you don't, then keep quiet about the Gu family's actions!"

"The position of leader belongs to the Gu family. No one should object, right? Everyone agrees, right?"

Gu Xuan had told him before he left that he was representing the Gu family!

Anyone who disrespects the Gu family will not be tolerated!

Outside the city, the Gu family is the law!

The law will not tolerate being bullied!

He recalled the looks of those people when he entered the city—contempt, disdain, mockery!

And the whispers after entering the hall!

He heard them all clearly!

He didn't react then because the time wasn't right!

Now the time has come.

"Agreed!" Li Cangqiong was the first to shout.

"Agreed!" Qi Tian followed.

"Agreed!" Everyone shouted in unison.

The voices were uneven, but the meaning was clear—no one dared to object!

Gu Chen nodded, "Then it's settled!"

Li Cangqiong struggled to his feet, wiping the cold sweat from his forehead!

The others also looked grim, heads bowed, not daring to look at Gu Chen!

They were so arrogant just moments ago, now they were obedient!

"From now on, if anyone dares to disrespect the Gu family again, don't blame the Gu family for being ruthless!"

Having said that, he turned and walked towards the hall's exit, Ru Shuang following behind.

Outside the hall, Gu Chen took a deep breath and turned to look at Jian Wuji, "Uncle Jian, was I too harsh just now?"

Jian Wuji glanced at him, "Still too gentle."

Gu Chen smiled awkwardly, "This is gentle? Then this is how it should be done?"

Jian Wuji said very seriously, "These people aren't very strong. Let's kill a couple to whet our appetites, so no one will be reckless later!"

Gu Chen hadn't expected Jian Wuji to be even more ruthless than he imagined!

He actually said he'd kill a couple to whet our appetites!

Those were all at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm!

If Uncle Jian hadn't been there, they probably wouldn't have been able to withstand his oppressive aura!

Jian Wuji believed that verbal instruction was never as effective as practical combat!

To put it bluntly, people can't teach you everything, but you'll learn something after just one demonstration!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 98 Plundering Humanity's Luck? The two little girls: What? Another pet?
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Xiaoyao was increasingly baffled by the Gu family's actions!

Back when he was the Great Emperor, he had to rally these forces to fight against the Demon Clan!
