However, these people, driven by self-interest, were rotten to the core and certainly needed a good spur!

Ru Shuang, walking beside Gu Chen, couldn't help but laugh, "You were very impressive just now, very much the aura of a strongman."

Gu Chen smiled, "I'll be even more impressive in the future."

Then he whispered in her ear, "Tonight I'll show you the impressive strength of a true warrior!"

Ru Shuang blushed instantly!

She couldn't let anyone else hear that!

She patted his chest and turned to walk aside!

Ru Feng found the couple's teasing style quite unique, but it was fine!

Enjoying life! Enjoying life!

Just then, a communication talisman pierced the air and landed in Bing Yun's hand!

Bing Yun scanned it with her divine sense, and her expression changed drastically!

She quickly walked to Bingru's side and whispered a few words.

"What did you say?" Bingru's voice was very low.

Leading them is the king of the Northwest Demonic Beast Forest, the Northern Dragon King! His strength has reached the peak of Quasi-Emperor level, and he's followed by six other Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts, no more than ten thousand miles away!"

Upon hearing this, the hall erupted in chaos!

"Demonic beasts? How could they appear at this time?"

"The Northern Dragon King? That old monster who has lived for ten thousand years? How could he be here?"

"And six more Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts? Including the Northern Dragon King, that makes seven!"

"Demonic beasts are physically stronger than humans. At the same level, one Quasi-Emperor level demonic beast requires at least three human Quasi-Emperors to contend with. With seven of them, we need twenty-one!"

They only have eighteen Quasi-Emperors, which is clearly insufficient!

Demonic beasts and humans are mortal enemies. Even if humans invited those demonic beasts, they would never agree!

"Why haven't the demons arrived yet, but the monsters have?"

"Is the heavens trying to destroy our Ancient Desolate Continent?"

Qi Tian suddenly spoke up, "No, this is too coincidental! The demons just made a move, and the monsters have already arrived. Attacking us from both sides simultaneously is very abnormal!"

Li Cangqiong frowned, "You mean, the demons and monsters have joined forces?"

Qi Tian shook his head, "Monsters and humans are mortal enemies, and they are also mortal enemies of the demons. Monsters wouldn't join forces with the demons unless the demons offered them some benefits."

Everyone fell silent!

This conjecture was even more terrifying than the demons and monsters joining forces!

If the demons could use benefits to motivate the monsters, then the Ancient Desolate Continent would not only face a two-front war, but would be attacked from both sides!

Gu Chen stood outside the hall, listening to the discussions inside. He turned to look at Xiaoyao.

The ancestor had said that he was a Great Emperor from hundreds of thousands of years ago, and also one of those who fought against the demons last time.

He must know quite a bit about this.

"Senior Xiaoyao, what's going on with the demonic beasts? Why did they choose this time?"

Xiaoyao pondered for a moment, then slowly replied, "It's likely the demon race is behind this. They want us to fight the demonic beasts first, to deplete our strength. After all, they won't be able to arrive for some time, and they can't let humans have an easy time."

Gu Chen was puzzled. Logically, demonic beasts and the demon race shouldn't have a good relationship.

Why did they seem to be joining forces?

Xiaoyao continued, "However, the demonic beasts' intentions are probably not so simple. They're not here to help the demon race; they're more likely here to reap benefits."

"Benefits? What do you mean?"

Xiaoyao looked up at the distant sky, "Luck, the luck of our human race."

Gu Chen was taken aback. "Luck?"

"You can't see or touch it, but it certainly exists," Xiaoyao explained.

"When a race's fortune is abundant, geniuses emerge in droves, and powerful figures abound. But once their fortune declines, talent dwindles, and powerful figures become scarce!"

"Hundreds of thousands of years ago, I encountered something similar. A quasi-Emperor level demon beast tried to plunder the human race's fortune to break through to the Great Emperor realm. I intervened and killed it."

He paused. "It's even harder for demon beasts to break through to the Great Emperor realm than for humans. In this era of declining spiritual energy and hidden laws, it's almost impossible for demon beasts to break through to the Great Emperor realm. But there is one way: plundering the human race's fortune. As long as the fortune is strong enough, they can force a breakthrough, even if it's only a temporary Great Emperor realm. That's enough for them to traverse the void, leave this continent, and find a new place to live."

Gu Chen was somewhat surprised; he hadn't expected such a saying!

There were still so many things he didn't know. It seemed his ancestor was right; he needed to travel more and see the world!

This also explained why those demon beasts came.

They wanted to use the fact that the Demon Race was behind them as a cover for plundering the human race's fortune!

"You mean, those demonic beasts want to plunder the human race's fortune to break through to the Great Emperor realm, and then run away with their people?"

Xiaoyao nodded. "Very likely, and I suspect that demons have already appeared in their territory. Otherwise, they wouldn't be in such a hurry, so blatantly. This must be related to the demons."

Gu Chen turned to Jian Wuji. "Uncle Jian, what do you think?"

Jian Wuji's tone was calm. "Seven Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts aren't enough."

Seven Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts, not enough? Only Uncle Jian would say that!

Just as he was about to speak, two little girls suddenly appeared, grabbing Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue's clothes from either side!

"Sister, sister!" Gu Qingqing looked up. "What demonic beasts? Do they have new pets?"

Gu Huahua nodded in agreement. "Yes, yes, we're tired of playing with the white tiger. All it does is sleep all day, it's not fun at all!"

The white tiger, lying not far away, twitched its ears. Was sleeping a crime?

"It's all because of their constant bickering! Even a mighty emperor's face gets scrunched up from their pulling, he looks like he's asleep!"

But he had nothing to say!

This was his master's precious darling; the White Tiger dared not speak!

Gu Qingqing continued shaking Yue Qingshuang's arm, "Sister, is that Northern Dragon King you mentioned stronger than the White Tiger? Can we catch it and keep it as a pet?"

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue couldn't help but laugh, "That's not a pet, it's a Quasi-Emperor peak-level demonic beast."

"What's a Quasi-Emperor peak-level? Is it very powerful?"

Yue Qingxue thought for a moment, "A little weaker than the White Tiger."

The White Tiger raised its head and glared at Yue Qingxue.

Was it really just a little weaker?

"They're worlds apart!"

"Who do they think they are?!"

Yue Qingxue pretended not to see.

"That's perfect! A little weaker than the White Tiger, but definitely smarter than the Black Tortoise!"

The Black Tortoise poked its head out of its shell, glanced at Gu Qingqing, and then retreated back inside.

"What the heck?

Why am I involved?

I didn't do anything, so why am I in this?"

To these two little girls, these weren't demonic beasts at all, but new pets!

They were already tired of the three they'd acquired before and needed other pets!

It's just that their pets were slightly stronger than normal pets!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 99 The Demon Beast Alliance, A Mighty Army Presses In
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Gu Chen looked at the two little girls, his head throbbing with headache.

“That’s a peak Quasi-Emperor level demonic beast, not a pet. Do you know that? One swipe of its claw could kill you both!”

These two girls really treated these two like babysitters!

They’re so shameless!

For them, this was far too easy. A few peak Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts, right?

If these two little girls wanted, they could catch them anytime!

They could even catch some weaker Great Emperor level demonic beasts!

Then, the two girls vanished instantly!

No sound of air being torn apart, no fluctuation of spiritual energy—they simply disappeared into thin air!

Where did they go?

So fast!

Gu Chen was stunned. "Them?"

Jian Wuji replied calmly, "They went to catch pets."

Xiaoyao, standing to the side, was speechless. What was going on?

The little girl from the Gu family had taken a liking to these demonic beasts and wanted to keep them as pets!

These were, after all, peak Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts.

But that didn't seem so bad, considering the Four Divine Beasts were still guarding the Gu family's gate!

These peak Quasi-Emperor level demonic beasts were nothing compared to the Four Divine Beasts!

Those were truly top-tier bloodlines, possessing inherited memories and other powerful attributes!

And not just one, but four!

Gu Qingqing clapped her hands excitedly, "My sisters are the best!"

"I want that Dragon King, it sounds so majestic!"

Gu Chen sighed, speechless.

Northwest Land, Demonic Beast Camp.

The Northern Dragon King sat coiled atop a mountain peak, with six figures flanking him on either side.

Each one was at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm, each a ruler of their own domain.

The Wolf King of the Western Desert, the Tiger King of the Southern Frontier, the Flood Dragon Sovereign of the Eastern Sea, the Bear Emperor of the Northern Plains, the Saint of the Central Cloud Sparrow, and the Netherworld Cat Queen.

Seven demon beast kings, gathered together!

Behind them were countless demon beasts, enough to devastate any major continent!

Those demon beasts awaited the command of these seven demon beast kings; at their command, they would attack!

A vast and unstoppable force!

The Southern Tiger King spoke first, "Our whereabouts have probably been discovered."

The Western Desert Wolf King scoffed, "So what if they've discovered us? In this Dharma-ending Age, it's difficult to become a Great Emperor. Even among humans, there are barely any at the peak of Quasi-Emperor level. What can they possibly use to stop us?"

The Northern Plains Bear Emperor shook his head, "Don't underestimate us. If the human race has a Great Emperor, the seven of us might not be enough."

The Western Desert Wolf King sneered, "A Great Emperor of the human race? Hundreds of thousands of years ago, there was Xiaoyao. And then? Who else?"

The Central Cloud Sparrow Saint also spoke, "The Xiaoyao Great Emperor fought five against one, killing three and injuring two of the Demon Emperors of the Demon Race, but he himself was also seriously injured and died. For hundreds of thousands of years, the human race hasn't produced a Great Emperor! Becoming a Great Emperor is now harder than ascending to heaven. It's impossible for the human race to have one!"

The Northern Dragon King nodded, "The Bear Emperor is overthinking it! The Xiaoyao Great Emperor has been dead for hundreds of thousands of years. Those imperial clans of the human race may have had Great Emperors in their ancestors, but that's all ancient history! Nowadays, even peak Quasi-Emperor level humans are extremely rare. What can they possibly use to fight us?"

The East Sea Flood Dragon Lord suddenly spoke up, “You can’t say that. Although the human race hasn’t produced a Great Emperor, their heritage remains. Each Genius Battle is like a selection of successors, and this year’s champion is said to have obtained a portion of Xiaoyao’s heritage.”

The Northern Dragon King, however, disagreed. “A portion of the heritage? In this Dharma-ending Age, even with a complete heritage, one can’t become a Great Emperor! Let alone a portion?”

Everyone agreed. In an era of abundant spiritual energy, they could become Great Emperors without any heritage!

But now, with the Dharma-ending Age upon us, the peak of Quasi-Emperor is the highest realm they can reach!

Although it’s difficult for them, as demon beasts, to become Great Emperors, they could break through in an environment rich in spiritual energy!

They might even be three points stronger than humans after breaking through!

As for the so-called heritage, it’s dispensable to them. Even if they obtained it, what difference would it make?

Without sufficient spiritual energy, it’s impossible to break through to Great Emperor!

Moreover, the appearance of the Demon Race has already caused widespread panic among humans, preventing them from settling down!

This is undoubtedly a very good opportunity for them!

Plundering the human race's destiny, forcibly becoming an emperor!

Then leading their people across the void, they could leave this place, avoiding both the demon race and their masters' desperate battles!

This was both an opportunity and a challenge for them!

After all, the human race was not to be trifled with; to seize their destiny, a bloody battle was inevitable!

The Northern Bear Emperor remained silent for a moment. "Then let's fight! These humans have slaughtered so many of our people; it's time they paid the price!"

The Western Desert Wolf King echoed, "Fight! Those human cultivators kill any of our demon beasts they see, skinning and butchering them, using them for alchemy and weapon crafting. How many of my people have died at their hands?"

The Central Cloud Sparrow Saint said softly, "We must fight, but not in a direct confrontation. Our goal is to seize the Qi Luck. Once we plunder enough Qi Luck, we can break through to the Great Emperor realm and leave this continent. The Demon Clan's passage is about to open; we can't stay here."

The Netherworld Cat Queen nodded. "Someone from the Demon Clan has already contacted me. They promised not to touch our territory before the passage opens, on the condition that we hold off the humans."

The Northern Dragon King sneered, "The Demon Clan isn't any better. Once they open the passage, they'll probably be the first to wipe us out! So we must plunder enough Qi Luck before the Demon Clan arrives, break through to the Great Emperor realm, and traverse the void!"

Following this, several demon beasts expressed their opinions.

The seven quasi-Emperor peak demon beasts were of one mind: fight!

The Northern Dragon King surveyed his surroundings. "It's settled then. The final assault in three days. Humans are in front, demons behind. We have no way out!"

"Remember, don't linger! Our goal is fortune, not slaughter. Plunder enough fortune, then retreat immediately. Don't give the humans a chance to breathe, and don't give the demons an opening!"

The Western Desert Wolf King nodded. "Understood. I'll lead the wolf vanguard and tear through the human defenses!"

The Southern Tiger King also nodded. "I'll lead the main attack, a frontal assault!"

The Northern Plains Bear Emperor muttered, "I'll cover the rear, preventing the humans from flanking us."

The Eastern Sea Flood Dragon Venerable, the Central Cloud Sparrow Saint, and the Netherworld Cat Queen also received their respective tasks.

The Northern Dragon King stood up, his aura at the peak of the Quasi-Emperor realm overwhelming, his eyes flashing with cold light. "Move out!"

The demon army moved!

A vast, dark mass, blotting out the sky, surged towards the Central Plains!

Wherever they passed, the earth shook, and countless beasts stampeded!

Seven demon beast kings stood at the forefront, looking down at their countless kinsmen, their spirits soaring.

Yet, they were also filled with worry. This journey was fraught with danger, but they had no other choice!

Ahead lay the humans, behind lay the demons; death awaited them on both sides, and they gained nothing from either!


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Chapter 100: Those who took you in as pets, I slapped them into a bloody mist.
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Just then, a scout from a demonic beast flew in from the front, then tumbled and crawled to the ground in front of the Northern Dragon King!

"Aura? What about it?"

The scout swallowed hard, his face deathly pale. "The aura is too strong! Your subordinate... your subordinate has never sensed such a powerful aura! It's probably even stronger than Your Majesty!"

The Northern Dragon King was shocked. Stronger than him?

He was a peak Quasi-Emperor. Stronger than him? That meant a Great Emperor?

That was impossible. The human race hadn't produced a Great Emperor for hundreds of thousands of years!

There wouldn't be a Great Emperor now!

It was most likely a powerful human cultivator.

The Western Desert Wolf King spoke, "Could it be a human spy?"

The Southern Frontier Tiger King shook his head, "Spies wouldn't be so blatant. Besides, with an army of millions of demon beasts, spies should come stealthily, not fly straight at us."

"Then who is it?" the Northern Plains Bear Emperor asked疑惑地.

Someone actually dared to fly so boldly towards their demon beast army!

Keep in mind, they had seven peak Quasi-Emperor demon beasts and hundreds of other Quasi-Emperor demon beasts—their strength was immense!

Now, two auras were flying towards them; this was practically courting death!

But it didn't matter!

With so many demon beasts, he refused to believe that just these two could overthrow his entire demon beast army!

The Northern Dragon King pondered for a moment, "We'll know who it is once they arrive. Order the army to halt its advance and prepare for battle!"

The demon beast army halted, millions of demon beasts standing ready.

Seven demon beast kings stood at the forefront, their eyes fixed on the horizon!

A moment later, two figures appeared in the distance!

It was two women!

It was Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue!

At the same time, they deliberately released some of their auras, summoning these demonic beasts!

They've come to subdue you!

The demonic beasts were astonished for a moment; the human race had only sent two women!

It seemed the human race had no one left to rely on!

But soon, the demonic beasts regretted their decision!

They moved with incredible speed, closing the distance in the blink of an eye!

The Northern Dragon King probed with his divine sense, and then his expression changed. His divine sense couldn't even get close to the two women!

It was as if an invisible wall was blocking his way, repelling his divine sense.

The Northern Dragon King's heart sank. "Everyone, prepare for battle!"

The auras of the seven demonic beast kings were released simultaneously, the overwhelming pressure of a quasi-emperor peak overwhelming!

Millions of demonic beasts also released their auras, their killing intent soaring to the heavens. Such a spectacle was enough to terrify any human army!

But the two figures didn't stop; they drew closer and brighter.

Then, they vanished in an instant!

The Northern Dragon King was utterly shocked; he couldn't detect their presence!

The other demonic beasts couldn't detect their presence either!

"What happened?"

The scout's voice trembled. "It's that kind of disappearance! Those two human women just now vanished into thin air like that too!"

Everyone's faces turned grim!

Vanishing into thin air, not teleportation, without spatial fluctuations, without any residual spiritual energy!

"Watch out!" the Netherworld Cat Queen suddenly shouted.

Two female figures emerged from the void!

Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue stood in the void, overlooking the millions of demonic beasts below.

The Northern Dragon King looked up at them, "Who are you?"

Yue Qingshuang smiled, "We've come to take you as pets!"

The seven demonic beast kings' expressions changed simultaneously!

Seven quasi-Emperor level peak cultivators, with millions of demonic beasts behind them, their aura overwhelming, yet they were blocked halfway there by two people!

And these two actually said they'd come to take them as pets!

It was an absolute joke!

Each of them was a powerful demonic beast king, a force to be reckoned with. To make them pets?

Absolutely impossible!

These two women were blatantly insulting them, practically asking for trouble!

They never expected the humans to underestimate them so much, sending two to their deaths!

The Western Desert Wolf King sneered, "Take us as pets? You think you're worthy?"

The Central Cloud Sparrow Saint said softly, "Ladies, leave now, and we can pretend nothing happened."

The demonic beasts all had eyes that looked like they wanted to devour them!

Although they couldn't sense the two's aura just now, don't forget they outnumbered them!

Numbers matter!

Numbers give you confidence!

The Northern Dragon King raised his hand, stopping the crowd.

He warily stared at Yue Qingshuang and Yue Qingxue, and said in a deep voice, "I don't know who you two are, but there are only two of you. We are seven Quasi-Emperors at the peak of our power, with millions of troops! What do you have to offer?"

Yue Qingxue smiled, "We can use our hands."

The Northern Dragon King's face darkened!

These humans are truly arrogant!

Extremely arrogant, and conceited!

The Western Desert Wolf King could no longer hold back, "Why waste words with them? Just kill them!"

He sprang up, transforming into a black shadow, and pounced on Yue Qingxue!

The speed of a peak Quasi-Emperor erupted at full power, so fast that even space trembled!

The wolf claw tore through the void, its sharp barbs piercing the air, aimed directly at Yue Qingxue's face!

The power of this claw was enough to rip everything apart!

Yue Qingxue glanced at him disdainfully, raised her hand, and lightly waved it!

The movement was light, but when that palm struck, the heavens and earth changed color!

Where the palm's wind passed, space shattered inch by inch, revealing pitch-black void fissures!

The Western Desert Wolf King was terrified!

He used all his strength but couldn't escape!

That palm struck him!

It transformed into countless fine blood mists, scattering in the air!

In an instant, the peak Quasi-Emperor Western Desert Wolf King didn't even have time to scream before being directly crushed into blood mist!

Hiss!!

The millions of demon beasts gasped in shock!

My God!

One slap killed their demon king!

That was a peak Quasi-Emperor!

She instantly turned him into a bloody mist!

Leaving nothing behind!

How could they possibly fight now?

Humans were so strong, what was the point of fighting?

The remaining six demon kings turned ashen-faced. These two young girls weren't going to back down; they'd made an example of him!

And their power had shattered their understanding!

A slap that could kill a peak Quasi-Emperor was definitely a Great Emperor!

Turning him into a bloody mist was definitely a high-level Great Emperor!

These two weren't Quasi-Emperors; they were Great Emperors!

Living Great Emperors!

When they said they wanted to take them as pets, they weren't humiliating them; they were simply stating the facts!

Yue Qingxue withdrew her hand, glanced at her palm, and said calmly, "My young lady doesn't like disobedient pets."

Then, the two unleashed the terrifying pressure of Great Emperors at their peak!

Instantly, the million-strong army was pinned down, unable to raise their heads!

Even the remaining six Quasi-Emperor peak demonic beasts were unable to lift their heads!

Yue Qingxue looked at the six Quasi-Emperor peak beasts, "Do you six have any objections? My young lady wants a new pet, and you're quite something."

The Northern Dragon King's face darkened completely.

He, the mighty Northern Dragon King, had lived for tens of thousands of years, dominating a region, and now he was to be treated like a pet by a woman?

But he dared not move, for standing before him were two Great Emperors!

He had clearly witnessed the fate of the Western Desert Wolf King!

A single slap to a Quasi-Emperor peak beast, reduced to ashes!

Not even a trace remained!
